### [97] [A Two-level Filtered Subspace Iterative Method for Generalized Hermitian Interior Eigenvalue Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03804)
*Qi Li,Shi Shu,Xiaofei Xu,Juan Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出两级滤波子空间迭代(TLFSI)方法，用于求解自伴PDE特征值问题离散后区间$[a,b]$内的全部特征对，以围道积分滤波为例，理论上证明近似不变子空间与目标子空间的距离仅取决于目标谱区与其余谱的分离度及粗网格尺寸$H$，弹性问题数值实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自伴PDE特征值问题经有限元离散后，需要高效计算给定区间$[a,b]$内的所有特征对。传统滤波子空间迭代在大规模问题上收敛慢、计算代价高，而单纯两级方法对目标不变子空间的近似精度缺乏理论保证，因此需要将两级思想与现代滤波子空间迭代框架结合，以在降低计算成本的同时获得可控的近似精度。

Method: 提出两级滤波子空间迭代(TLFSI)方法，将两级方法与现代滤波子空间迭代框架相结合。以围道积分滤波作为具体滤波手段；其中两级方法根据目标特征值的谱位置构造粗空间，并由该粗空间得到目标不变子空间的近似。

Result: 理论上证明了近似不变子空间与目标不变子空间之间的距离仅依赖于目标谱区与其余谱之间的分离程度，以及粗网格尺寸$H$。在基准弹性问题上的数值实验验证了上述理论预测。

Conclusion: TLFSI方法能够有效计算区间$[a,b]$内的全部特征对，其近似精度由谱分离度与粗网格尺寸$H$共同决定，理论分析与数值实验一致，为自伴PDE特征值问题提供了一种兼具效率与可控精度的求解途径。

Abstract: We propose a two-level filtered subspace iterative (TLFSI) method for computing all eigenpairs whose eigenvalues in the interval of interest $[a,b]$ after finite element discretization of self-adjoint PDE eigenvalue problems. The method combines a two-level approach with a modern filtered subspace iteration framework. In this paper, we use contour integral filtering as an example. The two-level method constructs a coarse space according to the spectral location of the eigenvalues of interest and obtains from it an approximation to the target invariant subspace. We prove that the distance between the approximate and target subspaces depends only on the separation between the spectral region of interest and the remainder of the spectrum, together with the coarse mesh size \(H\). Numerical experiments on benchmark elasticity problems confirm the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [98] [On the involution preserving properties of semi-discrete Active Flux methods on simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04349)
*Remi Abgrall,Yongle Liu,Mario Ricchiuto*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究非结构三角网格上Active Flux方法的保对合性质，针对二维线性声学系统（速度场curl对合）和二维Maxwell方程（divergence对合），推导出被格式精确保持的离散curl算子和离散divergence算子的显式公式，适用于一般单纯形网格及任意多项式次数，并通过三阶数值实验验证理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：单一的离散化如何同时满足守恒形式和对合（involution）约束。现有方法在非结构网格上如何兼顾守恒性与对合的精确保持尚不清晰。

Method: 在单纯形上的离散Active Flux框架内，针对两个可写成守恒形式的模型问题（二维线性声学系统满足速度场的curl对合，二维含面内磁场的Maxwell方程满足divergence对合），推导出离散curl算子和离散divergence算子的显式表达式；分析覆盖一般单纯形网格和任意多项式次数，并扩展到三阶以上的高阶方法。

Result: 推导出的离散curl算子被声学格式精确保持，离散divergence算子被Maxwell系统精确保持；结论适用于一般单纯形网格和任意多项式次数；三阶数值实验证实了理论结果。

Conclusion: Active Flux方法在非结构三角网格上能够同时保持守恒形式与离散对合，所给出的显式离散curl/divergence算子为高阶推广提供了理论基础，并得到数值验证。

Abstract: We study the involution-preserving properties of the Active Flux method on unstructured triangular meshes. Two model problems are considered: the two-dimensional linear acoustic system, which satisfies a curl involution on the velocity field, and the two-dimensional Maxwell equations with an in-plane magnetic field, which satisfy a divergence involution. Both systems can be written in conservative form. The question we address is how a single discretization can satisfy the conservative form and the involution at the same time. Within a discrete Active Flux framework on simplices, we derive explicit formulas for a discrete curl operator that is exactly preserved by the scheme for acoustics, and for a discrete divergence operator preserved for the Maxwell system. The analysis applies to general simplex meshes and to any polynomial degree, including extensions of the method beyond third order. Numerical experiments with the third-order scheme confirm the theoretical results.
  Highlights: 1-Active Flux on unstructured triangular meshes preserves discrete involutions, 2-Explicit discrete curl operator preserved by the scheme for linear acoustics, 3- Explicit discrete divergence operator preserved for 2D Maxwell equations, 4- Results hold on general simplex meshes,% and for any polynomial degree 5- Third-order numerical experiments confirm the theoretical results. \end{itemize}

</details>


### [99] [Inference of Hamiltonian and Lie-Poisson structures from vector field samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04350)
*Jason E. Frank,Georg A. Gottwald*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出从向量场样本数据驱动推断哈密顿与Lie-Poisson动力系统的方法，联合估计哈密顿量与泊松张量，并在Lorenz-86和Kirchhoff刚体系统上高精度恢复结构常数与Casimir不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统哈密顿系统辨识通常假设泊松结构已知，限制了方法的适用范围；本文旨在从数据中同时推断哈密顿量与未知的泊松/辛结构，覆盖已知结构、常值结构矩阵及Lie-Poisson/仿射Poisson等递增一般性的情形。

Method: 哈密顿量用固定随机内权重的随机特征映射线性组合表示，使估计化为线性回归。处理三类情形：已知泊松张量、未知常值可逆（辛）结构矩阵、结构与状态线性相关的Lie-Poisson与仿射Poisson结构。常值情形下通过特征值问题联合恢复哈密顿量与结构矩阵，避免平凡零解；Lie-Poisson/仿射情形则交替进行基于广义特征值问题的近似首次积分哈密顿初估与结构常数的约束非线性最小二乘拟合，通过罚参数延拓强制满足Jacobi恒等式及仿射情形的余循环条件。用正交匹配追踪选取紧凑且良态的随机特征子集。因哈密顿量为单特征项之和，可显式构造Poisson/辛分裂积分器用于长时间Poincaré截面验证。

Result: 在四维和五维Lorenz-86模型及六维Kirchhoff刚体系统上验证，高精度恢复哈密顿量、结构常数与Casimir不变量；推断的Poincaré截面随随机特征数增加收敛到正确的不变环面拓扑。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了哈密顿量与泊松结构的联合数据驱动辨识，适用于多类泊松结构，并通过可积分裂积分器与长时间Poincaré截面验证了学习系统的保结构正确性，为无先验结构知识的动力系统辨识提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We present a method for the data-driven inference of Hamiltonian and Lie-Poisson dynamical systems from vector-field samples, in which the Hamiltonian and the underlying Poisson tensor are estimated jointly rather than assuming the structure is known. The Hamiltonian is represented as a linear combination of random feature maps with fixed random internal weights, so its estimation is a linear regression problem for any given Poisson structure. We treat three cases of increasing generality: a known Poisson tensor, an unknown constant invertible (symplectic) structure matrix, and Lie-Poisson and affine Poisson structures, with structure matrix linear in the state. In the constant case, the Hamiltonian and structure matrix are recovered jointly from an eigenvalue problem that avoids the trivial zero solution due to the scaling symmetry of Hamiltonian systems. For Lie-Poisson and affine Poisson systems we alternate between an initial Hamiltonian estimate from a generalized eigenvalue problem for an approximate first integral, and a constrained nonlinear least-squares fit of the structure constants that enforces the Jacobi identity and, for affine structures, the cocycle condition, via continuation in a penalty parameter. Orthogonal matching pursuit selects a compact, well-conditioned subset of random features. Because the learned Hamiltonian is a sum of single-feature terms, the model admits an explicit Poisson/symplectic splitting integrator, which we use to validate the learned systems via long-time Poincare sections. We demonstrate the approach on the four- and five-dimensional Lorenz-86 model and a six-dimensional Kirchhoff rigid-body system, recovering Hamiltonians, structure constants and Casimir invariants to high accuracy, and showing the inferred Poincare sections converge to the correct invariant-tori topology as the number of random features increases.

</details>


### [100] [Operator splitting methods for non-autonomous evolution equations comprising three parts, with application to the Bloch-Torrey equation of MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04355)
*Barbara Kaltenbacher,Pablo Muñoz,Mechthild Thalhammer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文设计并分析了针对非自治演化方程的Lie-Trotter分裂格式，采用两种方法：完全非自治格式与基于Magnus积分子的分裂格式。在一般框架下推导了一阶收敛性，并应用于磁共振成像中的Bloch-Torrey方程，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非自治演化方程的时间离散问题，希望利用某些子问题的显式解来构造高效的分裂格式，并借助Magnus积分子思想简化算子处理，同时为Bloch-Torrey方程这类具有明确物理算子分解的方程提供收敛性保证。

Method: 提出两种Lie-Trotter分裂格式：1）完全非自治格式，动机在于可利用某些子问题的显式解；2）基于Magnus积分子概念的分裂格式，在每个时间区间起点冻结算子，因为这对Lie-Trotter积分子的一阶收敛已足够。在一般框架下进行收敛性分析，并将其应用于可分解为三个子问题的Bloch-Torrey方程，数值上结合有限元空间离散进行时间步进。

Result: 在一般设定下证明了一阶收敛性；将分析应用于磁共振成像的Bloch-Torrey方程，其按算子物理意义分解为三个子问题；数值测试（时间步进结合有限元空间离散）验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种Lie-Trotter分裂格式对非自治演化方程具有一阶收敛性，适用于具有物理动机算子分解的Bloch-Torrey方程，理论与数值结果一致。

Abstract: In this paper we devise and analyze Lie Trotter splitting schemes for non-autonomous evolution equations, following two approaches: 1) A fully non-autonomous scheme, motivated by the potential use of explicit solutions of some subproblems. 2) A splitting scheme relying on the concept of Magnus integrators, where we freeze the operators at the beginning of each time interval as this suffices for first order convergence of the Lie-Trotter integrator. We derive first order convergence in a general setting and apply the analysis to the Bloch-Torrey equation of magnetic resonance imaging, where a decomposition into three subproblems is well-motivated by the physical role of the underlying operators. Numerical tests, complementing the time stepping scheme by a finite element space discretization, illustrate the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [101] [Conditioning and Singular Value Distribution of Jacobi Weighted Histopolation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04383)
*Allal Guessab,Federico Nudo,Stefano Serra-Capizzano*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究Jacobi加权直方图插值产生的未缩放矩阵的奇异值分布，利用GLT理论显式识别其符号，并刻画符号零点集及其测度，据此判定问题的离散病态性与条件数增长行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已证明在α,β>1/2及拟一致网格下矩阵增长慢于对数任意正幂，但数值行为暗示存在非平凡的奇异值符号，需显式识别该符号并处理任意α,β>-1的情形。

Method: 从Jacobi多项式的标准渐近公式出发，推导定义直方图插值矩阵元素的加权单元平均的渐近表示，进而借助广义定位（GLT）理论分析相应的Gram序列，识别其GLT符号。

Result: 得到任意α,β>-1下直方图插值矩阵的奇异值分布；刻画符号零点集Z并计算其测度，发现Z仅依赖网格，而Jacobi参数仅影响符号在支撑上的取值。当Z测度为正时蕴含离散渐近病态性，且与Toeplitz情形比较暗示谱条件数指数增长；当Z测度为零时问题适定，条件数呈代数增长。

Conclusion: 数值实验支持理论结论，文末给出结论及若干开放问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we first study the singular value distribution of the unscaled matrices arising from Jacobi weighted histopolation. For the Jacobi weight with parameters $(α,β)$, and using the Jacobi basis with parameters $(α+1,β+1)$, we previously proved that, for $α,β>1/2$ and quasi-uniform meshes, the growth of these matrices in the sense of singular value distribution is slower than any positive power of the logarithm of the matrix size. However, the numerical behavior of the unscaled matrices suggested a nontrivial singular value symbol, which we identify explicitly in this paper. Starting from the standard asymptotic formula for Jacobi polynomials, we derive an asymptotic representation of the weighted cell averages defining the entries of the histopolation matrices. This representation allows us to analyze the associated Gram sequence by means of GLT theory. We identify its GLT symbol and, consequently, we obtain the singular value distribution of the histopolation matrices for any $α,β>-1$. We also characterize the set $\mathcal Z$ where the resulting symbol vanishes and compute its measure, showing that this set depends only on the mesh, while the Jacobi parameters affect the values of the symbol on its support. When this measure is positive, the singular value distribution implies the discrete asymptotic ill-posedness of the problem, while a comparison with the Toeplitz setting suggests exponential growth of the spectral condition number. When the measure of $\mathcal Z$ is zero, the problem is well-posed and the observed conditioning is algebraic in the matrix size. Finally, numerical experiments supporting the theoretical findings are reported and discussed, while conclusions are given in the final section of the current work together with a few open problems.

</details>


### [102] [Arbitrarily High-Order Structure-Preserving Parametric Finite Element Methods for Geometric PDEs via Local Pullback Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04389)
*Weizhu Bao,Yifei Li,Dongmin Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文基于局部拉回流映射，为表面扩散和保体积平均曲率流等几何偏微分方程构造了任意高阶的保结构参数化有限元方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何偏微分方程（如表面扩散、保体积平均曲率流）的数值模拟需要同时具备高阶精度与保结构（保面积/体积、耗散周长/表面积）性质，已有方法往往难以兼得，因此有必要发展高阶且结构保持的离散格式。

Method: 在任意中间超曲面上重写几何偏微分方程，并用局部拉回流映射表示后续演化；基于Laplace-Beltrami算子的拉回恒等式，用容许局部拉回导出共形（conformal）与等分布（equidistribution）两种形式；两种形式均采用任意次等参有限元与正权Runge-Kutta配点法离散。

Result: 共形形式中，法向-雅可比向量的加权平均保证封闭面积或体积的精确保持，Runge-Kutta方法的代数稳定性进一步保证周长或表面积的耗散；等分布形式中，路径平均的法向-雅可比向量与几何离散梯度在无需代数稳定性的前提下，同时保证封闭面积或体积精确保持与周长或表面积耗散。数值实验验证了预期的高阶精度与保结构性质。

Conclusion: 所提基于局部拉回流映射的保结构参数化有限元框架可实现任意高阶精度，并在两种形式下分别通过不同机制严格保证面积/体积保持与周长/表面积耗散，数值实验证实了方法的有效性与结构保持特性。

Abstract: We develop arbitrarily high-order structure-preserving parametric finite element methods for geometric PDEs including surface diffusion and volume-preserving mean curvature flow based on local pullback flow maps. The central idea is to reformulate the geometric PDEs on an arbitrary intermediate hypersurface and represent the subsequent evolution through a local pullback flow map. Using admissible local pullbacks, we derive two formulations from the corresponding pullback identities for the Laplace-Beltrami operator, referred to as the conformal and equidistribution formulations. We discretize both formulations using arbitrary-degree isoparametric finite elements and Runge-Kutta collocation methods with positive weights. For the conformal formulation, weighted averages of the normal-Jacobian vector ensure exact preservation of the enclosed area or volume, while algebraic stability of the Runge-Kutta method additionally guarantees dissipation of the perimeter or surface area. For the equidistribution formulation, a path-averaged normal-Jacobian vector and a geometric discrete gradient guarantee both exact preservation of the enclosed area or volume and dissipation of the perimeter or surface area, without requiring algebraic stability. Numerical experiments confirm the expected high-order accuracy and structure-preserving properties.

</details>


### [103] [A Locally Conservative Enriched Linearized Neural Network Approximation to Elliptic PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04524)
*Seungil Kim,Gwanghyun Jo,Young Ju Lee*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种局部守恒的ENN方法求解Darcy流，利用浅层ReLU网络逼近并结合分片常数修正实现局部质量守恒，在无逆不等式条件下建立最优H^1阶误差估计，数值结果验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络函数不满足逆不等式，导致标准间断Galerkin内罚格式的稳定性与误差分析失效；同时需要保证Darcy流数值格式的局部质量守恒性质。

Method: 采用线性化浅层ReLU^k网络（隐层参数固定在球面准一致集上）进行逼近，在辅助剖分上叠加分片常数函数保证局部守恒；剖分可为弯曲单元。通过边缘恒等式结合Galerkin最小二乘（GLS）项弥补缺失的逆不等式，并使用在L^2、H^1、H^2范数下同时最优的单一神经逼近；采用对任意正罚参数均强制的非对称内罚（NIPG）格式。

Result: 在EG范数下证明误差估计达到最优H^1阶：对H^r解（r≥2）为O(n^{-(r-1)/d})，对H^s解（3/2<s≤2）为O(n^{-(s-1)/d})，适用于任意形状规则剖分，条件为GLS参数min{h,n^{-1/d}}^2≲τ≲n^{-2/d}且边缘项按min{h_e,n^{-1/d}}即网络分辨率缩放。构造的数值Darcy通量局部守恒，与单元大小和数量无关。数值实验表明固定4×4剖分与随网络细化的剖分精度相同，质量损失保持在舍入误差水平，而非守恒神经网络方法则不然。

Conclusion: ENN方法在无需逆不等式的前提下实现了Darcy流的局部守恒逼近与最优阶误差估计，数值实验验证理论正确性，且以网络分辨率而非网格分辨率控制精度，显著提升守恒性与精度表现。

Abstract: This paper presents a locally conservative Enriched Linearized Neural Network (ENN) method for the Darcy flow model. A linearized shallow ReLU$^k$ network, whose hidden-layer parameters are fixed on a quasi-uniform set of the sphere, carries the approximation, and piecewise constant functions on an auxiliary subdivision of the domain are added to ensure local mass conservation. The subdivision may consist of general, possibly curved, elements. Since neural network functions do not satisfy the inverse inequality, the stability and error analysis of the standard discontinuous Galerkin interior penalty formulation do not apply. The missing inverse inequality is remedied by an edge identity combined with a Galerkin least-squares (GLS) term, together with a single neural approximant that is optimal in the $L^2$, $H^1$ and $H^2$ norms simultaneously. We use the nonsymmetric interior penalty (NIPG) form, which is coercive for every positive penalty parameter. With $n$, the width of the shallow neural network, used in ENN, we prove an error estimate in the EG norm that is of the optimal $H^1$ order, i.e., $O(n^{-(r-1)/d})$ for solutions in $H^r$, $r\ge2$, and $O(n^{-(s-1)/d})$ for solutions in $H^s$, $\frac32<s\le2$, for any shape-regular subdivision, when the GLS parameter satisfies $\min\{h,n^{-1/d}\}^2\lesssimτ\lesssim n^{-2/d}$ and the edge terms are scaled with $\min\{h_e,n^{-1/d}\}$, i.e., by the resolution of the network rather than of the mesh. A numerical Darcy flux is constructed, which is locally conservative, regardless of the size or the number of the elements. Sample numerical results demonstrate the correctness of the theory. In particular, a fixed $4\times4$ subdivision gives the same accuracy as a subdivision refined with the network, and the mass loss stays at round-off unlike a non-conservative neural network method.

</details>


### [104] [Pressure Monotonicity for a Rotation-Based Biot Discretization with Circumcentric Mass Lumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04535)
*Huipeng Gu,Mingchao Cai,Jingzhi Li*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对基于旋转的Biot系统的混合有限元离散，证明了单元压力单调性。空间离散采用相容离散de Rham复形（最低阶旋转、位移、Darcy通量与压力单元），时间离散采用后向Euler。离散复形的精确性将力学贡献约化为缩放压力质量矩阵（正对角力学容量）；Darcy部分经通量消元后得到含正外心面权重的加权单元图Laplacian，且质量集中乘积与标准Darcy乘积一致等价。由此凝聚压力矩阵为Stieltjes矩阵，压力传播算子逐元非负，导出单元离差极大值原理。数值实验验证了收敛率并消除了单元压力伪振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Biot系统的混合有限元离散中，压力场容易出现非物理的单元尺度伪振荡，破坏离散极大值原理。本文旨在为基于旋转的Biot系统建立单元压力单调性，从离散结构上保证压力传播算子非负，从而抑制伪振荡。

Method: 空间上采用相容离散de Rham复形（compatible discrete de Rham complexes）进行混合有限元离散，旋转、位移、Darcy通量与压力均用最低阶标准单元；时间上用后向Euler。利用离散复形的精确性，将力学对压力Schur补的贡献约化为缩放的压力质量矩阵，得到正对角力学容量；Darcy部分用正外心（circumcentric）面权重，通量消元后形成加权单元图Laplacian，质量集中乘积与标准Darcy乘积一致等价。以此分析凝聚压力矩阵的Stieltjes性质。

Result: 正对角力学容量与加权单元图Laplacian使凝聚压力矩阵成为Stieltjes矩阵，进而压力传播算子逐元非负，导出单元离差极大值原理。数值实验证实了理论预测的收敛率，并显示该离散能抑制单元压力的伪振荡。

Conclusion: 通过相容离散de Rham复形与后向Euler的混合有限元离散，可利用力学容量正对角性与加权单元图Laplacian使凝聚压力矩阵具有Stieltjes结构，从而保证单元压力单调性与离差极大值原理，有效抑制伪压力振荡。

Abstract: We establish cellwise pressure monotonicity for a mixed finite element discretization of the rotation-based Biot system. The spatial discretization is based on compatible discrete de Rham complexes, using standard lowest-order elements for rotation, displacement, Darcy flux, and pressure. For the temporal discretization, we employ the backward Euler method. Exactness of the discrete complexes reduces the mechanics contribution to the pressure Schur complement to a scaled pressure mass matrix, yielding a positive diagonal mechanics capacity. For the Darcy part, positive circumcentric facet weights yield a weighted cell graph Laplacian after flux elimination, while the mass-lumped product remains uniformly equivalent to the standard Darcy product. The diagonal mechanics capacity and the weighted cell graph Laplacian make the condensed pressure matrix Stieltjes. Consequently, the pressure propagator is entrywise nonnegative, which yields a cellwise discrete maximum principle. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence rates and show that the proposed discretization suppresses spurious cellwise pressure oscillations.

</details>


### [105] [Quasi-Optimal Randomized Recovery of $\mathcal{H}^2$ Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04574)
*Anna Yesypenko*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于自适应几何层次结构的随机恢复算法，仅通过矩阵及其伴随的乘积来构造显式、强容许的H²近似。Carried recovery仅需O(k+p)次算子应用且与树深无关，resketched recovery需O((k+p)log(N/k))次，并给出全局拟最优Frobenius误差界；实验验证了在保持精度的同时显著减少算子调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造显式H²近似通常依赖矩阵的显式元素或特定层次探测，代价高。本文希望仅用矩阵-向量乘积（含伴随）来构造强容许H²近似，以适配黑箱算子场景。

Method: 在自适应几何层次结构上提出两种随机恢复算法。Carried recovery仅使用一对稠密高斯测试矩阵，不做层次特定探测，将前向-伴随草图沿层级更新，所有算子样本可单批获取；Resketched recovery在每层使用新高斯测试矩阵，并证明其相对于规定层次、秩和近邻模式最优近似的期望Frobenius误差的全局拟最优界。

Result: Carried recovery只需O(k+p)次矩阵及伴随应用，与树深无关；resketched recovery在对数深度树上需O((k+p)log(N/k))次。假设近邻数有界且局部维度为O(k)。在二维和三维的Laplace、Dirichlet-to-Neumann及Helmholtz算子上，最大128万未知量实验显示：充分过采样下，carried recovery误差几乎与细化无关，精度与resketched recovery相当且算子调用大幅减少；压缩应用代价在固定秩下线性增长。

Conclusion: 所提方法可在黑箱算子设置下高效构造显式强容许H²近似，carried recovery在保持近似精度的同时显著降低算子调用次数。

Abstract: An $\mathcal H^2$ matrix represents well-separated interactions using nested low-rank bases while retaining nearby interactions explicitly. We consider the problem of constructing an explicit, strongly admissible $\mathcal H^2$ approximation using only products with the matrix and its adjoint, and develop two randomized recovery algorithms on an adaptive geometric hierarchy.
  Carried recovery begins with a single pair of dense Gaussian test matrices, without hierarchy-specific probing, and updates the resulting forward--adjoint sketches across levels. All operator samples can therefore be acquired in one batch. Resketched recovery applies the same recovery procedure using fresh Gaussian test matrices at each level. For this variant, we prove a global quasi-optimal bound on the expected Frobenius error relative to the best approximation with the prescribed hierarchy, rank bounds, and retained near-neighbor pattern.
  Carried recovery requires only $O(k+p)$ applications of the matrix and its adjoint, independent of tree depth, whereas resketched recovery requires $O((k+p)\log(N/k))$ on trees of logarithmic depth. Here $N$ is the matrix dimension, $k$ the maximum prescribed box rank, and $p$ the oversampling parameter. These bounds assume bounded near-neighbor counts and $O(k)$ local dimensions. Experiments on Laplace, Dirichlet-to-Neumann, and Helmholtz operators in two and three dimensions with up to $1.28$ million unknowns show that, with sufficient oversampling, carried recovery achieves nearly refinement-independent error and accuracy comparable to resketched recovery while using many fewer operator applications. Its compressed application cost scales linearly for a fixed rank.

</details>


### [106] [Instability of Gaussian elimination is exponentially rare (proof of partial result)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04761)
*Lloyd N. Trefethen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明：对独立同分布正态随机矩阵，高斯消去法部分主元下的增长因子上角元 $u_{nn}$ 超过 $n^{1/2}$ 的概率呈指数稀少，部分印证了实践中该算法高度稳定的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯消去法部分主元求解稠密线性方程组是标准算法，理论最坏情形增长因子可达 $2^{n-1}$ 导致不稳定，但实践中长期表现极为稳定。实验表明：对独立正态随机矩阵，增长因子 $\rho \gg n^{1/2}$ 在精确意义下指数稀少。作者希望从理论上证实这一观察。

Method: 完整的增长因子依赖于上三角因子 $U$ 的所有元素，难以直接分析。作者转而分析增长因子的角元 $u_{nn}$，通过随机矩阵与高斯消去过程的分析工具，研究该角元的概率分布及大偏差行为。

Result: 证明了对于独立同分布正态随机矩阵，角元 $u_{nn}$ 超过 $n^{1/2}$ 的概率是指数小的，即在精确意义下指数稀少，从而为增长因子 $\rho$ 的稀有性观察提供了部分理论支持。

Conclusion: 该工作是对"高斯消去法实践中稳定"这一长期经验的重要理论推进，虽然只处理了增长因子的角元 $u_{nn}$ 而非完整增长因子 $\rho$，但为后续完整结论奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gaussian elimination with partial pivoting is the standard algorithm for solving a dense $n\times n$ system of linear equations $Ax=b$. The ``growth factor'' $ρ\,$ for this process may be as large as $2^{n-1}$, and when $ρ$ is large, the algorithm is unstable. Nevertheless, long experience has shown that Gaussian elimination is resoundingly stable in practice. Experiments indicate that among random matrices with independent normally distributed entries, growth factors $ρ\gg n^{1/2}$ are in a precise sense exponentially rare. Here we take a step toward confirming this observation by proving it not for the full growth factor, which depends on all the entries of the upper-triangular factor $U$, but for the corner entry $u_{nn}^{}$.

</details>


### [107] [A general synthetic iterative scheme with high-order spatial discretization for rarefied gas mixture flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04880)
*Jianan Zeng,Wei Su*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出稳态稀薄气体混合物流动的高阶间断伽辽金（DG）广义合成迭代格式（GSIS-DG），通过动力学与宏观合成方程交替求解实现快速收敛与高阶精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学迭代在近连续区收敛缓慢，且经典GSIS多为二阶有限体积格式，精度受限；需要一种能在粗网格上同时具备高阶空间精度、快速稳态收敛且无需解析分子平均自由程的高效数值方法。

Method: 对单原子气体混合物的介观动力学方程与宏观合成方程采用相同多项式阶数的DG离散，并交替求解：动力学解提供应力与热流的非平衡修正，合成方程加速向稳态收敛；并通过渐近分析考察连续极限行为。

Result: 渐近分析表明GSIS-DG无需空间网格解析分子平均自由程即可恢复连续极限；在宽Knudsen数范围内数值结果展现高阶空间精度与快速稳态收敛，近连续区计算成本较传统动力学迭代降低一个数量级以上；与二阶有限体积GSIS相比，在更粗物理网格和更少总空间自由度下达到相当或更优精度。

Conclusion: GSIS-DG是一种适用于稳态稀薄气体混合物流动的高阶、高效数值框架，兼顾精度与收敛速度，并在粗网格下优于二阶有限体积GSIS。

Abstract: We develop a high-order discontinuous Galerkin formulation of the general synthetic iterative scheme (GSIS) for steady rarefied gas mixture flows. The resulting GSIS-DG discretizes the mesoscopic kinetic equations for monatomic gas mixtures and the macroscopic synthetic equations with the same polynomial degree and solves them alternately. The kinetic solution supplies nonequilibrium corrections to the stress and heat flux, while the synthetic equations accelerate the convergence toward the steady state. Asymptotic analysis shows that GSIS-DG recovers the continuum limit without requiring the spatial mesh to resolve the molecular mean free path. Numerical results over a wide range of Knudsen numbers demonstrate high-order spatial accuracy and rapid steady-state convergence, with more than an order-of-magnitude reduction in computational cost relative to conventional kinetic iteration in the near-continuum regime. Comparisons with second-order finite-volume GSIS further demonstrate that GSIS-DG achieves comparable or better accuracy on coarser physical meshes with fewer total spatial degrees of freedom.

</details>


### [108] [A second-order energy-stable low-regularity integrator for the Navier--Stokes equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05045)
*Shu Ma*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的二阶指数型低正则积分器，仅需线性求解、满足离散能量衰减，并证明了粘性一致的二阶时间收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程的解在粘性趋于零时正则性下降，传统积分格式往往依赖解的高正则性假设，且需要求解非线性系统。因此需要一种对低正则解稳健、计算高效、并能保持物理结构（能量衰减）的二阶数值方法。

Method: 构造二阶指数型低正则积分器：利用指数积分思想处理线性（粘性）项，仅需线性求解即可完成时间推进；在Fourier--Galerkin空间离散下分析最优空间收敛阶，并可推广至协调有限元方法，同时保持离散能量衰减结构。

Result: 在解的一致$H^4$界假设下证明了二阶时间收敛，且误差常数在粘性趋于零时保持有界；对固定粘性建立了Fourier--Galerkin离散的最优空间收敛率；数值实验验证了收敛阶及对粘性的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该二阶指数型低正则积分器兼具效率与结构保持性，误差对粘性一致有界，适用于低正则及近无粘情形，并可推广至有限元框架。

Abstract: A second-order exponential low-regularity integrator is proposed for the incompressible Navier--Stokes equations. The method requires only linear solves and satisfies a discrete energy-decay property. Second-order temporal convergence is established under a uniform $H^4$ solution bound, with an error constant that remains bounded as the viscosity tends to zero. For the Fourier--Galerkin discretization, the optimal spatial convergence rate is established for fixed viscosity. The method can also be extended to conforming finite element methods while retaining the discrete energy-decay structure. Numerical results confirm the convergence orders and robustness with respect to the viscosity.

</details>


### [109] [Oriented point-cloud varifolds for estimating the perimeter and computing its Wasserstein gradient flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05064)
*Tokuhiro Eto,Noboru Isobe*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出无网格数值方法求解单相Mullins-Sekerka流，用有向点云（有向变分簇）表示界面，通过检测法向抵消量识别隐藏边界，构造周长估计子并用于极小化运动格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该流中界面为周长的Wasserstein梯度流，计算难点在于：当两组具有相反法向的界面相遇并合并时，会形成"隐藏边界"，传统表示难以处理。

Method: 将有向点云视为有向变分簇来表示界面；引入度量邻近法向相互抵消程度的量以检测隐藏边界；基于该量构造周长估计子，并在二维中用于极小化运动格式。

Result: 证明周长估计子的期望误差随点数按代数速率衰减，且在有隐藏边界时依然成立；数值实验表明该格式能通过隐藏边界计算组件的合并，同时每步周长下降、面积近似守恒。

Conclusion: 有向表示可有效处理隐藏边界问题，并有望推广到其他由周长驱动的界面问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a mesh-free numerical method for the one-phase Mullins-Sekerka flow, in which the interface bounding a set moves as the Wasserstein gradient flow of its perimeter. One difficulty in computing this flow is that two pieces of the interface with opposite normals form a hidden boundary when components merge. Our approach represents the interface by an oriented point cloud, which we treat as an oriented varifold. We introduce a quantity that measures how much nearby normals cancel each other and use it to detect the hidden boundary. Based on this quantity, we construct a perimeter estimator, prove that its expected error decays at an algebraic rate in the number of points, also in the presence of a hidden boundary, and use it in a minimizing movement scheme for the flow in two dimensions. Numerical experiments show that the scheme computes mergers of components through the hidden boundary, while the perimeter term in the scheme decreases at every step and the area is nearly conserved. As hidden boundaries also appear in other perimeter-driven interface problems, we expect our oriented representation to be applicable to them as well.

</details>


### [110] [Dispersion-corrected compact finite difference discretizations for the elastic Helmholtz equation in two and three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05110)
*Rachel Yovel,Eli Turkel,Eran Treister*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出广义MAC(GMAC)紧致有限差分框架与弹性色散校正(EDC),用于弹性Helmholtz方程,证明GMAC无耗散、无横波分离且二阶精度,EDC显著降低色散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量Helmholtz问题的色散校正已较成熟,但弹性问题的相关方法较少,且多采用非紧致模板,因此需要发展紧致、带色散校正的弹性Helmholtz离散方法。

Method: 构建一族紧致、色散校正的标记网格(MAC)型有限差分离散格式;引入广义MAC(GMAC)框架以实现弹性Helmholtz方程的紧致离散,并扩展实移位波数色散校正思想,提出弹性色散校正(EDC)过程。

Result: 证明GMAC虽非对称但无数值耗散、无横波模式分离,特定权重下极化误差低于标准MAC;证明并数值验证GMAC具二阶精度;EDC大幅降低标准MAC色散,并在部分GMAC情形下提供额外校正;色散分析显示该方法在色散精度上优于现有紧致有限差分格式,并与部分非紧致格式相当,且在弱非均匀介质中改善相位对齐。

Conclusion: 所提GMAC+EDC方法为弹性Helmholtz方程提供了高色散精度的紧致离散方案,兼具紧致性与精度优势。

Abstract: We develop a family of compact, dispersion-corrected marker-and-cell (MAC)-based finite-difference discretizations for the elastic Helmholtz equation. While dispersion correction is well established for scalar Helmholtz problems, fewer approaches exist for the elastic problems, and most use non-compact stencils. To this end, we introduce a generalized MAC (GMAC) framework that enables compact discretization of the elastic Helmholtz equation. We prove that even though GMAC is non-symmetric, this discretization produces no numerical dissipation and no shear-mode separation. For certain weights, GMAC has lower polarization error than standard MAC. We also introduce an elastic dispersion correction (EDC) process, extending real-shifted-wavenumber dispersion correction methods from acoustic to elastic Helmholtz problems. We prove and demonstrate numerically that GMAC is second-order accurate. Furthermore, we show that EDC substantially reduces dispersion for standard MAC and can provide additional correction in some instances of GMAC. Dispersion analysis shows that the resulting method outperforms existing compact finite-difference discretizations in dispersion accuracy and is competitive with some non-compact schemes. We give numerical evidence for improved phase alignment in mildly heterogeneous media.

</details>


### [111] [Convergence and error analysis for numerical approximations of stochastic Leray-Lions equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05171)
*Ngan Le,Huateng Zhu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文用梯度离散化方法分析Leray-Lions型散度形式随机微分方程，提出稳定通用格式，证明格式解在概率意义下收敛于强解，并对乘性Lipschitz噪声给出收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含Leray-Lions通量函数的散度形式随机微分方程，需要一种能统一覆盖多种数值格式的通用分析框架，以研究其在乘性噪声与运输噪声下的适定性与数值逼近。

Method: 采用梯度离散化方法（GDM），这是一个涵盖协调/非协调有限元、混合杂交混合法（HMM）及高阶杂交法（HHO）等的通用框架。对乘性Lipschitz连续噪声与运输噪声两类情形，分别构造稳定通用格式。

Result: 对两类噪声均证明格式解在概率意义下收敛于强解；在乘性Lipschitz连续噪声情形下，进一步建立了收敛速率估计。

Conclusion: 梯度离散化方法为Leray-Lions型随机微分方程提供了统一、稳定的数值逼近与收敛性分析框架，并可在特定噪声下获得定量收敛速率。

Abstract: We analyse a divergence form stochastic differential equation featuring a Leray-Lions flux function that satisfies standard Carathéodory, growth, monotonicity, and coercivity conditions. We consider two types of noise: multiplicative Lipschitz continuous noise and a transport noise. Our approach utilises the gradient discretisation method, a generic framework encompassing various numerical schemes such as conforming and nonconforming finite element methods, hybrid mimetic mixed methods, and hybrid high order methods. For both types of noise, we propose a stable generic scheme, prove that scheme solutions converge to a strong solution in the probabilistic sense. For the multiplicative Lipschitz continuous noise, we establish the rate of convergence.

</details>


### [112] [Multiresolution Sobolev Trained Fourier Neural Operator for Cross-Resolution Surrogate Modelling of Parametric Two-Phase Darcy Flows in Porous Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05188)
*Zhao Zhang,Chengjin Guo,Zhenglong Chen,Kai Zhang,Piyang Liu,Xia Yan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多分辨率Sobolev学习方法，用于训练FNO构建跨分辨率代理模型，以提升含随机参数两相瞬态Darcy流的跨分辨率预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理建模可提升含随机参数物理过程不确定性量化的计算效率；FNO相较传统代理的优势在于跨分辨率预测能力，但其跨分辨率预测精度有待提升。

Method: 针对多孔介质中含随机参数的两相瞬态Darcy流，构建基于FNO的跨分辨率代理；提出多分辨率Sobolev学习方法，在不同分辨率训练样本的损失函数中引入一阶与二阶梯度，梯度采用与数值模拟网格兼容的、与分辨率相关的有限差分格式近似。

Result: 实验结果验证了新方法在训练FNO以构建跨分辨率代理模型方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 多分辨率Sobolev学习方法能够有效提升FNO跨分辨率代理的预测精度，适用于含随机参数的多孔介质两相瞬态Darcy流代理建模。

Abstract: Surrogate modelling is important to enhance the computational efficiency of uncertainty quantification for physical processes involving random parameters. The advantage of Fourier neural operator (FNO) compared to conventional surrogates is its cross-resolution prediction capability. In the current study, a cross-resolution surrogate based on FNO is built for two-phase transient Darcy flows in porous media with random parameters. A multiresolution Sobolev learning method is proposed for FNO to enhance the cross-resolution prediction accuracy by incorporating first and second-order gradients in the loss function using training samples of different resolutions. The gradients are approximated by resolution-related finite difference schemes compatible with the grid for numerical simulation. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the new method in training FNO for building cross-resolution surrogates.

</details>


### [113] [On finite elements for geometrically-exact planar beams with hyperelastic material models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05231)
*Abhishek Ghosh,Chennakesava Kadapa,Djordje Peric,Mokarram Hossain*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种几何精确平面梁有限元框架，可集成超弹性本构；通过增加面内厚度变形场并分别采用平面应力/应变缩减，发现平面应力对泊松比不敏感，而平面应变随泊松比增大显著变刚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在几何精确平面梁模型中集成超弹性材料模型时，截面变形假设与本构行为往往相互耦合，且文献中常用的平面应变条件会引入泊松比敏感性，缺乏简单一致的建模框架。

Method: 先构建含三场变量、完全几何非线性和线弹性本构的常规梁模型；随后引入一个额外的全局场以允许面内厚度方向变形；面外方向通过平面应变与平面应力两种本构缩减分别处理，使截面变形假设与本构行为可独立定义。

Result: 基准算例表明：平面应力条件下，所考察泊松比取值对应的中轴线变形基本一致；而文献广泛采用的平面应变条件对泊松比耦合敏感，泊松比越高梁显著变刚。Saint Venant-Kirchhoff 与 Neo-Hookean 超弹性模型的对比显示，整体变形与面内厚度响应吻合良好，本构差异主要体现在局部面外伸长。

Conclusion: 该框架为在平面几何精确梁模型中引入超弹性材料模型提供了简单且一致的途径，并使截面变形假设与本构缩减可解耦处理。

Abstract: A geometrically-exact planar beam finite-element framework is developed for the analysis of beams with hyperelastic constitutive models. A conventional three-field beam model is first formulated with full geometrical nonlinearity and a linear elastic material law. The kinematics are then enriched by an additional global field that permits deformation through the in-plane thickness. The out-of-plane direction is incorporated separately through plane-strain and plane-stress constitutive reductions, allowing the cross-sectional deformation assumptions and constitutive behaviour to be formulated independently. Numerical results for the present benchmarks show that the plane-stress condition gives essentially coincident centreline deformations for the Poisson's ratio values considered. In contrast, the plane-strain condition, which is widely employed in the literature, makes the formulation sensitive to Poisson's ratio coupling, with the beam becoming significantly stiffer at higher Poisson's ratios. Comparisons between Saint Venant-Kirchhoff and Neo-Hookean hyperelastic models further demonstrate close agreement in the global deformation and in-plane thickness response, with small constitutive differences becoming more apparent in the local out-of-plane stretch. The proposed approach offers a simple and consistent framework to integrate hyperelastic material models into planar geometrically-exact beam models.

</details>


### [114] [A Finite Element Method for the Grad--Mercier Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05313)
*Ben Ashby,Tristan Pryer*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析Grad–Mercier方程（源项依赖磁通函数全局秩的非局部椭圆磁平衡模型）的协调分片仿射有限元方法，通过将秩律替换为等价截断非负律、用质量集中节点测度替代Lebesgue测度，得到具有严格凸离散能量的唯一有限元平衡解，并证明其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grad–Mercier方程是非局部椭圆模型，其源项取决于磁通函数的全局秩。其主要困难在于唯一的连续平衡解具有指定正面积的极大平台：解仅在平台质量之上对秩律采样，而分离相等值的扰动可能将该质量分散到多个数值秩上，从而改变非严格秩源，导致离散化处理困难。因此需要一个能稳健处理"结值"（相等节点值）的数值方法。

Method: 利用连续问题的适定性结构，将秩律替换为等价的截断非负律，并将Lebesgue测度替换为质量集中的节点测度；对相等的节点值赋予共同的秩区间，从而保留每个结层的全部集中质量。由此得到的加权重排给出严格凸的离散能量，进而获得唯一有限元平衡解。在满足离散比较原理的网格上，离散平衡非负且有可计算上界屏障。采用Poisson预条件重排迭代，在每个固定网格上收敛，并提供可计算的后验能量与能量范数界。

Result: 所得加权重排产生严格凸的离散能量，保证唯一有限元平衡解；在满足离散比较原理的网格上，离散平衡非负且被可计算屏障上有界；Poisson预条件重排迭代在固定网格上收敛并给出可计算的后验能量及能量范数界；当多边形有限元域收敛于物理截面时，离散平衡在H^1_0(Ω)中强收敛且在Ω上一致收敛于唯一Grad–Mercier平衡解；重构的平衡源项在L^∞(Ω)中弱-*收敛于原非局部源。

Conclusion: 该工作为Grad–Mercier方程提供了完整数值分析：通过等价截断秩律与节点质量集中测度处理平台与结秩问题，构造出唯一、良态的有限元离散系统，并证明了迭代可解性与向连续平衡解的H^1_0强收敛、一致收敛及源项弱-*收敛。

Abstract: We develop and analyse a conforming piecewise affine finite element method for the Grad--Mercier equation, a nonlocal elliptic model for magnetic equilibria whose source depends on the global rank of a flux function. The principal difficulty is that the unique continuum equilibrium has a maximum plateau of prescribed positive area. The solution therefore samples the rank law only above the plateau mass, while perturbations that separate equal values may distribute this mass over several numerical ranks and thereby alter the non-strict rank source.
  We exploit the continuum well-posedness structure to replace the rank law by an equivalent clipped, nonnegative law, and replace Lebesgue measure by a mass-lumped nodal measure. Equal nodal values are assigned a common rank interval, thereby retaining the full lumped mass of each tied level.
  The resulting weighted rearrangement gives a strictly convex discrete energy and hence a unique finite element equilibrium. On meshes satisfying a discrete comparison principle, the discrete equilibrium is nonnegative and bounded above by a computable barrier. A Poisson-preconditioned rearrangement iteration converges on each fixed mesh and admits computable a posteriori energy and energy-norm bounds. As polygonal finite element domains converge to the physical cross-section, the discrete equilibria converge strongly in $H^1_0(Ω)$ and uniformly on $\overlineΩ$ to the unique Grad--Mercier equilibrium. Reconstructed equilibrium sources converge weak-* in $L^{\infty}(Ω)$ to the original nonlocal source.

</details>


### [115] [Structure Preserving High-Order FDTD Methods for the Nonlinear Kerr-Debye Model with Lorentz Dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05507)
*Emmanuel E. Oguadimma,Vrushali A. Bokil,Nathan L. Gibson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在FDTD框架下提出并分析了一类全离散高阶格式，用于非线性色散电磁模型（Maxwell方程耦合Kerr-Debye与Lorentz方程），格式兼具渐近保持、保正性与能量稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超短脉冲在非线性光学介质中传播的数值模拟需要高效稳健的格式；已有AP-PP、能量稳定的方法主要在间断Galerkin框架下发展，本文旨在将其拓展到计算上更高效的FDTD框架。

Method: 采用2M阶交错空间离散，时间上分别采用二阶显式leapfrog或隐式梯形积分；对Kerr-Debye方程中的刚性松弛项采用修正指数积分器以保证渐近保持（AP）；并通过Richardson外推获得时间高阶精度。

Result: 证明了格式满足连续能量恒等的离散模拟并保持非线性极化率的正性（PP）；leapfrog格式在CFL条件下能量稳定，梯形格式无条件能量稳定；Richardson外推使时空均达到2M阶精度；数值实验验证了收敛率，并能捕捉孤子传播、自陡峭和三倍频产生等非线性波现象。

Conclusion: 该工作将已有的AP-PP与能量稳定方法从DG框架推广到FDTD框架，为非线性色散光学介质中电磁波传播的模拟提供了计算高效、灵活且稳健的工具。

Abstract: We develop and analyze several families of fully discrete, high-order methods in the finite difference time domain (FDTD) framework for a nonlinear dispersive electromagnetic model for ultrashort pulse propagation in a nonlinear optical medium. The model consists of the time domain Maxwell's equations coupled to two ordinary differential equations; the nonlinear Kerr-Debye equation and the linear dispersive Lorentz equation, in one space dimension. The proposed methods combine staggered spatial discretizations of $2M$th-order, for integer $M>0$, with second-order explicit leapfrog or implicit trapezoidal time integrators. To handle the stiff relaxation in the nonlinear Kerr-Debye equation, we adopt a modified exponential integrator, introduced in \cite{PengJCP2020}, which ensures asymptotic-preserving (AP) behavior in the limit of vanishing relaxation time. We prove that the resulting schemes satisfy a discrete analogue of the continuous energy identity and preserve the positivity (PP) of the nonlinear susceptibility. For the schemes based on the leapfrog time integrator, we establish energy stability under a CFL condition, while the schemes based on the trapezoidal time integrator are unconditionally energy stable. High-order temporal accuracy in the trapezoidal time integrator based schemes is further achieved via Richardson extrapolation yielding $2M$th-order accurate methods in both space and time. Numerical experiments demonstrate the theoretical convergence rates and the ability of the proposed schemes to capture nonlinear wave phenomena such as soliton propagation, self-steepening, and odd harmonic generation. This work extends previous AP-PP and energy stable approaches in a discontinuous Galerkin framework to the FDTD setting, offering a computationally efficient, flexible and robust tool for simulating electromagnetic wave propagation in nonlinear dispersive optical media.

</details>


### [116] [Global error estimators for parametric monotone nonlinearities and neural approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05767)
*Pablo Cortés Castillo,Wolfgang Dahmen,Jay Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文构造了可计算的误差估计器（同时可作神经网络损失函数），针对具有单调性的参数化非线性偏微分方程，证明了其全局可靠性（reliability）与有效性（efficiency），并给出显式常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为神经网络逼近参数到解的映射提供"变分正确"的损失函数，需要误差估计器的界不仅在精确解附近成立，而是在整个试探函数空间上全局成立；这依赖单调性，并可覆盖不符合有限元离散空间的任意逼近。

Method: 将非线性算子拆分为线性部分与强单调闭合项；在线性残差的离散对偶范数中度量残差。由于试探范数强于 L2 范数时闭合项对应的对偶范数无闭式，构造仅需满足配对界（pairing bound）与 Lipschitz 界的可计算代理量。给出两类实例：①符合试探空间上的一阶系统最小二乘（FOSLS）估计器，其次双侧界在整个试探空间成立；②每个参数值下有限元试探空间上的间断 Petrov-Galerkin（DPG）估计器，采用破碎检验空间，对偶范数由独立的单元局部问题计算。

Result: 主定理给出带显式常数的双侧界；对非线性通量的模型类验证了全部假设，并按参数范围显式跟踪常数。两类估计器均对任意输入可计算，因此可作为神经网络逼近参数到解映射的变分正确损失函数。

Conclusion: 单调性使误差估计器具备全局可靠性-有效性；基于算子拆分与对偶范数代理的构造可实例化为 FOSLS 与 DPG 两类估计器，二者均可计算并可直接用于神经网络训练的损失函数。

Abstract: We construct computable error estimators, which double as loss functions for neural networks, for a class of parametric nonlinear partial differential equations with a monotonicity property, and prove that they are globally reliable and efficient. The value of such a loss function is bounded above and below by the squared error in the natural trial norm, for every trial function, not merely for those near the exact solution; this global property rests on monotonicity. The construction rests on splitting the nonlinear operator into a linear part and a strongly monotone closure, and on measuring the linear residual in a discrete dual norm. Since the closure contributes a dual norm that admits no closed form when the trial norm is stronger than an $L_2$ norm, the estimator is built around a computable surrogate for it, required only to satisfy a pairing bound and a Lipschitz bound. The main theorem then yields two-sided bounds with explicit constants and covers two instances. A first-order system least-squares estimator on conforming trial spaces is the first instance studied: its two-sided bound holds on the whole trial space and therefore applies to arbitrary approximations, including those not in a discrete finite element space. The second instance is a discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin estimator on trial spaces of finite element functions for each parameter value, built with broken test spaces, for which no conformity is required and the dual norm is computed by independent element-local problems. All assumptions are verified for a model class of nonlinear fluxes, with the constants tracked explicitly in terms of the parameter range. Being computable for an arbitrary input, both estimators serve as variationally correct loss functions for neural network approximations of parameter-to-solution maps.

</details>


### [117] [Entropy-Stable and Well-Balanced Discontinuous Galerkin Methods for the Compressible Euler Equations in Vector-Invariant Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05820)
*Marco Artiano,Kieran Ricardo,Oswald Knoth,Peter Spichtinger,Hendrik Ranocha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在通量差分DGSEM框架下，为含重力的可压缩Euler方程（矢量不变形式、以位温为预报变量）构造了保结构格式；通过对称/反对称乘积离散非守恒项，导出同时守恒热力学熵与总能量的两点数值通量，并给出对等温与等熵背景态均良平衡的熵稳定通量，性质推广至高阶曲线网格DGSEM，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向现代大气流动动力核心，需要在曲线网格上的高阶离散中同时保证熵守恒/熵稳定、总能量守恒以及对静止背景态的良平衡性，而现有含重力Euler方程（矢量不变形式、位温预报变量）的DGSEM格式尚缺乏统一满足这些性质的构造。

Method: 在高阶通量差分DGSEM框架下，将非守恒项离散为对称与反对称乘积形式，据此推导保持热力学熵与总能量守恒的两点数值通量；进一步设计对等温与等熵背景态均良平衡的熵稳定数值通量；并将全部性质推广到一般曲线网格上的高阶DGSEM。

Result: 理论上证明了所构造的两点通量同时守恒热力学熵与总能量，且所设计通量对等温、等熵背景态良平衡，相关性质在一般曲线网格高阶DGSEM中保持成立；数值算例验证了理论结论，并显示格式的鲁棒性与精度。

Conclusion: 所提保结构DGSEM格式兼具熵守恒、熵稳定性、总能量守恒与良平衡性，适用于现代大气流动动力核心，具有良好的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: We develop structure-preserving methods for the compressible Euler equations with gravity in vector-invariant form and potential temperature as a prognostic variable within the flux-differencing discontinuous Galerkin spectral element method (DGSEM) framework. By discretizing the nonconservative terms as symmetric and antisymmetric products, we derive two-point numerical fluxes that conserve both the thermodynamic entropy and the total energy. Moreover, we design an entropy-stable numerical flux that is well-balanced for both isothermal and isentropic background states. All properties are shown to carry over to the high-order DGSEM on general curvilinear meshes. Several numerical examples confirm the theoretical findings and show the robustness and accuracy of the scheme for use in modern dynamical cores for atmospheric flows.

</details>


### [118] [A TVD Active Flux method for scalar conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05920)
*Wasilij Barsukow*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文为一阶双曲守恒律的完全离散、单步Active Flux方法建立了TVD理论，通过推广总变差概念与Harten引理，并选取可高效计算的参数以满足引理条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Active Flux方法虽为高阶方法，但缺乏保证其无振荡（TVD）性质的理论框架，需要为其建立相应的稳定性理论以保证数值解不产生非物理振荡。

Method: 针对标量守恒律，考虑经典自由度选取方式（得到三阶方法），将离散总变差（TV）概念推广至Active Flux方法，并据此实现Harten引理的相应推广；最后为高阶方法引入参数，使其可高效计算以满足引理条件。

Result: 成功构建了Active Flux方法的TVD理论，包括适配的离散总变差定义与Harten引理的推广，并给出满足引理条件、可高效计算的参数选取方案。

Conclusion: 该工作将TVD稳定性理论拓展到高阶Active Flux框架，为该方法提供无振荡保证，并给出了实用且可计算的条件。

Abstract: Focusing on scalar conservation laws, a TVD theory is developed for the fully discrete, single-stage Active Flux method. The classical choice of degrees of freedom is considered, yielding a third-order method. Starting from an adaptation of the concept of (discrete) total variation to Active Flux, a corresponding adaptation of Harten's lemma is achieved. Finally, the high-order method is endowed with parameters chosen such that they can be efficiently computed to fulfill the conditions of the lemma.

</details>


### [119] [Quasi-Trefftz spaces for the first-order time-harmonic Maxwell's equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05975)
*Ilaria Fontana,Lise-Marie Imbert-Gérard*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究一阶时谐麦克斯韦方程的最优高阶Taylor型多项式拟Trefftz空间，识别出最优空间、提出两种基构造方法并证明其最佳逼近性质，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多项式空间维数较大、效率不高。拟Trefftz空间通过仅逼近控制方程的光滑解（而非一般光滑函数），在保持高阶最佳逼近性质的同时显著减小空间维数。对向量值方程而言，朴素的拟Trefftz性质无法系统性地定义具有所需最佳逼近性质的最小空间，因此需要针对一阶时谐麦克斯韦方程识别真正的最优空间。

Method: 针对一阶时谐麦克斯韦方程，识别最优拟Trefftz空间；提出两种构造拟Trefftz基的方法；证明该空间的最佳逼近性质。理论论证充分利用多项式Helmholtz分解以及多项式de Rham序列的正合性。

Result: 确定了时谐麦克斯韦方程的最优多项式拟Trefftz空间，给出两种基构造途径，并证明了空间的最佳逼近性质，结果通过数值实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 与标量方程不同，向量值方程的拟Trefftz空间构造需谨慎处理；本文为时谐麦克斯韦方程提供的最优拟Trefftz空间在保持高阶最佳逼近的同时具有更小维数，兼顾精度与效率。

Abstract: The goal of this article is to study optimal high-order Taylor-based polynomial quasi-Trefftz spaces for first-order time-harmonic Maxwell's equations. Compared with standard polynomial spaces, the purpose of quasi-Trefftz spaces is to retain the same high-order best approximation property but more efficiently: their dimension is considerably smaller. To achieve this goal, they approximate only smooth solutions to the governing equation rather than to approximate general smooth functions. While there is a natural definition of Taylor-based polynomial quasi-Trefftz spaces for scalar equations, the situation is fundamentally different for vector-valued equations, as the naive quasi-Trefftz property does not systematically define the smallest space with the desired best approximation property. This article identifies the optimal quasi-Trefftz spaces for first-order time-harmonic Maxwell's equations, proposes two approaches to construct quasi-Trefftz bases, and proves the spaces' best approximation property. The theoretical results, which strongly leverage polynomial Helmholtz decompositions and the exactness of the polynomial de Rham sequences, are validated with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [120] [Nonparametric face-based finite element methods on complex hybrid three-dimensional meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06208)
*Aubin Brunel,J. -C. Latch{é}*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一类基于面的低阶非协调有限元方法,用于复杂混合三维多边形网格上的椭圆问题,采用非参数化局部逼近空间以避免畸变网格导致的逼近性能退化,并给出棱柱与棱锥单元的多项式空间构造,理论及数值结果均验证了方法的稳定性与最优收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂混合三维网格(含任意多边形/多面体单元)上的椭圆问题求解需求,以及参数化映射在畸变网格上会导致逼近性能退化的问题。

Method: 构造基于面的低阶非协调有限元方法;采用非参数化方式构建局部逼近空间;在非常一般的单元上定义有限元格式;借鉴间断Galerkin思想,在强畸变网格(含曲面)上消除相容性误差;并针对三角基底棱柱和四边形基底棱锥单元给出多项式空间的具体构造。

Result: 非参数化构造可避免参数化映射在畸变网格上引起的逼近性能退化;所提出格式在强畸变网格上可消除相容性误差;理论与数值实验均表明格式稳定、收敛且达到最优收敛率。

Conclusion: 所提基于面的低阶非协调有限元方法适用于任意多面体单元的复杂混合三维网格,具有稳定性、收敛性与最优收敛阶,并可张量式推广至Crouzeix–Raviart与Rannacher–Turek方法的混合形式推广。

Abstract: In this work, we propose and analyze low-order nonconforming face-based finite element methods for elliptic problems on complex hybrid three-dimensional meshes, composed of arbitrary polytopal cells. The focus is on scalar problems, but the approach can be extended in a tensor manner to design generalizations of the classical Crouzeix--Raviart and Rannacher--Turek methods for mixed formulations. We adopt a nonparametric construction of the local approximation spaces. We demonstrate that it avoids the degradation of approximation properties commonly induced by parametric mappings on distorted meshes. Using these approximation spaces, we define finite element schemes for very general cells. We further demonstrate that, on strongly degraded meshes, an approach inspired by discontinuous Galerkin methods enables removing consistency errors, even in the presence of potentially curved faces. Explicit examples of the construction of the polynomial space are given for prismatic (of triangular basis) and pyramidal (of quadrilateral basis) cells. The resulting schemes are shown theoretically and numerically to be stable and convergent, with an optimal convergence rate.

</details>


### [121] [IGA-KAN: Isogeometric Analysis with Physics-Informed Closed-Form Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Forward and Inverse PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06348)
*Sima Naraghi,Kourosh Parand,Amirhossein Sadr,Dara Rahmati*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出IGA-KAN：在等几何分析(IGA)Galerkin解基础上，用闭式拟合的局部Kolmogorov-Arnold网络修正解，并由最大原理启发的后验保护准则决定局部模型的使用位置。在8个基准（含MRI脑切片拟合域）上，L²误差较IGA降低4.2–90倍、H¹降低4.1–220倍，L²误差比从头训练的最佳KAN小6–6×10⁴倍；反问题中恢复常数源精度比IGA高167倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IGA能在精确NURBS几何上高精度求解偏微分方程，但神经求解器虽无网格，精度却常低数个数量级，且依赖无误差控制的无约束非凸优化。因此需要一个既保留IGA高精度与几何优势、又能借助神经网络表达能力的混合方案，而不是用神经网络替代IGA。

Method: 提出IGA-KAN，用局部Kolmogorov-Arnold网络改进而非替代IGA解。先由IGA Galerkin求解得到u_h；在每个节点-顶点patch上，以方程的强形式、精确边界数据和u_h为监督，拟合Kolmogorov-Arnold ridge模型；各局部模型由IGA hat函数混合。内层函数固定时，拟合等价于一个批量线性最小二乘问题，无需优化器、学习率和初始化。另设基于最大原理界的后验保护准则，判定何处采用局部模型，其余区域保留IGA解。

Result: 在8个具有精确解的基准（5个来自文献，1个还建立在由MRI脑切片拟合的域上）上，参考网格下Galerkin未知量数量不变时，方法将IGA误差在L²中降低4.2–90倍、H¹中降低4.1–220倍；其L²误差比在相同方程、固定预算下从头训练的最佳Kolmogorov-Arnold网络小6–6×10⁴倍。在反问题中，仅凭一次无噪声观测恢复未知常数源的精度比IGA高167倍。增益归因于Galerkin解局部平均的超收敛性。

Conclusion: IGA-KAN以闭式局部拟合与后验保护实现了对IGA解的稳定增强，在不增加Galerkin未知量的前提下显著提升精度，并远优于从头训练的KAN，其效果源于Galerkin解局部平均的超收敛性。

Abstract: Isogeometric analysis (IGA) solves partial differential equations accurately on exact NURBS geometry, whereas neural solvers are mesh-free but often orders of magnitude less accurate and typically trained by non-convex optimization without error control. We propose IGA-KAN, which uses local Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, fitted in closed form, to improve the IGA solution instead of replacing it. An IGA Galerkin solve produces u_h; on every knot-vertex patch a Kolmogorov-Arnold ridge model is fitted to the strong form of the equation, the exact boundary data and u_h, and the models are blended by IGA hat functions. With fixed inner functions the fit is one batched linear least-squares problem, without optimizer, learning rate or initialization. An a posteriori safeguard, motivated by a maximum-principle bound, decides where local models are used, keeping the IGA solution elsewhere. On eight benchmarks with exact solutions, five from the literature and one also posed on a domain fitted to a brain slice from MRI, the method reduces the error of IGA, at an unchanged number of Galerkin unknowns, by factors of 4.2 to 90 in L^2 and 4.1 to 220 in H^1 on the reference meshes, and its L^2 error is 6 to 6x10^4 times smaller than that of the best Kolmogorov-Arnold network trained from scratch on the same equations with a fixed budget. In an inverse problem it recovers an unknown constant source from one noise-free observation 167 times more accurately than IGA. The gain is attributed to the superconvergence of local averages of the Galerkin solution.

</details>


### [122] [A Mixed-Precision Model for Matrix-Splitting Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06414)
*Neil Lindquist,David Appelhans,Chales W. Jackson,Joseph M. Derlaga*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出混合精度技术减少矩阵分裂迭代方法的数据移动量，理论上和实验上证明其收敛速率与精确精度迭代相近，并可用迭代精化恢复全双精度精度；在Jacobi、Gauss-Seidel上取得加速，并在NASA的OVERFLOW代码中实现整体应用加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态迭代方法算术强度低，性能受限于内存带宽（memory-bound），数据移动成为主要瓶颈，因此需要减少数据移动以提升性能。

Method: 提出混合精度技术，降低矩阵分裂迭代方法中的数据移动量；采用迭代精化（iterative refinement）将解精化至完整双精度；在标量与块Jacobi、标量与块Gauss-Seidel中混合使用半精度与双精度。

Result: 理论上与实验上表明该技术通常以与精确精度迭代相似的速率收敛，收敛性取决于所用精度与M矩阵的条件数；相比统一双精度，标量与块Jacobi平均加速1.60倍，标量与块Gauss-Seidel平均加速1.25倍；将OVERFLOW（NASA可压缩流体动力学模拟代码）中SSOR迭代的精度降低后，整体应用获得1.14倍加速。

Conclusion: 混合精度可有效缓解稳态迭代方法的内存瓶颈，在保持收敛性与双精度精度的同时获得显著加速，并已在真实科学计算应用中验证其有效性。

Abstract: The performance of stationary iterative methods is memory-bound, due to the low arithmetic intensity. So, we propose a mixed-precision technique to reduce the amount of data movement in matrix-splitting iterative methods. We demonstrate both theoretically and experimentally that this technique will generally converge at a similar rate as an exact-precision iteration, depending on the precision used and the conditioning of the $M$ matrix. Furthermore, we demonstrate that iterative refinement can be used to refine the solution to full double-precision accuracy. Using this approach, we mixed half and double precision to achieve an average speedup over uniform double precision of $1.60\times$ for scalar and block Jacobi and $1.25\times$ for scalar and block Gauss-Seidel. Furthermore, we reduced the precision in the SSOR iteration used by OVERFLOW, a NASA code for compressible fluid dynamics simulations, and achieved a $1.14\times$ speedup to the overall application.

</details>


### [123] [Delta Redistribution: a variation of State Redistribution for steady-state embedded boundary computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06459)
*Marsha Berger,Andrew Giuliani*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种处理笛卡尔切割网格中小单元问题的算法，可精确保留基格式的稳态，并在二维有限体积格式上给出算例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笛卡尔切割网格中，边界切割会产生体积远小于规则网格的"小单元"，导致显式格式的稳定性条件（CFL）被严重限制，并可能破坏格式的守恒性与稳态精度。已有小单元处理方法往往难以在不损失精度的前提下保持原格式的稳态性质，因此需要一种能精确保留基格式稳态的改进算法。

Method: 针对笛卡尔切割网格的小单元问题设计了一种新的处理算法，其核心是在消除小单元带来的稳定性限制的同时，使改进后的格式在稳态解上与原基格式完全一致（精确保持稳态）。方法以有限体积框架为基础，在二维空间中对多种有限体积格式进行了实现与分析。

Result: 通过有限体积格式在二维空间中的多个数值算例，验证了所提算法的可行性与稳态保持特性。

Conclusion: 该算法能够有效处理切割网格中的小单元问题，且其显著优势在于精确保留基格式的稳态解；二维有限体积算例表明该方法具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for handling the small cell problem in Cartesian cut cell grids. The advantage of this variation is that it preserves the base scheme's steady state exactly. We show several computational examples using finite volume schemes in two space dimensions.

</details>


### [124] [One Extra Dimension Suffices for the Complete-Graph Squared-Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06531)
*Lilin Yan,Hongwei Zhao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 证明：当 k > ℓ 时，完整图平方应力目标在 R^k 中的每个二阶稳定点残差为零，无需一般位置假设，并在 Lean 4 中完成形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究距离几何/多维标度中平方应力目标的非凸优化景观，解决"多一维猜想"（one-extra-dimension conjecture）在完整、均匀加权数据下的情形。

Method: 结合残差应力的正性、候选构型的极坐标归一化、逐行秩-零度论证，以及通过完整 Hessian 传播零曲率；并给出从原始四次目标导数到矩阵表述的过渡。

Result: 证明在 k > ℓ 时每个二阶稳定点残差为零；结论对任意点数成立，无需一般性假设，允许重复点与退化真值构型；定理已在 Lean 4 中形式化。

Conclusion: 解决了完整均匀加权数据下的多一维猜想，并提供零信任级别的内核检查与传递公理审计作为验证记录。

Abstract: Let all pairwise distances of an arbitrary configuration of points in $\mathbb{R}^{\ell}$ be known exactly. We prove that every second-order stationary point of the complete-graph squared-stress objective over configurations in $\mathbb{R}^{k}$ has zero residual whenever $k > \ell$. The result holds for every number of points, without a genericity assumption, and allows repeated points and degenerate ground-truth configurations. It resolves the one-extra-dimension conjecture for complete, uniformly weighted data. The proof combines positivity of the residual stress, polar normalization of the candidate configuration, a row-wise rank-nullity argument, and propagation of zero curvature through the full Hessian. The theorem, including the passage from derivatives of the original quartic objective to its matrix formulation, has been formalized in Lean 4. The accompanying verification records include a kernel check with zero trust level and a transitive axiom audit.

</details>


### [125] [Singular parameters and missing limits in neural PDE solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06770)
*Daniel Fernández*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究深度 tanh 神经网络求解 PDE 时参数无界增长导致极限解无法在模型内表示的问题，将“缺失极限”归因于隐藏参数无界或神经元冗余，并通过为平移核模型加入核导数来补全函数空间，使最佳逼近可达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经 PDE 求解器在追求精度时参数会无限增长，其极限解可能在所选模型中不存在有限表示，导致最佳损失无法达到；需要刻画这些“缺失极限”并恢复其可达性。

Method: 针对由平移核（translated kernels）构成的一类模型，分析深度 tanh 网络中缺失极限与隐藏参数无界或神经元日益冗余之间的联系；通过向模型中加入核导数（kernel derivatives）来补全缺失函数并恢复极限。

Result: 描述了缺失的函数族，并证明在标准假设下，加入核导数后的补全模型能够达到最佳逼近；数值实验跟踪了相应的参数增长过程并考察补全对 PDE 优化过程的影响。

Conclusion: 缺失极限源于参数无界或冗余神经元，通过核导数补全可使最佳逼近可达，并影响 PDE 优化行为。

Abstract: Neural solvers for partial differential equations (PDEs) can approach an accurate solution while their parameters grow without bound. In such cases, the limiting solution may have no finite representation in the chosen model, leaving the best loss unattained. Our analysis connects missing limits in deep neural tanh- networks to unbounded hidden parameters or increasingly redundant neurons. For a class of models built from translated kernels, we describe the missing functions and recover them by adding kernel derivatives to the model. This completion makes the best approximation attainable under standard assumptions. Numerical studies follow the associated parameter growth and explore how completion affects PDE optimization.

</details>


### [126] [On the Growth Factor of Random Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06785)
*John Urschel*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究随机矩阵高斯消元的增长因子，给出了无主元情形下的紧估计与渐近分布，并证明了部分主元下高斯矩阵增长因子很少显著超过√n，解决了Trefethen的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯消元是最古老且最流行的线性方程组求解方法，其数值稳定性由增长因子控制（衡量消元过程中元素能变多大）。对随机矩阵增长因子的理解尚不充分，且部分主元下的增长因子行为存在长期未决的猜想。

Method: 针对由Haar正交矩阵预条件的矩阵，在无主元情形下给出增长因子的紧估计，并刻画高斯矩阵无主元时增长因子的渐近分布；同时发展相应技术用于部分主元下的分析，结合平滑分析进行论证。

Result: 证明了n×n高斯矩阵在部分主元下的增长因子很少显著大于√n，解决了Nick Trefethen的旧猜想；所提技术还改进了部分主元下增长因子的平滑分析。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了对随机矩阵高斯消元数值稳定性的理论认识，验证了部分主元能有效控制随机矩阵的增长因子，并给出了更优的平滑分析界。

Abstract: Gaussian elimination is the oldest and most popular method for solving a linear system. Its numerical stability for a given matrix is controlled by the growth factor, a measure of how large entries can become during elimination. In this work, we prove a number of new results regarding the growth factor of random matrices. First, we provide tight estimates for the growth factor of a matrix preconditioned by a Haar orthogonal matrix without pivoting and characterize the asymptotic distribution of the growth factor of Gaussian matrices without pivoting. Second, and most notably, we prove that the growth factor of an $n \times n$ Gaussian matrix under partial pivoting is rarely much larger than $\sqrt{n}$, resolving an old conjecture of Nick Trefethen. The same techniques used to prove this conjecture also provide an improved smoothed analysis of the growth factor under partial pivoting.

</details>

### [127] [Concave Roofs and Downward Recovery for DR-Submodular Polynomial Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04027)
*Donglei Du*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在单位立方体的非空紧致下闭凸子集上最大化非负递减回报（DR）子模多项式，通过精确分解为根原子与仿射项并构造可解凹上界（roof），给出二次型1/2、三次型8/17的确定性多项式时间近似因子，并扩展到更一般的乘性证书与局部函数和，同时给出多重线性情形、拟阵取整及在线保持性结果，最后证明约0.82843的UGC不可近似界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大化非负子模函数在一般约束下通常是NP难的，经典做法依赖多重线性松弛与连续化，但对具有非负Hessian符号结构（即DR子模多项式）且约束为下闭凸集的问题，如何获得统一、可证明的确定性近似因子仍缺乏系统刻画。作者希望利用DR多项式的Hessian非正性，将目标精确分解为结构清晰的组件，从而构造可高效求解的凹上界并实现可证明的舍入/恢复框架。

Method: 作者从二次或三次系数出发，将目标精确分解为非负的根原子（rooted atoms）与仿射项。对这些组件分别构造凹上界，组合得到可解的roof（上界优化问题）。随后对roof最优解进行坐标递减操作，所用的映射对所有组件同时有效，从而在保持可行性的前提下回收roof值的某一比例；对二次型还精确保留凹对角残差。方法进一步推广到供给相容根证书（文字度至多12）以及非负子模局部函数（元数至多4）之和等类。对任意有限文字度，刻画了同一标量映射的最优单映射因子。针对多重线性局部函数和，利用全局单调性与凹基数结构给出更强保证且仅需恒等映射，并借助标准拟阵取整无损传递。此外证明有向超图割的在线保持性与基数的UGC不可近似界。

Result: 在给定的优化访问假设下，构造给出确定性多项式时间近似因子：二次型为1/2，三次型为8/17，允许任意给定加性误差。三次型结果涵盖正三次系数与重复变量。8/17因子同样适用于两类更大问题：限制文字度至多12的供给相容根证书，以及元数至多4的非负子模局部函数之和。在任意有限文字度下，存在公共标量映射达到最优单映射因子2√3−3。对于多重线性局部函数和，全局单调性与凹基数结构带来更强保证，无需超出恒等映射的恢复步骤；标准拟阵取整可无损转移多重线性保证。还证明了有向超图割的在线保持性，并在基数上界下给出显式二次输入的UGC不可近似界约为0.82843。

Conclusion: 作者建立了一套基于精确原子分解、凹上界roof与坐标递减恢复的统一近似框架，用于在下闭凸约束下最大化DR子模多项式，得到二次/三次及更广函数类的确定性多项式时间因子与最优单映射因子。多重线性与拟阵结构可加强保证，在线场景与不可近似性结果刻画了问题的计算边界。

Abstract: We give approximation algorithms for $\max\{F(x):x\in\K\}$, where $\K$ is a nonempty compact down-closed convex subset of the unit cube and $F$ is a nonnegative diminishing-returns (DR) submodular polynomial: every entry of its Hessian is nonpositive on the cube.
  Starting from quadratic or cubic coefficients, we decompose the objective exactly into nonnegative rooted atoms and affine terms. Concave upper bounds for these components give a tractable roof. We then decrease the coordinates of a roof optimizer using maps that work for all components at once, recovering a fraction of the roof value while preserving feasibility. For quadratics, we also retain the concave diagonal residual exactly.
  Under the stated assumptions on optimization access, these constructions give deterministic polynomial-time factors of $1/2$ for quadratics and $8/17$ for cubics, up to any prescribed additive error. The cubic result includes positive cubic coefficients and repeated variables. The factor $8/17$ also holds for two larger classes: supplied compatible rooted certificates of literal degree at most twelve, and sums of nonnegative submodular local functions of arity at most four. At arbitrary finite literal degree, a common scalar map attains the optimal single-map factor $2\sqrt3-3$. Global monotonicity and concave-cardinality structure give stronger guarantees for multilinear local-function sums, with no recovery beyond the identity map. Standard matroid rounding transfers the multilinear guarantees without loss. We also prove online preservation for directed-hypergraph cuts and, separately, a Unique-Games inapproximability bound of approximately $0.82843$ for explicit quadratic input under a cardinality upper bound.

</details>


### [128] [Stochastic Sequential Feedback Optimization for Wind Farm Control under Time-Varying Inflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04055)
*Shijie Huang,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出随机序列反馈优化（S2FO），用于时变随机工况下非线性动力系统的稳态优化，兼顾闭环性能理论与风电场功率最大化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标函数是随当前工况分布变化的期望值函数，分布变化会导致随机最优解漂移，传统稳态优化方法难以跟踪这种移动的随机最优。

Method: S2FO 将投影反馈梯度更新与序列灵敏度估计相结合，灵敏度通过在已实现的随机工况处对系统（plant）线性化获得。

Result: 在期望动态遗憾、期望意义下的瞬时跟踪以及递减步长下的几乎必然收敛三方面给出闭环性能分析；在基准布局与真实海上风电场的仿真中，当存在持续尾流相互作用时 S2FO 提升平均发电量，且其优势随风向变化性增强而减小。

Conclusion: S2FO 能在时变随机工况下实现带理论保证的稳态反馈优化，并在风电场功率最大化任务中验证了有效性，其适用性受工况变化速率（如风向变化性）制约。

Abstract: We consider steady-state optimization of nonlinear dynamical systems under time-varying stochastic operating conditions, where the objective is an expected-value function over the current operating-condition distribution, so that changes in the distribution induce a moving stochastic optimum. To address this problem, we propose stochastic sequential feedback optimization (S2FO), which combines projected feedback-gradient updates with sequential sensitivity estimates obtained by linearizing the plant at realized stochastic operating conditions. We analyze the closed-loop performance of S2FO through expected dynamic regret, instantaneous tracking in expectation, and almost-sure convergence under diminishing step sizes. We then apply the method to wind farm power maximization under time-varying stochastic inflow. Simulations on a benchmark layout and on a realistic offshore wind farm show that S2FO improves average power generation when persistent wake interactions are present, and that its advantage decreases as wind-direction variability increases.

</details>


### [129] [Accelerated Benders decomposition for bi-objective mixed integer programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04070)
*Duleabom An,Chungmok Lee,Sophie N. Parragh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究双目标混合整数规划（可应用Benders分解），针对可持续供应链网络设计问题，提出复用加权和法迭代中已生成Benders割的加速技术，显著缩短计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双目标混合整数规划在现实中应用广泛，Benders分解可有效利用问题结构。但用加权和法处理双目标时需对不同权重反复求解相似问题，计算开销大，亟需加速手段。

Method: 以同时最小化经济与环境目标的可持续供应链网络设计为研究对象，采用加权和法处理双目标；提出直接加速技术，将加权和法先前迭代中生成的Benders割复用于后续求解，从而用预生成割初始化Benders分解主问题。

Result: 计算实验表明，复用Benders割能显著降低计算时间。

Conclusion: 复用历史Benders割可有效加速加权和法下的双目标混合整数规划求解，验证了该方法在可持续供应链网络设计中的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate bi-objective mixed integer programming to which Benders decomposition can be applied, a widely used technique for exploiting problem structures in real-world applications. Our study focuses on a sustainable supply chain network design problem that aims to minimise both economic and environmental objectives. To address the bi-objective nature, we employ the weighted sum method, which requires solving similar problems repeatedly with different weight values. To tackle this, we propose a straightforward acceleration technique that reuses Benders cuts generated in previous iterations of the weighted sum method. This approach enables the Benders decomposition master problem to be initialised with pre-generated cuts. Our computational experiments demonstrate that reusing these cuts significantly reduces computing time.

</details>


### [130] [Learning in Large Populations With Anticipatory Payoffs: Bregman Meets Willems and Lyapunov](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04086)
*Tzuyu Jeng,Nuno C. Martins*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文解决了CCW（逆时针耗散性）框架下演化纳什均衡学习缺乏Lyapunov函数与Lyapunov稳定性的空白，通过从收益机制的CCW存储函数构造与学习规则无关的Lyapunov函数，为非线性预期机制建立了全局渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在由动态机制生成收益的大规模种群中研究演化纳什均衡学习时，CCW（逆时针耗散性）提供了一个类无源性的框架，可在满足收益对齐条件的广泛凸锥内的"良态"学习规则下建立学习/收敛。但现有CCW结果虽然无需知道具体学习规则即可证明收敛，却未提供Lyapunov函数或Lyapunov稳定性，留下了理论空白。

Method: 构建了一套方法论：从收益机制的CCW存储函数出发，构造与具体学习规则无关的Lyapunov函数。针对一类非线性预期机制（agent将当前收益与平滑化种群状态对应的收益进行比较），证明其存储函数可表示为Bregman散度；所构造的Lyapunov函数由势能间隙与上述Bregman散度组合而成。

Result: 所构造的Lyapunov函数建立了全局渐近稳定性（global asymptotic stability），并将收敛性从单纯"收敛"提升到具有Lyapunov稳定性的更强结论。以一个带非线性拥堵增长收费的拥堵博弈验证了该结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了CCW理论中缺乏Lyapunov分析工具的缺口：证明可从收益机制的CCW存储函数构造规则无关的Lyapunov函数，且对非线性预期机制，存储函数可由Bregman散度给出，从而确立全局渐近稳定性。方法适用于包含最佳响应、复制动态等广谱无噪声学习规则。

Abstract: We study evolutionary Nash equilibrium learning in large populations whose payoffs are generated by dynamic mechanisms. Counterclockwise dissipativity (CCW) provides a passivity-like framework for establishing learning/convergence under a broad convex cone of so-called well-behaved learning rules satisfying a natural payoff-alignment condition according to which, away from equilibria, the population's state (strategic profile) moves in a direction positively correlated with the payoffs. The cone is closed under conic combinations and encompasses a broad range of noise-free rules, including discontinuous rules such as best response, imitative rules such as the replicator rule that is not $δ$-passive, and all noise-free $δ$-passive rules. Existing CCW results require no knowledge of the particular rule, but establish convergence without providing a Lyapunov function or Lyapunov stability. We develop a methodology that closes this gap by constructing rule-independent Lyapunov functions from CCW storage functions of the payoff mechanism. For a nonlinear class of anticipatory mechanisms, in which agents compare current payoffs with those associated with a smoothed population state, we show that a storage can be derived as a Bregman divergence. The resulting Lyapunov function combines a potential gap with this divergence and establishes global asymptotic stability. A congestion game with a nonlinear congestion-growth toll illustrates the results.

</details>


### [131] [Time-reliability optimization of multi-pass underfill dispensing sequences on printed circuit boards: a schedule-dependent reliability model solved by a multiset-permutation binary-addition-tree algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04186)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对多道次底部填充点胶中顺序决定道次间流动时间、进而耦合周期时间与可靠性的问题，建立了三目标多重集置换路径模型，权衡名义周期时间、附加时长方差与可靠性；采用多重集置换二进制加法树算法（无重复枚举+支配剪枝）求解，在六个实例（含三个BeagleBone Black基准）上给出参考Pareto前沿，最大搜索空间为1.47×10^12条路径，20核CPU耗时38分钟；并量化了标称成本优选路线的最大估计超支成本与启发式决策后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多道次底部填充点胶中，道次顺序直接决定道次间流动时间，从而使周期时间与工艺可靠性相互耦合。论文旨在量化该顺序相关的权衡关系，并评估启发式调度相对参考最优解的决策后悔。

Method: 建立三目标多重集置换路径（multiset-permutation routing）模型：最小化名义周期时间与附加时长方差，同时最大化可靠性，且分别针对硬停留与软停留策略；可靠性由道次项、调度依赖的流动项与行进项组合而成。在独立时长与确定性停留阈值假设下，证明该方差度量等于软停留下的周期时间方差，并在硬停留下构成其上界。求解采用多重集置换二进制加法树算法，无重复地枚举可行路径，并按支配关系对前缀进行剪枝。

Result: 在六个实例（含三个BeagleBone Black基准）上获得参考Pareto前沿，最大搜索空间达1.47×10^12条路径，20核CPU耗时38分钟。在模拟候选中，按名义成本选出的路径在建模时长变异性下最大估计超支成本为每板0.0014美元，变异性加倍时为0.0035美元。轮询调度在测试成本下与参考最优解相差不超过3.7%；NSGA-II与简化群优化算法的观测成本后悔至多0.53%，同时恢复最大硬停留前沿约30%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够量化排序权衡与启发式决策后悔；但物理可靠性的预测仍需基于检测数据的标定。

Abstract: Multi-pass underfill dispensing couples cycle time with process reliability because the sequence determines the inter-pass flow times. This paper formulates a tri-objective multiset-permutation routing model that minimises nominal cycle time and additive duration variance while maximising reliability under hard- and soft-dwell policies; reliability combines pass, schedule-dependent flow and travel terms. Under independent durations and deterministic dwell thresholds, the variance measure equals the cycle-time variance under soft dwell and bounds it from above under hard dwell. A multiset-permutation binary-addition-tree algorithm enumerates the feasible routes without duplication and prunes prefixes by dominance. Six instances, including three BeagleBone Black benchmarks, yield reference Pareto fronts, the largest search space of $1.47 \times 10^{12}$ routes in 38 minutes on 20 CPU cores. Among simulated candidates, the nominally cost-selected routes incur maximum estimated excess costs of USD 0.0014 per board at the modelled duration variability and USD 0.0035 with it doubled. Round-robin dispatch is within 3.7 % of the reference optimum at the tested costs; NSGA-II and simplified swarm optimisation show at most 0.53 % observed cost regret while recovering about 30 % of the largest hard-dwell front. The framework quantifies sequencing trade-offs and heuristic decision regret; physical reliability prediction requires inspection-based calibration.

</details>


### [132] [An inverse-stochastic-control Galerkin estimator for drift potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04268)
*Yumiharu Nakano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究从离散、可能不规则的观测位置恢复过阻尼Langevin扩散的漂移势这一类不适定反问题，通过将逆随机控制的次优性间隙专门化为漂移势恢复问题，在有限基中参数化值函数，得到一个仅需求解正定线性系统的Galerkin估计量，并给出最优逼近恒等式、能量半范数一致性、收敛率及噪声偏差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离散且可能不规则的观测数据中恢复Langevin扩散的漂移势是一个不适定反问题。作者希望避免传统方法中对转移密度的依赖以及Euler-Maruyama离散带来的O(Δt)漂移离散化偏差，从而构建一个更稳健、更精确的漂移势估计框架。

Method: 将逆随机控制的次优性间隙专门化到漂移势恢复问题，在有限基中参数化关联的值函数。在基非退化条件下，由经验时间边缘分布的平滑泛函构成一个正定线性系统，从而得到Galerkin估计量，无需转移密度。作者将样本波动与时间求积误差分离，并分析有限基作为正则化的作用，推导具体基族的维度相关收敛率，同时给出观测噪声偏差界。

Result: 该估计量的次优性间隙等于到真实势的平方能量距离的一半，由此得到最优逼近恒等式，并证明当基趋于完备时的能量半范数一致性。通过分离样本波动与时间求积误差，避免了Euler-Maruyama的O(Δt)漂移离散化偏差。作者还给出了具体基族的维度相关收敛率并界定了观测噪声偏差。一维和二维实验验证了预测的采样间隔行为，而不规则动物遥测数据的应用在单条长轨迹上展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个无需转移密度、能规避Euler-Maruyama离散偏差的漂移势恢复Galerkin框架，具备最优逼近性质和能量半范数一致性，并通过实验与真实遥测数据验证了理论预测。

Abstract: We consider the ill-posed inverse problem of recovering the drift potential of an overdamped Langevin diffusion from discrete, possibly irregular position observations. Specializing the suboptimality gap for inverse stochastic control to drift-potential recovery, we parametrize the associated value function in a finite basis. Under a basis nondegeneracy condition, the resulting Galerkin estimator is obtained from one positive-definite linear system whose entries are smooth functionals of the empirical time marginals; no transition density is required. The gap equals one half of the squared energy distance to the true potential, yielding a best-approximation identity and energy-seminorm consistency as the basis becomes complete. At a fixed basis, we separate the sample fluctuation from the time-quadrature error, thereby avoiding the Euler--Maruyama $O(Δt)$ drift-discretization bias. We also analyze the finite basis as regularization, give a dimension-dependent convergence rate for a concrete basis family, and bound observation-noise bias. One- and two-dimensional experiments verify the predicted sampling-interval behavior. An application to irregular animal telemetry illustrates the method on a single long trajectory.

</details>


### [133] [Computing Equilibria in Integer Programming Games with Shared Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04279)
*Bainian Hao,Hyunwoo Lee,Robert Hildebrand,Carla Michini*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出割平面算法计算有限整数博弈（共享约束）的纯纳什均衡，用条件均衡不等式精确刻画均衡集，嵌入分支切割框架形成广义零后悔算法，并在3300个实例上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限整数博弈中，参与者偏离的可行性依赖于对手策略组合（对某个对手剖面可行、对另一个不可行），经典均衡条件难以直接刻画，需要处理这种依赖关系并求解均衡优化问题。

Method: 引入含显式激活项的条件均衡不等式，给出成本与激活条件的仿射编码并证明其精确刻画均衡集；将其作为lazy约束嵌入branch-and-cut，得到Generalized Zero-Regret算法以在线性目标下优化精确或近似均衡；用二分法维持最小可达近似因子的认证界；证明整数顶点条件下的结构性结论。

Result: 在3300个实例上评估：除43例外均求得社会最优均衡，对16例认证均衡不存在，在无精确均衡时认证的近似因子在数个百分点内；理论上证明条件多面体具整数顶点时凹成本在包含均衡策略的最小面上取常值（严格凹则迫使顶点策略），并对均匀整数可分装箱问题通过保成本变换得到顶点均衡。

Conclusion: 条件均衡不等式的仿射编码能精确刻画有限整数博弈的均衡集，配合割平面与分支切割可有效优化均衡，并给出近似因子的认证；该框架适用于装箱、网络形成与共享容量背包博弈，兼具理论刻画与计算实用性。

Abstract: We develop a cutting-plane algorithm for computing pure Nash equilibria in finite integer games with shared constraints, where a deviation may be feasible against one opponent profile and infeasible against another. Conditional equilibrium inequalities capture this dependence through an explicit activation term. We give affine encodings of costs and activation conditions and prove that the resulting inequalities characterize the equilibrium set exactly. Embedded as lazy constraints in branch-and-cut, they yield the Generalized Zero-Regret algorithm for optimizing a linear objective over exact or approximate equilibria; a bisection procedure maintains certified bounds on the smallest achievable approximation factor. We also prove that, when the conditional polytopes have integral vertices, a concave cost is constant on the minimal face containing an equilibrium strategy, so strict concavity forces vertex strategies; for uniform integer-splittable bin packing, a cost-preserving transformation yields vertex equilibria even without strict concavity. We give formulations for bin packing, network formation, and knapsack games with shared capacities and evaluate the algorithm on 3,300 instances, computing a socially optimal equilibrium on all but 43, certifying nonexistence on 16, and certifying approximation factors within a few percent where no exact equilibrium exists.

</details>


### [134] [Amortized Score-Hamiltonian Policy Iteration: A Grid-Free Scheme for Relaxed Stochastic Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04285)
*Qi Feng,Gu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种无网格、摊销式的连续Langevin动力学策略-价值迭代方法，用于熵正则化无限时域松弛随机控制；将score残差投影到共享条件采样器、价值动力学投影到参数化critic，得到耦合的actor-critic流，并给出HJB残差分解与策略次优性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确连续Langevin动力学策略-价值迭代需要对每个状态单独运行Langevin动力学，在高维问题中不可行；因此需要一种可摊销、无网格的实现，把逐状态的动力学共享化，同时保留原迭代的改进机制与误差可解释性。

Method: 1) 迭代改进率刻画为策略与其Hamiltonian Gibbs律之间的相对Fisher信息的折扣聚合，score残差即控制Langevin动力学输运其分布的速度；2) 将该速度投影到一个跨状态共享的条件采样器（actor），将价值动力学投影到参数化critic，在采样状态上估计两个投影，形成耦合的actor-critic流；3) 定义score损失（actor与当前critic的一致性）与策略评估残差（critic与actor的一致性）；4) 推导梯度与Hessian残差，包括梯度方程的Feynman-Kac表示，以控制投影价值迭代检测不到的误差；5) 对HJB残差给出精确分解，在验证性与对数Sobolev假设下合并为策略次优性界；6) 在线性二次类中验证两个投影是精确的并退化为逐点迭代。

Result: 在线性二次类中两个投影均为精确，方法可恢复逐点迭代；同时给出一般模型上的数值实验，展示高维情形下耦合actor-critic学习的有效性；得到基于验证性与对数Sobolev假设的策略次优性界。

Conclusion: 该工作将连续Langevin策略-价值迭代转化为可扩展的无网格actor-critic算法，通过score与策略评估残差的对偶耦合实现高维控制学习，并借助HJB残差分解给出理论上的次优性保证。

Abstract: We develop an amortized, grid-free implementation of continuous Langevin dynamics based policy-value iteration for entropy-regularized, infinite-horizon relaxed stochastic control problems. The improvement rate of the exact iteration is a discounted aggregate of relative Fisher information between the policy and the Gibbs law of its Hamiltonian. The associated score residual is the velocity with which the control's Langevin dynamics transport its law. We project this velocity onto a conditional sampler shared across states, instead of one Langevin dynamics per state, and the value dynamics onto a parametric critic, estimating both projections at sampled states to obtain coupled actor--critic flows. The score loss measures the actor's agreement with the current critic, while the policy-evaluation residual measures the critic's agreement with the actor. We also derive gradient and Hessian residuals, including a Feynman--Kac representation for the gradient equation, to control errors not detected by the projected value iteration. An exact decomposition of the HJB residual combines these errors into a policy-suboptimality bound under verification and logarithmic Sobolev assumptions. In the linear-quadratic class, both projections are exact and recover the pointwise iteration, and we provide numerical experiments on general models to demonstrate the coupled actor--critic learning in high-dimensions.

</details>


### [135] [Intrinsic Path Following on $\mathsf{SO}(3)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04332)
*Adeel Akhtar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究全驱动刚体姿态动力学沿SO(3)上任一光滑嵌入闭曲线的内在路径跟随问题，基于法丛与拉回丛几何构造切向–横向状态分解，实现切向与横向协变加速度的独立指派，并给出横向PD控制下的局部指数稳定性与自由切向调节能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体姿态路径跟随通常局限于特定路径（如测地线或特定纤维路径），缺乏对SO(3)上任一光滑嵌入闭曲线的一般性处理。作者希望建立一个内在的几何框架，使路径跟随的稳定性与切向运动解耦，从而统一支持沿路径进展调节、路径上定点镇定以及时变轨迹跟踪等多种任务。

Method: 在管状邻域上，利用法丛与拉回丛几何构造切向–横向状态分解。将全驱动刚体姿态动力学转化为二阶形式，并设计光滑反馈变换以独立指派切向与横向协变加速度。横向采用比例–微分（PD）律，使路径跟随流形不变并在显式前向不变邻域上局部一致指数稳定，同时保持切向动力学可自由指派。对代表性纤维路径，给出管状坐标的闭式表达（受自然割迹阻碍限制），并通过仿真验证。

Result: 横向PD律使路径跟随流形在该邻域内不变且局部一致指数稳定；切向动力学保持完全可指派，可实现沿路径进展的独立调节、路径上选定姿态的镇定以及时变运动跟踪。对代表性纤维路径，管状坐标可闭式求得（除自然割迹障碍外）。仿真展示了点镇定与轨迹跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 该工作为SO(3)上任意光滑嵌入闭曲线的全驱动刚体姿态路径跟随提供了统一的几何分解与反馈设计方法，通过切向–横向解耦实现了稳定性与任务灵活性的兼顾，并给出了管状坐标的闭式构造与仿真验证。

Abstract: This paper studies intrinsic path following for fully actuated rigid-body attitude dynamics over a broad class of attitude paths, namely arbitrary smooth embedded closed curves on $\mathsf{SO}(3)$.
