We construct a tangential--transverse state decomposition on a tubular neighborhood using normal and pullback bundle geometry. The resulting second-order dynamics admit a smooth feedback transformation that independently assigns tangential and transverse covariant accelerations. A transverse proportional--derivative law renders the path-following manifold invariant and locally uniformly exponentially stable on an explicit forward-invariant neighborhood, while leaving the tangential dynamics freely assignable. This allows independent regulation of progression along the path, stabilization of a selected attitude on the path, or tracking of a time-varying motion. For a representative fiber path, the tubular coordinates are obtained in closed form up to the natural cut-locus obstruction, and simulations illustrate point stabilization and trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [136] [Asymptotic analyis of an optimal control problem for a phase field tumor growth model with hyperbolic relaxation of the chemical potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04380)
*Pierluigi Colli,Elisabetta Rocca,Jürgen Sprekels*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究具有奇异势的Cahn–Hilliard型相场肿瘤生长模型的最优控制，将化学势的抛物型松弛替换为双曲型松弛，考察松弛参数α→0时控制问题的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典相场肿瘤生长模型常假设化学势满足抛物型松弛，本文改用双曲型松弛（可能含奇异势），并关注松弛参数趋于零时松弛问题与未松弛问题之间的关系，以刻画该松弛近似的合理性。

Method: 对带松弛参数α>0的最优控制问题与未松弛（α=0）问题进行比较分析：研究状态变量与伴随状态变量的收敛性、最优控制的弱极限性质，并引入"adapted"代价泛函，使未松弛问题的任一固定最优控制u*成为松弛问题极小化元族{u*_α}的极限；同时分析相应变分不等式（一阶必要最优性条件）的收敛。

Result: 松弛问题的状态与伴随状态变量在适当意义下收敛到未松弛（α=0）情形下的对应量；松弛问题最优控制的弱极限是未松弛问题的最优控制；未松弛系统的每个固定最优控制u*是采用adapted代价泛函的松弛问题极小化元族{u*_α}的极限；且松弛问题adapted代价下u*_α满足的变分不等式收敛到未松弛问题原始（非adapted）代价下u*满足的变分不等式。

Conclusion: 双曲型松弛相场肿瘤生长模型的最优控制问题在α→0时具有良好的渐近一致性：状态、伴随状态、最优控制及一阶最优性条件均以适当方式与未松弛问题对应，说明用adapted代价泛函的松弛逼近可刻画未松弛问题的最优控制。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the optimal control of a phase field tumor growth model of Cahn--Hilliard type with possibly singular potential in which the often assumed parabolic relaxation of the chemical potential is replaced by a hyperbolic one. We investigate the asymptotic behavior of the control problem as the relaxation parameter $\,α\,$ approaches zero. It is shown that the corresponding state and adjoint state variables of the relaxed problem converge in a suitable sense to their counterparts for the unrelaxed regime where $α=0$. Also, weak limits of optimal controls for the relaxed problem can be shown to be optimal controls for the unrelaxed case. Moreover, every fixed optimal control $\,u^*\,$ for the unrelaxed system turns out to be the limit of a family $\{u^*_α\}_{α>0}$ of minimizers of suitable optimal control problems for the relaxed systems, where the original cost functional is replaced by the so-called ``adapted'' cost functional. It is also shown that the variational inequalities satisfied by $u^*_α$, which constitute the first-order necessary optimality conditions of the relaxed problems with adapted cost functional, converge to the corresponding variational inequality fulfilled by $u^*$ for the unrelaxed problem with the original (nonadapted) cost functional.

</details>


### [137] [Largest Rashomon sets of decision trees for robust contextual optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04385)
*Lorenzo Bonasera,David Pisinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出联合Rashomon集与鲁棒性优化框架，用于在决策树预测多重性下选择满足成本、短缺或风险目标的运营决策，通过约束生成算法高效求解，并在报童与餐厅需求数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多决策树对同一数据的拟合效果几乎相同，但会将查询点导向不同叶节点，从而诱导不同的局部经验分布。这种预测多重性使得基于单一最优树的决策可能无法稳健地满足预设的成本、短缺或风险目标。

Method: 提出联合Rashomon与鲁棒性优化框架，同时选择一个运营决策和最大的Rashomon树集合，使得目标在该集合中每棵树于查询点诱导的局部经验分布下均成立。针对回归设定，证明树仅通过共享查询叶节点的训练观测（即查询邻域）影响决策，故鲁棒问题只涉及有限个不同约束，可按估计损失递增顺序检查。据此开发约束生成算法，将查询路径定价与动态规划结合以识别违规邻域，避免枚举所有树。

Result: 在合成报童实例上，算法通常只需少量邻域且比完全邻域枚举显著更快。在餐厅需求数据上，相对于最优树的样本平均近似订单，鲁棒订单将容忍的均值超额估计损失提高17.6%，并将最差10%实现成本的实证条件风险价值降低8.3%，而平均成本差异在统计上不显著。可解释性分析进一步表明保留的邻域如何解释决策及其鲁棒性极限。

Conclusion: 该框架为预测多重性下的决策树决策提供了兼顾目标满足与鲁棒性的方法，约束生成算法高效可行，并在实际数据上改善了风险指标且不显著增加平均成本，同时具备可解释性。

Abstract: Many decision trees fit the same data almost equally well, yet they can route a query point to different leaves and induce different local empirical distributions. We study decisions that meet prescribed cost, shortage or risk targets despite this predictive multiplicity. We propose the joint Rashomon and robustness optimization framework for optimal decision trees. It jointly selects an operational decision and the largest Rashomon set of trees, so that the targets hold under the local empirical distribution that every tree in this set induces at the query point. We specialize the framework to the regression setting, and we show that a tree affects the decision only through the training observations sharing the query leaf, which we call its query neighborhood. As a result, the robust problem involves only finitely many distinct constraints, which can be examined in order of increasing estimation loss. We develop a constraint generation algorithm that combines query-path pricing with dynamic programming to identify violating neighborhoods without enumerating trees. On synthetic newsvendor instances, the algorithm typically needs few neighborhoods and runs substantially faster than full neighborhood enumeration. On restaurant demand data, the robust orders increase the mean tolerated excess estimation loss by 17.6% and reduce the empirical conditional value-at-risk of the worst 10% of realized costs by 8.3% relative to the sample average approximation orders of the optimal tree, while the mean cost difference is not statistically significant. An interpretability analysis further shows how the retained neighborhoods explain the decision and its robustness limit.

</details>


### [138] [Rank-sensitive vertex bounds for semidefinite lifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04498)
*Avinash Bhardwaj*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究半正定松弛分解中因子秩如何约束分解规模，给出低秩顶点因子数目的界，确定固定秩约束下最小分解规模的渐近阶，并证明psd秩≤4的多面体至多12个顶点、构造出达到8的正方形对称八边形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半正定（PSD）松弛/扩展的规模（lifting 维度）与因子秩的关系此前缺乏定量刻画。作者希望量化因子秩对分解规模的约束，用以确定特定多面体（permutahedron、正N边形）在秩受限下的最小分解规模的阶，并厘清低维psd秩对应的顶点数上限问题。

Method: 1) 对任意固定分解，界定其中低秩顶点因子的个数；2) 在此界下分析最优分解规模，考虑固定顶点因子秩上限及无限制lifting两种情形；3) 通过计数由秩一因子曲线到达的多边形角点，并结合因子秩的关联（incidence）约束，证明psd秩≤4的多面体顶点数上界；4) 对正方形对称八边形显式构造规模为4的lifting。

Result: 固定顶点因子秩约束下最小分解规模：permutahedron Π_n 为 Θ(n log n)，正N边形为 Θ(log N)。秩一情形给出无对称性、无次数限制下使每个facet slack为平方和的最小实数函数空间维数的阶。无限制lift时，xc_PSD(Π_n) ≥ n + log_3 n − O(1)；且线性规模的Π_n分解需在除消失比例外的所有顶点处顶点因子秩为 Ω(log n)。psd秩≤4的多面体至多12个顶点；对每个正方形对称八边形构造出规模4的lift，故8可达。

Conclusion: 因子秩对PSD分解规模给出强约束，决定permutahedron与正多边形最小分解规模的渐近阶；psd秩≤4的多面体顶点数被限制在12以内，且正方形对称八边形达到8，但8是否为最大值仍为公开问题。

Abstract: We study how the ranks of factors in a positive semidefinite slack factorization constrain its size. We bound the number of low-rank vertex factors in any fixed factorization, and use this to determine the asymptotic order of the minimum factorization size under any fixed bound on vertex-factor ranks: $Θ(n\log n)$ for permutahedra $Π_n$ and $Θ(\log N)$ for regular $N$-gons. In the rank-one case this gives the order of the minimum dimension of a space of real functions on the vertices in which every facet slack is a sum of squares, with no symmetry or degree restriction. For unrestricted lifts, the bound gives $\mathrm{xc}_{\mathrm{PSD}}(Π_n)\ge n+\log_3 n-O(1)$, and shows that a permutahedron factorization of linear size would need vertex factors of rank $Ω(\log n)$ at all but a vanishing fraction of vertices.
  We also prove that every polytope of real positive semidefinite rank at most four has at most twelve vertices, and construct explicit size-four lifts for every square-symmetric octagon, so eight is attained. The upper bound combines a count of the polygon corners reached by curves of rank-one factors with incidence constraints on factor ranks. Whether eight is the maximum remains open.

</details>


### [139] [Tensor Envelopes in the Nonconvex Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04610)
*Alireza Kabgani,Masoud Ahookhosh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出复合目标 $\varphi=f+g$ 的张量包络 TEE，建立张量下降引理并刻画三种参数区间行为，证明其保持全局极小值与水平有界性且具良好分析性质，并将其作为精确张量迭代的 Lyapunov 函数，给出 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)})$ 的复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为张量型优化方法的设计与分析提供兼具良好分析结构与原始优化地形保持性的代理函数；并突破以往对 $f$ 的 $q$ 阶导数需全局 Lipschitz 或 Hölder 连续的限制。

Method: 基于光滑项 $f$ 的 $q$ 阶泰勒模型并用 $p$ 次幂（$p>q$）正则化构造张量包络 TEE；建立张量下降引理并刻画 $q<p<q+1$、$p=q+1$、$p>q+1$ 三种区间行为；在 prox-有界性与参数条件下分析张量算子 TOP 及 TEE 的性质（有限值、连续、极小值保持、水平有界、局部 Lipschitz、方向可微、Fréchet 可微、TOP 单值时 $C^1$）；将 TEE 用作精确张量迭代的 Lyapunov 函数进行收敛与复杂度分析。

Result: TOP 具非空紧值；TEE 有限值且连续，保持 $\varphi$ 的全局极小子集并继承其水平有界性；TEE 局部 Lipschitz 连续且方向可微，Fréchet 可微性得到刻画，TOP 单值时 TEE 为 $C^1$。作为 Lyapunov 函数，精确张量迭代的每个聚点均为临界点；对 $q<p\le q+1$ 的有界轨迹，可在 $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-p/(p-1)})$ 次迭代内达到 $\varepsilon$-稳定点。

Conclusion: TEE 在保留 $\varphi$ 关键优化地形性质的同时具备更丰富的分析结构，便于张量型优化方法的设计与分析；所得张量下降引理及复杂度界均为首次在不对 $f$ 的 $q$ 阶导数作全局 Lipschitz 或 Hölder 连续性假设下取得。

Abstract: We introduce a tensor envelope (TEE) for composite objectives $\varphi=f+g$, built from a $q$th-order Taylor model of the smooth term $f$ regularized by a $p$th power with $p>q$. To construct it, we first establish a tensor descent lemma and characterize its behavior in the three regimes $q<p<q+1$, $p=q+1$, and $p>q+1$. Under suitable prox-boundedness and parameter conditions, the associated tensor operator (TOP) has nonempty compact values, and TEE is finite-valued and continuous, preserves global minimizers, and inherits the level-boundedness of $\varphi$. We further show that TEE is locally Lipschitz continuous and directionally differentiable, characterize its Fréchet differentiability, and prove that it is $C^1$ whenever TOP is single-valued. As such, TEE preserves the essential optimization landscape properties of $\varphi$ while enjoying a richer analytical structure, which facilitates the design and analysis of tensor-based optimization methods. Finally, TEE serves as a Lyapunov function for the exact tensor iteration: every cluster point is critical, and, for bounded trajectories with $q<p\le q+1$, an $ε$-stationary point is reached within $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-p/(p-1)})$ iterations. To our knowledge, both the tensor descent lemma and this complexity bound are the first of their kind obtained without assuming global Lipschitz or Hölder continuity of the $q$th-order derivative of $f$.

</details>


### [140] [Asking the Crowd the Right Question: Bias-Cancelling Weights for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04671)
*Ilya Kuruzov,Dmitrii Vishovan,Kirill Novoselov,Yuriy Dorn,Darina Dvinskikh,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将联邦目标的聚合权重视为"群体智慧"机制的工具，提出CROWD方法：利用优化轨迹中的客户端分歧，通过良态线性反演精确辨识各客户端的期望二阶矩，从而在无额外成本下逼近最优（误差能量倒数）权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦目标通常是客户端风险的加权和，且权重几乎总是预先固定。作者质疑这一惯例：最优权重应反比于客户端误差能量，但这些误差能量属于哪一类、群体能否自我恢复，是既有答案所预设却未回答的问题。作者希望无需外部信息、仅凭群体自身恢复最优权重。

Method: 将客户端视为同一真值的带噪视角，各自经过在群体间无偏的独立失真。理论分析表明：真值上的超额风险量级恰好等于聚合偏差能量，故任何优化器都无法修复坏权重；真值仅可辨识到线性倾斜，减去估计的客户端偏差必然退化为均匀加权，因此只能利用期望的各客户端二阶矩。CROWD通过优化轨迹读取群体分歧，用良态线性反演估计期望二阶矩，其实现版本可估计到算法可测的"不相干下限"。

Result: CROWD以零额外成本从优化轨迹读取分歧，在与贝叶斯极小极大下界相同的常数上达到最优：逐实例随视界增长，先验弥散时无条件成立。对任意失真，其与最优权重之比由算法可测的几何不相干度控制。在真实扫描数据（按站点划分、各具误校准探测器）上达到oracle超额风险。

Conclusion: 在实证中，当数据将偏差与噪声分离时，按噪声方差加权反而不如不加权，而CROWD仍保持竞争力；因此在联邦学习中应依据群体分歧自适应确定权重，而非预先固定或简单按噪声方差加权。

Abstract: A federated objective is a weighted sum of client risks, and the weights are almost always fixed in advance. We treat them instead as the only instrument of a wisdom-of-crowds mechanism: clients are noisy views of one truth, each seeing it through an independent distortion that is unbiased across the crowd. That the optimal weights are inversely proportional to the clients' error energies is classical; we begin at the question that answer presupposes, which energies belong there and whether a crowd can recover them from itself. Excess risk on the truth is of the exact order of the aggregate bias energy, so no optimizer can repair a bad weight vector; the truth itself is identifiable only up to a linear tilt, so subtracting estimated client biases provably reproduces uniform weighting. The expected per-client second moments, however, are exactly identified from the law of the crowd's disagreement by a well-conditioned linear inversion, a step random-effects meta-analysis cannot take because a source reports once; their realized counterparts are estimable up to an incoherence floor the algorithm can measure. This yields CROWD, which reads the disagreement off the optimization trajectory at no extra cost and matches a Bayesian minimax lower bound in the same constant: per instance as the horizon grows, and unconditionally as the prior becomes diffuse. For arbitrary distortions it stays competitive with the optimal weights, at a ratio governed by a geometric incoherence the algorithm can measure. On real scans split into sites with their own miscalibrated detectors it attains the oracle excess risk; on a companion federation that pulls bias and noise apart, weighting by noise variance is worse than not weighting at all, and CROWD is not.

</details>


### [141] [On randomized batch-sampling coordinate descent methods for solving linear least-squares problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04685)
*Dong-Yue Xie,Xi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出细粒度随机批量采样坐标下降框架，给出列缩放不变的线性收敛率界，并通过可学习的采样分布加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机块坐标下降的收敛率界通常依赖采样规则，且现有结果对数据矩阵的列缩放敏感，界不够紧、与实际情况不吻合。作者希望刻画坐标级批量采样分布，从而获得更紧、更鲁棒（列缩放不变）的收敛率界，并利用该框架设计能自动捕捉问题结构的采样策略以进一步加速。

Method: 1) 引入坐标级批量采样分布刻画，构造细粒度随机批量采样坐标下降框架，适用于任意块坐标下降方法在任意静态随机采样规则下求解大规模线性最小二乘问题；2) 基于该框架推导对数据矩阵列缩放不变的新的线性收敛率界；3) 将批量采样分布参数化，用基于梯度的训练优化其参数，得到学习型采样策略。

Result: 理论与数值实验表明新收敛率界显著优于现有结果，且更符合实际收敛速度。在CT重建问题上的初步数值结果显示：所提方法比采用其他固定采样规则的变体收敛明显更快，在计算效率上优于所测试的自适应坐标下降方法和全梯度下降。

Conclusion: 坐标级批量采样分布刻画为块坐标下降提供了统一的收敛分析工具，所得界更紧且列缩放不变；学习到的采样分布能够有效捕捉问题特定结构以加速收敛，验证了该框架的有效性与实用价值。

Abstract: This paper refines randomized block coordinate descent by introducing a coordinate-level batch-sampling distribution characterization, yielding a fine-grained randomized batch-sampling coordinate descent framework, which applies to any block coordinate descent method under an arbitrary static stochastic sampling rule for solving large-scale linear least-squares problems. Based on this framework, we derive new linear convergence rate bounds that are invariant to the column scaling of data matrices. Theoretical analysis and numerical experiments validate that the new bounds are significantly sharper than existing results and better aligned with the practical convergence rate. Furthermore, we proposed a learning-based sampling strategy by parameterizing the batch-sampling distribution and optimizing its parameters through gradient-based training. Preliminary numerical results on computed tomography (CT) reconstruction problems demonstrate that the resulting method converges significantly faster than variants using other fixed sampling rules, and outperforms tested adaptive coordinate descent methods and full gradient descent in computational efficiency, confirming that learned sampling distributions can effectively capture problem-specific structure to accelerate convergence within the framework.

</details>


### [142] [Box-iLQR: Constraint-Aware Feedback via Finite-Barrier Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04712)
*Abhijeet,Suman Chakravorty*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Box-iLQR，一种针对状态与控制盒约束的对数障碍iLQR方法，重点在于其反馈策略：保留有限障碍参数可平滑衰减反馈、保持状态与执行器裕度，并在闭环执行不确定下保证约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iLQR与DDP的反向递推同时给出标称轨迹和局部时变反馈律，但硬状态与输入约束会把解推向约束边界，使闭环执行时缺乏修正权限。因此作者希望借助障碍函数改进反馈策略，使标称轨迹与反馈增益同时远离约束边界并保留裕度。

Method: 提出Box-iLQR：对状态与控制盒约束施加对数障碍。障碍梯度将标称轨迹推离约束边界；障碍曲率进入Riccati递推以决定反馈增益。控制障碍曲率在作动器接近饱和时平滑衰减反馈；状态障碍曲率通过值函数反向传播，在遇到未来状态约束前预先塑造反馈。方法主张保留有限障碍参数而非始终将障碍参数趋于零。

Result: 在摆、倒立摆小车、Acrobot与泊车问题上与CL-DDP和ALTRO比较，收敛性与基线相当，部分情形更优。蒙特卡洛实验显示有限障碍下闭环行为改善；六输入MuJoCo鱼形例演示了用仿真rollout估计动力学雅可比的数据驱动Box-iLQR。有限障碍得到的反馈策略在执行不确定性下仍满足约束。

Conclusion: 有限障碍参数可同时保持状态与作动器裕度，并随约束接近平滑调节反馈；Box-iLQR在收敛性与闭环约束满足方面具备竞争力，且可扩展到基于数据估计动力学雅可比的情形。

Abstract: Iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR) and Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) are attractive trajectory-optimization methods because their backward recursions provide both nominal trajectories and local time-varying feedback laws. Hard state and input constraints, however, can drive solutions toward constraint boundaries where little corrective authority remains during closed-loop execution. This paper presents Box-iLQR, a log-barrier iLQR method for box-constrained states and controls, with emphasis on the resulting feedback policy. Barrier gradients bias nominal trajectories away from constraint boundaries, while barrier curvature enters the Riccati recursion that determines the feedback gains. We show that control-barrier curvature smoothly attenuates feedback as an actuator approaches saturation, while state-barrier curvature propagates backward through the value function to shape feedback before future state constraints are encountered. This motivates retaining a finite barrier rather than always driving the barrier parameter toward zero, thereby preserving state and actuator margin. Comparisons with CL-DDP and ALTRO are presented on pendulum, cart-pole, Acrobot, and car-parking problems. Monte Carlo experiments demonstrate improved closed-loop behavior with finite barriers, while a six-input MuJoCo fish example demonstrates data-driven Box-iLQR using dynamics Jacobians estimated from simulator rollouts. Box-iLQR achieves convergence comparable to, and in some cases better than, the baseline methods. Moreover, the feedback policy obtained with a finite barrier ensures constraint satisfaction even under execution uncertainty.

</details>


### [143] [GPU-Accelerated Bregman Douglas-Rachford Splitting for Discrete Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04715)
*Yifan Xu,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出GPU加速的Bregman Douglas-Rachford分裂算法(BDRS)，针对三种输入格式的离散最优传输问题，设计硬件感知的等价表示以提升数值稳定性与运行速度，并在统一最优性度量下与八个GPU基线求解器对比，取得各输入格式下的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散最优传输(DOT)求解在GPU上面临不同输入格式（显式代价矩阵、点云+代价、规则网格可分离代价）下数值稳定性与运行效率的挑战，且现有GPU求解器缺乏统一的跨求解器比较与最优性度量。作者旨在提升各输入格式下的求解性能并提供公平的基准评估。

Method: 提出GPU加速的Bregman Douglas-Rachford分裂算法(BDRS)。针对三种输入格式（显式代价矩阵、带ground cost的点云、规则网格上的可分离代价），分别设计硬件感知的、数学等价的BDRS迭代表示，以提高数值稳定性和经验运行时间。在同一设备上与文献中八个GPU基线求解器进行基准测试。

Result: 在各自输入格式下，所提出的BDRS实现均达到state-of-the-art性能；据作者所知，这是首个采用统一最优性度量的GPU DOT求解器跨求解器研究。

Conclusion: 硬件感知的等价BDRS表示能有效提升GPU上离散最优传输的数值稳定性与运行效率，并在统一度量下验证了其优于现有GPU基线求解器的性能，为GPU DOT求解器提供了首个统一最优性度量的跨求解器基准。

Abstract: We present GPU-accelerated Bregman Douglas--Rachford splitting algorithm (BDRS) for discrete optimal transport problem in three input formats: an explicit cost matrix, a point cloud with a ground cost between them, and a separable cost on a regular grid. For each input format, we propose hardware-aware designs of mathematically equivalent representations for the BDRS iterations to enhance numerical stability and empirical runtime. We benchmark the three proposed implementations against eight GPU baseline solvers from the literature on the same device. We demonstrate that our implementations of BDRS achieve state-of-the-art performance on their respective input formats. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first cross-solver study of GPU DOT solvers with a unified measure of optimality.

</details>


### [144] [Safe Leader-Follower Density Control via Higher-Order Mean-Field Control Barrier Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04733)
*Cinzia Tomaselli,Mario di Bernardo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对"被保护群体不直接被驱动、而由受控群体的场输运"的密度动力学，提出高阶平均场控制障碍函数框架，证明间接驱动不破坏安全条件的控制仿射结构，可在凸二次规划下施加相对阶为二的安全约束，并应用于领导者-跟随者密度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均场控制障碍函数（MF-CBF）多为一阶形式，要求控制输入显式出现在安全泛函的一阶时间导数中；高阶扩展也仅最近针对单一、直接受驱动的群体提出。对于安全群体并非直接受控、而是被第二个受控群体所生成场输运的情形，尚缺乏相应的高阶安全过滤框架。

Method: 针对密度动力学构建高阶 MF-CBF 框架，处理相对阶大于一的安全约束。推导安全泛函二阶时间导数中依赖控制的部分，得到其闭式表达：关于控制速度场线性，核为受驱动密度与一阶变分的空间梯度之积。由此将相对阶为二的安全约束转化为凸二次规划。随后将该框架特化到存在危险区域的领导者-跟随者密度控制问题，以"各群体与危险区域的重叠量低于给定阈值"定义安全；分析跟随者安全泛函相对领导者控制场为相对阶二、领导者安全泛函为相对阶一，并刻画两个同时约束的不可行条件。

Result: 主要结论是间接驱动不会破坏安全条件的控制仿射结构，二阶控制相关项可闭式获得且关于控制速度场线性，故相对阶二的安全约束仍可通过凸二次规划施加。在领导者-跟随者问题中，明确了跟随者约束（相对阶二）与领导者约束（相对阶一）的相对阶差异；给出了两个约束同时不可行的精确条件，提出自适应增益机制以缓解潜在冲突，并对安全修正引起的跟踪误差给出界。杂波环境下的数值仿真展示了由此产生的安全-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作将高阶 MF-CBF 从"直接受驱动的单一群体"推广到"由受控群体场间接输运的安全群体"，证明间接驱动下仍保持控制仿射结构并可用凸二次规划实现安全过滤，同时给出不可行性判据、冲突缓解机制与跟踪误差界，为多群体密度安全控制提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Safety filters for large-scale multi-agent systems can be formulated at the population level through mean-field control barrier functions (MF-CBFs). Most existing formulations are first-order, requiring the control input to appear explicitly in the first time derivative of the safety functional; higher-order extensions have only very recently been proposed for a single, directly actuated population. We consider instead the case in which the population to be kept safe is not directly actuated, but is transported by a field generated by a second, controlled population. For this setting, we develop a higher-order MF-CBF framework for density dynamics with safety constraints of relative degree larger than one. Our main result is that indirect actuation of this kind does not destroy the control-affine structure of the safety condition: the control-dependent part of the second time derivative of the safety functional is obtained in closed form and is linear in the control velocity field, with a kernel given by the actuated density times the spatial gradient of a first variation. Relative-degree-two safety constraints can therefore still be enforced through a convex quadratic program. We then specialize the framework to a leader-follower density control problem in the presence of dangerous regions, where safety is ensured by keeping the overlap of each population with those regions below a prescribed threshold. We show that the follower safety functional has relative degree two with respect to the leader control field, while the leader safety functional has relative degree one. We further characterize exactly when the two simultaneous constraints are infeasible, introduce an adaptive-gain mechanism to mitigate potential conflicts, and bound the tracking errors induced by the safety correction. Numerical simulations in cluttered environments illustrate the resulting safety-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [145] [Global Optimization of Mixed-Integer Bound-Constrained Quadratic Fractional Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04755)
*Marco Locatelli,Veronica Piccialli,Antonio M. Sudoso*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究含二值与连续箱约束的混合整数二次分式优化问题，提出基于Dinkelbach参数化重构的精确算法，并用变邻域搜索启发式为外层迭代提供初始可行解，数值实验表明其优于通用精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数二次分式优化问题（目标为两个二次函数之比）在投资组合选择、信号处理与数据分析中广泛出现，但该问题非凸且含离散变量，通用求解方法效率不高，因而需要专门的精确求解框架。

Method: 采用Dinkelbach参数化重构将分式问题逐次转化为非凸混合整数二次子问题（箱约束）；每个子问题由专门的branch-and-bound全局求解器求解；外层用变邻域搜索启发式提供初始可行解以加速收敛，该启发式在连续局部搜索与二值空间探索（固定当前可行解的连续变量）之间交替。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提方法在性能上优于基于Dinkelbach参数化重构的通用精确算法。

Conclusion: 将Dinkelbach精确框架、专用分支定界全局求解与VNS启发式初始化相结合，可有效提升混合整数二次分式优化问题的求解效率，验证了该组合策略的实用价值。

Abstract: We study mixed-integer quadratic fractional optimization problems with binary and continuous box-constrained variables. The objective is the ratio of two quadratic functions, a structure that arises naturally in portfolio selection, signal processing, and data analytics. We propose an exact algorithm based on Dinkelbach's parametric reformulation. At each Dinkelbach iteration, the fractional problem is replaced by a nonconvex mixed-integer quadratic subproblem with box constraints. This subproblem is solved globally by a specialized branch-and-bound solver. To accelerate the outer scheme, we use a Variable Neighborhood Search heuristic to provide an initial feasible solution to the Dinkelbach scheme. The heuristic alternates between continuous local searches and a binary-space exploration phase in which the continuous variables of a feasible incumbent are fixed. Numerical results show that the proposed approach outperforms general-purpose exact algorithms based on Dinkelbach's parametric reformulation.

</details>


### [146] [From Inverse Problems to Adjoint Gradients: A Discrete, Implementation-Oriented Tutorial on PDE-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04789)
*Solmaz S. Kia*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 这是一篇以概念理解和实现为导向的伴随方法教程，从PDE约束反问题出发，通过迭代下降法的视角推导伴随梯度，并以振动弦反问题为贯穿案例展示完整工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随方法是PDE约束优化中计算梯度的标准手段，在参数估计、模型校准和最优控制中处于核心地位。然而现有资料往往直接假定伴随方程，缺乏对推导过程、实现细节及离散化影响的清晰阐释，学习者难以建立数学推导与计算算法之间的联系。因此本文旨在提供一份面向实现的、循序渐进的教程以弥补这一空白。

Method: 采用离散化、面向实现的讲解方式：从一个由偏微分方程支配的反问题出发，构建相应的优化问题，并从迭代下降法的角度推导伴随梯度；明确状态、参数、目标函数与约束的角色，使伴随方程直接从梯度计算中获得而非被假定；重点关注显式推导、实现细节、离散化对伴随构造的影响、时间反向伴随递推的来源以及梯度验证的实用流程。

Result: 给出伴随梯度的显式推导过程及其与计算算法的对应关系，阐明离散化对伴随构造的影响，解释时间反向伴随递推的成因，并提供梯度验证的实用方法；通过振动弦反问题完整演示了从模型构建到基于伴随的优化的全流程。

Conclusion: 该教程面向高年级本科生、研究生及从业者，提供了伴随梯度计算的逐步理解路径，强调概念清晰与实现可行性，有助于读者将数学推导与计算算法紧密结合。

Abstract: The adjoint method is a standard approach for computing gradients in PDE-constrained optimization and central to parameter estimation, model calibration, and optimal control. This tutorial presents a discrete, implementation-oriented introduction to the method with an emphasis on conceptual understanding. Starting from an inverse problem governed by a partial differential equation, it develops the corresponding optimization problem and derives the adjoint gradient from the perspective of iterative descent methods. The roles of the state, the parameters, the objective, and the constraint are made explicit, and the adjoint equation is obtained directly from the gradient computation rather than postulated. Particular attention is given to explicit derivations, implementation details, and the connection between the mathematical development and the resulting computational algorithm. The tutorial also examines the influence of discretization on the adjoint construction, explains the origin of the backward-in-time adjoint recursion, and presents practical procedures for gradient verification. A vibrating-string inverse problem serves as a running example, illustrating the complete workflow from model formulation to adjoint-based optimization. The presentation is intended for advanced undergraduate students, graduate students, and practitioners seeking a step-by-step understanding of adjoint gradient computation.

</details>


### [147] [Communication-Efficient Differentially Private Gradient Tracking for Distributed Optimization via Local Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04854)
*Mihitha Maithripala,Chenyang Qiu,Zongli Lin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在相邻通信轮之间仅进行一次无通信本地更新的梯度追踪分布式优化，通过在通信轮对原始状态和梯度追踪方向加噪实现隐私保护，证明了均值收敛与无限时域纯差分隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化中节点间共享梯度信息可能泄露本地数据隐私；仅靠本地计算无法保护梯度信息，需要在通信过程中引入扰动，同时尽量不牺牲收敛性与优化精度。

Method: 采用梯度追踪（gradient tracking）框架，通信轮之间只做一次无通信的本地更新；在通信迭代时对原始状态（primal state）与梯度追踪方向同时施加噪声，而本地更新保持无噪；构造聚合变量用于追踪，并采用几何衰减的拉普拉斯扰动，在仿射目标函数邻接关系下分析隐私。

Result: 刻画了含噪情形下的极限一致点，在给定参数条件下证明了均值收敛；在仿射目标邻接关系与几何衰减拉普拉斯扰动下，建立了针对完整通信记录的无限时域纯差分隐私；仿真显示该方法在给定隐私预算下在收敛性与优化精度方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 通过在通信轮对状态与追踪方向加噪、并保持本地更新无噪，可在单次无通信本地更新的梯度追踪框架下同时实现收敛性保证与无限时域纯差分隐私，且在隐私预算约束下保持较好的收敛与精度表现。

Abstract: This paper studies privacy-preserving distributed optimization using gradient tracking with one communication-free local update between consecutive communication rounds. Since local computation alone does not protect gradient information, we perturb both the primal state and the gradient-tracking direction at communication iterations while keeping local updates noise-free. We establish an aggregate variable for tracking, characterize the limiting consensus point in the presence of added random noise, and prove convergence in mean under certain parameter conditions. We also establish infinite-horizon pure differential privacy for the complete communication transcript under an affine objective adjacency relation and geometrically decaying Laplace perturbations. Simulation results illustrate the advantages of the proposed method in terms of the convergence and optimization accuracy given a privacy budget.

</details>


### [148] [From Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman to Riccati via Carleman: The Scalar Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04873)
*Philip J. Elias,Qiyu Sun,Nader Motee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文揭示非线性系统的HJB方程隐藏着一个Riccati方程，通过Carleman线性化将标量输入仿射解析系统的HJB提升为下三角Toeplitz矩阵形式的无穷维Riccati方程，其解为值函数的导数，可逐项精确计算并指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统的HJB方程是非线性偏微分方程，其形式本身难以提供有用信息。作者希望揭示其内在结构，将非线性最优控制问题转化为更易于处理的形式，并为解析非线性系统的最优控制建立一般性理论。

Method: 采用Carleman线性化方法。针对标量输入仿射且数据解析的系统，将提升后的HJB方程表示为下三角Toeplitz矩阵中的单一无穷维Riccati方程，其首项对应平衡点处标准线性化的Riccati方程，其余项包含非线性项。在标准线性化可控且代价可检测的条件下求解该方程。

Result: 在标准线性化可控、代价可检测的条件下，该方程存在唯一具有正对角元的解，可逐项计算，且该解即为值函数的导数。解的有限截断是精确的，并在原点附近指数收敛于值函数导数。标量情形存在闭式解，作为检验所有提升方程结果的基准。该结构不依赖于状态维数。

Conclusion: Carleman线性化揭示了HJB方程隐藏的无穷维Riccati结构，且不涉及任何近似。该方法论指向一种一般性最优控制理论，其中解析非线性系统的HJB方程可转化为无穷维Riccati方程。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) equation of a nonlinear system is a nonlinear partial differential equation, and in that form it gives little away. This paper shows that it hides a Riccati equation. Carleman linearization reveals this structure. For scalar input-affine systems with analytic data, the lifted HJB equation is a single infinite-dimensional Riccati equation in a lower-triangular Toeplitz matrix. Its first entry is the Riccati equation of the standard linearization at equilibrium. Its remaining entries hold the nonlinear terms. Nothing about this is an approximation.
  Under controllability of the standard linearization and detectability of the cost, the equation has exactly one solution with positive diagonal entries, and it can be computed one entry at a time. This solution is the derivative of the value function. We prove that finite-sections of the solution are exact and converge exponentially to the derivative of the value function near the origin. The scalar case has a closed-form solution. This closed form is our benchmark: every result on the lifted equation is checked against it. Nothing in this structure depends on the state dimension. Our methodology points to a general theory of optimal control in which the HJB equation of analytic nonlinear systems becomes an infinite-dimensional Riccati equation.

</details>


### [149] [Near-Optimal Complexity of Finite-Sum Nonconvex-Strongly-Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04944)
*Qihao Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了有限和、非凸-强凹优化在均方平均光滑条件下的极小极大期望查询复杂度，上至对数因子。对随机零尊重增量一阶算法，复杂度为 $\tildeΘ(n+\min\{\sqrt{n}κ,n^{3/4}\sqrtκ\}LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$（$κ\ge1$，精确对偶初始化，$0<\varepsilon^2\le c_0LΔ$）。下界由对偶链构造给出，上界由 Catalyst 与作者提出的递归近端下降-上升（RPDA）算法共同实现；在个体光滑条件下还给出对应下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限和、非凸-强凹（finite-sum nonconvex-strongly-concave）优化的查询复杂度，是极小极大（minimax）优化中重要的理论问题。已有结果（如 Catalyst 上界）未能完整刻画在均方平均光滑（mean-squared averaged smoothness）条件下的最优复杂度，特别是在条件数 $κ$ 的不同区间上界与下界是否匹配尚不明确，因此需要给出紧的极小极大复杂度刻画并填补中段条件数区间的算法空白。

Method: 1) 设定算法类为随机零尊重增量一阶算法（randomized zero-respecting incremental first-order algorithms），在均方平均光滑条件下分析期望查询复杂度。2) 构造对偶链（dual-chain）实例以导出下界，证明其与现有 Catalyst 上界在 $κ\ge\sqrt{n}$ 时匹配。3) 针对 $1\leκ\le\sqrt{n}$，提出递归近端下降-上升（RPDA）算法，在近端子问题间保留递归梯度估计器，并采用固定查询预算。4) 在个体光滑（individual smoothness）条件下另行构造下界证明 $Ω(n+\min\{κ,\sqrt{nκ}\}LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$。

Result: 主要结果给出，上至对数因子，期望查询复杂度为 $\tildeΘ(n+\min\{\sqrt{n}κ,n^{3/4}\sqrtκ\}LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$，对全部 $κ=L/μ\ge1$ 成立（精确对偶初始化，$0<\varepsilon^2\le c_0LΔ$，$c_0>0$ 为通用常数）。对偶链下界在 $κ\ge\sqrt{n}$ 时与 Catalyst 上界匹配；RPDA 在 $1\leκ\le\sqrt{n}$ 时达到 $\tilde{O}(n+\sqrt{n}κLΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$ 且查询预算固定。两个上界均保证期望平方原始梯度不超过 $\varepsilon^2$。个体光滑条件下证明下界 $Ω(n+\min\{κ,\sqrt{nκ}\}LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$，在 $κ\ge n$ 时与双线性子类的多项式上界匹配，中间条件数区间的差距仍开放。

Conclusion: 论文给出了有限和、非凸-强凹优化在均方平均光滑条件下（对随机零尊重增量一阶算法）上至对数因子的极小极大期望查询复杂度紧刻画，并通过对偶链下界与 Catalyst、RPDA 上界完成了 $κ\ge\sqrt{n}$ 与 $1\leκ\le\sqrt{n}$ 两个区间的匹配。个体光滑条件下亦给出下界，其中 $κ\ge n$ 时对双线性子类紧，而中间条件数区间的最优复杂度仍为开放问题。

Abstract: We characterize, up to logarithmic factors, the minimax expected query complexity of finite-sum nonconvex-strongly-concave optimization under mean-squared averaged smoothness. For randomized zero-respecting incremental first-order algorithms, the complexity is $\tildeΘ(n+\min\{\sqrt{n}\,κ,n^{3/4}\sqrtκ\}\,LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$ throughout $κ=L/μ\ge1$, under exact dual initialization and for $0<\varepsilon^2\le c_0LΔ$, where $c_0>0$ is universal. This characterization is our main result. A dual-chain construction provides the lower bound and matches the existing Catalyst upper bound for $κ\ge\sqrt{n}$. For $1\leκ\le\sqrt{n}$, we introduce recursive proximal descent-ascent (RPDA), which retains a recursive gradient estimator across proximal subproblems and attains $\tilde{O}(n+\sqrt{n}\,κLΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$ with a fixed query budget. Both upper bounds guarantee an expected squared primal gradient at most $\varepsilon^2$. Under individual smoothness, we also prove $Ω(n+\min\{κ,\sqrt{nκ}\}\,LΔ\varepsilon^{-2})$ for every $κ\ge1$. It matches the polynomial upper rate for a bilinear subclass when $κ\ge n$; the intermediate-condition-number gap remains open.

</details>


### [150] [Social Optimality Does Not Imply Coalitional Stability in Unweighted Max-k-Cut Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04948)
*Hritiz Gogoi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文否定了 Max-$k$-Cut 博弈中"每个最优染色都是强均衡"这一猜想（对所有 $k\ge3$），给出反例，并建立了联盟收益的精确恒等式及强偏离下单一色边数的紧致上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中反复出现一个猜想：对于无权重图，Max-$k$-Cut 博弈的每个最优染色都具备强均衡这一稳定性。作者旨在检验并推翻该猜想，同时刻画联盟偏离的收益结构。

Method: 构造有限、简单、连通、无权重的图，其中存在最优染色而非强均衡；建立一个关于联盟收益、割值变化与内部冲突之间的精确恒等式；并对一般 Nash 均衡的强偏离，推导联盟内部单一色边数的上界。

Result: 对每个 $k\ge3$ 给出了反例：存在最优染色不是强均衡，有利的联盟偏离导向另一个最优染色，联盟成员全部获益而联盟外玩家承担相应损失、社会福利不变。精确恒等式在最大度至多 $2k-1$ 时恢复了最优染色的已知稳定性保证。对一般 Nash 均衡的强偏离，给出了联盟内单一色边数的紧致上界，并证明该数量可超过联盟规模。

Conclusion: 该猜想对所有 $k\ge3$ 不成立；最优染色与强均衡之间的等价性仅在受限条件（如最大度 $\le 2k-1$）下成立。联盟偏离的收益可通过割值与内部冲突的恒等式精确刻画，强偏离中联盟内单一色边数不受联盟规模限制。

Abstract: In Max-$k$-Cut games, optimal colorings maximize social welfare, but are they stable against coordinated deviations that strictly benefit every participating player? A recurring conjecture in the literature asserts that, for unweighted graphs, every optimal coloring has this stability property, known as strong equilibrium. We disprove the conjecture for every $k\ge3$ by exhibiting a finite, simple, connected, unweighted graph with an optimal coloring that is not a strong equilibrium. The profitable coalitional deviation leads to another optimal coloring: every coalition member gains, while players outside the coalition bear the corresponding losses and social welfare remains unchanged. We also give an exact identity relating coalitional gains, changes in cut value, and internal conflicts, recovering the known stability guarantee for optimal colorings when the maximum degree is at most $2k-1$. For strong deviations from general Nash equilibria, we establish a sharp upper bound on the number of monochromatic edges remaining within the coalition and show that this number can exceed the coalition size.

</details>


### [151] [Optimal Oracle Complexity for Finite-Sum Monotone Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05038)
*Qihao Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种求解有限和单调包含问题的切换正则化方法，在均方Lipschitz连续条件下达到最优期望算子复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方差缩减求解器在正则化的每个阶段重启会带来额外的 n log n 代价，限制了有限和单调包含问题的最优复杂度。

Method: 切换正则化方法：在正则化强度 L/√n 处切换为带中心化的随机近端迭代，并在剩余阶段之间携带算子估计以控制总代价。

Result: 以 O(n+√n·LR/ε) 次期望分量与resolvent评估找到满足 (E‖F(y)+g‖²)^{1/2}≤ε 的点 y 和证书 g；并证明匹配的下界 Ω(n+√n·LR/ε)。

Conclusion: 对 0<ε≤LR/2，该方法在随机线性张成分量算子模型下达到最优最坏情况期望分量复杂度（至通用常数）。

Abstract: We present an oracle-optimal method for finite-sum monotone inclusions under mean-square Lipschitz continuity. Our switching regularization method finds a point $y$ and a certificate $g\in G(y)$ with $(\mathbb{E}\|F(y)+g\|^2)^{1/2}\le\varepsilon$ using $\mathcal{O}(n+\sqrt{n}LR/\varepsilon)$ expected component evaluations and resolvent evaluations. It removes the additive $n\log n$ cost of restarting a variance-reduced solver at every regularization stage by switching to a centered stochastic proximal iteration at regularization strength $L/\sqrt{n}$. Carrying an operator estimate between the remaining stages limits their total cost to $\mathcal{O}(n)$. A matching $Ω(n+\sqrt{n}LR/\varepsilon)$ lower bound holds for randomized linear-span component-oracle algorithms with adaptive stopping and expected query budgets. Thus, for $0<\varepsilon\le LR/2$, our method attains the optimal worst-case expected component complexity in this oracle model, up to universal constants.

</details>


### [152] [A Local Geometric Analysis of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction via Error Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05072)
*Peng Wang,Po Chen,Huikang Liu,Rujun Jiang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了MCR²目标在局部极大值附近满足误差界性质，并据此导出Polyak–Łojasiewicz不等式与二次增长等正则性条件，进而证明一阶方法可线性收敛到局部极大值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR²已成为学习紧致且具判别性表示的重要原则，但其优化几何的理论理解仍然有限，尤其是为何一阶方法在其局部极大值附近常表现出快速收敛，缺乏理论解释。

Method: 作者在MCR²目标的局部极大值邻域内建立误差界（error-bound）性质，并由此推导出Polyak–Łojasiewicz不等式和二次增长等正则性条件；在此基础上，利用该性质分析一阶方法的收敛性，并结合数值实验验证理论预测。

Result: MCR²目标在局部极大值邻域满足误差界性质，点的梯度范数可上界其到局部极大值集合的距离；在温和条件下，一阶方法能线性收敛到MCR²的局部极大值。数值实验验证了预测的局部收敛行为，并表明优化MCR²在保持有竞争力的下游精度的同时改善了表示几何。

Conclusion: 该工作从优化几何角度为MCR²的快速局部收敛提供了理论解释，误差界性质是其线性收敛的核心机制，理论与实验共同支持MCR²在表示学习中的有效性。

Abstract: While the maximal coding rate reduction (MCR$^2$) objective has become a useful principle for learning compact and discriminative representations, a theoretical understanding of its optimization geometry remains limited, especially regarding why first-order methods often exhibit fast convergence near its local maximizers. In this work, we show that the MCR$^2$ objective satisfies an error-bound property in a neighborhood of its local maximizers. This property guarantees that the distance to the local maximizer set can be bounded above by the gradient norm at any point in the neighborhood, directly implying regularity conditions such as the Polyak--Łojasiewicz inequality and quadratic growth. Leveraging this property, we prove that first-order methods converge linearly to a local maximizer of the MCR$^2$ objective under mild conditions. Numerical experiments validate the predicted local convergence behavior and provide empirical evidence that optimizing the MCR$^2$ objective improves representation geometry while maintaining competitive downstream accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [Single-call Polyak-Type Extragradient Methods for Monotone Root-Finding Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05138)
*Keke Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出单次算子评估的Polyak外推梯度变体SPolyakEG，通过复用上一次外推算子评估将每迭代算子评估次数由两次降为一次，并在延迟临界条件下证明了单调算子的O(1/K)残差收敛率及强单调下的线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polyak外推梯度（PolyakEG）每迭代需两次算子评估，计算开销较大。作者希望在不损失收敛性的前提下降低单次迭代的计算成本。

Method: 通过回收（复用）前一步的外推算子评估结果，构造了单次调用的PolyakEG变体SPolyakEG；在延迟临界条件（delayed critical condition）下进行收敛性分析。

Result: 在延迟临界条件下，SPolyakEG对单调算子达到O(1/K)的残差收敛速率；在强单调性假设下达到线性收敛；每迭代算子评估次数由两次减少为一次。

Conclusion: SPolyakEG在保持PolyakEG收敛阶的同时将每迭代算子评估次数减半，降低了计算代价，适用于单调及强单调算子问题。

Abstract: We propose a single-call variant of Polyak extragradient, termed SPolyakEG, by recycling the previous extrapolation operator evaluation. Under a delayed critical condition, we establish that SPolyakEG achieves an $O(1/K)$ residual convergence rate for monotone operators and linear convergence under strong monotonicity. Compared with PolyakEG, SPolyakEG reduces the number of operator evaluations from two to one per iteration.

</details>


### [154] [Ordered Weighted Average Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05156)
*Luisa I. Martínez-Merino,Justo Puerto,Antonio M. Rodríguez-Chía*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出融入OWA算子的新型SVR模型，按偏差在有序向量中的位置差异化惩罚，给出连续与混合整数两种非线性建模方式及启发式求解方法，在真实与合成数据上验证精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SVR对ε-带外偏差施加对称、等权重惩罚，无法体现不同偏差的相对重要性差异；本文希望借助OWA算子对偏差按次序赋权，实现非对称、可定制的惩罚结构，并使其可扩展至非线性核函数场景。

Method: 在SVR中引入OWA算子，依据各偏差在全体偏差有序向量中的排序位置决定其惩罚权重。构建两类非线性模型：针对非递减单调权重向量的连续型模型，以及针对一般权重向量的混合整数模型；通过对偶方法引入非线性核函数。提出两种启发式方法快速获取回归超平面，并针对非线性核函数提出第三种启发式方法。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上的计算实验表明，精确模型在MAE和MSE两项标准指标上均得到更优的回归函数；启发式方法在较大数据集上计算效率显著提升，在解质量与计算开销之间实现权衡。

Conclusion: OWA-SVR通过按偏差次序赋权拓展了标准SVR的惩罚机制，配套的精确定式与启发式算法分别适用于小规模高精度与大规模高效率场景，为非线性回归提供灵活且实用的建模框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel Support Vector Regression (SVR) model that incorporates Ordered Weighted Average (OWA) operators to differently penalize deviations of observations from the ε-strip. The penalty is determined based on the position of each deviation in the ordered vector of all deviations. A key contribution of this work is the development of two nonlinear formulations: a continuous formulation for non-decreasing monotone weight vectors and a mixed-integer formulation for general weight vectors. By leveraging dual approaches associated with these formulations, the model accommodates nonlinear kernel functions. To enhance computational efficiency, two heuristic approaches are proposed for deriving suitable regression hyperplanes in reduced computation times, as compared to exact methods. Additionally, a third heuristic approach is developed to handle nonlinear kernel functions effectively. Computational experiments conducted on both real and synthetic datasets demonstrate that the exact formulations yield remarkable regression functions with respect to the following standard metrics: mean absolute errors (MAE) and mean squared errors (MSE). The heuristic approaches are shown to be particularly efficient for larger datasets, striking a balance between solution quality and computational effort.

</details>


### [155] [Chebyshev-Exact Acceleration under Hessian Variation, II: Computer-Assisted Variational Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05164)
*Dmitry Pasechnyuk-Vilensky,Takáč*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究终止残差为Chebyshev极小极大多项式的两步递推，证明Part I中的sine-Jacobi递推在该响应范数下渐近最优（误差因子1.000004），并通过Jacobi Green矩阵、密度打包与Fredholm行列式等工具给出紧凑下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有正步长与非负动量系数的两步递推在固定二次函数上终止误差相同，但对逐步Hessian扰动的响应可以不同。作者希望确定哪种终止精确递推对这类扰动响应最优，并量化sine-Jacobi递推的最优性程度。

Method: 将增益归约到Jacobi Green矩阵的对角能量；用中心化提升把该矩阵表示为指数核；将对角线打包为直线上的密度以保持能量并控制Birman-Schwinger谱变化；用校准恒等式结合基态参数与一个Fredholm行列式给出下界；再用另外两个行列式与迹观察通过显式紧致状态区域上的逐点不等式改进界；最后用区间算术验证有理证书，并构造满足观测与归一化且能量低于sine值的密度。

Result: 证明sine-Jacobi递推在该响应范数下渐近最优至因子1.000004，且对0<μ<L一致成立。任何终止精确递推满足A_N≥(2√c_cert - o(1))N^{3/2}ε_N^★/(L-μ)，其中c_cert>1.14268353624；而sine-Jacobi递推达到常数2√c_sin，其中c_sin<1.14269218。此外，不存在由指定类型的有限多个观测构成的C^1函数能在sine密度处给出局部尖锐下界。

Conclusion: sine-Jacobi递推在两步递推类中对Hessian扰动的响应范数上渐近最优，且最优性常数与理论下界非常接近；所用下界证书与密度构造方法可独立用于相关谱优化问题。

Abstract: We study two-step recurrences with positive step sizes and nonnegative momentum coefficients whose terminal residual is the Chebyshev minimax polynomial on a prescribed spectral interval $[μ,L]$. These recurrences have the same terminal error on a fixed quadratic, but their first-order responses to stepwise Hessian perturbations can differ. We prove that the sine-Jacobi recurrence of Part I is asymptotically optimal for this response norm within a factor of $1.000004$, uniformly over $0<μ<L$. Every terminal-exact recurrence satisfies $A_N\ge(2\sqrt{c_{cert}}-o(1))N^{3/2}ε_N^\star/(L-μ)$ with $c_{cert}>1.14268353624$, whereas the sine-Jacobi recurrence attains the constant $2\sqrt{c_{\sin}}$ with $c_{\sin}<1.14269218$.
  The proof reduces the gain to the diagonal energy of a Jacobi Green matrix. A centering lift represents this matrix by an exponential kernel; packing its diagonal into a density on the line preserves the energy and controls the change in the Birman-Schwinger spectrum. A calibrated identity gives a lower bound using the ground parameter and one Fredholm determinant. Two further determinants and a trace observation improve the bound through a pointwise inequality on an explicit compact state region. One rational certificate verifies this inequality by interval arithmetic. A second certificate constructs a density satisfying the selected observations and both normalizations with energy below the sine value. Finally, no $C^1$ function of finitely many observations of the specified kinds gives a locally sharp lower bound at the sine density.

</details>


### [156] [Ambiguity-Aware Multi-Agent Framework for Automated Operations Research under Logical Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05169)
*Chengxi She,Xingyu Lu,Yue Zhao,Qitao Shi,Caihua Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出AMAO——一种面向自动化运筹学（OR）问题求解的歧义感知多智能体框架。该框架通过两阶段对齐（逻辑对齐与来源充分性验证）先处理描述中的逻辑不一致，再进行建模与编码；同时提出了歧义感知基准及对话扩展用于评测。32B模型实现86.8%的错误恢复成功率和60.4%的求解准确率，8B模型达到47.9%求解准确率，交互修复在需澄清案例中达90.71%的完整描述准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际场景中，利益相关者对运筹学问题的描述常包含不完整知识与模糊表达，此类歧义描述存在逻辑不一致，无法直接用于问题建模。因此，自动化求解OR问题的前提是事先处理这些逻辑不一致。

Method: 提出AMAO框架，在下游建模与编码前通过两阶段对齐解决逻辑不一致：1）逻辑对齐智能体（采用OR引导的专家结构）通过对变量、参数、目标与约束的监督式路由生成对齐候选；2）来源充分性验证器判断原始描述是否支持所需修补；当证据不足时，交互式修复智能体请求额外信息并在建模编码前修订描述。此外提出歧义感知基准及其匹配的对话扩展，用于两阶段评测。

Result: 32B模型取得86.8%错误恢复成功率与60.4%求解准确率，分别达基线的1.98倍和1.86倍；8B模型求解准确率达47.9%，超过所评估的最佳变体（如DeepSeek-V4-Flash与Claude-sonnet-4.5）；交互修复在需澄清案例中达90.71%完整描述准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将基于上下文的修复与证据评估及针对性用户澄清相结合，可有效支持自动化OR建模。

Abstract: Operations research (OR) problems are often described by stakeholders with incomplete knowledge and vague expressions in real-world settings. Such ambiguous descriptions can not be used to formulation directly. Thus, automating OR problems solving requires processing this logical inconsistency in advance. To address this challenge, we propose an \textbf{A}mbiguity-Aware \textbf{M}ulti-Agent Framework for \textbf{A}utomated \textbf{O}R Problem Solving, \textbf{AMAO}, which first addresses logical inconsistency through two-stage alignment before downstream formulation and coding. Specifically, a logical alignment agent with OR-guided experts structure first produces an aligned candidate using supervised routing over variables, parameters, objectives, and constraints. Then, a source-sufficiency verifier determines whether the original description supports the required repairs. When evidence is insufficient, an interactive repair agent requests additional information and revises the description before modeling and coding. In addition, an ambiguity-aware benchmark and its matched dialogue extension are proposed to support evaluation of both stages. The 32B model achieved 86.8\% error-recovery success and 60.4\% solution accuracy, reaching 1.98 and 1.86 times the respective base-model scores. The 8B model achieved 47.9\% solution accuracy, exceeding the best evaluated variant such as DeepSeek-V4-Flash and Claude-sonnet-4.5. Interactive repair further achieved 90.71\% complete-description accuracy when cases requiring clarification. These results support combining context-based repair with evidence assessment and targeted user clarification for automated OR modeling.

</details>


### [157] [Harnessing LLMs with Direction-Scope Control for Combinatorial Solver Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05221)
*Yili Cao,Anqi Li,Haoran Li,Yongtang Shi,Zicheng Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出DiSH，一个联合控制修订方向与范围的harness，用于缓解LLM智能体在自动启发式设计中的“可行性震荡”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在组合优化的自动启发式设计中，LLM通过执行与验证反馈迭代修订求解器，但每次修订常破坏此前已验证的求解器行为，导致精炼过程不稳定且代价高昂，该现象被称为可行性震荡。

Method: 作者在多个组合优化基准、AHD方法与骨干LLM上刻画了可行性震荡现象，分析发现可行性退化常伴随大幅求解器修订，且反馈冗余严重，难以识别关键失败。基于此提出DiSH harness：选取代表性失败证据指导每次更新，并随着可行行为累积逐步收窄修订范围。

Result: 在九个组合优化基准上，DiSH一致提升QYI分数并减少可行性震荡，同时相比使用完整反馈的先前方法将反馈token消耗减半。

Conclusion: DiSH可作为harness控制下求解器精炼的强基线。

Abstract: Harnesses are increasingly unlocking the potential of large language model (LLM) agents by making their actions more controllable. In automatic heuristic design (AHD) for combinatorial optimization (CO), LLMs iteratively revise solvers using execution and verification feedback. However, these revisions often break previously validated solver behavior, making refinement unstable and costly. We characterize this pattern as feasibility oscillation and document it across CO benchmarks, AHD methods, and backbone LLMs. Tracing this oscillation, our analysis reveals that feasibility degradation often follows large solver revisions, while substantial feedback redundancy makes it harder to identify which failures deserve attention. Motivated by these findings, we introduce DiSH, a harness that jointly controls revision direction and scope. It selects representative failure evidence to guide each update and progressively narrows the revision scope as feasible behavior accumulates. Across nine CO benchmarks, DiSH consistently improves QYI scores and reduces feasibility oscillation, while roughly halving feedback token consumption relative to prior methods that use full feedback. These results establish DiSH as a strong baseline for solver refinement under harness control.

</details>


### [158] [Curvature, Scaling, and Complexity in Riemannian Projection-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05272)
*Binh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为黎曼Frank-Wolfe优化建立了刻画可行域几何依赖性的几何框架，引入幂型双测地模量，证明精确转移原理并给出收敛率与紧性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影无关方法以线性最小化预言机替代投影，其收敛性对可行域几何高度敏感；然而在黎曼流形上这种几何依赖尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 提出幂型双测地模量以量化可行集的内在几何，建立精确转移原理，将该模量转化为由黎曼对数映射微分调制的Frank-Wolfe标度余量，从而分离可行集几何与环境黎曼扭曲的贡献；用Rauch比较给出曲率相关的扭曲显式证书，并在仿射不变度量的正定锥上给出对数微分的相对特征值展布的精确谱刻画。

Result: 结合二次误差界，幂型几何导出gap-feedback short-step策略的收敛率O(t^{-p/(p-1)})；对每个固定p>3，双曲平面中的紧可行集在同一策略下达到匹配速率Θ(t^{-p/(p-1)})。

Conclusion: 几何导出的指数p/(p-1)在所考虑的几何受控类上对gap-feedback short-step策略是尖锐的。

Abstract: Projection-free methods replace projections by linear minimization oracles, making their convergence fundamentally sensitive to the geometry of the feasible region. We develop a geometric framework for this dependence in Riemannian Frank--Wolfe optimization. A power-type double-geodesic modulus quantifies the intrinsic geometry of the feasible set, and an exact transfer principle shows that this modulus induces a Frank--Wolfe scaling margin modulated by the differential of the Riemannian logarithm map. The resulting factorization separates the contributions of feasible-set geometry and ambient Riemannian distortion. Rauch comparison provides explicit curvature-dependent certificates for this distortion, while on the positive-definite cone equipped with the affine-invariant metric the logarithm differential admits an exact spectral characterization in terms of relative eigenvalue spread. Combined with a quadratic error bound, the power-type geometry yields the convergence rate $O(t^{-p/(p-1)})$ for the gap-feedback short-step policy. For every fixed $p>3$, a compact feasible set in a hyperbolic plane attains the matching rate $Θ(t^{-p/(p-1)})$ under the same policy. The geometry-derived exponent $p/(p-1)$ is therefore sharp for the gap-feedback short-step policy over the geometry-controlled class considered here.

</details>


### [159] [Polynomial Acceleration of Alternating Projections: Chebyshev Acceleration, Convergence Rates, and Retractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06150)
*Dengyu Zheng,Shixiang Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出定点流形加速（FPMA）框架，用于分析逐点固定一个光滑流形的光滑映射的局部收敛与多项式加速，并给出交替投影（AP）在Friedrichs角很小时的加速收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替投影（AP）在光滑流形干净交集附近的收敛速度会因Friedrichs角较小而变得很慢，需要对该类问题的局部收敛性与加速方法给出统一的理论框架与保证。

Method: 建立定点流形加速（FPMA）框架：在适当的正则性与一致法向谱稳定性条件下，分析逐点固定流形的光滑映射的局部收敛；研究满足p(1)=1的一致多项式加速，保持所选极限对法向初始扰动的一阶响应；在足够光滑性下给出二阶诱导收缩的曲率判据；并将框架推广至非精确、松弛与广义AP。

Result: FPMA给出局部R-线性收敛及诱导局部收缩的光滑极限映射；一致多项式加速在变换后的法向块一致谱稳定时保持一阶响应，并保持二次收缩展开与该曲率判据；作为特例，给出固定次数Chebyshev加速精确AP与二阶诱导收缩的新的局部R-线性收敛保证；当Friedrichs角θ_F∈(0,π/2)一致，加速在每次AP求值的局部收敛率上严格改进，大次数极限谱因子预测当θ_F↓0时达到固定相对误差约为O(θ_F^{-1})次求值，而普通AP为O(θ_F^{-2})。

Conclusion: FPMA为定点流形映射提供了统一的局部收敛与多项式加速理论，在Friedrichs角很小时显著改进AP的收敛复杂度，并可推广到非精确、松弛及广义AP情形。

Abstract: Alternating projections (AP) near a clean intersection of smooth manifolds can converge slowly when the Friedrichs angle is small. We develop the \emph{Fixed-Point Manifold Acceleration (FPMA) framework} for {local convergence and polynomial acceleration of} smooth maps that fix a manifold pointwise. {Under suitable regularity and uniform normal spectral stability, FPMA establishes local R-linear convergence and a smooth limiting map inducing a local retraction. At fixed points, consistent polynomial acceleration with $p(1)=1$ preserves the selected limit's first-order response to normal initial perturbations whenever the transformed normal blocks are uniformly spectrally stable. Under sufficient smoothness, we give a curvature criterion for second-order induced retractions and show that stable consistent polynomial acceleration preserves both the quadratic retraction expansion and this criterion. {As a special case, we establish new local R-linear convergence guarantees for fixed-degree Chebyshev-accelerated exact AP and second-order induced retractions. For uniform Friedrichs angle $θ_F\in(0,π/2)$, acceleration strictly improves the local convergence rate per AP evaluation. The large-degree limiting spectral factor at fixed $θ_F$ predicts $O(θ_F^{-1})$ evaluations for fixed relative error reduction as $θ_F\downarrow0$, versus $O(θ_F^{-2})$ for ordinary AP.}} {The framework also covers} inexact, relaxed, and generalized AP {under their respective regularity and stability conditions.}

</details>


### [160] [Minimax Call Complexity, Rounds and Admissible Noise in Zeroth-Order Optimization of Highly Smooth Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05517)
*Timofei Loginov,Darina Dvinskikh,Nazarii Tupitsa,Osman Osmanov,Yuriy Dorn,Alexander Gasnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带噪声的凸与强凸函数零阶优化（Hölder 光滑阶 β>2），给出函数调用次数的下界，并提出加速有限差分方法达到该下界，同时刻画了并行轮数与可容忍的偏差噪声水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化中函数值常受随机噪声、Lipschitz 系统误差及有界对抗误差污染，已有下界对维度 d 的依赖不够紧，且高阶光滑（β>2）情形下的最优调用复杂度、并行轮数与噪声容忍度尚不清楚，因此需要建立更精确的下界并设计达到该下界的方法。

Method: 作者采用信息论/构造硬实例的方式推导下界，针对 Hessian 有界的强凸函数证明调用次数下界为 d²ε^{-β/(β-1)}，并在更强 Hilbert–Schmidt 光滑下确定 d 的精确幂次；随后提出加速有限差分方法，结合 Nesterov 型加速，在 O(√κ log(1/ε)) 轮内达到上述下界，并分析其对系统误差与对抗误差的容忍性；还将结果推广到一般范数与约束，并在 ℓ₁ 球上分析镜像下降。

Result: 强凸情形下证明调用次数下界 d²ε^{-β/(β-1)}，Hilbert–Schmidt 光滑下得到 d 的精确幂次；加速有限差分法在 O(√κ log(1/ε)) 轮内达到该下界，容忍最优系统误差及至多对数因子的对抗误差，而速率最优方法需 Ω(log d) 轮；当 Hessian 至多线性增长、或对中心化噪声无 Hessian 上界时同样达到该调用数且仅需对数轮数；凸情形下新下界相对已知结果在 d 的多项式上改进，并在硬实例曲率处紧，系统误差水平最优；结果可推广到一般范数与约束，ℓ₁ 球上镜像下降调用数近线性于 d；实验验证理论。

Conclusion: 该工作为 Hölder 光滑零阶凸/强凸优化提供了紧的调用复杂度下界与达到下界的加速有限差分方法，明确了并行轮数、维度依赖与噪声容忍之间的最优权衡，并将结论扩展到一般范数、约束与 ℓ₁ 球情形。

Abstract: We study zeroth-order optimization of convex and strongly convex functions with Hölder smoothness of order $β>2$ from values corrupted by random noise with bias independent of the method's random directions, a Lipschitz systematic error and a bounded adversarial error. We measure the number of function evaluations (calls), of sequential rounds of parallel calls, and the largest tolerable systematic and adversarial errors. For strongly convex functions with a bounded Hessian, we prove a lower bound of order $d^2\varepsilon^{-β/(β-1)}$ on the number of calls, where $d$ is the dimension and $\varepsilon$ the accuracy, and find the sharp power of $d$ under the stronger Hilbert--Schmidt smoothness for all $β>2$. An accelerated finite-difference method attains them in $O(\sqrtκ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ rounds for condition number $κ$, like Nesterov's method with exact gradients, and tolerates the optimal systematic and, up to logarithms, adversarial errors; rate-optimal methods need $Ω(\log d)$ rounds. The same number of calls is attained when the Hessian grows at most linearly with the gradient and, for centered noise, without an upper bound on the Hessian, also in logarithmically many rounds. For convex functions, our lower bound improves the known one polynomially in $d$ and is tight at the curvature of the hard instances; the systematic level is again optimal. These bounds extend to general norms and constraints; on the $\ell_1$ ball, mirror descent needs calls nearly linear in $d$. Experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [161] [A Relaxed Maximum-Based Normal \texorpdfstring{$S$}{S}-Iteration Method for Generalized Absolute Value Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06154)
*Abhishek Kumar Singh Sengar,Bharat Kumar,Deepmala*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出松弛极大值型正态S迭代法（RMNSI）求解广义绝对值方程，建立全局与局部收敛理论，并通过数值实验验证其高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义绝对值方程（GAVE）的求解通常需要选取辅助矩阵或构造复杂的不动点格式，现有方法在收敛条件和参数选取上存在局限，亟需一种无需辅助矩阵、迭代结构简单且理论保证充分的迭代方法。

Method: 将极大值型不动点格式与常数松弛参数结合，提出RMNSI方法；在A+B非奇异假设下，两阶段迭代共用同一系数矩阵A+B，可复用一次分解；通过建立单一谱条件保证GAVE的唯一可解性与任意初值下的全局收敛，刻画松弛参数的容许范围，并推导R-线性收敛及误差估计；进一步基于解符号模式给出局部收敛分析。

Result: 理论上得到保证唯一可解与全局收敛的谱条件、松弛参数容许区间以及R-线性收敛速率与误差界；数值实验在互补问题导出、稠密混合符号与非对称岭回归问题上的结果优于若干近期方法，且表明优选松弛参数主要取决于矩阵结构与对角位移，对问题维度的依赖较弱。

Conclusion: RMNSI避免了辅助矩阵选取，单次分解即可完成迭代，兼具理论完备性与计算效率，是求解GAVE的一种有竞争力的方法。

Abstract: A relaxed maximum-based normal $S$-iteration method (RMNSI) is proposed for solving generalized absolute value equations (GAVEs). The method combines a maximum-based fixed-point formulation with a constant relaxation parameter and avoids the selection of an auxiliary matrix. Assuming that $A+B$ is non-singular, both stages require linear systems with the same coefficient matrix $A+B$, allowing a single factorization to be reused throughout the iteration. A single spectral condition is established that guarantees unique solvability of the GAVE and global convergence from an arbitrary initial vector. An admissible range of the relaxation parameter is characterized, and $R$-linear convergence and error estimates are derived. Since the global condition can be conservative, a local convergence analysis based on the solution sign pattern is also presented. Numerical experiments on complementarity-derived, dense mixed-sign, and asymmetric ridge-regression problems are presented, and comparisons with several recent methods demonstrate the competitive efficiency and accuracy of RMNSI. The results further indicate that the preferred relaxation parameter depends mainly on the matrix structure and diagonal shift, while its dependence on the problem dimension is generally weak.

</details>


### [162] [More on Disjunctive Convex Hulls of Generalized Cross Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05560)
*Yushan Qu,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了当 n+1 个多胞体均为广义交叉多胞体（generalized cross polytopes）时，其析取（由 n 个二元指示变量管理）的凸包 D⊂R^{d+n} 的垂直面（vertical facets）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对 D 的刻画仅覆盖 d∈{1,2} 的任意多胞体、共享约束矩阵的多胞体（含超矩形）以及两个广义交叉多胞体（n=1）的情形；对任意 n 和 d 的一般刻画尚属空白，尤其是非成对（non-pairwise）不等式是否存在及其作用尚不清楚。

Method: 将每个垂直面用归一化支撑不等式（normalized support inequality）描述；以“相等图”（多胞体与坐标构成的二分图）是否连通作为该不等式是否为面描述（facet describing）的充要条件；由可行生成树（feasible spanning trees）显式生成全部垂直面，并建立垂直面与 Δ_n×Δ_{d-1} 正则剖分（regular subdivision）极大胞之间的双射。

Result: 给出全部垂直面的完整描述及其显式生成方法；刻画某垂直面属于已知成对不等式（pairwise inequalities）的条件，证明 d≤3 时成对不等式足够，但一般情况下不成立；导出 D 的面数上确界 n+1+2^d·C(n+d-1, n)，且在一般数据下可达；提出多项式时间的分离算法（separation algorithm），返回一个被违反的面，且该面在所有归一化支撑不等式中被最大程度违反；计算实验表明非成对不等式的面弥补了成对不等式留下的大部分间隙。

Conclusion: 该工作将 D 的刻画从低维或特殊结构推广到任意 n、d 下的广义交叉多胞体族，明确了垂直面的组合结构（连通相等图、生成树、正则剖分双射），给出面数的紧界与可达性，并通过分离算法与实验证明非成对不等式在收紧松弛（relaxation）中的显著作用。

Abstract: We continue the study of the convex hull $D\subset\mathbb{R}^{d+n}$ of a disjunction of $n+1$ polytopes in $\mathbb{R}^d$, managed by $n$ binary indicator variables. Previously, $D$ had been characterized for arbitrary polytopes when $d\in\{1,2\}$, for polytopes sharing a common constraint matrix (under a technical condition, including hyper-rectangles), and for two generalized cross polytopes ($n=1$). Here we give a complete description of $D$ for arbitrary $n$ and $d$ when all $n+1$ polytopes are generalized cross polytopes. Every vertical facet is described by a normalized support inequality, and such an inequality is facet describing if and only if its equality graph, a bipartite graph on the polytopes and the coordinates, is connected; all vertical facets are generated explicitly from feasible spanning trees. We characterize when such a facet is one of the previously known pairwise inequalities, and we show that pairwise inequalities suffice when $d\leq3$, but not in general. We establish a bijection between the vertical facets and the maximal cells of a regular subdivision of $Δ_n\timesΔ_{d-1}$\,, which yields the sharp bound $n+1+2^d\binom{n+d-1}{n}$ on the number of facets of $D$, attained for generic data. We also give a polynomial-time separation algorithm that returns a violated facet, which is maximally violated among the normalized support inequalities. Computational experiments show that the facets that are not pairwise inequalities close a large part of the gap left by the pairwise inequalities.

</details>


### [163] [Target-Generated Dirichlet Problems in State-Constrained Stochastic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05687)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Haichuan Ding,Ibrahim Ekren*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究标量盈余 $R\ge0$ 的状态约束随机控制问题，证明内部值函数在边界退化时的极限满足一维边界HJB方程，并在特定假设下识别其唯一极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 状态约束随机控制中，当控制使 $R$ 的漂移与波动率在零点退化时，会产生降维的边界HJB方程；需要刻画内部值在边界的极限并给出其满足的方程。

Method: 从控制逐点的状态约束粘性不等式出发；对盈余正漂移与生成元增长施加单侧界，并假设被边界生成元支配；证明内部值的上、下极限分别为边界方程的子解与超解；结合终端相容性与比较原理识别共同极限；对光滑随机目标值 $w$ 使用变换 $R=Y-w(t,X)$ 拉平可行上图。

Result: 内部值函数的上下极限分别构成边界方程的子解与超解；在终端相容与比较原理下两者极限一致；在紧或强制性控制情形，单侧界可由局部下界与边界控制集连续性推出；光滑随机目标值情形下边界控制值提供Dirichlet数据。

Conclusion: 建立了状态约束随机控制中边界退化HJB方程的粘性解框架及极限识别方法；应用涵盖集值边界控制、带二次成本的无界漂移以及具有非常数目标边界的冗余对冲工具。

Abstract: We study state-constrained stochastic control problems with a scalar surplus $R \geq 0$. Controls for which the drift and volatility of $R$ vanish at zero define a lower-dimensional Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation. Starting from control-wise state-constraint viscosity inequalities, we prove that the upper and lower limits of the interior value are a subsolution and a supersolution of this boundary equation. Terminal compatibility and comparison identify their common limit. We require one-sided bounds on positive surplus drift and generator growth, together with domination by boundary generators. For compact or coercive controls, these follow from a local lower bound and continuity of the boundary control set. For a smooth stochastic target value $w$, the transformation $R = Y - w(t,X)$ flattens the viable epigraph, and, under the stated hypotheses, the boundary control value supplies the Dirichlet datum. Applications include set-valued boundary controls, unbounded drift with quadratic costs, and redundant hedging instruments with a nonconstant target boundary.

</details>


### [164] [Observability from Measurable Sets for One-Dimensional Heat Equations with Bounded Spacetime Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05704)
*Xiaomin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带有时空变系数位势的 Dirichlet 热方程在可测时空集合上的可观测性，证明可观测性不等式与时空正测度等价，并给出常数一致性及对观测集切片位置的均匀估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热方程的可观测性/零可控性通常要求观测集满足几何条件（如时间切片有正测度或有控厚条件），本文旨在将条件弱化到仅需时空集合具有正测度，并研究可观测常数对位势与观测集位置的依赖。

Method: 结合有限维空间上的观测估计（由演化方程传输）与到这些空间的距离的衰减估计；通过构造有限维逼近并利用对偶论证处理时空变位势。

Result: 证明从任意正测度可测时空子集出发的可观测性不等式，常数对具有给定 L∞ 界的位势一致；由对偶得正时空测度等价于平方可积分布控制的零可控性，控制可在时间上有界且取值于 L2；若观测集的几乎所有空间切片测度有固定正下界，则观测常数上界为 C0 e^{C1/T}，且对切片位置一致。

Conclusion: 时空正测度足以保证热方程的可观测性与零可控性，且可观测常数在指定 L∞ 位势类中一致、对观测集切片位置具有均匀性，推广了以往依赖几何条件的结论。

Abstract: This paper study observability for the Dirichlet heat equation on a bounded interval with a real bounded potential depending on space and time. We prove an observability inequality from every measurable subset of spacetime with positive measure. The constant is uniform over potentials with a prescribed $L^\infty$ bound. By duality, positive spacetime measure is equivalent to null controllability by square-integrable distributed controls. Controls can also be chosen bounded in time with values in $L^2$. If almost every spatial slice of the observation set has a fixed positive lower bound on its measure, the observability constant is bounded above by $C_0e^{C_1/T}$, uniformly in the locations of the slices. The proof combines observation estimates on finite-dimensional spaces transported by the evolution with decay estimates for the distance to those spaces.

</details>


### [165] [Data-driven control of linear systems using quantized data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05724)
*Zhenghao Li,Guosong Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究仅用量化状态测量对未知离散时间线性系统进行数据驱动镇定，提出两阶段方法：设计阶段用满足量化误差界的数据构造SDP并给出可验证的镇定条件；镇定阶段用基于Lyapunov的量化器更新规则保证指数收敛，且量化单元数有限并保持恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知离散时间线性系统的数据驱动镇定通常依赖精确状态测量，而实际中测量往往被量化且取值有限。如何在仅有量化状态数据、量化单元数有限的条件下实现可保证镇定的控制器设计，是关键问题。

Method: 方法分两阶段。控制器设计阶段：在给定量化误差界下收集量化数据，构造半定规划（SDP），建立可验证条件，使得任意SDP可行解都能给出镇定反馈增益，并证明量化误差足够小时总能获得镇定增益。镇定阶段：设计基于Lyapunov的量化器更新规则，实现量化状态反馈下的指数收敛；两阶段量化单元数均有限，且镇定过程中保持恒定。

Result: 给出了可验证的镇定条件：SDP的任意可行解对应镇定反馈增益；证明量化误差足够小时存在镇定增益。所提Lyapunov量化器更新规则保证指数收敛，量化单元数有限且在镇定阶段恒定。仿真结果验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为仅有量化状态测量下的未知离散时间线性系统提供了具有理论保证的数据驱动镇定框架，兼顾可验证性、指数收敛性与有限且恒定的量化复杂度。

Abstract: This paper studies data-driven stabilization of unknown discrete-time linear systems using only quantized state measurements that take values in finite sets. The proposed approach consists of two stages. In the controller design stage, we collect quantized data while maintaining a prescribed quantization error bound and use them to formulate a semidefinite program (SDP). We establish a verifiable condition under which any feasible solution to the SDP yields a stabilizing feedback gain, and show that a stabilizing gain can always be obtained when the quantization error is sufficiently small. In the stabilization stage, a Lyapunov-based quantizer update rule is developed to guarantee exponential convergence under quantized state feedback. As a key feature, the number of quantization cells remains finite in both stages and constant during stabilization. Simulation results illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [166] [An Inexact Halpern-accelerated Preconditioned Generalized Proximal Point Algorithm for the Maximal Monotone Inclusion Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05742)
*Lei Yang,Haihang Lan,Di Hou,Ling Liang,Kim-Chuan Toh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种带可容许半正定预条件子的非精确Halpern加速广义邻近点算法，用于求解极大单调包含问题，统一覆盖ρ∈(0,2]的欠松弛与过松弛情形，并给出显式收敛率及非精确pADMM/PDHG的加速版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极大单调包含问题在优化中具有基础地位，邻近点算法及其预条件、加速、非精确变形广受关注。然而，将Halpern锚定、resolvent非精确性与全权重范围松弛统一在同一框架下，并给出显式收敛率界限，尚缺乏系统研究；同时端点ρ=2与内部情形的非精确行为差异也需刻画。

Method: 构建带半正定预条件子M的非精确Halpern加速广义邻近点算法，融合Halpern锚定、resolvent非精确性与ρ∈(0,2]的松弛；在一般锚定参数与特定非可和容差条件下证明非精确resolvent序列收敛；对β_k=1/(k+r)（r≥2）推导M-半模下平方不动点残差的显式上界；并据此发展非精确加速的pADMM与PDHG，基于子问题残差给出非精确准则。

Result: 对β_k=1/(k+r)，r≥2，当容差ε_k=O((k+1)^{-α})，且0<ρ<2时α>3/2、ρ=2时α>2，可获得O(1/k^2)收敛率；ρ=2处需更强衰减条件，揭示端点与内部机制在非精确设定下的定性差异；所构造的非精确加速pADMM与PDHG的迭代序列具有非遍历O(1/k)的KKT残差率。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了预条件、Halpern加速、非精确resolvent与全范围松弛，给出明确的收敛率界限，并成功应用于非精确加速pADMM与PDHG，表明端点ρ=2在非精确情形下需要更严格容差条件，具有定性与定量双重意义。

Abstract: This paper studies an inexact Halpern-accelerated generalized proximal point algorithm with an admissible positive semidefinite preconditioner $\mathcal{M}$ for solving maximal monotone inclusion problems. The proposed framework combines Halpern anchoring, resolvent inexactness, and relaxation over the full weight range $ρ\in(0,2]$, thereby covering both under-relaxed and over-relaxed proximal iterations within a unified scheme. We first establish convergence of the inexact resolvent sequence under conditions that allow general anchoring parameters and certain nonsummable tolerances. For anchoring parameters $β_{k}=1/(k+r)$ with $r\geq2$, we then derive explicit bounds on the squared fixed-point residual in the $\mathcal{M}$-seminorm. In particular, if the tolerances satisfy $\varepsilon_{k}=\mathcal{O}((k+1)^{-α})$ with $α>3/2$ for $0<ρ<2$ and $α>2$ for $ρ=2$, these bounds yield an $\mathcal{O}(1/k^{2})$ convergence rate. The stronger decay condition on the inexactness tolerances at $ρ=2$ highlights a qualitative distinction between the endpoint and the interior regime in the inexact setting. Finally, we develop inexact accelerated versions of the preconditioned alternating direction method of multipliers (pADMM) and the preconditioned primal--dual hybrid gradient (PDHG) method based on this framework, derive inexactness criteria based on subproblem residuals, and establish a nonergodic $\mathcal{O}(1/k)$ KKT residual rate for their inexact iterates.

</details>


### [167] [Learning to Solve Mixed-Integer Nonlinear Programming with Neural Networks and Sequential Quadratic Programming Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05746)
*Viet-Anh Le,Rahul Mangharam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种学习优化框架，用神经网络预测整数解与连续热启动，再用可微SQP层精化连续变量，用于求解MPC与轨迹优化中的参数化MINLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化混合整数非线性规划在模型预测控制与轨迹优化中广泛应用，但求解困难；纯神经预测对不完美预测敏感，纯优化求解计算开销大且不可预测。

Method: 先以监督与自监督组合损失训练神经网络，预测整数解及兼容的连续热启动，并生成多个候选整数解及其连续初始化；固定整数解后，用可微SQP层在预定迭代次数内精化连续变量，同时训练SQP层参数（曲率正则化与步长）以逐次改善可行性与目标值。

Result: 在平面推箱（planar box-pushing）示例的接触与轨迹优化任务上验证了所提框架。

Conclusion: 将整数预测与基于优化的连续精化解耦，可提升对神经网络预测误差的鲁棒性，并保持可预测的计算预算。

Abstract: We propose a learning-to-optimize framework for solving parametric mixed-integer nonlinear programs arising in model predictive control and trajectory optimization. The proposed method combines neural network prediction with a sequential quadratic programming (SQP) refinement layer. First, neural networks are trained using a combination of supervised and self-supervised losses to predict integer solutions together with compatible continuous warm starts. To account for prediction inaccuracies, the trained predictor generates multiple candidate integer solutions, each paired with a continuous initialization. The integer solutions are then fixed, and a differentiable SQP layer refines the associated continuous variables over a prescribed number of iterations. The SQP-layer parameters, including the curvature regularization and step size, are trained to improve both feasibility and objective value after every iteration. By separating integer prediction from continuous optimization-based refinement, the proposed framework improves robustness to imperfect neural predictions while maintaining a predictable computational budget. We demonstrate the proposed framework on contact and trajectory optimization for a planar box-pushing example.

</details>


### [168] [Dimension-Free Decentralized Nonsmooth Nonconvex Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05789)
*Yuanyu Wan,Lan Xue,Haomin Bai,Tong Wei,Mingli Song*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究去中心化非光滑非凸随机优化,提出新算法将样本复杂度降至 $O(\delta^{-1}\epsilon^{-3})$、通信复杂度降至 $\widetilde{O}(\gamma^{-1/2}\delta^{-1}\epsilon^{-3})$,消除了已有算法对维度 $d$ 的多项式依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化网络上寻找 $(\delta,\epsilon)$-Goldstein 稳定点的现有最优算法,其复杂度对问题维度 $d$ 存在多项式依赖,在高维场景下成为主要瓶颈。作者希望去除该维度依赖,同时保持甚至改善样本与通信复杂度。

Method: 提出一种新颖的去中心化在线到非凸转换(decentralized online-to-nonconvex conversion),将原非光滑非凸随机优化问题归约为去中心化在线凸优化(D-OCO)问题。该转换的关键性质是:其一致性(consensus)需求可直接继承自底层 D-OCO 决策的一致性,从而建立维度依赖与一致性误差之间的显式联系,进而仅用对数级额外通信即可去除对 $d$ 的多项式依赖。

Result: 新算法达到 $O(\delta^{-1}\epsilon^{-3})$ 的样本复杂度与 $\widetilde{O}(\gamma^{-1/2}\delta^{-1}\epsilon^{-3})$ 的通信复杂度,相较已有最优算法的 $O(\delta^{-1}(\epsilon^{-3}+d\epsilon^{-1}))$ 样本复杂度和 $\widetilde{O}(\gamma^{-1/2}\delta^{-1}(\epsilon^{-3}+d\epsilon^{-1}))$ 通信复杂度,成功消除了多项式 $d$ 依赖。

Conclusion: 通过去中心化在线到非凸转换,并利用一致性需求可继承的性质,论文证明了维度依赖可由一致性误差控制,从而在仅增加对数级通信的情况下去除对 $d$ 的多项式依赖,显著提升了高维去中心化非凸优化的复杂度表现。

Abstract: We investigate decentralized nonsmooth nonconvex stochastic optimization over a network of $n$ nodes, with the goal of finding an $(δ,ε)$-Goldstein stationary point. The best existing algorithm achieves $O(δ^{-1}(ε^{-3}+dε^{-1}))$ sample complexity and $\widetilde{O}(γ^{-1/2}δ^{-1}(ε^{-3}+dε^{-1}))$ communication complexity, where $d$ is the problem dimension and $γ$ is the spectral gap of the communication matrix. However, the polynomial dependence on $d$ can be a major bottleneck in high-dimensional regimes. In this paper, we propose a novel algorithm that achieves $O(δ^{-1}ε^{-3})$ sample complexity and $\widetilde{O}(γ^{-1/2}δ^{-1}ε^{-3})$ communication complexity. The primary technique is an elegant decentralized online-to-nonconvex conversion that reduces the original problem to a decentralized online convex optimization (D-OCO) problem. A key property of our conversion is that its consensus requirements can be inherited directly from the consensus of the underlying D-OCO decisions. In particular, this property enables us to establish an explicit connection between the dimension dependence and the consensus error, which in turn shows that the polynomial dependence on $d$ can be removed with only logarithmic additional communication.

</details>


### [169] [Partial-State Deadzone-Adapted Disturbance Suppression Control For Strict-Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05797)
*Iasson Karafyllis,Alexandros Aslanidis,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将部分状态的Deadzone-Adapted Disturbance Suppression (DADS)自适应控制方案扩展至严格反馈系统，涵盖含积分器与不含积分器两种情形，并进一步推广至无穷维系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DADS自适应控制方案此前仅应用于部分状态场景（如单输入单输出反馈线性化系统），尚未处理严格反馈结构；而严格反馈系统是控制领域中一类重要且广泛存在的非线性系统形式，因此需要将其扩展以覆盖该类系统。

Method: 在严格反馈系统中扩展部分状态的DADS自适应控制方案，分别研究含积分器与不含积分器两种情形。控制器通过自适应律将非线性控制增益"泵升"至足够大的值以适配未知不确定性，并在输出进入残差集后停止继续泵升增益。随后将结果直接推广至无穷维情形，并提供有限维与无穷维两个示例。

Result: DADS控制器能够在存在时变扰动、未知时变参数和未知时变输入系数（其界任意且未知）的情况下，将输出衰减至可指定的较小水平。有限维示例给出了与现有文献结果的详细对比，无穷维示例验证了推广的有效性。

Conclusion: DADS方案的核心机制在严格反馈系统中同样适用，且可自然推广至无穷维情形，表明该方案具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper we provide an extension of the partial-state Deadzone-Adapted Disturbance Suppression (DADS) adaptive control scheme in the case of strict-feedback systems. We study two cases: the cases of strict-feedback systems with and without integrators. The DADS controller achieves attenuation of the output to an assignable small level, despite the presence of (time-varying) disturbances, unknown (time-varying) parameters and unknown (time-varying) input coefficients; all of them with arbitrary and unknown bounds. The mechanism that makes DADS work is the same in all previously studied cases where DADS has been applied: the update law "pumps up" the nonlinear controller gain to a sufficient size, adapting the gain to the unknown uncertainties, and shuts off further pumping up of the gain once the output is in the residual set. The results are directly extended to the infinite-dimensional case. Two simple examples are provided: one finite-dimensional example and one infinite-dimensional example. The finite-dimensional example is used for a detailed comparison with existing results in the literature.

</details>


### [170] [Decision-Tree Surrogates for Weak Counterfactual Explanations in Integer Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05887)
*Marc Goerigk,Jannis Kurtz,Sebastian Merten*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文针对反事实解释（CE）计算中双层优化问题的高复杂度（整数情形为Σ₂^p完全），提出一种基于决策树近似可行域的快速启发式算法，并在背包与设施选址问题上验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实解释对数学优化问题日益重要，但求解弱CE需解计算量巨大的双层优化问题；当决策变量与可变参数限制为整数时该问题为Σ₂^p完全，已知精确方法在实用规模上难以求解，因此亟需快速近似方法。

Method: 通过用决策树近似CE问题的可行域来设计快速启发式算法，使CE可在该决策树上快速计算。为此分析可行域的数学结构，并据此手工构造应用相应分裂规则的决策树；此外开发若干提升算法性能的技术。

Result: 在背包问题与设施选址问题上的计算实验验证了所提方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过决策树近似CE可行域的启发式方法能够绕开精确双层求解的复杂度瓶颈，在实用问题规模上有效计算弱反事实解释，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: The concept of counterfactual explanations (CEs) has recently gained increasing attention for deriving explanations of mathematical optimization problems. For a given problem, a counterfactual is an ideally minimal change in the mutable problem parameters such that an optimal solution of the changed problem exists fulfilling some desired criterion. It was shown that so-called weak CEs can be calculated by solving a computationally demanding bilevel optimization problem, which is $Σ_2^p$-complete if decision variables and mutable parameters are restricted to integers. Hence, the known exact solution methods are expected to fail on practically relevant problem sizes. In this work we derive a fast heuristic algorithm by approximating the feasible region of the CE problem with a decision tree for which CEs can be calculated more quickly. To this end we analyze the mathematical structure of the feasible region and develop a hand-crafted decision tree which applies splits imitating this structure. Additionally, we develop several techniques to increase the performance of our method. The practicability of our methods is supported by computational experiments on the knapsack problem and the facility location problem.

</details>


### [171] [Structural Foundations of Nonlinear Systems with Unknown Inputs: The UID-Induced Normal Form and Minimal-Sensing Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05939)
*Agostino Martinelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次提出非线性未知输入系统状态估计的一般结构解，通过UID诱导标准型统一实现未知输入解耦与重构，并用极简SfM配置验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统在未知输入驱动下的状态估计长期缺乏一般性的结构解，且已有方法往往需要模型或随机假设；作者希望建立一个不依赖此类假设的统一框架。

Method: 基于非线性未知输入可观测性理论，证明任意此类系统均存在结构等价的UID诱导标准型表示；该表示将未知输入信息分解为与可观测动态结构解耦的未知输入方向，以及完全表征影响可观测动态的未知输入信息的可观测部分。

Result: UID诱导标准型为未知输入解耦与未知输入重构提供了统一的结构解，无需对未知输入作任何模型或随机假设；在一个此前未被探索的极简Structure-from-Motion配置中，仅用三个点特征和单轴陀螺仪即可递归估计状态，恢复三维结构与相机运动（至多相差未知全局尺度因子），真实数据实验验证了该框架及该极简感知配置的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了未知输入非线性系统状态估计的首个一般结构解，统一了解耦与重构问题，并展示了其在实际视觉估计任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: This paper establishes the first general structural solution to the problem of state estimation for nonlinear systems driven by unknown inputs. Building upon nonlinear unknown-input observability theory, we show that every such system admits a structurally equivalent representation, referred to as the UID-induced normal form. The proposed representation decomposes the information carried by the unknown inputs into two complementary components: unknown-input directions that are structurally decoupled from the observable dynamics and observable quantities that completely represent the unknown-input information affecting the observable dynamics. As a consequence, the UID-induced normal form provides a unified structural solution to unknown-input decoupling and unknown-input reconstruction, without requiring any model or stochastic assumption on the unknown inputs. The practical significance of the proposed framework is demonstrated through a previously unexplored minimal Structure-from-Motion configuration. The proposed representation enables recursive state estimation from only three point features and a single-axis gyroscope, allowing the recovery of the three-dimensional structure and camera motion up to an unknown global scale factor. Experiments on real-world data validate the proposed framework and demonstrate the feasibility of this minimal sensing configuration.

</details>


### [172] [Last-Iterate Convergence Rate of Normalized Gradient Descent under Hölder Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06070)
*Yuki Takezawa,Eduard Gorbunov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究归一化梯度下降（NGD）在凸、$(ν,M_ν)$-Hölder光滑目标下的最后迭代收敛性，证明常步长有$\mathcal{O}((\log^2 T/T)^{(1+ν)/2})$上界，线性衰减步长可达$\mathcal{O}(T^{-(1+ν)/2})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NGD分析多关注最优迭代或迭代加权平均，而实际实现通常返回最后迭代，二者之间存在理论空白；且已有最优/平均迭代的$\mathcal{O}(T^{-(1+ν)/2})$保证，需明确最后迭代是否可达到同阶。

Method: 针对凸、$(ν,M_ν)$-Hölder光滑目标，分析常步长NGD的最后迭代收敛，得到含对数因子的上界；并用性能估计问题（PEP）进行有限时域数值实验；进而分析线性衰减步长的最后迭代收敛。

Result: 常步长下最后迭代上界为$\mathcal{O}((\log^2(T)/T)^{(1+ν)/2})$，比最优/加权平均的$\mathcal{O}(T^{-(1+ν)/2})$多对数开销，且$ν=0$时该开销不可避免；PEP实验考察光滑情形有限时域最坏情况并探究对数开销是否本质；线性衰减步长给出$\mathcal{O}(T^{-(1+ν)/2})$的最后迭代保证。
