Conclusion: NGD的最后迭代在常步长下存在对数开销，而线性衰减步长可匹配最优/加权平均阶且无需知道$ν$与$M_ν$。

Abstract: Normalized gradient descent is a widely studied adaptive optimization method. Most existing analyses focus on the best iterate or a weighted average of the iterates, whereas practical implementations typically return the last iterate. In this paper, we study the last-iterate convergence of normalized gradient descent for convex, $(ν,M_ν)$-Hölder-smooth objectives. For a constant stepsize, we establish an upper bound of $\mathcal{O}\bigl((\log^2(T)/T)^{(1+ν)/2}\bigr)$, which contains a logarithmic overhead relative to the known $\mathcal{O}\bigl(T^{-(1+ν)/2}\bigr)$ guarantees for the best and weighted-average iterates. For $ν= 0$, this overhead is known to be unavoidable. We complement this analysis with numerical results based on the performance estimation problem (PEP), investigating the finite-horizon worst-case behavior in the smooth setting and whether the logarithmic overhead reflects an intrinsic limitation of constant-step normalized gradient descent. We then show that a linearly decreasing stepsize yields a last-iterate guarantee of $\mathcal{O}\bigl(T^{-(1+ν)/2}\bigr)$, matching the order of the best-iterate/weighted-average guarantees without requiring knowledge of $ν$ and $M_ν$.

</details>


### [173] [Descent--Ascent Structure of Uniform Nondegeneracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06115)
*Hua Su,Lei Zhang,Jin Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"一致非退化"概念，用一族下降-上升不等式（DAI）刻画曲率零分离与方向可变，并据此构造交替下降上升法（ADA），实现全局线性收敛且复杂度与维数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强凸极小化与强凸-强凹优化混淆了两种曲率信息：曲率与零的一致分离和曲率正负方向的预先指定。作者希望解耦这两者，保留与零的分离性，同时允许曲率方向随域变化，以覆盖更广泛的优化情形。

Method: 提出"一致非退化"（uniform nondegeneracy）概念，并识别出一族下降-上升不等式（Descent-Ascent Inequalities, DAI）来刻画该性质。基于DAI自然导出交替下降上升法（Alternating Descent Ascent, ADA）——一种交替执行全梯度下降与上升的显式简单方法。

Result: ADA从任意初始化出发均可全局且线性收敛到唯一驻点，且具有与维数无关的一阶复杂度（dimension-free first-order complexity）。该框架以极限形式恢复光滑强凸情形，并涵盖强凸-强凹及线性约束情形。

Conclusion: 一致非退化揭示了多种经典优化范式中共同存在的一阶结构，将强凸、强凸-强凹及线性约束统一到同一框架下，并提供了简单高效的求解算法ADA。

Abstract: Classical models such as smooth strongly convex minimization and strongly convex--strongly concave optimization combine two types of curvature information: curvature remains uniformly separated from zero, and its positive and negative orientations are prescribed in advance. Uniform nondegeneracy disentangles these roles, retaining the separation from zero while allowing the curvature orientation to vary across the domain. We identify a family of descent--ascent inequalities (DAI) that characterizes uniform nondegeneracy and naturally gives rise to Alternating Descent Ascent (ADA), a simple explicit method that alternates full-gradient descent and ascent. ADA converges globally and linearly to the unique stationary point from arbitrary initialization, with dimension-free first-order complexity. The framework recovers the smooth strongly convex setting as a limit and encompasses the strongly convex--strongly concave and linearly constrained settings, highlighting uniform nondegeneracy as a common first-order structure across various classical regimes.

</details>


### [174] [Model-Guided Design of Nanoparticle Dispersity through Process-Chain Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06155)
*Andrea Gilch,Nabi E. Traoré,Frauke Liers,Lukas Pflug*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个耦合纳米颗粒合成与色谱分离过程的集成数学优化模型，兼顾过程性能与经济目标，采用精确矩量法评估合成模型，并通过局部优化求解非凸设计问题，以金纳米颗粒为例验证了模型的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量纳米颗粒需要窄粒径分布，而粒径分布强烈影响最终产品功能。因此，将纳米颗粒合成与后续色谱分离视为耦合工艺链来协同设计，有望提升颗粒质量并优化整体过程性能与经济性，但此类集成优化问题此前缺乏系统的数学建模方法。

Method: 构建了针对耦合纳米颗粒合成与分离过程的集成数学优化模型。模型通过成本函数同时纳入生产成本与由颗粒质量决定的可实现利润，兼顾过程性能与经济目标。为高效评估合成模型，采用精确矩量法（method of moments）；所得非凸过程设计问题通过局部优化方法进行数值求解，并以金纳米颗粒的原型设计为例开展验证。

Result: 针对金纳米颗粒原型设计的计算结果表明，耦合合成—分离过程的优化解能够准确表征两个工艺阶段，同时实现优化的颗粒质量，验证了模型在集成设计中的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的集成优化模型展示了数学优化在耦合纳米颗粒生产工艺链设计中的潜力，为同时优化过程性能与经济目标、提升颗粒质量提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: The design of high-quality nanoparticles requires the production of materials with narrow particle size distributions, as these strongly influence the functionalities of the final product. To achieve this goal, it is useful to consider a coupled process chain in which nanoparticles are first synthesized and subsequently fractionated by chromatographic separation. We introduce a novel mathematical optimization model for the integrated optimization of coupled nanoparticle synthesis and separation processes. The proposed model simultaneously accounts for process performance and economic objectives through a cost function that incorporates both production costs and achievable profit defined by the particle quality. For efficient evaluation of the synthesis model, we employ the exact method of moments. The resulting non-convex process-design problem is solved numerically using local optimization. Optimization results of our approach are demonstrated for the prototypical design of gold nanoparticles. The computational results show that optimized solutions of the coupled synthesis--separation process are capable of accurately representing both process stages while achieving optimized particle quality. The proposed model highlights the potential of mathematical optimization for the design of coupled nanoparticle production process-chains.

</details>


### [175] [Note on the Exponential Stability of the Viscoelastic Wave Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06281)
*Ludovick Gagnon*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究带局部历史记忆项、无摩擦阻尼的粘弹性波动方程，在记忆区域满足几何控制条件下，利用Gearhart-Prüss-Huang定理证明粘弹性能量的指数衰减，并在高频区域证明导数损失可由频率衰减补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘弹性波动方程通常依赖摩擦阻尼或全局记忆项来实现能量衰减；本文关注更具挑战性的情形：仅有局部历史记忆项且完全没有摩擦阻尼，此时能量是否仍能指数衰减、以及局部化记忆带来的正则性损失如何影响衰减，是需要解决的问题。

Method: 研究对象为带局部历史记忆项的粘弹性波动方程（无摩擦阻尼）。在记忆区域上施加几何控制条件，将问题转化为半群框架下的稳定性分析，运用Gearhart-Prüss-Huang定理判定指数稳定性；并针对高频部分进行频域分析，处理导数损失与频率衰减之间的权衡关系。

Result: 在记忆区域满足几何控制条件的前提下，证明了粘弹性能量的指数衰减；同时在高频情形下证明了导数损失可被频率衰减所补偿。

Conclusion: 局部历史记忆项在几何控制条件下足以替代摩擦阻尼使粘弹性能量指数衰减，且局部化记忆引起的正则性缺陷在高频下由频率衰减补偿，从而保证了稳定性结论的成立。

Abstract: In this article, we study the viscoelastic wave equation with a localized history memory term, and without frictional damping. Under the geometric control condition on the memory region, we prove exponential decay of the viscoelastic energy using the Gearhart-Pr{ü}ss-Huang Theorem. We prove in high frequency that a loss of derivative is compensated by a decay in frequency.

</details>


### [176] [Learning controls in mean field games: the fictitious play and related gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06292)
*Charles Meynard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究两类学习过程与势能平均场博弈（MFG）均衡形成的关系：一是基于控制量更新的虚拟博弈，二是仅依赖局部梯度信息的主代理梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同学习规则下势能平均场博弈均衡的形成机制，并拓展其在含公共噪声情形下的收敛性保证。

Method: 第一类方法为一种虚拟博弈变体：玩家直接观测其他玩家的动作（控制量），并通过控制量上的更新规则学习MFG的分布；第二类方法考虑一个主代理在仅有局部梯度信息下求解平均场控制问题，采用梯度下降。

Result: 所提出的控制量型迭代过程在一大类势能MFG中收敛，且在存在公共噪声时仍成立；在很一般的假设下，主代理的梯度下降收敛到相应势能MFG的解。

Conclusion: 两类学习过程均与势能MFG均衡存在紧密联系，分别从玩家分布式学习与单主体梯度优化角度给出了收敛到均衡的途径。

Abstract: We investigate two learning procedures and their relationship to the formation of equilibrium in potential mean field games (MFGs). The first one is a version of the fictitious play in which players observe directly the actions (controls) of other players and learn the distribution of the MFG through an update rules on controls. This leads to a new iterative procedure which converges for a wide class of potential MFGs of controls, even in the presence of common noise. On the other hand we also consider the learning procedure of a principal agent trying to solve a mean field control problem with only local gradient information. We prove that under very general assumption, the gradient descent of such agent converges to a solution of the associated potential MFG.

</details>


### [177] [CorleoneGame: A library of biological dynamic games with a companion Julia solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06307)
*Sebastian Sager,Christoph Plate,Julius Martensen,Frank W. Ohl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含25个生物动态博弈的库，覆盖分子、细胞、个体和种群尺度，以及竞争、对抗、互利和调控等交互类型，每个博弈均配有可执行的Julia规范与参考开环广义纳什均衡候选解，并通过求解器进行可行性与单边偏离审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物主体（如调控模块、微生物菌株、生物个体及种群）在共享环境中行动，但各自受不同的成本、收益和限制约束。动态博弈能够显式刻画这类交互，明确谁控制哪些决策、各参与者重视什么，以及哪些限制约束单边偏离。作者希望为生物学中的此类交互提供一个统一、可执行的建模与求解框架。

Method: 构建了包含25个生物动态博弈的库，参与者数量为2至10个，跨越分子、细胞、个体、种群四个尺度，涵盖竞争、对抗、互利、调控四类交互。每个博弈提供生物动机、可执行的Julia规范（含参与者自有控制、目标与约束）、一个参数实例，以及一个参考开环广义纳什均衡候选解及可解释轨迹。求解器CorleoneGame.jl通过序贯最优控制最佳响应计算候选解，并在粗、细两种控制网格上审计其可行性与有利可图的单边偏离。

Result: 全部25个参考计算在两种网格上均满足审计容差。以植物—食草动物博弈为贯穿示例，展示了可执行规范如何生成经审计的参考候选解并定性重现已发表的预测，生物假设如何转化为具有不同候选解的实例变体，以及候选解如何生成用于逆向博弈的合成数据。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可执行、可审计的生物动态博弈库与配套求解器，将生物假设、博弈规范、均衡计算与数据生成串联起来，为生物交互的建模、验证和逆向博弈分析提供了可复用的基础框架。

Abstract: Biological agents, from regulatory modules and microbial strains to organisms and populations, act on a common environment while each responds to its own costs, benefits, and limits. A dynamic game makes such interactions explicit by assigning who controls which decision, what each participant values, and which restrictions constrain unilateral deviations.
  We present a library of 25 biological dynamic games with 2 to 10 players spanning molecular, cellular, organismal, and population scales and competitive, antagonistic, mutualistic, and regulatory interactions. Instances cover single seasons, cycles that return to their initial state, or episodic tasks; seven include duration choice and some players' feasible sets are coupled. For each game we provide biological motivation, an executable Julia specification with player-owned controls, objectives and restrictions, a parameter instance, and a reference open-loop generalized Nash equilibrium candidate with interpreted trajectories.
  The solver CorleoneGame.jl computes candidates by sequential optimal-control best responses and audits them for feasibility and profitable unilateral deviations on a coarse and a refined control grid. All 25 reference computations satisfy the audit tolerances on both grids. Using a plant--herbivore game as a running example, we show how an executable specification yields an audited reference candidate that qualitatively reproduces a published prediction, how biological hypotheses become instance variants with different candidates, and how the candidates generate synthetic data for inverse games.

</details>


### [178] [A Lagrange-Newton method for solving optimal control problems on manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06310)
*Laura Weigl,Anton Schiela*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究几何变分方程约束下的最优控制问题，状态为到非线性流形的映射。作者定义拉格朗日函数、推导一阶最优性条件，提出Lagrange-Newton数值方法及局部分裂技术，并给出数值算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE约束优化通常通过施加控制（如力场）使PDE解达到期望构型，并在控制代价与状态偏差之间求最优平衡。本文关注将这一框架推广到几何变分方程约束、状态为非线性流形映射的情形。

Method: 针对该几何最优控制问题定义拉格朗日函数并推导一阶最优性条件；提出Lagrange-Newton方法进行数值求解；引入基于局部分裂技术的可实现算法，并证明其等价于对拉格朗日导数应用牛顿法。

Result: 通过数值算例验证理论框架，包括用力场对弹性测地线与最小能量曲面的最优控制，以及不可伸长杆与壳的控制。

Conclusion: 所提框架与算法适用于流形上几何变分方程约束的最优控制问题，并为弹性、曲面、杆壳等几何模型的控制提供有效数值手段。

Abstract: In PDE-constrained optimization a control is applied within a partial differential equation (PDE), e.g., as a force field, to achieve a desired configuration of the PDE solution. The objective is usually to find an optimal balance between control cost and deviation of the controlled state from the desired configuration. We consider this problem class in a setting, where the PDE is given by a geometric variational equation and the state is a mapping into a nonlinear manifold. We define a Lagrangian function corresponding to this geometric optimal control problem and derive first-order optimality conditions. Moreover, we present a Lagrange-Newton method for their numerical solution. We introduce an implementable algorithm using a local splitting technique and show its equivalence to Newton's method applied to the derivative of the Lagrangian. The theoretical framework is illustrated by numerical examples, including the optimal control of elastic geodesics and surfaces of minimal energy by force fields and the control of inextensible rods and shells.

</details>


### [179] [Direct Vertex-Derivative Coefficient Propagation forHomogeneous Numerical Integration (HNI)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06431)
*Jean B Lasserre*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文提出一种直接通过面复形传播泛函的方法，在齐次数值积分（HNI）框架下，无需先构造矩向量，即可获得定义在次数q齐次形式空间上、由多面体面几何与锚点决定的带符号顶点导数系数泛函，并用Riesz框架刻画其非唯一性与误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HNI算法（结合Euler恒等式与Stokes定理对多面体P上的齐次多项式积分）通常返回标量积分或可复用矩族；矩表虽可在事后重编码为单点支撑的纯q阶分布表示，但需先形成矩向量。作者希望绕过该中间步骤，直接将泛函沿面复形传播，从而获得与P及其面几何、锚点直接关联的顶点导数系数泛函。

Method: 将泛函沿面复形传播一次，针对给定(P, q)，不预先构造矩向量，直接在H_q（q阶形式空间）上得到带符号的顶点导数系数泛函；该泛函由所给面几何与选定锚点直接确定。采用有限维Riesz框架分析其非唯一性，给出受限公式的内在L^2(P)误差，并支持加权后处理；分别独立生成q阶与2q阶系数向量，并建立二者的显式相容关系。利用顶点锚点对传播表示进行剪枝，在若干多边形示例及离线基准中验证。

Result: 所得q阶与2q阶系数向量满足显式相容关系；顶点锚点可在多边形示例中剪枝传播表示，离线基准量化了该效应（不声称全局基数最优或改善条件数）；构造适用于任意维凸多面体及有向非凸多面体域。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了HNI的一种直接泛函传播构造，避免了矩向量的显式形成，并通过Riesz框架统一描述非唯一性、误差与加权后处理，可推广至任意维凸与非凸有向多面体域。

Abstract: Homogeneous numerical integration (HNI) combines Euler's identity and Stokes' theorem to integrate homogeneous polynomials over a polytope $P$. Existing algorithms return scalar integrals or reusable moment families. Any resulting moment table can be recoded afterward: once all degree-$q$ moments are known, integration on $\mathcal H\_q$ (degree-$q$ forms) has a trivial representation by a pure order-$q$ distribution supported at any single arbitrary point. Our contribution is instead to propagate functionals through the face complex, once for $(P,q)$ and without first forming the moment vector, to obtain a signed vertex-derivative coefficient functional on $\mathcal H\_q$, specific to $P$ and determined directly by its supplied face geometry and chosen anchors. A finite-dimensional Riesz framework describes its nonuniqueness, gives an intrinsic $L^2(P)$ error for restricted formulas, and supports weighted post-processing. The independently generated degree-$q$ and degree-$2q$ coefficient vectors satisfy an explicit compatibility relation. Vertex anchors can prune the propagated representation as shown in some polygon examples and an offline benchmark quantify this effect without claiming global cardinality optimality or improved conditioning. The construction covers convex polytopes and oriented non-convex polyhedral domains in arbitrary dimension.

</details>


### [180] [A posteriori error bounds for the Uncertain Volatility Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06495)
*Lokman A Abbas-Turki,Chassagneux Jean-Fran{\c c}ois,Jean-Philippe Lemor,Gr{é}goire Loeper,Simon Sananes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为不确定波动率模型(Uncertain Volatility Model, UVM)下欧式期权价格的数值近似发展了后验原始-对偶界(primal-dual bounds):由候选值函数的Hessian诱导反馈控制得原始下界,基于矩阵值Gamma场与哈密顿惩罚得对偶上界,并给出离散化误差分析与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定波动率模型下期权定价对应Black-Scholes-Barenblatt方程,其数值解通常缺乏可靠误差估计。作者希望为神经网络等近似方法得到的候选解提供可计算的严格上下界,以定量评估近似质量,尤其是揭示二阶导数(二阶信息)精度对界紧致性的影响。

Method: 1) 原始侧:给定光滑候选近似值函数,由其次阶导数(Hessian)诱导反馈控制,构造原始下界。2) 对偶侧:引入矩阵值Gamma场表示,通过非负哈密顿惩罚项给出上界;并证明当对偶场由候选本身生成时,该上界可等价表示为Black-Scholes-Barenblatt方程残差的表示。3) 离散化:研究原始量与对偶量的离散时间近似,量化相应离散化误差。4) 数值实验:对随机策略梯度与物理信息神经网络(PINN)得到的候选解进行数值评估,并与随机控制文献中的鞅对偶方法比较。

Result: 建立了原始-对偶后验界的连续与离散理论,证明由候选自身生成的对偶上界可用BSB方程残差等价刻画,并给出离散化误差量化。数值实验表明:导数精度(尤其二阶信息)对获得紧致的后验界至关重要,且所得估计与鞅对偶方法进行了对比。

Conclusion: 二阶信息(尤其是Hessian/Gamma)的准确性是获得紧致后验上下界的关键;所提原始-对偶框架为UVM下期权定价的数值近似提供了可验证的误差界,适用于神经网络类求解器的可靠性评估。

Abstract: We develop a posteriori primal-dual bounds for numerical approximations of European option prices in the Uncertain Volatility Model. Given a smooth candidate approximation of the value function, a feedback control induced by its Hessian yields a primal lower bound. On the dual side, we derive a representation based on a matrix-valued Gamma field, which provides an upper bound through a nonnegative Hamiltonian penalty. We further show that, when the dual field is generated by the candidate itself, this upper bound admits an equivalent representation in terms of the residual of the associated Black-Scholes-Barenblatt equation. We then study discrete-time approximations of these primal and dual quantities and quantify the corresponding discretization errors. Finally, we investigate their numerical evaluation for candidates obtained by stochastic policy-gradient and physics-informed neural-network methods, and compare the resulting estimates with a martingale dual approach from the stochastic-control literature. The numerical experiments highlight the importance of derivative accuracy, and in particular of second-order information, for obtaining tight a posteriori bounds.

</details>


### [181] [CaCuTe Meets SESOP: Better Theory and Practice with Lipschitz Hessians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06557)
*Nazarii Tupitsa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出CaCuSESOP方法，将CaCuTe与SESOP结合，在Hessian Lipschitz连续下实现全局O(k^{-2})速率并自动获得局部加速线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SESOP存在局限（需精确最小化原目标等），需在保持全局速率的同时去除这些限制，并在Hessian于最优解处正定时自动实现加速局部收敛且无需已知强凸参数。

Method: 将CaCuTe与sequential subspace optimization (SESOP)结合；用低维三次上模型的最小化替代原目标的精确最小化，仅需解到可计算精度；三次正则化参数通过回溯法选取；每步使用固定次数的Hessian-向量乘积；给出进入加速区域的有限界并自适应调整重启策略。

Result: CaCuSESOP达到全局O(k^{-2})速率，每步Hessian-向量乘积次数固定；若最优解处Hessian正定，则自动达到加速局部线性速率，复杂度O(\sqrt{κ_\star}\log(1/\varepsilon))，且不需向算法提供局部强凸参数、条件数κ_\star或最优值。同时证明在该固定预算梯度/Hessian-向量乘积线性张成预言机类下，即便允许动量与无限记忆，全局O(k^{-2})速率也是最优的。

Conclusion: CaCuSESOP在克服经典SESOP限制的同时保持全局最优速率，并能自动转入加速局部收敛，全局速率的O(k^{-2})最优性在该预言机类中得到证明。

Abstract: We combine CaCuTe with sequential subspace optimization (SESOP) for smooth convex minimization under Lipschitz continuity of the Hessian. The resulting method, CaCuSESOP, achieves the global rate $O(k^{-2})$ with a fixed number of Hessian--vector products per iteration, while removing key limitations of classical SESOP. In particular, exact minimization of the original objective is replaced by minimization of a low-dimensional cubic upper model, which needs only be solved to a computable accuracy. The cubic regularization parameter is selected by backtracking.
  If the Hessian at the minimizer is positive definite, CaCuSESOP automatically attains an accelerated local linear rate with complexity $O(\sqrt{κ_\star}\log(1/\varepsilon))$. Neither the local strong-convexity parameter, the condition number $κ_\star$, nor the optimal value is supplied to the algorithm. We give finite bounds for entering this regime and adapting the restart schedule. Finally, we prove that the global $O(k^{-2})$ rate is optimal for the considered fixed-budget gradient/Hessian--vector-product linear-span oracle class, even with momentum and unrestricted memory.

</details>

### [182] [Simultaneous measurement of the Higgs boson decay rates to WW and ZZ in fully hadronic final states at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04397)
*Jan Eysermans,Mila Pandurovic,Christoph Paus,Michele Selvaggi,Aman Desai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了FCC-ee上Higgsstrahlung过程的全强子末态，通过联合提取同时测量H→WW和H→ZZ信号强度，预期对σ(ZH)×BR的相对不确定度分别为1.48%和8.20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定Higgs玻色子与电弱规范玻色子的耦合是FCC-ee物理项目的关键目标，尤其H与W、Z的耦合对模型无关地确定Higgs总衰变宽度起核心作用。

Method: 研究Higgsstrahlung产生过程中H→WW和H→ZZ衰变，聚焦伴随Z玻色子及Higgs衰变产生的矢量玻色子均强子衰变的六喷注末态；采用喷注聚类与专门的喷注配对技术优化强子衰变规范玻色子的重建，并由于WW与ZZ衰变模式间存在显著运动学重叠而对两信号强度进行同时提取。假设质心能量240 GeV、积分亮度10.8 ab^-1。

Result: 对H→WW，σ(ZH)×BR的预期相对不确定度为1.48%；对H→ZZ，为8.20%。

Conclusion: 全强子道中H→WW因大分支比具有高信号统计量，H→ZZ则因Z玻色子大强子分支比部分补偿了其较小分支比；联合提取可有效分离两个信号成分，实现高精度耦合测量。

Abstract: The precise determination of the Higgs boson couplings to electroweak gauge bosons is a key objective of the FCC-ee physics program. In particular, measurements of the Higgs boson couplings to W and Z bosons play a central role in the model-independent determination of its total decay width. This paper presents a study of Higgs boson decays to H -> WW and H -> ZZ in the Higgsstrahlung production process. The analysis focuses on events in which the associated Z boson and the vector bosons from the Higgs decay all decay hadronically, resulting in six-jet final states. The large H -> WW branching fraction leads to high signal statistics in the fully hadronic channel, while for H -> ZZ the much smaller Higgs branching fraction is partly compensated by the large hadronic branching fractions of the Z bosons. Due to the substantial kinematic overlap between the WW and ZZ decay modes, their signal strengths are extracted simultaneously. Jet-clustering and dedicated jet-pairing techniques are employed to optimize the reconstruction of the hadronically decaying gauge bosons and enhance the separation of the two signal components. Assuming a center-of-mass energy of 240 GeV and an integrated luminosity of 10.8 ab^-1, expected relative uncertainties of 1.48% and 8.20% are obtained on the production cross section times branching fraction, sigma(ZH) x BR, for the H -> WW and H -> ZZ decay modes, respectively.

</details>


### [183] [Search for Higgs boson compositeness effects in high-mass dilepton final states in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 and 13.6 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04782)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS利用13/13.6 TeV、207 fb$^{-1}$数据搜索高质标双轻子末态中的B SM效应，重点针对Higgs玻色子复合性，给出斜参数$\mathcal{W}$、$\mathcal{Y}$的严格限制，并在含Z'共振的新物理场景中设定更严格的复合性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的效应，特别是Higgs玻色子复合性假说，通过高质标双电子和双缪子末态寻找复合性导致的轻子质量谱畸变及束缚态（如Higgs玻色子和新的大质量矢量玻色子共振）。

Method: 使用LHC在13和13.6 TeV质心能量下采集的、总积分亮度为207 fb$^{-1}$的CMS数据，采用有效场论方法分析双轻子质量谱，并对斜参数$\mathcal{W}$和$\mathcal{Y}$进行单参数拟合；同时在假设存在Z'共振的新物理场景中设定复合性限制，并首次探索大共振衰变宽度情形。

Result: 在单参数拟合下，95%置信水平给出 $\mathcal{Y}$ 的区间为 $-$3.9 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ $\lt$ $\mathcal{Y}$ $\lt$ 3.5 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$，$\mathcal{W}$ 的区间为 $-$2.1 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ $\lt$ $\mathcal{W}$ $\lt$ 1.6 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$；在若干假设Z'共振存在于或略高于可见双轻子质量谱的新物理场景中，设定了TeV能标下更严格的复合性限制。

Conclusion: 该搜索对斜参数$\mathcal{W}$和$\mathcal{Y}$给出了严格的实验限制，并在含Z'共振的新物理情形下设定了更严格的复合性限制，首次涵盖大共振衰变宽度的情形。

Abstract: A search for beyond the standard model effects in high-mass dielectron and dimuon final states with the CMS detector is performed, with a specific focus on Higgs boson compositeness. The search uses data collected at the LHC at 13 and 13.6 TeV center-of-mass energies, corresponding to a total integrated luminosity of 207 fb$^{-1}$. Compositeness would manifest as distortions in the observed dilepton mass spectrum and the presence of bound states, such as the Higgs boson and new massive vector boson resonances. Assuming the existence of new boson states with masses well above the TeV scale, an effective field theory approach provides stringent bounds on the oblique parameters $\mathcal{W}$ and $\mathcal{Y}$, which are zero in the standard model. One-dimensional fits, in which only one of the oblique parameters is allowed to deviate from zero, provide the following limits: $-$3.9 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$$\lt$ $\mathcal{Y}$ $\lt$ 3.5 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ and $-$2.1 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ $\lt$ $\mathcal{W}$ $\lt$ 1.6 $\times$ 10$^{-4}$ at 95% confidence level. More stringent compositeness limits at the TeV scale are set in several new physics scenarios under the assumption that a new Z' resonance is physically present within or slightly above the visible dilepton mass spectrum. In this context, cases with large resonance decay widths are explored for the first time.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于BESIII在3.773 GeV采集的20.3 fb$^{-1}$数据，首次对双Cabibbo压低衰变$D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$进行振幅分析，首次观测到$D^+\to K^{*0}\rho^+$与$D^+\to K^{*+}\rho^0$，并测量其分支比、纵向极化分数及$D^+\to K^+\omega$分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双Cabibbo压低衰变$D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$此前缺乏振幅分析，其中的$D\to VV$末态及极化信息尚未被观测，测量这些过程有助于检验标准模型、理解D介子衰变动力学与极化机制。

Method: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV处采集的积分亮度20.3 fb$^{-1}$的$e^+e^-$对撞数据，对$D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$进行首次振幅分析。

Result: 首次观测到$D^+\to K^{*0}\rho^+$和$D^+\to K^{*+}\rho^0$，分支比分别为$(5.67\pm0.41_{\rm stat}\pm0.17_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$和$(5.32\pm0.57_{\rm stat}\pm0.24_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$；两者均具有大的横向极化，纵向极化分数分别为$0.111\pm0.024_{\rm stat}\pm0.008_{\rm syst}$和$0.263\pm0.049_{\rm stat}\pm0.015_{\rm syst}$；测得$D^+\to K^+\omega$分支比为$(4.76\pm0.84_{\rm stat}\pm0.13_{\rm syst})\times10^{-5}$。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现$D^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\pi^0$振幅分析并首次观测到两个$D\to VV$衰变模式$D^+\to K^{*0}\rho^+$与$D^+\to K^{*+}\rho^0$，给出其分支比与纵向极化分数，同时更新了$D^+\to K^+\omega$分支比。

Abstract: By analyzing an $e^+e^-$ collision data sample with an integrated luminosity of 20.3 fb$^{-1}$ collected with the BESIII detector at the center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV, we perform the first amplitude analysis on the doubly Cabibbo-suppressed decay $D^+ \to K^+π^+π^-π^0$ and report the first observation of $D^+ \to K^{*0}ρ^+$ and $D^+\to K^{*+}ρ^0$. The corresponding branching fractions are $(5.67\pm0.41_{\rm stat}\pm0.17_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$ and $(5.32\pm0.57_{\rm stat}\pm0.24_{\rm syst})\times10^{-4}$, respectively. These two $D\to VV$ decay both have large transverse polarizations. The longitudinal polarization fractions are measured to be $0.111\pm0.024_{\rm stat}\pm0.008_{\rm syst}$ and $0.263\pm0.049_{\rm stat}\pm0.015_{\rm syst}$, respectively. The branching fraction of the decay $D^+\to K^+ω$ is measured to be $(4.76\pm0.84_{\rm stat}\pm0.13_{\rm syst})\times 10^{-5}$.

</details>


### [185] [Precision measurement of ocean tides using cosmic-ray muons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05294)
*Jiwon Seo,Jongseok Chung,Chang Hyon Ha,TongZhou Huang,Jinyoung Kim,Sooa Kim,Hani Kimku,Byoung-cheol Koh,Minsu Kwak,Sihyun Lee,Yujin Lee*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 宇宙线缪子可用于监测海底隧道上方海水负荷的潮汐变化，其计数调制与验潮仪测量高度一致，并能分辨米级海水厚度变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统验潮仪只能测量局部海平面高度，无法反映探测器上方沿路径的积分质量。作者希望探索缪子成像（muography）能否定量分辨环境负荷随时间变化的小幅波动，从而为动态自然系统监测提供新手段。

Method: 利用海底隧道中的宇宙线缪子探测器，记录缪子通量随海水厚度变化的衰减调制；将观测到的计数率调制与独立验潮仪数据对比，提取衰减响应系数；并开展空间缪子成像扫描，验证上方负荷模型及海水贡献的必要性。

Result: 观测到的调制与验潮仪测量紧密吻合，得到衰减响应系数 k=43.2±1.0 counts/h/m，可分辨米级海水厚度变化；空间缪子成像扫描验证了负荷模型，并确认海水贡献对重现观测衰减是必不可少的。

Conclusion: 缪子成像能够定量分辨环境负荷中微小的时间依赖变化，且测量的是探测器上方的积分负荷，区别于局部验潮仪；这为监测动态自然系统提供了新途径。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray muons provide a natural probe of the integrated mass along their trajectories. Here we show that cosmic-ray muons can monitor ocean-tide-induced variations in seawater overburden within an underwater tunnel. The observed modulation closely tracks independent tide-gauge measurements, yielding an attenuation response coefficient of $k=43.2\pm1.0$~counts$\rm /h/m$ and resolving meter-scale variations in seawater overburden. A spatial muography scan validates the overburden model, confirming that the seawater contribution is essential to reproduce the observed attenuation. Unlike conventional tide gauges that measure sea level locally, muography measures the integrated overburden above the detector. These results demonstrate that muography can quantitatively resolve small, time-dependent variations in environmental overburden, providing a new approach to monitoring dynamic natural systems.

</details>


### [186] [Search for Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background of All Flavors with PandaX-4T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05980)
*Yingxin Zhang,PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用PandaX-4T液氙探测器首次通过相干弹性中微子-原子核散射(CEνNS)搜寻弥散超新星中微子背景(DSNB)，未观测到显著过量，给出90%置信度下的通量上限(2.27-2.43)×10³ cm⁻²s⁻¹ per flavor，与国际最优限值相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥散超新星中微子背景是宇宙所有历史超新星中微子累积形成的信号，探测它有助于研究超新星演化与恒星形成历史。相干弹性中微子-原子核散射(CEνNS)截面在低能区增强，为利用液氙探测器搜寻DSNB提供了新途径，此前尚无此类搜寻报道。

Method: 使用中国锦屏地下实验室的PandaX-4T液氙探测器，采用相干弹性中微子-原子核散射(CEνNS)通道。基于调试运行与首次科学运行累计1.54 tonne·year的曝光量，分析中微子能量高于19.3 MeV的ν_x通量(ν_μ, ν̄_μ, ν_τ, ν̄_τ各味)，与预期本底比较。

Result: 未观测到相对于预期本底的显著事件过量。在90%置信度下，对高于19.3 MeV入射中微子能量的DSNB ν_x通量给出上限(2.27-2.43)×10³ cm⁻²s⁻¹ per flavor，该结果与当前世界最优限值相当。

Conclusion: 该工作首次展示了液氙探测器通过CEνNS搜寻DSNB的可行性，其灵敏度已达到与世界最优限值可比的水平，表明液氙时间投影室技术在未来更大曝光量下有望进一步提升DSNB探测能力。

Abstract: We present the first search for the diffuse supernova neutrino background utilizing coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering with the PandaX-4T liquid xenon detector at the China Jinping Underground Laboratory. Based on a total exposure of $1.54~\mathrm{tonne \cdot year}$ from the commissioning and first science runs, we observe no significant event excess over the expected background. We therefore set an upper limit of $(2.27 - 2.43) \times 10^3~\mathrm{cm^{-2}s^{-1}}$ per flavor at 90\% confidence level on the $ν_x$ flux (each of $ν_μ, \barν_μ, ν_τ, \barν_τ$) of the diffuse supernova neutrino background for incident neutrino energies above 19.3~MeV. The result is comparable to the current world best limit.

</details>


### [187] [A graph neural network for deep learning-based full event interpretation at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06054)
*Elena Graverini,Samuel Morand,Rafael Silva Coutinho,Felipe Luan Souza de Almeida*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的深度学习方法，用于FCC-ee上Z→bb̄事件中b强子衰变树的完整事态识别与重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为未来电子-正电子环形对撞机（FCC-ee）的物理分析提供完整的b强子衰变链识别能力，传统方法难以同时识别并重建事件中分层衰变树，因此需要引入深度学习方案。

Method: 基于模拟的e+e-→Z→bb̄事件（√s=91 GeV），以IDEA探测器概念为案例，构建图神经网络框架；将重建带电粒子作为图节点，粒子间成对可观测量编码为边特征，同时识别并重建每个事件中b强子的分层衰变树。

Result: 在测试样本上，90%的重建衰变链包含完全正确的粒子集合（与推断的衰变层次无关）；在多个基准b强子衰变中，对于最多三代衰变的衰变链，聚类效率超过93%。

Conclusion: 图神经网络可实现高精度的b强子完整衰变链识别与重建，为FCC-ee上基于IDEA探测器的完整事态解释提供了可行原型。

Abstract: This paper presents a deep learning-based full event interpretation prototype developed for the electron-positron Future Circular Collider (FCC-$ee$) using simulated $e^{+}e^{-}\rightarrow Z\rightarrow b\bar{b}$ events at $\sqrt{s} = 91 \ \mathrm{GeV}$. The study was carried out using the Innovative Detector for Electron positron Accelerators (IDEA) detector concept as case study for future application. The proposed graph neural network framework aims to simultaneously identify and reconstruct the hierarchical decay trees of $b$-hadrons in each event using information from reconstructed charged particles. Events are represented as graphs, where reconstructed charged particles form the nodes and pairwise observables are encoded as edge features describing the relations between particles. On a test sample, 90% of reconstructed decay chains contain exactly the correct set of particles, irrespective of their inferred decay hierarchy. Across a range of benchmark $b$-hadron decays, this clustering efficiency exceeds 93% for decay chains spanning up to three generations of decays.

</details>


### [188] [SysVar: Consistent and scalable treatment of systematic uncertainties in template-based analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06117)
*Ilias Tsaklidis,Agrim Aggarwal,Georgios Alexandris,Tristan Fillinger,Giacomo De Pietro,Daniel Ivanov,Melisa-Melek Akdag,Markus Prim,Florian Bernlochner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: SysVar 是一个轻量开源 API，通过将完整协方差压缩为正交本征变分（eigenvariations）来一致地传播修正权重引入的系统不确定性并保持相关性，简化了信号提取中名义与变分模板的生成流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着分析规模增大，将修正权重带来的系统不确定性传播到模拟数据生成的模板中，同时在信号提取变量间保持相关性，变得越来越困难；而这一流程对高精度测量又必不可少，且人工处理容易出错。

Method: SysVar 提供轻量 API，隐藏了应用修正权重、构建系统变分以及对名义与变分模板进行直方图化的繁琐簿记工作。它通过在分析全程传播完整协方差，并将其压缩为一小组正交本征变分，每个变分定义一个普通的形状 nuisance 参数，从而保持相关性。

Result: 作者通过合并两个独立的伪测量（pseudo-measurements）展示了 SysVar 的工作流程与效果，这两个测量受益于以模板形状编码的一致相关系统不确定性。

Conclusion: SysVar 具有开源、可通过 pip 安装、与具体实验无关的特点，能够简化原本易错但对高精度测量至关重要的分析流程。

Abstract: Propagating systematic uncertainties from correction weights into templates generated from simulated data while preserving correlations in the signal extraction variables becomes increasingly challenging as analyses scale in size. SysVar's lightweight API hides most of the bookkeeping complexity of applying correction weights, building systematic variations, and histogramming nominal and varied templates. This streamlines workflows that are otherwise error-prone but essential for high-precision measurements. Correlations are preserved by propagating the full covariance across the analysis and compressing it into a small set of orthogonal eigenvariations, each defining an ordinary shape nuisance parameter. In this work, we demonstrate SysVar's workflow and impact by combining two independent pseudo-measurements that benefit from consistently correlated systematic uncertainties encoded in the template shapes. SysVar is open-source, pip-installable, and experiment-agnostic.

</details>


### [189] [FPGA-based muon shower identification and graph neural network tracking algorithms for HL-LHC triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06379)
*Daniel Estrada,Elena Aller,Andrea Cardini,Santiago Folgueras,Pelayo Leguina,Javier Prado*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出两种面向CMS一级触发升级的硬件加速策略，分别用于识别桶部辐射μ子簇射和重建端盖过渡区位移μ子径迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能辐射μ子会导致效率损失，长寿命粒子等非常规物理信号也对现有CMS一级触发（L1T）提出挑战，亟需在低层探测器击中层面引入新的触发手段。

Method: 第一种策略在桶部Drift Tube中监测击中多重数，实现专用μ子簇射识别算法以生成Shower Primitives；第二种策略针对桶-端盖过渡区，评估图神经网络重建位移μ子径迹，先以GraphSAGE代理模型验证FPGA可行性，并采用INT8-PO2量化与数据驱动位宽优化，用编译期算术移位替代DSP定点乘法。

Result: 簇射识别算法具有亚束团交叉延迟和极低资源消耗，可标记簇射μ子并为下游径迹寻找器提供上下文；图神经网络实现将DSP占用降至20%，推理延迟确定为19个时钟周期。

Conclusion: 这些硬件-软件协同设计为在L1T触发长寿命粒子衰变等非标准μ子信号奠定了基础。

Abstract: This work presents two hardware-accelerated strategies for the upgrade of the CMS Level-1 Trigger (L1T) to target efficiency losses due to highly energetic radiating muons and unconventional physics signatures, such as Long-Lived Particles (LLPs). Operating directly on low-level detector hits, the first strategy implements a dedicated muon shower identification algorithm in the CMS barrel region that monitors hit multiplicities in the Drift Tube chambers to generate Shower Primitives. Executing with sub-bunch-crossing latency and ultra-low resource consumption, this algorithm successfully tags showered muons and provides essential context for downstream track finders. The second approach targets the barrel-endcap transition (overlap) region by evaluating Graph Neural Networks to reconstruct displaced muon tracks. Following a divided methodology that first isolates hardware feasibility from evolving physics refinements, an initial proxy model based on a GraphSAGE architecture is evaluated to validate its viability on FPGAs within strict latency limits. This implementation utilizes an INT8-PO2 quantization technique coupled with a data-driven bit-width optimization. By replacing DSP-heavy fixed-point multiplications with fast compile-time arithmetic bit-shifts and allocating the minimum required bits per signal, this hardware-software co-design reduces DSP utilization to $20\%$ and achieves a deterministic inference latency of just $19$ clock cycles. Together, these developments pave the way for triggering on non-standard muon signatures, such as those from LLP decays, at L1T.

</details>


### [190] [Design optimization of the inter-electrode structures in DC-RSD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06676)
*N. Cartiglia,R. Arcidiacono,G. Bardelli,M. Boscardin,A. Cassese,M. Centis Vignali,T. Croci,M. Durando,M. Ferrero,A. Fondacci,S. Gaiero,O. Hammad Ali,L. Lanteri,A. Losana,L. Menzio,A. Morozzi,F. Moscatelli,D. Passeri,G. Paternoster,G. Sguazzoni,F. Siviero,V. Sola,L. Viliani*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文开发了ROOT工具DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator，将电阻表面建模为二维Kirchhoff网络，比较了沟槽、电阻条及无结构三种电极间结构对C-coupled RSD位置分辨率的影响，发现电阻条分辨率优于沟槽约25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C-coupled RSD通过电阻n+表层实现电荷共享，电极间结构（沟槽、电阻条或无结构）直接决定可实现的位置分辨率及其均匀性，但何种结构最优缺乏定量答案。

Method: 基于ROOT开发DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator，将电阻表面建模为二维Kirchhoff电阻网络，求解电荷共享随击中位置、表面电阻率和电极间结构的变化；用FBK DC-RSD传感器（像素间距300、500、1000 μm）的测试束数据进行验证，再对三种结构进行比较。

Result: 模拟器正确复现了位置分辨率、其空间均匀性及信号共享模板；在所有测试的条电阻（500–2000 Ω）与输入阻抗（0–100 Ω）、表面电阻率1000 Ω/□下，电阻条分辨率比沟槽优约25%。

Conclusion: 差异根源在于电荷共享分数的空间梯度：沟槽产生的梯度明显更弱且更不均匀（尤其远离像素中心处），而电阻条或裸电阻表面的梯度更强更均匀，因此电阻条分辨率更优。

Abstract: C-coupled Resistive Silicon Detectors (RSD) share charge among electrodes through a resistive n$^+$ surface layer, and the choice of inter-electrode structure --- a trench, a resistive strip, or no structure at all --- directly determines the achievable position resolution and its uniformity. This paper addresses the question of which structure best optimises the spatial resolution. To answer it quantitatively, DCRSD_SurfaceSimulator was developed, a ROOT-based tool that models the resistive surface as a two-dimensional Kirchhoff resistive network and solves for charge sharing as a function of hit position, surface resistivity, and inter-electrode structure. The simulator is validated against test-beam data from FBK DC-RSD sensors with 300, 500, and 1000~$μ$m pixel pitch, reproducing the position resolution, its spatial uniformity, and the signal-sharing templates correctly. Using the validated simulator, trenches, resistive strips, and no containment are then compared on position resolution and its spatial uniformity, inter-electrode resistance, and signal leakage to outer pixels. Resistive strips are found to outperform trenches in resolution by approximately 25\% for all tested strip resistances (500--2000~$Ω$) and input impedances (0--100~$Ω$), at a surface resistivity of 1000~$Ω/\square$, and trace the origin of this difference to the spatial gradient of the charge-sharing fraction: trenches produce a markedly weaker and less uniform gradient, particularly away from the pixel centre, than either a resistive strip or a bare resistive surface.

</details>


### [191] [How to scale your HEP ML models: A recipe for robust architecture comparisons at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06784)
*Matthias Vigl,Nikita Pond,Jackson Barr,Alexander Froch,Dan Guest,Nicole Hartman,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一套系统化方法，用于在高能物理（HEP）任务中推导稳健的缩放律，并在ATLAS JetSet2数据集（约110亿喷注）上验证多任务Transformer在计算受限与数据受限两种情形下的缩放行为，首次给出早停条件下模型规模、训练时长、学习率与批大小的联合最优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型等领域的进展多来自预测性能随训练投入变化的缩放律，高能物理中也已观察到类似行为。为支持进一步研究，需要一个系统化流程来推导稳健缩放律，并在相关预算维度上比较不同设计选择。

Method: 先在玩具问题上验证完整的缩放轨迹，再将流程应用于ATLAS JetSet2数据集（约110亿喷注）上的多任务Transformer，分别在计算受限和数据受限两种情形下进行分析；在数据受限情形下对早停条件下的模型规模、训练时长、学习率与批大小进行联合优化预测。

Result: 在计算最优缩放时，模型规模与数据集规模近似呈√C依赖关系；在相同计算预算下，辅助目标可降低主喷注分类损失；将输入扩展到更低层级数据可系统性降低损失，而缩放指数几乎不变。幂律区的起点由规模决定：当数据集规模低于某阈值时，损失几乎不包含关于高计算缩放的信息。

Conclusion: 大规模、高质量的全模拟数据集是开展缩放研究与构建HEP基础模型的重要基础。

Abstract: Much of the recent progress in machine learning domains such as language models has come from scaling laws that predict performance as a function of training effort. In high-energy physics (HEP) similar behavior has now been observed. To aid further study, we present a systematic procedure to derive robust scaling laws and compare design choices on the relevant budget axes for HEP tasks. We first validate the full scaling trajectory on toy problems and then apply the procedure to multi-task transformers on the ~11 billion-jet ATLAS JetSet2 dataset, in both the compute- and data-constrained regimes. For the latter, we predict, to the best of our knowledge for the first time, the jointly optimal model size, training horizon, learning rate and batch size under early stopping. At compute-optimal scaling, we recover a near-equal $\sqrt{C}$ dependence of model and dataset size, and find that auxiliary objectives lower the primary jet-classification loss at equal compute budget. Expanding the inputs toward lower-level data systematically lowers the loss while leaving the scaling exponent nearly unchanged. The onset of the power-law regime is itself set by scale: below a threshold in dataset size the loss carries little information about high-compute scaling, underscoring the value of large, high-quality full-simulation datasets as a foundation for scaling studies and the development of foundation models in HEP.

</details>


### [192] [Reconfigurable On-Chip AI for Particle Physics Detectors with Logic Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06816)
*Mila Bileska,Lino Gerlach,Jennet Dickinson,Lindsey Gray,Giuseppe Di Guglielmo,Peter Elmer,Isobel Ojalvo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评估逻辑神经网络（LNNs）用于像素探测器的片上推理，展示其资源效率、抗辐射鲁棒性及可重配置性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧机器学习在极高数据率或资源受限场景中愈发重要。像素探测器因高粒度和高读出频率而产生无法集中处理的数据率，且空间与功耗约束严格；同时辐射损伤会逐步改变探测器响应，要求模型在部署后可重新训练与重配置。传统硬连线方案仅针对窄定义任务优化，缺乏对数据分布变化的适应能力。

Method: 评估逻辑神经网络（LNNs）用于像素探测器片上推理。LNNs 直接学习布尔逻辑而非传统算术运算，适合定制硅实现。工作包括：分析其资源效率以支持更复杂架构；测试其对辐射引起的随机位翻转的鲁棒性；并提出可重配置 LNN 的新方法，使模型在部署后能适应变化的工作条件。

Result: LNNs 展现出优越的资源效率，可能带来模型能力的跨越式提升，使更复杂架构在可用硬件预算内可行；同时对辐射诱发的随机位翻转具有更强的鲁棒性；并实现了部署后的可重配置与自适应。

Conclusion: LNNs 适合像素探测器的片上推理，兼具高效、抗辐射与可重配置优势，为下一代粒子物理像素探测器提供可行方案。

Abstract: On-device machine learning is increasingly important in applications where extreme data rates or resource constraints make centralized processing infeasible. Logic neural networks (LNNs), which directly learn Boolean logic instead of conventional arithmetic operations, have recently emerged as a state-of-the-art approach for efficient inference and are particularly well suited for deployment in custom silicon. However, hard-wired solutions are typically optimized for narrowly defined tasks and lack the ability to adapt to changing data distributions. Next-generation pixel detectors in particle physics present a particularly challenging use case: their high granularity and readout frequency generate data rates that cannot be centrally processed, while stringent space and power constraints necessitate highly efficient on-chip inference. Moreover, radiation damage progressively alters detector response, requiring models that can be re-trained and reconfigured after deployment. In this work, we evaluate LNNs for on-chip inference in pixel detectors. We show that their superior resource efficiency may enable a step change in model capability, making more complex architectures feasible within the available hardware budget. LNNs also provide increased robustness against radiation-induced random bit flips. Finally, we present novel work on reconfigurable LNNs, enabling models to adapt to changing operating conditions after deployment.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Poincaré duality and module topological complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04153)
*Paul-Eugène Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限有理同伦型、有理上同调为有限维Poincaré对偶代数的单连通空间上，MTC、TC₀与TC₀ᴹ三者相等，并给出映射的相对截面与相对范畴的sharp界，推广到高阶拓扑复杂度和乘积可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Farber引入的拓扑复杂度衡量配置空间上连续局部运动规划规则的数量。已有结果通常需要formality或ellipticity假设，本文希望去掉这些限制，在更一般的Poincaré对偶情形下建立拓扑复杂度各有理不变量的相等关系及其sharp界。

Method: 使用控制Sullivan消解（controlled Sullivan resolutions）与相对范畴的理想值判定准则（ideal-valued criterion）；对Poincaré对偶纤维的纤维化截面，证明导出自交公式、Thom乘在增广理想上的消去与理想值求值三者等价。

Result: 对单连通有限有理同伦型且有理上同调为有限维Poincaré对偶代数的X，证明MTC(X)=TC₀(X)=TC₀ᴹ(X)，无需formality或ellipticity假设；结果推广到高阶拓扑复杂度并在对偶下给出乘积可加性。对有理化具有同伦收缩的映射f，得到sharp界Msecat(f_Q)≤secat(f_Q)≤relcat(f_Q)≤Msecat(f_Q)+1；特别地无对偶假设时MTC(X)≤TC₀(X)≤MTC(X)+1。对Poincaré对偶纤维的纤维化截面，三种有理截面不变量相等，涵盖有理双曲纤维；当纤维总有理同伦有限维时（基上同调维数无界）及合适的有限相对Frobenius模型也成立。几何应用包括球面、Grassmann与射影丛。

Conclusion: 在Poincaré对偶框架下统一了拓扑复杂度与相对截面范畴的有理不变量，去除了formality与ellipticity限制，并给出sharp不等式与广泛几何应用。

Abstract: Topological complexity, introduced by Farber, measures the number of continuous local motion-planning rules on a configuration space. For every simply connected space $X$ of finite rational homotopy type whose rational cohomology is a finite-dimensional Poincaré duality algebra, we prove $\mathrm{MTC}(X)=\mathrm{TC}_0(X)=\mathrm{TC}_0^M(X)$, where $\mathrm{TC}_0(X)=\mathrm{TC}(X_{\mathbb Q})$ and $M$ denotes the monoidal invariant. No formality or ellipticity assumption is required. The result extends to higher topological complexity and gives product additivity under duality. For every map $f$ between simply connected spaces of finite rational homotopy type whose rationalization admits a homotopy retraction, we establish the sharp bound $\mathrm{Msecat}(f_{\mathbb Q})\leq\mathrm{secat}(f_{\mathbb Q})\leq\mathrm{relcat}(f_{\mathbb Q})\leq\mathrm{Msecat}(f_{\mathbb Q})+1$. In particular, $\mathrm{MTC}(X)\leq\mathrm{TC}_0(X)\leq\mathrm{MTC}(X)+1$ holds without a duality assumption. The proofs use controlled Sullivan resolutions and an ideal-valued criterion for relative category. For sections of fibrations with Poincaré duality fibre, we prove that a derived self-intersection formula, vanishing of Thom multiplication on the augmentation ideal, and ideal-valued evaluation are equivalent. This criterion yields equality of the three rational sectional invariants for smooth sections of smooth bundles over simply connected smooth bases of finite rational homotopy type, with closed simply connected manifold fibre, including rationally hyperbolic fibres. Equality also holds when the fibre has finite-dimensional total rational homotopy, without a cohomological dimension bound on the base, and for suitable finite relative Frobenius models. Geometric applications include sphere, Grassmann, and projective bundles.

</details>


### [194] [The sublevel Flood bifiltration: towards scalable 2-parameter persistent homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05441)
*Mattéo Clémot,Julie Digne,Julien Tierny*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文提出子水平Flood双滤流（sublevel Flood bifiltration），将单参数Flood滤流扩展到双参数持久同调，为大规模点集提供可扩展的子水平偏移双滤流近似，并具备理论稳定性与高效算法，在密度敏感的合成数据及真实时间序列分类任务上验证了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数持久同调虽比单参数方法对离群点更稳健且能刻画数据的度量特征，但缺乏可扩展性，难以应用于大规模点集。

Method: 将原本用于单参数持久化的Flood滤流推广到双参数场景，构造子水平Flood双滤流，作为子水平偏移双滤流的可扩展近似；并给出高效计算算法。

Result: 证明了所构造双滤流具有理论稳定性，并实现了高效计算；在密度感知至关重要的低维合成数据集分类任务以及真实时间序列数据集上展示了方法性能。

Conclusion: 子水平Flood双滤流为大规模点集的2参数持久同调计算提供了兼具稳定性与可扩展性的实用方案。

Abstract: Multiparameter persistent homology is a rapidly developing branch of topological data analysis that improves the robustness of single-parameter persistent homology to outliers, while still capturing the metric characteristics of the data. However, a notable limitation is its lack of scalability. In this paper, we introduce a novel approach for efficiently computing 2-parameter persistent homology on large point sets. Our work extends the Flood filtration, originally developed for single-parameter persistence. Our construction, called the sublevel Flood bifiltration, offers a scalable approximation of the sublevel offset bifiltration. We show that it benefits from theoretical stability properties and describe how to compute it efficiently. We demonstrate the performance of our approach in classification tasks on low-dimensional synthetic datasets, where density awareness is critical, as well as on real-world time series datasets.

</details>


### [195] [Integral string topology of the symplectic group and BV rigidity for compact Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04164)
*Mukilraj K*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了辛群自由环空间的整系数BV代数，并在同调外代数的条件下刻画了紧致连通李群自由环空间BV代数的结构，给出其由指数与基本群扭部分群环决定及其反向确定的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧致连通李群自由环空间的上同调/BV代数结构，揭示其与群本身（指数、基本群扭部分）以及Poincaré对偶下的Hochschild上同调之间的联系。

Method: 计算 $\mathbb{H}_*(L\mathrm{Sp}(n);\mathbb{Z})$ 的整系数BV代数；对同调为奇数本原生成元外代数的紧致连通李群 $G$，将 $\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$ 分解为对应奇球乘积自由环空间的BV代数与 $\pi_1(G)$ 扭部分群环的张量积。

Result: 对同调为奇数本原生成元外代数的紧致连通李群 $G$，$\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$ 等于具有 $G$ 经典指数的奇球乘积自由环空间BV代数与 $\pi_1(G)$ 扭部分群环的张量积；在整环上，分次代数 $\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$ 唯一决定指数与群环，从而决定BV代数；若 $G$ 单连通，则该BV代数同构于 $H^*(G;R)$ 的Hochschild上同调，其BV算子由Poincaré对偶诱导。

Conclusion: 自由环空间的BV代数在同调外代数条件下可完全由其指数和基本群扭部分群环刻画，并在整环上可反向唯一确定；单连通情形下该结构与Poincaré对偶诱导的Hochschild上同调一致。

Abstract: We compute the integral Batalin-Vilkovisky (BV) algebra $\mathbb{H}_*(L\mathrm{Sp}(n);\mathbb{Z})$ of the free loop space of the symplectic group. We then show that for a connected compact Lie group $G$ whose homology over a commutative ring $R$ is exterior on odd primitive generators, $\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$ is the BV algebra of the free loop space of the product of odd spheres with the classical exponents of $G$, tensored with the group ring of the torsion of $π_1(G)$. Conversely, over an integral domain the graded algebra $\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$ already determines the exponents and the group ring, hence the BV algebra. If moreover $G$ is simply connected, then $\mathbb{H}_*(LG;R)$, as a BV algebra, is the Hochschild cohomology of $H^*(G;R)$ with the BV operator induced by Poincaré duality.

</details>


### [196] [Homotopy rigidity for quasitoric manifolds over a product of simplices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04234)
*Xin Fu,Tseleung So,Jongbaek Song,Stephen Theriault*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究拟环面流形的上同调刚性变体问题：若两个拟环面流形具有同构的上同调环，且其中一个的关联多面体为单纯形之积，则在局部化去除一组显式素数后，二者同伦等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典上同调刚性问题探讨上同调环同构是否蕴含同伦等价。本文考虑其在拟环面流形范畴内的变体，关注带有多面体结构约束条件下的刚性现象。

Method: 利用拟环面流形的多面体-上同调对应关系，针对关联多面体为单纯形乘积的情形进行分析，并采用局部化技术，在去掉一组显式指定的素数后比较两流形的同伦类型。

Result: 证明了：若两拟环面流形上同调环同构，且其中一个的关联多面体是单纯形的乘积，则在局部化去除一组显式素数后，二者同伦等价。

Conclusion: 在关联多面体为单纯形乘积的约束下，拟环面流形满足一种局部化的上同调刚性：上同调环同构可在去除特定素数后推出同伦等价，给出了刚性成立的显式素数范围。

Abstract: We consider a variation of the Cohomological Rigidity Problem: whether two quasitoric manifolds whose cohomology rings are isomorphic are homotopy equivalent. We show that if two quasitoric manifolds have isomorphic cohomology rings and the associated polytope of one of them is a product of simplices, then they are homotopy equivalent after localizing away from an explicit list of primes.

</details>


### [197] [Torsion of every finite order in the homology of graph braid groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04305)
*Byung Hee An*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文确定了图$K_{m+1,m+r-1}$的匹配复形（\(\mathbb{B}_m\)构造）在$H_{m-1}$中的挠子群结构，给出了显式生成元、极小性结果，并计算了若干同调与表示分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的匹配复形（\(\mathbb{B}_m\)）上的整同调挠子群，特别是$H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_m K_{m+1,m+r-1})$的挠部分，理解其来源（广义theta类）、极小图结构以及奇挠首次出现的位置。

Method: 将$H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_m K_{m+1,m+r-1};\mathbb{Z})$识别为无符号子集包含矩阵的核；通过其整对角形式确定所有主summands；用广义theta类构造cokernel的内嵌副本；结合minor序极小性论证与显式扩图操作跟踪挠阶变化；并使用表示论方法（顶点置换下的无重分解）及p-primary分析。

Result: 对$m\ge2,r\ge0$，确定了$H_{m-1}$的挠子群；每种有限阶都出现且有显式代表；广义theta类张成包含矩阵cokernel的内嵌副本且包含全部挠。当$r\ge m$时它们生成挠，每个阶为$\operatorname{lcm}(1,\ldots,m)$。对每个素数幂$q$和$m\ge q$，$K_{m+1,m+q-1}$在minor序下对$H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_m)$中的order-$q$挠是极小的。特别地，奇挠首次出现在$H_2(\mathbb{B}_3 K_{4,5})\cong\mathbb{Z}^{155}\oplus(\mathbb{Z}/2)^4\oplus\mathbb{Z}/3$，且$K_{4,5}$的任何真minor在$H_2(\mathbb{B}_3)$中无奇挠。对任意部尺寸，给出了$H_m(\mathbb{B}_m K_{a,b};\mathbb{Q})$在顶点置换下的无重分解，并证明当$a,b\ge2m-1$且$p\ge m$为奇素数时$H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_mK_{a,b};\mathbb{Z})$无$p$-primary挠。

Conclusion: 图的匹配复形（\(\mathbb{B}_m\)）整同调中的挠可由广义theta类显式给出，且在minor序下由图$K_{m+1,m+q-1}$极小实现；奇挠仅在特定规模出现并可通过扩大图结构控制其存亡，同时给出了无挠的充分条件与表示论分解。

Abstract: We determine the torsion subgroup of $H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_mK_{m+1,m+r-1};\mathbb{Z})$ for $m\ge2$ and $r\ge0$: top homology with arbitrary coefficients is the kernel of an unsigned subset-inclusion matrix, and its integral diagonal form determines all primary summands. Every finite order occurs, with explicit representatives. Generalized theta classes span an embedded copy of the cokernel of the inclusion matrix, containing all torsion; for $r\ge m$ they generate the torsion, each of order $\operatorname{lcm}(1,\ldots,m)$. For every prime power $q$ and $m\ge q$, the graph $K_{m+1,m+q-1}$ is minimal in the minor order for order-$q$ torsion in $H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_m)$. In particular, odd torsion first appears in $H_2(\mathbb{B}_3K_{4,5})\cong\mathbb{Z}^{155}\oplus(\mathbb{Z}/2)^4\oplus\mathbb{Z}/3$, and no proper minor of $K_{4,5}$ has odd torsion in $H_2(\mathbb{B}_3)$. For arbitrary part sizes, we give a multiplicity-free decomposition of $H_m(\mathbb{B}_mK_{a,b};\mathbb{Q})$ under vertex permutations and prove that $H_{m-1}(\mathbb{B}_mK_{a,b};\mathbb{Z})$ has no $p$-primary torsion when $a,b\ge2m-1$ and $p\ge m$ is an odd prime. The explicit order-$q$ class retains its order under every enlargement of the second part of the graph, while for $m=q=p$ an odd prime it is killed by a specified enlargement of the first part.

</details>


### [198] [Geometric Realizations of Capability Spaces of Large Models and Zigzag Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04374)
*Yu-Zhe Liu,Shengda Liu,Jianyong Pi,Chao Zhang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 该文提出用有限正则CW复形的子复形刻画大模型训练中的能力变化，证明单胞粘附只影响相邻维同调，并用zigzag持久模区分检查点、过渡与桥敏感类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型训练中能力"涌现/丧失"缺乏严格的代数刻画，需要把知识状态与预注册能力探针纳入统一拓扑结构，以区分真实能力变化与编码、阈值带来的假象。

Method: 将知识状态与预注册能力探针表示为有限正则CW复形的子复形；证明粘附单个n-胞只会在H_n产生类或在H_{n-1}消灭类；以可靠性阈值与训练检查点为两参数轴构造zigzag持久模，用并/交桥接；以边界矩阵计算四阶段有限算例。

Result: 证明单胞粘附的同调影响局限于相邻两维；训练轴上能力得失使空间序列非嵌套；得到zigzag持久模并区分检查点类、过渡类与桥敏感类；完成四阶段有限例的边界矩阵计算。

Conclusion: 该框架仅在给定编码下记录代数变化，同调变化须经稳健性检验、零模型比较与独立行为验证后方可称为候选能力涌现。

Abstract: We propose a topological model for capability changes during large-model training. The model represents knowledge states and preregistered capability probes as subcomplexes of a finite regular CW complex. We prove that attaching a single $n$-cell can only create a class in $\mathrm H_n$ or kill a class in $\mathrm H_{n-1}$. With the reliability threshold and the training checkpoint as two parameter axes, capability gains and losses make the spaces along the training axis nonnested. Union or intersection bridges produce zigzag persistence modules, and we distinguish checkpoint, transition, and bridge-sensitive classes. A finite four-stage example is computed by boundary matrices. The framework records algebraic changes under a chosen encoding, and a homological change is called a candidate capability emergence only after robustness tests, null-model comparisons, and independent behavioral validation.

</details>


### [199] [Realization of permutation modules as homology of CW-complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04516)
*Yang Bai,Chaoshun Yang,Xiugui Liu*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究将作用于分次模上的群实现为CW复形自同伦等价群（作用于同调群）的可实现性问题，对任意群及集中在特定次数的置换模给出部分解答，并由此完整解决了任意连通性CW复形范畴中Kahn可实现性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究群作用在分次模上能否实现为CW复形自同伦等价群在其同调群上的作用，即同伦论中的可实现性问题；其中Kahn提出的可实现性问题在一般情形下尚未解决，本文旨在推进这一问题的解答。

Method: 针对任意群与集中在特定次数的置换模（permutation modules）情形进行构造性分析，给出该情形下的部分解答；以此为基础推广处理任意连通性的CW复形范畴。

Result: 对任意群和集中于特定次数的置换模给出了可实现在部分情形下的肯定回答；并据此完整解决了任意连通性CW复形范畴中任意群的Kahn可实现性问题。

Conclusion: 置换模与特定次数限制下的构造方法足以刻画可实现性，进而彻底解答了任意连通性CW复形范畴中Kahn的可实现性问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the realizability problem, which asks to realize groups acting on graded modules as the groups of self-homotopy equivalences of CW-complexes acting on their homology groups. We provide a partial answer in the case of arbitrary groups and permutation modules concentrated in certain degrees. As a consequence, we also provide a complete answer to Kahn's realizability problem for arbitrary groups in the categories of CW-complexes with arbitrary connectivity.

</details>


### [200] [Iterated Topological Hochschild Homology of $\mathbb{F}_p$ at odd primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05137)
*Jiaxi Zha*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文计算了奇素数下 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 的 $n$ 重迭代拓扑 Hochschild 同调的同伦群，通过构造 bar 谱序列并证明其在 $E^2$ 页坍塌，最终确定了环结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代拓扑 Hochschild 同调是同伦代数与代数 K 理论中的核心不变量，但其同伦群的具体计算，尤其是对 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 这类基本对象的高重迭代情形，仍缺乏完整的显式描述。本文旨在填补这一计算空白，给出完整的同伦群及环结构。

Method: 将 $n$ 重迭代拓扑 Hochschild 同调等同于 Loday 构造 $T^n\otimes\mathbb{F}_p$，据此构造一个计算 $\pi_*(T^n\otimes\mathbb{F}_p)$ 的 bar 谱序列，证明该谱序列在 $E^2$ 页坍塌，并进一步解决乘法扩张问题。

Result: 对于 $n\geq1$ 与奇素数 $p$，获得了 $\mathbb{F}_p$ 的 $n$ 重迭代拓扑 Hochschild 同调的同伦群；bar 谱序列被证明在 $E^2$ 页坍塌；乘法扩张被完全确定，从而给出了相应的环结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为迭代拓扑 Hochschild 同调的同伦群计算提供了系统方法，其谱序列坍塌与环结构的确定结果可应用于同伦代数及相关计算问题。

Abstract: We compute homotopy groups of $n$-fold iterated topological Hochschild homology of $\mathbb{F}_p$ for $n\geq1$ and odd primes. Identifying this as the Loday construction $T^n\otimes\mathbb{F}_p$, we construct a bar spectral sequence computing $π_*(T^n\otimes\mathbb{F}_p)$ and prove that this spectral sequence collapses at the $E^2$-page. Finally, we resolve the multiplicative extensions and determine the ring structure.

</details>


### [201] [Failure of Ordinary TQFTs to Distinguish Homotopy Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05480)
*Katherine Novey*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究普通TQFT能否区分单连通闭流形的微分同胚类/同伦型，构造了三个bordism范畴中的反例对，证明TQFT无法区分特定流形的同伦型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Reutter与Schommer-Pries已证明在有限性条件下（如π₂有限），普通光滑TQFT可区分单连通闭6维流形的微分同胚类。但对于π₂无限的一般情形，该结论是否成立尚属开放问题，本文旨在解决这一开放问题并探究TQFT的区分能力边界。

Method: 作者考察拓扑与光滑bordism范畴中的TQFT，并新构造了"形式光滑（FS）bordism范畴"。在此框架下构造一对单连通闭6维流形，其π₂无限、不同伦等价，但在三个范畴的普通TQFT下均不可区分；并将反例推广至更高维、非单连通5维及高阶连通情形，同时分析自旋性与非自旋性（通过引入ℂP²连通和）。

Result: 构造出π₂无限的6维反例对，在拓扑、光滑、形式光滑三类bordism范畴中TQFT均无法区分。推广表明：普通TQFT无法区分维数≥6的单连通闭流形及非单连通闭5维流形的同伦型。给出可自旋反例类，并通过引入ℂP²连通和给出非自旋反例类。对任意n≥2、m≥2n，存在(2n-1)-连通闭(4n+m)维流形对，不同伦等价但TQFT无法区分。

Conclusion: 普通TQFT在区分闭流形时的能力存在根本性局限：即使在高连通、多种维数下，不同伦等价的流形仍可能无法被TQFT区分，从而否定了π₂无限情形的自然推广猜想。

Abstract: Work by David Reutter and Christopher Schommer-Pries has shown that ordinary smooth TQFTs can distinguish (stable) diffeomorphism classes of closed, connected, even-dimensional manifolds subject to certain finiteness conditions. In particular, simply connected, closed, smooth 6-manifolds with finite $π_2$ are diffeomorphic if and only if they cannot be distinguished by ordinary TQFTs. The question of whether or not this result holds for all simply connected closed 6-manifolds was open. We consider TQFTs out of the topological and smooth bordism categories, as well as out of a new bordism category constructed in this paper called the formally smooth (FS) bordism category. We present a pair of simply connected closed 6-manifolds with infinite $π_2$ that are not homotopy equivalent, yet are indistinguishable by ordinary TQFTs in all three categories. We extend this counter-example to show that ordinary TQFTs cannot distinguish the homotopy type of simply connected closed manifolds in all dimensions greater than or equal to 6 and non-simply connected closed 5-manifolds. We show that our class of counter-example pairs is spinnable and present a non-spinnable class of counter-examples by introducing a $\mathbb{C}P^2$ connected summand. Lastly, we use more highly connected analogues of our counter-examples to show that, for all $n\geq2$ and $m\geq 2n$, there is a pair of $(2n-1)$-connected closed $(4n+m)$-manifolds that are not homotopy equivalent, yet cannot be distinguished by ordinary TQFTs.

</details>


### [202] [Constant spectral Mackey functors and the equivariant Steenrod algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05654)
*Kailin Pan*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入常值谱$G$-Mackey函子概念，将其与某个$\mathbb{E}_1$-环谱$R_G$上的模范畴等同，并对循环$p$-群$C_{p^n}$给出$R_{C_{p^n}}$的显式构造，进而计算了$\mathbb{Z}$-分次$C_{p^n}$-等变mod $p$ Steenrod代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究真$G$-谱中固定点之间限制映射为等价的一类对象，即常值谱$G$-Mackey函子，并希望通过环谱上的模范畴对其进行代数刻画；同时对循环$p$-群情形给出具体构造，以应用于等变Steenrod代数的计算。

Method: 定义常值谱$G$-Mackey函子为限制映射为等价的真$G$-谱；证明其全子范畴等价于某个$\mathbb{E}_1$-环谱$R_G$上的模范畴。对$G=C_{p^n}$，利用真$C_{p^n}$-谱的重构定理显式构造$R_{C_{p^n}}$。

Result: 将常值谱$G$-Mackey函子的全子范畴识别为$R_G$-模范畴；对循环$p$-群$C_{p^n}$给出$R_{C_{p^n}}$的显式构造，并证明了一个可能具有独立意义的真$C_{p^n}$-谱重构定理；作为应用，计算了$\mathbb{Z}$-分次$C_{p^n}$-等变mod $p$ Steenrod代数。

Conclusion: 常值谱$G$-Mackey函子可由$\mathbb{E}_1$-环谱$R_G$上的模完全刻画，且该框架在循环$p$-群情形下可显式实现，并成功用于等变Steenrod代数的计算。

Abstract: For a finite group $G$, we define a constant spectral $G$-Mackey functor as a genuine $G$-spectrum in which the restrictions between categorical fixed points are equivalences. We further identify the full subcategory of constant spectral $G$-Mackey functors with the category of modules over a certain $\mathbb{E}_1$-ring spectrum $R_G$.
  When $G=C_{p^n}$ is a cyclic $p$-group, we provide an explicit construction of the $\mathbb{E}_1$-ring spectrum $R_{C_{p^n}}$. The proof uses a reconstruction theorem for genuine $C_{p^n}$-spectra, which might be of independent interest. As an application, we compute the $\mathbb{Z}$-graded $C_{p^n}$-equivariant mod $p$ Steenrod algebra.

</details>


### [203] [The Euler characteristic of $Pin^{\pm}$ manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06747)
*Renee S. Hoekzema,Luuk Stehouwer,Simona Veselá*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 完整确定了给定维数闭Pin±流形Euler示性数的可能奇偶性，并部分计算了此前未定的受控切割粘贴群SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin±}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭Pin±流形Euler示性数（进而Kervaire半示性数）的可能取值具有基本重要性，而受控切割粘贴群SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin±}此前尚未确定，需要借助Euler示性数奇偶性的信息来推进其计算。

Method: 通过对给定维数闭Pin±流形的Euler示性数奇偶性进行完整刻画，利用该刻画提供的信息作为工具，应用到受控切割粘贴群的计算中。

Result: 完全确定了任意给定维数闭Pin±流形Euler示性数的所有可能奇偶性；作为应用，部分算出了此前未确定的受控切割粘贴群SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin±}。

Conclusion: Euler示性数奇偶性的分类结果是有效的，可作为工具用于计算受控切割粘贴群等其他拓扑不变量，后续仍需完成SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin±}的完整计算。

Abstract: We completely determine the possible parities of the Euler characteristic of closed $Pin^{\pm}$-manifolds of a given dimension. As an application we partially calculate the controlled cut-and-paste groups $SKK_{2k+1}^{Pin^{\pm}}$, which were previously undetermined.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [TACET: Context-Appropriate Acoustic-Social Navigation for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03828)
*Sungsan Park,Young-Sik Shin,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TACET，一种面向四足机器人的情境自适应声学-社会导航方法，通过视觉语言推理器与反应式控制器耦合，同时决定"走到哪里"和"多大声"，实现空间与声学双重社会合规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人进入医院、养老院、安静办公室等场所，其足地冲击主导的运动噪声本身是一种社会变量。既有社会导航尊重人类空间但将机器人视为声学均质体，而静音运动方法只将噪声降至操作者指定、脱离情境的水平，二者均未联合考虑空间与声学的社会情境适配性。

Method: TACET从机器人第一视角推断社会情境，通过单一紧凑行为token<gait, speed, social_cost>将慢速微调的视觉语言推理器与快速反应式控制器耦合。该token同时调制社会代价地图（决定去哪）与静音运动策略（决定多大声）。采用结构化视野外记忆，使近期出现过的人在离开相机视野后仍保留在推理器上下文中。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上，情境条件化运动在相同速度下最高降低运动噪声9.3 dBA；在测试场景中，完整方法保持100%个人空间合规，且声学侵入低（<=2.9 dBA），在所评估场景中同时提升了空间与声学性能。

Conclusion: TACET验证了将运动噪声视为社会变量、联合优化空间与声学社会合规的可行性，为社交场所中四足机器人的情境适配导航提供了有效方案。项目主页：https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/tacet/。

Abstract: Quadruped robots entering hospitals, care homes, and quiet offices must be context-appropriate not only in where they move but in how loudly they move: a legged robot's locomotion noise, dominated by foot-ground impacts, is itself a social variable. Prior social navigation respects human space but treats the robot as acoustically uniform, while quiet-locomotion methods reduce noise to an operator-specified, context-blind level. We present TACET, a context-appropriate acoustic-social navigation method that infers social context from the robot's egocentric view and decides both where it walks and how loudly, coupling a slow fine-tuned vision-language reasoner to a fast reactive controller through a single compact behavior token, <gait, speed, social_cost>. The same token conditions both a social costmap (where to go) and a quiet locomotion policy (how loudly to move), while a structured out-of-view memory keeps recently seen people in the reasoner's context after they leave the camera view. On a real quadruped, context-conditioned locomotion lowers locomotion noise by up to 9.3 dBA at matched speed, and across our scenarios the full method keeps personal-space compliance at 100% with low acoustic intrusion (<=2.9 dBA), jointly improving spatial and acoustic performance in the evaluated scenarios. The project page is available at https://rcilab.khu.ac.kr/tacet/.

</details>


### [205] [GOTT: Object-centric Dexterous Manipulation with a Reusable Cross-Embodiment Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03861)
*Yulin Liu,Lai Wei,Yen-Jen Wang,Akash Sharma,Pieter Abbeel,Henrik I. Christensen,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOTT提出一个以跨本体接触获取原语为核心的“到达-获取-移动”框架，将操作意图转化为多指机器人行为，在仿真与真实实验中实现跨物体、跨手型的稳健接触获取与任务成功提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与大规模人类数据提供了丰富的操作意图来源，但将这些意图转化为多指机器人行为仍然困难。核心瓶颈在于灵巧手缺乏可复用、可靠的底层原语，难以在不同任务与不同本体（embodiment）间稳定建立接触。

Method: 提出GOTT框架，围绕单一跨本体接触获取原语构建“到达-获取-移动”三阶段流程：(1) 给定与机器人无关的物体轨迹和到达规范，先将手带到任务相关的接触区域附近；(2) 由共享的闭环接触获取原语从该近似初始化出发建立稳定接触；(3) 用位姿条件控制器跟踪期望的物体运动。到达规范可来自未来感知规划、外部模型或人类演示，而原语与跟踪后端保持不变。

Result: 仿真与真实世界实验表明，GOTT能在多样化物体、手臂-手平台以及已见和未见手部形态上建立稳健接触，并相对开环抓取执行持续提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: 通过可复用的跨本体接触获取原语解耦高层意图与低层执行，GOTT为多指灵巧操作提供了统一、可迁移的底层基础，并验证了闭环接触获取相较于开环抓取的优势。

Abstract: Foundation models and large-scale human data provide rich sources of manipulation intent, but translating this intent into multi-fingered robot behavior remains difficult. Dexterous hands still lack a reusable low-level primitive that reliably establishes contact across tasks and embodiments. We propose GOTT, a reach-acquire-move framework built around a single cross-embodiment contact-acquisition primitive. Given a robot-agnostic object trajectory and a reach specification, GOTT first brings the hand near a task-relevant contact region. The shared closed-loop primitive then establishes stable contact from this approximate initialization, and a pose-conditioned controller tracks the desired object motion. Reach specifications may come from future-aware planning, external models, or human demonstrations, while the primitive and tracking backend remain unchanged. Simulation and real-world experiments show that GOTT is able to establish robust contact across diverse objects, arm-hand platforms, and seen and unseen hand morphologies. It also consistently improves end-to-end task success over open-loop grasp execution.

</details>


### [206] [MOSAIC-SV: Real-Time Adaptive Identification of Vessel Dynamics for the Control and Deployment of Aquatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03898)
*Wensen Liu,Jerry Peng,Shravani Vedagiri,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOSAIC-SV：一种可部署的实时自适应水动力辨识与控制系统，从规格书先验出发在线估计动力学模型，无需专门辨识试验，并在闭环控制每一步重估参数；在CyberShip II仿真与BlueBoat实船试验中显著优于冻结先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的水面机器人控制依赖水动力模型，而该模型的辨识成本高，且强依赖于特定船体、载荷与测量工况，泛化性差；因此需要一种无需专门辨识试验、可在任务中实时自适应且控制可用的动力学模型。

Method: MOSAIC-SV由三部分组成：以规格书工程先验为起点，用物理可容许的无迹卡尔曼滤波器在闭环任务每个控制步重估水动力、扰动与执行器参数；采用命令相关的consider投影，对当前命令无法在“执行器效能”与“外力”之间归因的修正加以抑制；由模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器基于当前估计进行规划，实现边规划边在线辨识。

Result: CyberShip II仿真中，在静态失配与瞬态变化下恢复标定模型的航行性能；当惯性或阻尼先验偏差一个数量级时，航行时间仍控制在其未缩放先验下自身航行时间的20%以内。Blue Robotics BlueBoat（双推进器双体船）实水域试验中，相比冻结工程先验，航行至少快25%、自身运动预测误差至少降低56%，且在存在未建模载荷时依然成立。同一系统概念亦在6.3吨双舷外机单体船上完成可行性部署。

Conclusion: 结果表明MOSAIC-SV能够在不做专门辨识试验的前提下，实现控制充分的水动力在线辨识，并在仿真与实船上提升航行速度与预测精度、对先验严重错误与未建模载荷具备鲁棒性，且具备跨平台（含大型船只）推广可行性。

Abstract: Model-based control of an aquatic robotic platform depends on a hydrodynamic model that is costly to identify and specific to the hull, payload, and conditions it was measured in. Here, we present MOSAIC-SV, a deployable real-time adaptive dynamics identification and control system that identifies a control-sufficient dynamics model from a spec-sheet engineering prior, without dedicated identification trials, and re-estimates it at every control step of a closed-loop mission while the controller plans on it. A physically admissible unscented Kalman filter re-estimates hydrodynamic, disturbance, and actuator parameters at every control step of the closed-loop mission; while a command-dependent consider projection withholds corrections the current command cannot attribute between actuator effectiveness and external force; and a model predictive path integral controller plans on the current estimate. In simulation on a CyberShip II plant, MOSAIC-SV recovers the transit performance of the calibrated model under static mismatch and transient changes, and stays within 20% of its own transit time at the unscaled prior when its inertia or damping prior is wrong by an order of magnitude. In on-water field trials on the Blue Robotics BlueBoat, a twin-thruster catamaran, MOSAIC-SV transits at least 25% faster and predicts its own motion with at least 56% less error than its frozen engineering prior, including under an unmodeled payload. The same MOSAIC-SV system concept was also feasibly deployed on a 6.3-tonne dual outboard monohull.

</details>


### [207] [Sparse Calibration-Based Personalization of Kernel-Based Gait Phase and Speed Estimation Using Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03931)
*Myeongju Cha,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏标定的个性化方法，用于可穿戴IMU的步态相位与行走速度联合估计，并通过离线验证与单人在线试点评估其可行性与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群平均核（population-average kernels）难以适配个体差异，导致基于IMU的步态相位与速度联合估计精度受限，因此需要一种低成本、可个性化的核标定方案。

Method: 基于20名受试者的运动数据集，利用双侧大腿与小腿轨迹构建28个速度的参考库；不直接使用人群平均核，而是将三种标定速度下估计的用户特定基线，与以基线为中心的动力学偏差的主成分（PC）模型相结合，生成个性化核。

Result: 离线验证中，个性化核相较人群核降低了相位误差；单人在线试点中，个性化核的平均相位与速度误差在数值上更低，并可在嵌入式硬件上实时运行。但速度效应不显著，且经多重比较校正后，成对相位差异不再显著。

Conclusion: 该试点评估证明了可穿戴实现的可行性，同时揭示了参考库与在线数据集之间的不匹配问题，并指出需要更大规模的队列验证。

Abstract: This paper presents sparse calibration-based personalization for kernel-based gait phase and walking speed co-estimation from wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs). A 28-speed reference library was constructed from bilateral thigh and shank trajectories in a 20-subject locomotion dataset. Rather than directly applying population-average kernels, the method combines a user-specific baseline estimated from three calibration speeds with a principal-component (PC) model of baseline-centered kinematic deviations. Offline validation showed that personalized kernels reduced phase error relative to the population kernel. In a single-participant online pilot evaluation, the personalized kernels produced numerically lower mean phase and speed errors and ran in real time on embedded hardware. However, the speed effect was not significant, and pairwise phase differences did not remain significant after multiple-comparison correction. The pilot evaluation establishes wearable implementation feasibility while indicating reference-to-online dataset mismatch and the need for larger-cohort validation.

</details>


### [208] [REDIRECT: A 1% Fix for Bad Robot Habits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03997)
*Yu Zhang,Jiazhuo Li,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 REDIRECT 方法，仅用完整训练 1% 的样本-反向预算，基于回合级"保留/问题"标签定位坏习惯分支并施加一致性约束，将混合质量机器人数据的成功率从 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复干净重训练差距的 88.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人遥操作数据中，少量缺陷时刻会让机器人习得"坏习惯"，且正常与问题示范大部分行为共享、仅在局部动作延续上不同。完整重训练代价高昂，而仅用干净数据在小更新预算下微调恢复能力有限。因此作者希望把更新预算花在行为差异本身，而非重新学习共享行为。

Method: REDIRECT 仅依赖回合级的"保留/问题"标签，无需帧级标注或额外交互：先定位问题分支，为其原始观测分配一致的保留型动作延续，并对共享行为施加锚定约束，从而在极小更新预算下完成局部修复。

Result: 在三个随机化的 ManiSkill 任务、三个随机种子下，平均成功率由 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复干净重训练差距的 88.8%（同等计算量的微调仅为 22.1%）。在 PiPER 机械臂上的杯子插入与毛巾折叠任务中，恢复了仅用干净数据差距的 86.7%–92.9%。

Conclusion: 局部机器人坏习惯可通过将更新预算用于行为差异而非重学共享行为来修复，仅需完整训练 1% 的样本-反向预算。

Abstract: Robots can acquire bad habits from a few defective moments in otherwise useful teleoperation. In mixed-quality robot data, normal and problematic demonstrations share most task behavior and differ only at a local action continuation. Full retraining is costly, while fine-tuning on clean data alone offers limited recovery under a small update budget. We ask whether the local difference can instead be fixed with only 1% of the full-training sample-backward budget. Using only episode-level retained/problematic labels, REDIRECT localizes the branch, assigns a coherent retained continuation to the original observations around it, and anchors shared behavior, without frame-level annotations or additional interaction. Across three randomized ManiSkill tasks and three seeds, REDIRECT raises the mean success rate from 68.2% to 90.3%, recovering 88.8% of the clean-retraining gap versus 22.1% for matched-compute fine-tuning. On a PiPER arm, it recovers 86.7-92.9% of the clean-only gap across cup insertion and towel folding. Local robot habits can therefore be repaired by spending the update budget on the behavioral difference rather than relearning shared behavior.

</details>


### [209] [SUAVE: Unified Video-Action Models via Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04009)
*Rhythm Syed,Jean Mercat,Sedrick Keh,Kushal Arora,Paarth Shah,Aykut Onol,Mengchao Zhang,Tony Dear*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUAVE：一种基于掩码扩散Transformer的统一动作-视频模型，将语言、视频、动作全部离散化为共享序列的token，通过选择推理时的掩码位置切换世界模型/策略/视频-动作模型功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）语义基础强但只预测动作、不预测未来观测；基于视频扩散的世界动作模型（WAM）能想象未来，但语言仅作为冻结条件保留在连续潜空间。统一模型要么自回归逐token解码，要么保持视频连续并外挂动作头，均存在局限。因此需要一种模态统一、生成与决策兼顾的架构。

Method: 提出SUAVE（Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl）：用单个掩码扩散Transformer，以语言为条件同时生成视频与动作；语言、视频、动作三种模态全部表示为离散token，置于同一共享序列。推理时通过选择掩码哪些token，使同一网络充当世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型。无动作的协同训练中，未标注视频的动作位置填入mask token并从损失中排除。

Result: 1）单个SUAVE模型可预测长时程视频并作为策略，在静态与动态操作任务上媲美专用世界模型和专门动作策略；在真实机器人上，于RTX 5090上1030 ms内生成子目标图像及覆盖1秒运动的动作块，维持2.5次/秒的闭环控制。2）在机器人视频上预训练、在人类视频上协同训练，可显著提升策略性能及对分布偏移的零样本鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明掩码扩散是统一视频-动作建模的一种实用且通用的基础范式，单一模型即可兼顾未来视频预测与动作决策，并受益于跨域视频协同训练。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit strong semantic grounding from pretrained vision-language backbones but are typically optimized for predicting actions rather than future observations. They can see and act, but they do not imagine the future before acting. World action models (WAMs) built on video diffusion backbones can imagine but treat language as frozen conditioning on a continuous latent space. Unified models bring these modalities into one architecture, but they either decode autoregressively, one token at a time, or keep video continuous with an auxiliary action head. In this work, we present SUAVE, a Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl in which a masked diffusion transformer generates video and actions conditioned on language, with all three modalities represented as discrete tokens in a shared sequence. Choosing which tokens to mask at inference turns the same network into a world model, a robot policy, or a video-action model. For action-free co-training, the action positions of unlabeled video are filled with mask tokens and excluded from the loss. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate two findings. First, a single SUAVE model predicts long-horizon video and acts as a policy, competitive with dedicated world models and specialized action policies on static and dynamic manipulation tasks. On a real robot, our model generates subgoal images and an action chunk spanning one second of motion in 1,030 ms on an RTX 5090 GPU, sustaining closed-loop control at 2.5 actions per second. Second, pretraining on robot video and co-training on human video substantially improves policy performance and zero-shot robustness to distribution shift. Together, these results show that masked diffusion is a practical and versatile foundation for unified video-action modeling.

</details>


### [210] [Return-to-Home Feasible Micro-Aerial Vehicle Exploration for 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04013)
*Prajit Krisshnakumar,Fan Yang,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"拓扑优先"的主动重建框架，将高保真渲染与导航解耦：用稠密3DGS作为重建目标，用轻量TSDF占据栅格构建稀疏三维Voronoi骨架路图并做保守碰撞检测；通过挖空自由空间一致性与稠密可见性校验节点/边，抑制墙体后方幻影结构；基于飞行时间预算的目标函数与轻量滚动时域前瞻选择视点，在ReplicaCAD/Gibson/HM3D上验证覆盖度、误差、返航成功率与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型飞行器（MAV）可用于室内快速三维重建，但飞行时间预算严格且需保证安全，要求显式、带保守余量的返航（RTH）可行性。现有GS-based主动重建方法在在线噪声与不完整融合下难以直接从TSDF几何提取稳定路图，易产生穿墙幻影结构，导航规划不稳定。

Method: 1) 解耦式架构：稠密3D Gaussian Splatting地图作为重建目标，轻量TSDF/占据栅格作为碰撞检测与骨架路图支撑。2) 在线构建稀疏三维Voronoi骨架路图。3) 节点与边的校验：利用挖空自由空间一致性（carved free-space consistency）与稠密可见性检查，剔除墙体后方的幻影结构，稳定规划。4) 视点选择：在路图上使用飞行时间预算感知的目标函数，并引入轻量滚动时域（receding-horizon）前瞻以改善非短视探索。

Result: 在ReplicaCAD、Gibson、HM3D逼真室内仿真基准上评估，报告重建覆盖度、重建误差、RTH成功率与计算开销；在相同飞行时间预算下，相较近期GS-based基线取得更优或相当的性能。

Conclusion: 拓扑优先、渲染与导航解耦的主动重建框架可在严格飞行时间预算与安全约束下实现稳定规划与高质量重建，路图校验机制有效抑制在线融合噪声导致的幻影结构，具备与现有GS方法竞争或更优的表现。

Abstract: Micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) enable rapid indoor 3D reconstruction for inspection and time-critical situational awareness, but must operate under strict flight-time budgets and safety constraints that require explicit return-to-home (RTH) feasibility with a conservative margin. We present a topology-first active reconstruction framework that decouples high-fidelity rendering from navigation. A dense 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map is optimized as the reconstruction target, while a lightweight Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF)/occupancy scaffold supports conservative collision checking and online construction of a sparse 3D Voronoi skeleton roadmap. Since directly extracting roadmaps from TSDF geometry can be unstable under noisy and incomplete online fusion, we validate nodes and edges using carved free-space consistency and dense visibility checks, which suppress behind-wall phantom structure and stabilize planning. Viewpoints are selected on the roadmap using a flight-time-budget-aware objective and a lightweight receding-horizon lookahead to improve non-myopic exploration behavior. We evaluate on photorealistic indoor simulation benchmarks (ReplicaCAD, Gibson, and HM3D), reporting reconstruction coverage/error, RTH success, and computational cost. Results demonstrate improved or competitive performance relative to recent GS-based baselines under identical flight-time budgets.

</details>


### [211] [A Biomimetic Gaze Control to Restore Head-Neck Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04022)
*Colin Rubow,Haohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于前庭眼反射（VOR）的眼动追踪控制器，用于颈部外骨骼，并设计个性化调节工具，实现自然且个性化的头部运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颈部外骨骼多依赖手持输入设备，操作不直观且对颈部肌肉无力人群不可行；而已有眼动控制模型缺乏生物行为学依据和个性化手段，导致头颈运动不自然。

Method: 利用前庭眼反射（VOR）模拟自然的眼-头协调机制，设计新型眼动追踪控制器；同时开发个性化工具，通过滑块让用户调整控制器参数；并在人类用户中开展技术设计与验证。

Result: 相比此前的眼动追踪模型，新控制器产生更自然的速度曲线，并使准确运动意图检测平均提升24%，且头部运动可个性化适配用户。

Conclusion: 该系统为研究眼动控制器的个性化与偏好提供了有力平台，有望最终帮助患者恢复失去的头颈活动能力。

Abstract: Powered neck exoskeletons are developed to restore head-neck motions for those with neck muscle weakness. Current methods using hand-held input devices to control these robotic systems are unintuitive or non-viable for these populations. Alternatively, the eyes coordinate with the head to accomplish many daily tasks. Therefore, movements of the eyes can be a useful signal to control the neck exoskeletons without needing to use the hands. However, previous control models lack biobehavioral basis as well as means to personalize the control behavior, causing unnatural head-neck movements. This paper presents a novel eye-tracking controller design leveraging the vestibulo-ocular reflex to emulate natural eyehead coordination. Personalization tools were also designed, consisting of sliders to allow users to adjust the parameters of this controller. Here, we present the technical design and validation of this system in human users. Compared to the previous eye-tracking model, the new controller provides natural head motions personalized to the user through more natural velocity profiles and a 24 percent average increase of accurate motion intent detection. This system provides a powerful platform for studying eye-tracking controllers in the context of personalization and preference, and ultimately restoring the lost head-neck mobility in patient populations.

</details>


### [212] [Reward-DAgger: Robot-Gated Interactive Imitation Learning with General-Purpose Progress-Based Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04054)
*Ryan Li,Yigit Korkmaz,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Reward-DAgger，一种基于通用奖励模型稠密进度信号的机器人门控交互式模仿学习框架，无需任务/策略特定训练或超参调整，在八项仿真与真实任务中提升自主成功率并取得优于基线的失败检测精度-延迟权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在未见环境中性能下降，因此需检测失败并教授恢复行为；但现有运行时监控方法通常依赖任务与策略特定的训练或超参调优，难以跨任务部署，并在策略迭代更新时带来额外开销。

Method: Reward-DAgger采用机器人门控（robot-gated）交互式模仿学习框架，利用通用奖励模型提供的稠密进度信号判断何时需要人类干预；该方法与底层策略架构无关，不需要访问策略内部信息，且门控机制可跨任务使用而无需重新调参。

Result: 相比现有运行时监控基线，Reward-DAgger取得更优的失败检测精度-延迟权衡；在八个仿真与真实任务中，交互学习过程中持续提升下游策略的自主成功率，并实现较高的人类投入回报，多数设置下优于基线；同一门控配置无需任务特定超参调优即可跨任务、环境与策略架构迁移。

Conclusion: Reward-DAgger以通用奖励模型的稠密进度信号实现可迁移、低开销的干预门控，兼顾失败检测效果与人类投入效率，适用于跨任务、跨环境、跨策略架构的交互式模仿学习部署。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled generalist control policies capable of completing a wide range of tasks. However, their performance degrades when deployed in unseen environments, making it critical to detect failures and teach recovery behaviors.
