In lab flights the external pose is accurate to 12-16 mm, enough to fly the drone autonomously within 2-5 cm of motion-capture-fed control. Having the quadruped actively follow the drone reduces the tracking error from 11.0 cm to 6.9 cm by holding the camera in the close range, where the markers are most accurate.

</details>


### [320] [AUTOPILOT An Advanced Perception, Localization and Path Planning Techniques for Autonomous Vehicles Using YOLOv7 and MiDaS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06232)
*Harshkumar Devmurari,Gautham Kuckian,Prajjwal Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文结合YOLO目标检测、MiDaS深度估计、透视变换与决策路径规划，构建自动驾驶感知与定位系统，并通过仿真与实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶汽车是能够变革交通与出行的可靠技术，但其发展需要在感知、定位、决策与控制等多个领域取得显著进展。本文旨在探索一种创新的障碍物感知与定位方案，推动自动驾驶技术进步。

Method: 将YOLO目标检测、MiDaS深度感知用于障碍物的定位与感知，结合透视变换与路径规划决策，形成组合式系统；并通过仿真和实验对系统的性能进行评估。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明，YOLO与MiDaS的组合为目标检测与深度感知提供了一种新的鲁棒系统。

Conclusion: 本研究为自动驾驶技术发展做出贡献，并为环境中障碍物的感知与定位提供了新的创新性方法。

Abstract: Self driving vehicles have emerged as a reliable technology that has the capability to transform transportation and mobility. The development of self driving cars requires significant advances in a number of areas, including perception, localization, decision making, and control. This research paper is based on the project implementation of the combination of object detection using YOLO (You Only Look Once), depth sensing using MiDaS for the localization and perception of obstacles, perspective transform, and decision making for path planning in self driving cars. The contemporary state of the technology for object detection, depth sensing, localization, and path planning evaluates the performance of the combined system through simulations and experiments. The results show that the combination of YOLO and MiDaS provides a new robust system for object detection and depth sensing. This research paper contributes to the advancement of self driving car technology and provides new and innovative approaches to the perception and localization of obstacles in the environment. Keywords: YOLO, MiDaS, perception, localization, decision making

</details>


### [321] [Encoded but Not in Control: Revealing the Grounding Gap in Vision-Language Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06235)
*Shaohan Jiang,Jiahang Cao,Qiduo He,Fengting Deng,Kun Wu,Jingkai Sun,Jiaxu Wang,Qiang Zhang,Qihao Zheng,Chunfeng Song,Ping Luo,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过"场景保持的指令干预"实验，揭示VLA/WAM策略的任务成功率会掩盖其真正是否遵循指令，并指出编码的语言信息未能控制动作的"grounding gap"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件机器人策略的核心在于：当同一场景允许多种有效动作时，应由语言决定执行哪个动作。然而仅凭成功执行无法判定策略是在遵循指令，还是直接从场景推断任务，这一歧义此前缺乏系统研究。

Method: 提出场景保持的指令干预（scene-preserving instruction interventions）：固定场景，仅替换为有效目标、任意名词或无关句子。在仿真与真实世界中对VLA策略和世界动作模型（WAMs）进行评估，并结合逐层动作透镜（layerwise action lens）、线性探针、注意力分析、UMAP与共享非负矩阵分解进行表征诊断。

Result: (a) 任务成功不等于遵循指令：当指令要求另一可见物体时，所有被测策略仍主要接近并抓取场景关联的原目标；(b) 失败并非语言不敏感：指令扰动会影响任务表现，中间动作预测对扰动有响应，线性探针能准确恢复被指令目标，说明修改后的指令已被编码；(c) 编码语言难以控制动作：指令token对动作生成的贡献微弱，目标token注意力仍锁定原物体，目标信息虽可获取但表征日益按场景身份组织，暴露出目标编码与视觉grounding之间的错配。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了被名义成功率掩盖的grounding gap，提供了诊断框架，并提出衡量进展的具体标准：策略应可靠跟随用户意图的有效变化，即使这些变化与场景偏好行为相冲突。

Abstract: Instruction following is central to language-conditioned robot policies: language should determine what to do when the same scene permits multiple valid actions. Yet successful execution alone cannot establish whether a policy follows the instruction or infers the task from the scene. We study this ambiguity through scene-preserving instruction interventions, using valid target substitutions, arbitrary nouns, and unrelated sentences while holding the scene fixed. We evaluate vision-language-action (VLA) policies and world-action models (WAMs) in simulation and in real-world experiments. Our analysis addresses three questions: (a) Does task success imply instruction following? When instructions request a different visible object, all evaluated policies predominantly approach and pick up the incorrect original target associated with the scene. (b) Is this failure caused by language insensitivity? Instruction perturbations affect task performance. A layerwise action lens shows intermediate action predictions respond to these perturbations. Linear probes accurately recover instructed targets, indicating modified instructions are encoded despite rarely determining target selection. (c) Why does encoded language fail to control action? Attention analysis indicates weak instruction-token contributions to action generation. Target-token attention can remain focused on the original object, revealing a mismatch between target encoding and visual grounding. UMAP and shared non-negative matrix factorization show target information remains accessible within representations increasingly organized by scene identity. Our findings expose a grounding gap concealed by nominal success and provide a diagnostic framework. They further establish a concrete criterion for progress: policies should reliably follow valid changes in user intent, even when they conflict with scene-favored behavior.

</details>


### [322] [VLA-ZO: Fast Zeroth-Order Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06271)
*Jaemin Kim,Jiahn Kim,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ZO 通过将零阶适配限制在动作侧并复用冻结的视觉-语言前缀状态，实现了 VLA 模型的快速零阶适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署时的分布偏移需要 VLA 模型适配，但一阶适配超出推理平台的内存预算；零阶优化虽内存友好，却因需要大量扰动查询而对大型 VLA 模型过于缓慢。

Method: VLA-ZO 利用 VLA 计算结构：将适配限制在动作侧，冻结昂贵的视觉-语言前缀，并在多次扰动查询与优化器步之间复用其条件状态；同时采用调度感知预取隐藏状态传输开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 相机视角偏移下，VLA-ZO 相比基线 ZO 端到端适配时间在 q=16 时减少 25.59 倍，q=64 时减少 32.54 倍；平均任务成功率从无适配的 48.27% 提升至 58.17% 和 63.58%。

Conclusion: 加速零阶优化可使更大的查询预算变得可行，为在部署平台上进行资源高效的 VLA 适配提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language-action (VLA) models to deployment-time distribution shifts is important for reliable robotic operation, but conventional first-order adaptation can exceed the memory budget of inference-oriented deployment platforms. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a forward-only alternative with inference-level memory, but accurate gradient estimation requires many perturbation queries, making naive ZO prohibitively slow for large VLA models. We present VLA-ZO, a framework for fast ZO adaptation that exploits the structure of VLA computation. By confining adaptation to the action side, VLA-ZO keeps the expensive vision-language prefix frozen and reuses its conditioning states across perturbation queries and optimizer steps, while schedule-aware prefetching hides state-transfer overhead. On LIBERO camera-viewpoint shifts, VLA-ZO reduces end-to-end adaptation time by 25.59$\times$ at $q=16$ and 32.54$\times$ at $q=64$ relative to baseline ZO, while improving average task success from 48.27% without adaptation to 58.17% and 63.58%, respectively. These results show that making ZO faster can make larger query budgets practical, providing a promising path toward resource-efficient VLA adaptation on deployment platforms.

</details>


### [323] [Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06280)
*Zhibin Qin,Zhenxiong Tan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAVOR框架，利用WAM预测的未来帧作为锚点进行偏差验证与在线恢复，在LIBERO上从97.85%提升至98.10%，LIBERO-Plus上从72.60%提升至72.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）先预测任务执行过程再解码动作，但一旦实际执行偏离预测，剩余动作即失效；从已分布外（OOD）的状态重新规划难以恢复任务所需，现有执行监控器只能判断何时停止，无法决定如何恢复。

Method: FAVOR（Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery）：保留WAM在行动前预测的未来帧作为锚点。Anchor Verifier将每次观测与其锚点及已执行动作对比，标记破坏任务的偏差；Anchor-Guided Recovery用视觉语言模型将标记的锚点转化为简短纠正指令，在强化指令引导下由WAM执行该指令以回到预期未来，随后恢复任务。

Result: 在不修改策略的前提下，将基础WAM的任务成功率在LIBERO上从97.85%提升至98.10%，在LIBERO-Plus上从72.60%提升至72.98%。

Conclusion: FAVOR是一种轻量框架，验证与恢复均以WAM自身预测的未来帧为锚点，无需修改策略即可提升机器人操作任务的成功率。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. They act by first predicting how a task should be performed and then decoding the actions from that future. However, the remaining actions are invalid once execution drifts from the prediction. Simply replanning from the already out of distribution state rarely restores what the task still requires; existing execution monitors decide when to stop, but not what to restore. We observe that the answer is already in hand: the future the WAM predicted before acting depicts exactly the states it intended to pass through. We introduce FAVOR (Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery), a lightweight framework that keeps these predicted frames as anchors and uses them for verification and recovery. An Anchor Verifier compares each observation with its anchor, together with the executed actions, to flag deviations that break the task. Anchor-Guided Recovery uses a vision-language model to turn the flagged anchor into a short corrective instruction. Under strengthened instruction guidance, the WAM executes this instruction to return to the intended future. It then resumes the task. FAVOR raises the task success of the base WAM from 97.85% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 72.60% to 72.98% on LIBERO-Plus without modifying the policy.

</details>


### [324] [GAMBIT: Learning to Plan Continuous Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06290)
*Rishabh Jain,Akmaral Moldagalieva,Lorenzo Magnino,Michael Amir,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Wolfgang Hönig,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMBIT框架，通过模仿学习加强化学习微调来学习多机器人运动基元选择的协调启发式策略，并引入带备用轨迹的安全回滚机制保证全程无碰撞，在超千个机器人的连续域中实现毫秒级规划延迟并显著优于中心化与去中心化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协调中，个体常需放弃局部最优行为以提升团队整体表现（类似国际象棋中的"弃子"策略）。这类自我牺牲行为在密集、交互频繁的环境中难以用人工设计的启发式规则刻画。

Method: 聚焦双积分器连续动力学，研究如何在运动基元层面学习协调启发式。框架分两阶段：先通过模仿学习学习协调的运动基元选择，再用强化学习对策略进行微调；同时引入带备用轨迹的安全回滚机制，保证任意时刻执行均无碰撞。

Result: GAMBIT显著优于包括中心化运动规划器和去中心化反应式规划器在内的多种基线，并展现出良好的可扩展性，可协调超过一千个机器人在连续域中运行，规划延迟低于数百毫秒。

Conclusion: 通过运动基元层面的模仿学习与强化学习相结合，并配合安全保障回滚机制，能够有效习得难以人工设计的自我牺牲式协调策略，兼顾性能、可扩展性与执行安全性。

Abstract: GAMBIT is an opening chess move in which a player sacrifices a piece, typically a pawn, to gain a positional advantage later in the game. Analogously, in multi-robot coordination, individual robots may need to forgo locally reward-maximising behaviours to improve overall team performance. Such self-sacrificial behaviours are difficult to capture with manually designed heuristics, particularly in dense, interaction-rich environments. Focusing on double-integrator continuous dynamics, this work studies how to learn such coordinated heuristics over motion primitives for multi-robot trajectory execution. Our framework, GAMBIT, first learns coordinated motion-primitive selection through imitation learning and subsequently fine-tunes the policy through reinforcement learning. We further introduce a safeguarded rollout mechanism with backup trajectories that guarantees collision-free execution at all times. Experiments demonstrate that GAMBIT substantially outperforms a range of baselines, including centralised motion planners and decentralised reactive planners, while exhibiting strong scalability. In particular, it coordinates over a thousand robots with planning latency below a few hundred milliseconds in continuous domains.

</details>


### [325] [Inspect Robots: Evaluating the Capabilities and Safety of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06306)
*Christopher Leet,Achu Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sabrina Zou,Aayushya Patel,Aditya Kumar Singh,Anish Kr Singh,Galaba Vamsi,Javin Ahuja,Sai Asish Yamani,Tushar Anand,Vedang Alle,Zihan Jack Zhang,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 Inspect Robots——一个用于具身智能体评估的模块化开源框架，支持物理评估的定制化抽象、结果分析及自动化评测流程，并用于评测六种基于前沿语言模型的策略的能力与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型日益具备控制机器人硬件的能力，因此理解这些模型在具身情境下的能力与安全性对其社会影响和风险评估愈发重要，而目前缺乏系统化的评估工具。

Method: 提出 Inspect Robots 框架：提供可定制、可复用的抽象接口用于定义物理评估任务与结果分析，并配套自动化评估的配置、执行与终止基础设施；使用该框架对六种基于前沿语言模型的策略进行能力与安全性评估。

Result: 成功演示了 Inspect Robots 对六种前沿语言模型策略的能力与安全性评测；框架获得显著早期采用，发布三个月内下载量近 10 万次。

Conclusion: Inspect Robots 为具身智能体的评估提供了模块化、开源且可自动化的解决方案，具有良好的实用性与社区认可度，有助于推动具身模型能力与安全性研究。

Abstract: General purpose language models are increasingly able to control robotic hardware. Understanding the capabilities and safety of these models when embodied is therefore increasingly important for understanding their societal impact and risks. To this end, we introduce Inspect Robots, a modular, open-source framework for developing and running evaluations of embodied agents. Inspect Robots pairs customizable, reusable abstractions for specifying physical evaluations and analyzing their results with infrastructure that automates evaluation setup, execution and termination. We demonstrate Inspect Robots by using it to evaluate the capabilities and safety of six policies based on frontier language models. Inspect Robots has seen significant early uptake, receiving nearly 100,000 downloads in the three months since its release.

</details>


### [326] [Dual Variational Autoencoders for Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer in Low-Cost Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06327)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合域随机化与特征级域自适应的混合迁移学习框架，利用共享解码器的双卷积变分自编码器对齐仿真与真实域分布，仅需少量真实样本即可实现低成本机器人视觉导航策略的sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人的视觉自主导航面临仿真训练环境与真实世界条件之间的显著差异：直接迁移仿真策略往往失效，而仅用真实数据训练又不现实，因此需要一种能有效弥合sim-to-real鸿沟的方法。

Method: 提出混合迁移学习框架，将域随机化与特征级域自适应相结合。采用带共享解码器的双卷积变分自编码器架构，在45225张仿真图像与仅4556张真实样本上训练，学习紧凑的公共潜表示空间以对齐两个域的分布；并辅以两种互补的数据增强技术扩充有限的真实数据。

Result: 在真实室内导航图像分类任务上平均成功率达到约91%，显著优于仅仿真训练和仅真实数据训练的方法。通过真实世界直接部署验证，所提策略成功引导低成本机器人完成反应式探索任务；计算量估算结果确认该模型适用于Raspberry Pi 4与NVIDIA Jetson Nano等资源受限嵌入式平台。

Conclusion: 该工作为低成本机器人系统开发高效、实用的导航策略提供了可行方案，验证了混合迁移学习在弥合sim-to-real鸿沟方面的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-based autonomous navigation for low-cost robots remains a fundamental challenge, primarily due to the significant gap between simulated training environments and real-world operational conditions. Direct policy transfer from simulation is often ineffective, while training exclusively on real data is impractical. We propose a hybrid transfer learning framework that effectively bridges the sim-to-real gap by combining domain randomization with feature-level domain adaptation. Our method employs a dual convolutional variational autoencoder architecture with a shared decoder, trained on an extensive set of 45225 simulated images and a minimal set of only 4556 real-world samples. This architecture learns a compact, common latent representation space that aligns the distributions of both domains. The adaptation process is further enhanced by two complementary data augmentation techniques designed to expand the limited real-world data. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that our method achieves an average success rate of almost 91% on image classification tasks for real-world indoor navigation, significantly outperforming both simulation-only and real-world-only training. We validate these findings through a direct, real-world deployment, where the proposed policy successfully guides a low-cost robot in a reactive exploration task. Furthermore, we validate the model's efficiency through a rigorous computational estimation, confirming its suitability for resource-constrained embedded platforms such as the Raspberry Pi 4 and NVIDIA Jetson Nano. This work presents a practical solution for developing effective and efficient navigation policies for low-cost robotic systems.

</details>


### [327] [DexForge: High-Fidelity Physics-Informed Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06331)
*Meizhong Wang,Kun Cao,Ruiqi Ni,Lihua Xie,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexForge框架，将人类视频演示通过可微物理仿真转换为高保真机器人灵巧操作轨迹，成功率较基线提升约35-53个百分点，并显著降低物体位姿跟踪误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是灵巧操作的优质训练数据，但在物理约束下跨机器人本体高保真复现演示动作及手-物交互仍然困难。现有方法难以兼顾精确接触与物理可行性。

Method: DexForge是一个可微、基于物理的框架：先从视觉观测中重建球面高斯（spherical-Gaussian）物体模型与手-物运动；再构建结合高效高斯碰撞检测与现有可微动力学的可微仿真器；在此仿真器上，将接触感知的运动学重定向（contact-aware kinematic retargeting）与力感知的动力学重定向（force-aware dynamics retargeting）相结合——以机器人适配的稳定接触引导运动学参考构建，并通过基于梯度的控制优化实现精确的物理运动复现。

Result: 在130个DexYCB与HOT3D演示、七种灵巧手上的实验中，成功率较基线提升约35-53个百分点；成功轨迹上的物体位置与姿态跟踪误差分别降低约34-67%与71-78%。进一步实验验证了向MuJoCo的开环迁移与真机执行。

Conclusion: DexForge能够有效弥合人类演示与机器人灵巧操作之间的鸿沟，实现跨本体的高保真轨迹转换，并通过真机验证了其可行性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer rich examples of precise dexterous manipulation and a promising source of robot training data. However, high-fidelity reproduction of demonstrated motions and hand-object interactions across robot embodiments remains challenging under physical constraints. We present DexForge, a differentiable physics-grounded framework for converting human video demonstrations into high-fidelity robot trajectories. We reconstruct spherical-Gaussian object models and hand-object motion from visual observations, then build a differentiable simulator combining efficient Gaussian collision detection with existing differentiable dynamics. Based on this simulator, DexForge combines contact-aware kinematic retargeting with force-aware dynamics retargeting: robot-adapted stable contacts guide kinematic reference construction and subsequent gradient-based control refinement for precise physical motion reproduction. Experiments on 130 DexYCB and HOT3D demonstrations across seven dexterous hands show success-rate gains of approximately 35-53 percentage points over the baseline, with object position and orientation tracking errors on successful trajectories reduced by approximately 34-67% and 71-78%, respectively. Further experiments demonstrate open-loop transfer to MuJoCo and real-robot execution. Our project page is available at https://wmz1226.github.io/DexForge/

</details>


### [328] [Towards Robust Prehensile Manipulation in Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06376)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用质量-多样性（QD）算法解决开放环境中机器人抓取操控任务，可高效发现大量鲁棒抓取配置，作为在铰接物体上生成多样操控轨迹的可靠起点，从而提升泛化性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放、持续演化的真实环境中，机器人操控任务需要应对大量未知与变化的物体及场景。传统优化方法往往只收敛到单一最优解，难以提供足够多样的抓取与操控策略，导致泛化性和适应性不足，因此需要一种能同时追求质量与多样性的求解思路。

Method: 采用质量-多样性（QD）算法进行求解：首先在开放环境中高效搜索并发现大量鲁棒的抓取配置，将其作为可靠起始点；随后基于这些抓取配置，在铰接物体上生成多样化的抓取操控轨迹，从而构建覆盖广泛行为空间的策略集合。

Result: 方法能够高效地发现范围广泛且鲁棒的抓取配置，并以此为起点生成多样的操控轨迹；所获得的行为多样性有效提升了操控策略的泛化能力与适应性，支持在持续演化的开放世界环境中部署。

Conclusion: 将质量-多样性算法应用于机器人抓取操控，是应对开放、动态环境中泛化与适应需求的有效途径；以多样且鲁棒的抓取配置为基础，可系统性地生成多样操控行为，为开放世界部署提供支撑。

Abstract: We propose to use Quality-Diversity (QD) algorithms to solve robotic prehensile manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. Our approach enables the efficient discovery of a wide range robust grasp configurations, which serve as reliable starting points for generating diverse prehensile manipulation trajectories on articulated objects. The resulting diversity in manipulation behaviors enhances generalization and adaptability, enabling effective deployment continuously evolving open-world settings.

</details>


### [329] [Visual Swarm Navigation via Deep Reinforcement Learning and Evolutionary Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06400)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AI驱动的混合方法自动合成群体机器人视觉导航控制器，结合多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略（CEM与CMA-ES），在低成本单目视觉平台上实现高效、可扩展的集体探索，覆盖提升且能耗显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人在搜索救援、环境监测等复杂动态场景中具备鲁棒、低成本的自动化潜力，但核心难题在于以数据驱动方式设计去中心化控制器，以产生涌现式集体行为。同时，实际部署要求控制器在资源受限、低成本平台上保持计算与能效。

Method: 采用多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略协同的混合方法：先预训练单体导航策略，再分别用交叉熵方法（CEM）和协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）进行优化。深层架构设计面向低成本、资源受限平台，采用仅依赖单目相机图像的紧凑神经网络，强调计算与能效。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真器中的实验表明，所得控制器可实现多种室内环境中鲁棒、可扩展的集体探索。CEM训练的控制器探索覆盖更优，比CMA-ES多访问36.20%的区域；最佳视觉策略的探索性能与传统依赖昂贵测距传感器的方法在统计上相当，同时平均能耗降低31.40%。

Conclusion: 研究验证了一种有效且经济可行的自主控制系统，为在真实工程应用中部署高效群体智能提供了路径。视觉-only、低能耗的控制器可在性能上与测距传感器方案相当，显示出实际部署的可行性与经济性。

Abstract: Swarm robotics presents a robust and cost-effective paradigm for advanced automation in complex, dynamic environments, such as those encountered in search and rescue or environmental monitoring. A fundamental challenge for this field is the data-driven design of decentralized controllers capable of generating emergent collective behaviors. This paper proposes a novel, AI-driven hybrid methodology for the automatic synthesis of swarm robotic controllers for autonomous visual navigation. This approach synergistically combines multi-agent reinforcement learning with neuro-evolutionary strategies, specifically leveraging implementations of the cross-entropy method and the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy to optimize a pre-trained individual navigation policy. The underlying deep architecture is engineered for low-cost, resource-constrained platforms, utilizing a compact neural network that relies exclusively on monocular camera imagery. This vision-based design emphasizes computational and energy efficiency, a critical requirement for practical swarm deployments. Experiments, performed in a high-fidelity physics simulator, demonstrate that the resulting controllers enable robust and scalable collective exploration of diverse indoor environments. The controller trained using our cross-entropy method achieves superior exploration coverage, visiting 36.20% more regions compared to the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy. Critically, our best vision-based policy achieves exploration performance statistically comparable to traditional methods relying on more expensive distance sensors, while delivering a significant 31.40% average reduction in energy consumption. These findings validate an effective and economically viable autonomous control system, establishing a path for deploying highly efficient collective intelligence in real-world engineering applications.

</details>


### [330] [A Taxonomy on Collective Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06427)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martín Molina,Ricardo Sanz,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文澄清多机器人系统中Mutual Awareness、Shared Situational Awareness与Team Situational Awareness三个概念，指出它们构成包含层级与分布谱系，而非同义词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人团队在动态非结构化环境中执行复杂任务时，需要智能体对自身环境、队友与任务状态维持准确且对齐的表征。然而文献中MA、SSA、TSA常被混用，掩盖了各概念对协调的不同要求。

Method: 提出一个二维分类法，分别以“意识对象”和“意识分布”为轴，对三个概念进行定位。MA为完全个体化理解，SSA为完全统一理解，TSA处于混合位置。并通过异构空中与地面机器人的搜救案例研究，将每个分类位置对应到具体协调需求。

Result: 三个概念构成包含层级，每一类在范围上涵盖前一类；同时形成一个从个体化到统一化的分布谱系。将其等同会遮蔽系统所需的具体协调要求。搜救案例验证了各分类位置对应不同的协调要求。

Conclusion: 该研究为多机器人系统中集体意识的原则化规范、设计与比较提供了概念基础。

Abstract: As robotic teams tackle increasingly complex tasks in dynamic and unstructured environments, effective coordination requires agents to maintain accurate, aligned representations of their environment, teammates, and mission state. We argue that Mutual Awareness, Shared Situational Awareness, and Team Situational Awareness --- three concepts widely invoked in the multi-robot systems literature --- are not interchangeable: they form a containment hierarchy in which each type subsumes the previous in scope, and span a distribution spectrum from fully individualized understanding (MA) to fully uniform understanding (SSA), with TSA occupying a mixed position. We establish this through a two-dimensional taxonomy organized along the object of awareness and awareness distribution axes. Treating these terms as synonyms obscures the precise coordination requirements each imposes on a robotic system. A Search and Rescue case study with heterogeneous aerial and ground robots grounds each taxonomy position in concrete coordination requirements. These findings provide a conceptual foundation for principled specification, design, and comparison of collective awareness in multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [331] [Odyssey: A Closed-Loop Benchmark for Long-Horizon Real-World Driving with Explicit Navigation Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06469)
*Jungho Kim,Hongjae Shin,Seunghoon Yu,Heecheol Yoo,Myeongjun Kim,Jiyong Oh,Donghyuk Kwak,Seunghyeop Nam,Haesung Oh,Hyunju Kim,Hyungchan Cho,Jaehyun Park,Soo Won Seo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Odyssey——面向长时程端到端驾驶的闭环基准，含100个100秒nuPlan场景，用SD地图路线替代方向指令统一导航目标，并引入路线遵循度与变道准备度新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶基准仅评估短片段，无法揭示早期决策对后续驾驶的长期影响；同时模糊的方向指令掩盖了真实导航目标，导致导航意图难以评估。

Method: 构建含100个场景的闭环长时程基准，每个场景由100秒nuPlan日志重建以保留导航与交通交互上下文；以显式SD地图路线替代方向指令作为导航目标，局部动作仍由基于传感器的规划决定；对3DGS渲染图像进行扩散式精修以减少自车轨迹上的渲染伪影；提出SD Route Compliance与Pre-Lane Change Score指标，并扩展出带路线偏离与变道准备失败惩罚的RouteDS；适配VLA等先进规划器进行评测。

Result: 在长时程闭环rollout下，各先进规划器（含VLA模型）在路线遵循与变道准备方面暴露出性能差异与不足；扩散精修有效降低了渲染伪影。

Conclusion: Odyssey揭示了端到端驾驶中路线表征与集成方面的开放问题，为长时程闭环导航评估提供了新基准与指标，代码和适配基线将公开。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of end-to-end driving requires continuous rollouts that reveal how earlier decisions affect subsequent driving. However, existing benchmarks evaluate only short segments and fail to capture later consequences. Ambiguous directional commands also obscure the intended navigation objective. We introduce Odyssey, a closed-loop benchmark for long-horizon driving comprising 100 scenarios, each reconstructed from a 100-second nuPlan driving log to preserve the context of navigation maneuvers and traffic interactions. To provide a consistent navigation objective, Odyssey replaces directional commands with explicit standard-definition (SD) map routes that specify which roads to follow, while sensor-based planning determines local driving actions. Throughout these rollouts, diffusion-based refinement of 3DGS-rendered images reduces rendering artifacts along the ego trajectory. To assess how effectively planners follow these routes and prepare for upcoming maneuvers, we introduce SD Route Compliance and Pre-Lane Change Score. These assessments are complemented by RouteDS, which extends the Driving Score with penalties for SD-route deviations and failed lane preparation. We adapt state-of-the-art planners, including vision-language-action (VLA) models, and evaluate their navigation performance using these metrics. Odyssey highlights open questions in route representation and integration for E2E driving. Benchmark code and adapted baselines will be released publicly.

</details>


### [332] [Traversability-Aware Cooperative Path Planning for Human-UGV Casualty Evacuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06487)
*Kristian Dalland,Prithvi Poddar,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu,Ehsan T. Esfahani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人-无人地面车（UGV）双智能体在搜救伤员转运中的联合路径规划，通过优化"交接点"位置耦合人类代谢成本与UGV能量模型，实现时间与能耗双重优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多机器人路径规划虽处理异构智能体，但均将智能体视为机器人，成本模型为机械性的、可通行性来自传感器；在人与机器人协作中，人类伙伴仅被赋予指挥与监督角色，未被建模为具有独特移动约束和动态能量储备的物理协同导航者，因此缺乏对真实人机协同转运任务的合理建模。

Method: 构建双智能体人-UGV联合路径规划模型：人类采用Pandolf-Santee代谢成本模型并以疲劳调制速度；UGV采用滚动阻力能量模型与地形依赖的速度限制。利用两者互补的可通行性（人类可穿越密植被与浅水，UGV在开阔地形与道路速度更优），优化伤员交接点（switch points）位置以最小化总任务时间。在多个程序化生成高程与土地覆盖数据的1km²合成环境中评估。

Result: 优化策略相较纯人类基线平均任务时间减少5.3%，相较未优化交接点的朴素人-UGV策略减少7.0%，同时相较基线降低人类能耗17.8%。值得注意的是，朴素策略虽将人类能耗降幅更大（22.4%），但任务时间相较基线增加2%，说明交接点优化是实现人-UGV协同时间收益的必要条件。

Conclusion: 将人类建模为具有生理成本与疲劳动态的物理协同导航者，并优化人机交接点，可在人-UGV协同搜救转运中同时获得时间与能耗收益；仅引入UGV而不做交接点优化可能反而损害任务时间，交接点优化是获取协同收益的关键。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot path planning is a well-studied problem in which agents with disparate kinematic and dynamic models must coordinate to achieve shared objectives. These formulations, however, treat all agents as robotic-their cost models are mechanical and their traversability is sensor-derived. In human-robot teaming, the human partner remains relegated to command and supervisory roles rather than being modeled as a physical co-navigator with distinct mobility constraints and dynamic energy reserves. This work investigates joint path planning for a two-agent human-UGV team in search-and-rescue casualty retrieval scenarios. We model the human agent using the Pandolf-Santee metabolic cost model with fatigue-modulated speed, and the UGV using a rolling-resistance energy model with terrain-dependent speed limits. By exploiting the complementary traversability of each agent-the human's ability to traverse dense vegetation and shallow water versus the UGV's superior speed on open terrain and roads-we optimize casualty transfer locations, termed switch points, to minimize total mission time. Evaluated across multiple synthetic 1km2 environments with procedurally generated elevation and land-cover data, the optimized strategy reduces mean mission time by 5.3% relative to a human-only baseline and by 7.0% relative to a naive human-UGV strategy without switch point optimization, while reducing human energy expenditure by 17.8% relative to baseline. Notably, the naive strategy reduces human energy expenditure by a larger margin (22.4%) but incurs a 2% increase in mission time relative to baseline, illustrating that switch point optimization is necessary to realize time savings from human-UGV teaming.

</details>


### [333] [MarvisNav: Making Memory Visible on Route Choices for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06510)
*Jincheng Wang,Chi Pui Chan,Wei Zeng,Shuyang Zhang,Jianhao Jiao,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MarvisNav是一个零样本物体导航框架，将拓扑图候选节点及其探索状态投影到自我中心视图上，使VLM能直接可视地联合评估目标相关性与探索记忆，无需额外融合步骤即达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本物体导航方法中，VLM从自我中心图像推断搜索区域，而探索历史被单独表示（如文本或地图），两者分离导致需要额外融合步骤，或使记忆与路径选择的对应关系对VLM而言是隐式的、需自行恢复。作者认为记忆的表示方式本身会影响VLM决策，因此希望将探索记忆直接呈现在视觉路径选择上。

Method: 提出MarvisNav框架：维护一个拓扑图，将候选节点连同其探索状态投影到自我中心视图上，形成"带记忆的视觉路径选择"。探索状态记录局部探索进展而非仅二元访问标记。通过将探索状态直接绑定到每个视觉候选，VLM可在一次推理中联合评估目标相关性与探索状态，无需事后融合或重排序阶段，且无需策略训练。同时研究了记忆表示（文本/地图 vs 视觉投影）对VLM决策的影响。

Result: 在HM3D上达到SOTA（SR 81.2%，SPL 42.5%），在MP3D上也具竞争力；优于代表性VLM方法且VLM调用次数大幅减少（如仅为WMNav的7.5%）。真实机器人实验在多样场景中验证了其实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 记忆表示方式会塑造VLM决策及ZSON性能，有效利用记忆不仅取决于记忆是否可用，还取决于其如何被表示。MarvisNav证明了将探索记忆直接可视化在路径选择上是一种简洁有效的方式。

Abstract: When searching for an object, people choose their next move by considering both likely target locations and places already explored. The current view can cue place-associated memories, bringing target relevance and prior exploration into the same spatial context. In many zero-shot object navigation (ZSON) methods, however, vision-language models (VLMs) infer promising search areas from egocentric images, while exploration history is represented separately, e.g., as text or maps. This separation either requires an additional fusion step or leaves the correspondence between memory and route choices implicit for the VLM to recover. We instead make exploration memory directly visible on visual route choices. We propose MarvisNav, a ZSON framework that maintains a topological graph and projects candidate nodes together with their exploration states onto egocentric views as memory-bearing visual route choices. These states capture local exploration progress beyond binary visitation. By binding exploration state directly to each visual candidate, MarvisNav enables the VLM to jointly evaluate target relevance and exploration state without a separate post-hoc fusion or reranking stage. Without policy training, MarvisNav achieves state-of-the-art performance on HM3D (81.2% SR and 42.5% SPL), while remaining competitive on MP3D. It also outperforms representative VLM-based methods with far fewer VLM calls (e.g., 7.5% of WMNav). Real-robot experiments across diverse scenes further validate its practical deployability. Beyond MarvisNav, our study shows that memory representation shapes VLM decisions and ZSON performance, highlighting that effective memory use depends not only on its availability, but also on how it is represented. Code and project page will be available at \url{https://wangjincheng1998.github.io/MarvisNav/}.

</details>


### [334] [ArtifactArena: Evaluating Models by What They Build in the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06511)
*Kushagra Tiwary*,David Mayo*,Nikhil Behari,Xiangzhou Sun,Abdulrahman Alabdulkareem,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ArtifactArena，一个通过物理仿真机器人竞技来评估前沿模型工程能力的开放式平台与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿模型不应只看其言语输出，而应衡量其在真实物理环境中工程与构建的能力，但当前缺乏可量化、可持续的非饱和测试基准。

Method: 构建 ArtifactArena 软硬件协同设计平台，模型需设计可在仿真竞技场对战的完整机器人；通过三种测试框架评估零样本、验证器引导精炼与开放式物理设计能力，分别基于文本描述、物理仿真反馈与对局数据迭代优化产物；并采用 Elo 排名，依据模型所生成产物间的对战锦标赛结果进行基准评测。

Result: 得到一套可对前沿模型进行 Elo 排序的竞技式评测体系，并开放框架与锦标赛基础设施供社区持续提交。

Conclusion: ArtifactArena 提供了一个持续演进、非饱和的测试平台，用于长期衡量开放式智能在物理世界中的能力边界。

Abstract: To evaluate the frontier, we must measure models not by what they say, but by what they can engineer and build in grounded physical environments. We introduce \textsc{ArtifactArena}, an open-ended platform where models face a physically grounded hardware-software co-design challenge: engineering fully functional robots to compete in a simulated arena. We evaluate a frontier model's zero-shot, verifier guided refinement, and open-ended physical design capabilities through three harnesses that refine their bot artifacts based on text descriptions, physics simulator feedback, and gameplay data. We benchmark these capabilities with an Elo ranking of frontier models derived from head-to-head tournaments between their artifacts. By releasing this framework and tournament infrastructure for ongoing community submissions, we establish a living, non-saturating testbed to continuously measure the expanding limits of open-ended intelligence in the physical world. Please visit \href{https://artifactarena.ai}{https://artifactarena.ai} for more information.

</details>


### [335] [SimForcing: Distilling Simulation Motion Priors into Real-Domain Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06598)
*Xiaodong Wang,Tianle Li,Chuanxin Song,Junliang Xie,Zhanmi Zhong,Suiying Wu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimForcing：一种仿真引导的机器人世界模型框架，通过潜空间运动蒸馏与多块仿真条件化，兼顾运动响应精度与视觉真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件机器人世界模型需在响应机器人轨迹的同时保持真实视觉动态，但从异构机器人视频中同时学习二者困难：仿真提供结构化运动监督却存在外观差异，且其不准确预测会误导真实视频生成。

Method: 1) 通过潜空间运动蒸馏从仿真教师迁移运动知识，对齐潜空间中的时间变化以内化运动先验，减轻外观差异影响；2) 提出带条件dropout的多块仿真条件化，利用预测的仿真轨迹而不过度依赖其准确性；3) 仿真条件化无分类器引导方案统一上述两点，在内部化运动知识与额外仿真潜变量引导的预测间取得平衡；联合训练的学生模型同时生成仿真条件与真实域视频，推理时无需额外世界模型。

Result: 在Bridge上，在无外部具身预训练条件下取得最优PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS与FVD；在InternData-A1上的评估进一步支持其跨机器人数据集的适用性；用所训练世界模型初始化视觉-语言-动作模型提升了LIBERO成功率。

Conclusion: SimForcing有效利用仿真作为可迁移运动知识与可控参考，兼顾真实域视频生成质量与下游策略学习价值，具备跨数据集泛化能力。

Abstract: Action-conditioned robot world models must respond precisely to robot trajectories while preserving realistic visual dynamics, yet learning both from heterogeneous robot videos remains challenging. Simulation offers structured motion supervision, but appearance differences hinder direct transfer, and inaccurate simulation predictions can misguide real-video generation. We present SimForcing, a simulation-guided framework that uses simulation both as a source of transferable motion knowledge and as a controllable reference for prediction. First, we transfer motion knowledge from a simulation teacher through latent-motion distillation, aligning temporal changes in latent space to internalize motion priors while mitigating the influence of appearance differences. Second, we introduce multi-block simulation conditioning with condition dropout to exploit predicted simulation trajectories without relying excessively on their accuracy. Our simulation-conditioning classifier-free guidance scheme unifies these two ideas by balancing predictions based on internalized motion knowledge with those additionally guided by simulation latents. The jointly trained student generates both simulation conditions and real-domain videos, requiring no additional world model at inference. On Bridge, SimForcing achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, and FVD among the compared methods without external embodied pretraining. Evaluation on InternData-A1 further supports its applicability across robot datasets. Moreover, using our trained world model to initialize a vision-language-action model improves LIBERO success, suggesting its utility for downstream policy learning. \url{https://github.com/Wang-Xiaodong1899/SimForcing}

</details>


### [336] [Physics Residual Dynamics and Reduced Order Whole-Body Planning for Obstacle Aware Human Robot Cloth CoTransportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06641)
*Moein Forouhar,Kosar Behnia,Anirvan Dutta,Hamid Sadeghian,Ville Kyrki,Sami Haddadin,Gokhan Alcan,Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合学习布料模型与降阶全身模型的分层规划框架，实现双臂移动操作机器人与可变形物体的协同运输，兼顾预测精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机协同运输可变形物体时，避障间隙同时取决于抓取点与未驱动的内部形状，因此必须在运动过程中预测物体变形；同时全身模型计算开销大，难以满足规划实时性要求。

Method: 构建分层规划框架：(1) 物理残差条件循环变分自编码器（p-cRVAE）以线性化物理模型为基，学习残差修正，从抓取点观测预测完整布料构型；(2) 将预测动力学嵌入 MPPI 规划器，采用保留非完整基座约束与机械臂工作空间限制的双臂移动操作机器人降阶表示；(3) 由 MPC 层将采样运动细化为平滑、可执行的全身控制参考。

Result: 降阶模型在跟踪性能上与完整 17-DoF 模型相当，计算时间减少约 80%；在四种协同运输场景与两种搬运速度下，所提框架保持布料—障碍物间隙，而沿角跟随基线发生碰撞；全身细化使最终布料变形由 0.93 m 降至 0.28 m。

Conclusion: 所学布料模型与降阶全身模型的结合可在 40 步规划时域内限制误差累积，实现高效且安全的可变形物体协同运输规划，并显著降低计算成本与末端变形。

Abstract: Human--robot co-transportation of deformable objects requires predicting object deformation during motion, since obstacle clearance depends on both the grasp points and the unactuated interior. We present a hierarchical planning framework that combines a learned cloth model with a reduced-order whole-body model of a dual-arm mobile manipulator. A physics-residual conditional recurrent variational autoencoder (p-cRVAE) predicts the full cloth configuration from grasp-point observations by learning a residual correction to a computationally efficient linearized physics model, limiting error accumulation over 40-step planning horizon. The predicted cloth dynamics are embedded in a model predictive path integral (MPPI) planner using a reduced-order representation of a dual-arm mobile manipulator that preserves the non-holonomic base constraint and arm workspace limits. An MPC layer subsequently refines the sampled motion into smooth, executable references for whole-body control. The reduced-order formulation achieves tracking performance comparable to the full 17-DoF model while reducing computation time by approximately 80%. Across four co-transportation scenarios and two carrying speeds, the proposed framework maintains cloth-obstacle clearance where a corner-following baseline results in collisions, while whole-body refinement reduces final cloth deformation from 0.93\,m to 0.28\,m.

</details>


### [337] [AffordCraft: Scalable Construction of Task-Ready Simulation Assets from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06643)
*Haoyun Yang,Xueyang Zhou,Ziyi Xie,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordCraft 通过"检索而非生成"的方式，从单张 RGB 图像和任务指令构建物理有效的铰接物体资产：定位物体与可操作部件、从铰接资产库中选取匹配条目并拟合到图像，同时保持部件与关节结构完整。在 2,000 张图像中为 1,703 张生成有效资产（31 类），显著优于五种生成式方法（最多 45% 通过率、中位 GPU 时间为其 1/10 至 1/78）。资产库从 141 扩展到 11,372 条时方法无需改动，类别覆盖率由 46% 提升至 100%，标签匹配选择比例由 18% 升至 51%；并基于所构建资产搭建单物体与复合场景操作任务，策略可从训练未见初始状态完成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真中的机器人学习受限于模拟器所提供的物体：许多任务需要具有分离部件、可实现所需运动的关节，以及接触下仍成立的物理属性的物体。现有方法对每张图像都重新恢复这种结构：生成模型预测的部件与关节大多无法在仿真中稳定放置或运动；通用智能体则需为每张照片进行长时间的多轮模型调用，成本高且物理有效性差。

Method: 提出 AffordCraft，用"检索替代生成"从单张 RGB 图像与任务指令构建资产：(1) 定位物体及其可操作部件；(2) 从铰接资产库中选取匹配条目；(3) 将其拟合到图像，同时保持部件与关节结构不变。该方法无需物体框或掩码标注，且资产库规模扩展无需修改方法本身。

Result: 在来自 31 个类别的 2,000 张照片上，AffordCraft 为 1,703 张生成物理有效资产；五种生成式方法在同一批照片上通过率最高仅 45%，且生成每个有效资产的 GPU 时间中位数是本方法的 10–78 倍。在 50 张杂乱图像上，经自动检测后 237 个标注物体中有 162 个通过相同的物理测试。资产库由 141 扩至 11,372 条时，类别覆盖率从 46% 升至 100%，含目标标签的选择占比从 18% 升至 51%。基于构建资产搭建单物体及复合场景操作任务，在脚本化演示上训练的策略可在训练未见的初始状态下完成两类任务。

Conclusion: 以检索替代生成，可在无需框/掩码的前提下高效产出物理有效、部件与关节完整的铰接资产，在成功率和计算成本上均优于生成式方法，并具备良好的库扩展性与杂乱场景鲁棒性；所构建资产可直接用于训练可泛化至未见初始状态的机器人操作策略。

Abstract: Robot learning in simulation depends on the objects the simulator offers. Many tasks need objects with separate parts, joints that allow the required motion, and physical properties that remain valid under contact. Existing methods recover this structure anew for every image: generative models predict parts and joints that mostly fail to settle or move in simulation, and general-purpose agents need a long session of model calls for each photograph. AffordCraft builds such an asset from a single RGB image and a task instruction by retrieval instead of generation: it locates the object and the part to operate, selects a matching entry from a library of articulated assets, and fits it to the image while keeping its parts and joints intact. Without any box or mask marking the object, AffordCraft produces a physically valid asset for 1,703 of 2,000 photographs from 31 categories. Five generative methods pass on at most 45% of the same photographs and, at the median, need 10 to 78 times our GPU time per valid asset. On 50 cluttered images, 162 of 237 annotated objects pass the same physical test after automatic detection. Growing the library from 141 to 11,372 entries needs no change to the method and raises category coverage from 46% to 100% and the share of selections with the requested label from 18% to 51%. We also build manipulation tasks from the constructed assets, both with single objects and in composed scenes; policies trained on scripted demonstrations complete both kinds of tasks from initial states unseen in training.

</details>


### [338] [Recursive Video In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06843)
*Wenrui Bao,Xinxin Liu,Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法RV-ICL，将演示视频构建为可导航的层级结构，让LLM智能体按需读取细节，在LIBERO-PRO与LIBERO-Plus上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过文本记忆跨回合改进，但只记录"做了什么"而非"如何做"；演示视频虽能展示过程，却难以放入上下文——完整视频拖慢每轮推理，固定关键帧丢失决定抓取成败的接触细节，且智能体所需信息随阶段变化：规划时需要任务结构，执行时需要接触前后的帧。

Method: RV-ICL基于演示的子事件（如抓取、释放）构建层级结构，层级由粗到细：全任务关键帧→阶段→时刻→短片段，并通过只读工具暴露。规划前智能体读取粗粒度层级；执行中当某步骤需要更多细节时重新进入层级，仅加载当前子目标的片段。每个任务仅需一个演示。

Result: 在RPent基础上，LIBERO-PRO成功率从92.6%提升至96.5%，LIBERO-Plus从86.7%提升至95.8%。

Conclusion: 将演示视频组织为可递归导航的事件层级并按需检索，是免训练地增强VLA策略编排的有效途径，兼顾上下文效率与接触级细节。

Abstract: LLM agents that orchestrate frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies improve across episodes through text memory, which records what the agent did but not how the task is done. A demonstration video shows it, but fits poorly into an agent's context. The full video slows every turn, fixed keyframes lose the contact detail that decides whether a grasp holds, and what the agent needs shifts from the task's structure while planning to the frames around each contact. We introduce Recursive Video In-Context Learning (RV-ICL), a training-free method that turns a demonstration into a hierarchy the agent navigates rather than a prompt it receives. The hierarchy is built from the sub-events of the demonstration, such as grasps and releases. Its levels grow finer, from keyframes of the whole task to phases, moments and short clips, and are exposed through read-only tools. The agent reads the coarse levels before planning. During execution it re-enters the hierarchy whenever a step needs more detail and loads only the clip of its current sub-goal. One demonstration per task is enough. Built on RPent, RV-ICL raises success from 92.6% to 96.5% on LIBERO-PRO and from 86.7% to 95.8% on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [339] [InterMimicGen: Scaling Humanoid Loco-Manipulation through Self-Evolving Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06850)
*Yucheng Zhang,Sirui Xu,Jinhong Li,Liuyu Bian,Anatulya Nandi,Derek Zhang,Xiangchen Liu,Xueting Li,Umar Iqbal,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InterMimicGen 是一个自演化运动模仿框架，通过"重定向—通用跟踪器—数据飞轮"三个环节，将稀疏异构的人-物交互动捕数据转化为可被带灵巧手人形机器人执行、且持续扩张的运动资源，并实现真机迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-物交互动捕数据虽能为人形机器人的移动操作提供丰富监督，但存在稀疏、异构、无法被机器人直接执行的问题，导致难以直接用于策略学习。

Method: 1) 整合动捕人-物交互数据集，在保持全身协调性与灵巧手-物关系的前提下重定向为人形机器人参考动作，形成大规模多样化参考库；2) 训练基于物理的通用跟踪器，在仿真中用带灵巧手的人形机器人执行这些参考动作；3) 构建数据飞轮：每轮对交互发生位置与身体执行方式做小幅、保持任务语义的改动，微调跟踪器，仅保留仿真中成功完成任务的变体作为下一轮种子，迭代累积扩大覆盖范围。

Result: 实验表明：重定向能跨不同机器人构型保持接触关系；单一通用策略可实现广泛跟踪；可执行动作数量随增强轮次持续增长；并可迁移至真实机器人。

Conclusion: InterMimicGen 提供了一条从异构人类演示到持续扩张运动资源的统一路径，服务于人形机器人学习。

Abstract: Captured human-object interactions provide rich supervision for humanoid loco-manipulation, but they are sparse, heterogeneous, and not directly executable by robots. We introduce InterMimicGen, a self-evolving motion-imitation framework in which robot motion data and a tracking policy improve each other. First, we consolidate motion-captured human-object interaction datasets and retarget them into humanoid robot references while preserving whole-body coordination and dexterous hand-object relationships. This produces a large and diverse humanoid robot reference collection for dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation. Second, we train a physics-based generalist tracker that executes these references in simulation on a humanoid with dexterous hands, covering a scale and diversity beyond prior humanoid tracking systems for loco-manipulation. Third, we close a data flywheel: each round makes small, task-preserving changes to where an interaction takes place and how the body performs it, fine-tunes the tracker on them, and keeps only the variants whose simulated execution completes the task, which seed the next round. With more iterations, these small edits compound into broader coverage around the sparse original demonstrations while preserving task semantics and motion quality. Experiments show contact-preserving retargeting across robot configurations, broad tracking with a single generalist policy, executable motions that keep growing over augmentation rounds, and transfer to real robots. InterMimicGen provides a unified path from heterogeneous human demonstrations to a continually expanding motion resource for humanoid robot learning.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.HE'></div>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [340] [Polarization Transport and Polarized Images of a Rotating Braneworld Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03738)
*Wen-Hao Deng,Qing-Quan Jiang,Kai Lin,Pei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究光学薄吸积流在旋转膜世界黑洞周围的偏振同步辐射，通过追踪零测地线与偏振四矢量平行移动，系统分析潮汐电荷、自旋、观测倾角及顺/逆行轨道对偏振强度、EVPA、Stokes参数及净线偏振度的影响，表明这些量可用于分离时空、观测几何与吸积流效应并约束膜世界潮汐电荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨高维引力（膜世界黑洞潮汐电荷）如何改变光子传播与偏振传输，并检验偏振观测量能否用于区分不同时空、观测几何与吸积流效应，从而为利用视界尺度偏振测量约束膜世界潮汐电荷提供途径。

Method: 在共同的发射率与磁场设定下，对光学薄吸积流积分零测地线方程与偏振四矢量的平行移动方程，计算旋转膜世界黑洞的偏振同步辐射；考察潮汐电荷、黑洞自旋、观测倾角以及顺行/逆行轨道运动的影响；分析线偏振强度、电矢量位置角（EVPA）、Stokes参数、Q–U偏振态空间及累积净线偏振度，并与Kerr和Kerr–Newman时空的代表性构型进行对比。

Result: 在所考虑的参数范围内，潮汐电荷与自旋不产生定性上新的大尺度偏振形态，而主要重塑阴影边界与光子环附近强场区域的偏振强度、取向及偏振态分布；观测倾角主导投影形态，高倾角增强投影与多普勒不对称，使顺行与逆行流的差异更显著；Stokes分量及其联合态空间揭示强度图像中不明显的局部弯曲、拉伸与轨迹聚集；累积净线偏振度随累积半径增大而下降，且较大潮汐电荷或自旋降低其幅度。

Conclusion: 这些互补的偏振观测量为分离时空、观测几何与吸积流效应提供了潜在途径，并可用于通过视界尺度偏振测量约束膜世界潮汐电荷。

Abstract: We investigate polarized synchrotron emission from an optically thin accretion flow around a rotating braneworld black hole, focusing on how higher-dimensional gravity modifies photon propagation and polarization transport. We integrate the null-geodesic and parallel-transport equations for the polarization four-vector and, within a common emissivity and magnetic-field prescription, examine the effects of tidal charge, black-hole spin, observer inclination, and prograde or retrograde orbital motion. We analyze the linearly polarized intensity, electric-vector position angle (EVPA), Stokes parameters, the joint $Q$--$U$ polarization-state space, and the cumulative net linear polarization degree, and compare representative braneworld configurations with Kerr and Kerr--Newman spacetimes. Within the parameter range considered, tidal charge and spin do not generate a qualitatively new large-scale polarization morphology. Instead, they primarily reorganize the polarized intensity, orientation, and polarization-state distribution in the strong-field region near the shadow boundary and photon ring. Viewing inclination controls the dominant projected morphology, while high inclinations enhance projection and Doppler asymmetries and make the distinction between prograde and retrograde flows more pronounced. The Stokes components and their joint state space expose local bending, stretching, and trajectory concentration that are less apparent in intensity images. The cumulative net linear polarization degree decreases with increasing cumulative radius, and larger tidal charge or spin lowers its amplitude for the representative models considered. Together, these complementary polarization observables provide a potential route for separating spacetime, viewing-geometry, and accretion-flow effects and for constraining braneworld tidal charge with horizon-scale polarimetry.

</details>


### [341] [The Virgo Cluster as a Case Study for Ancient Cosmic Ray Halos in Radio and X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03881)
*Philip F. Hopkins,Emily M. Silich,Jack Sayers,Sam B. Ponnada,Isabel S. Sands*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出，Virgo星系团核心区（≲40 kpc）内的宇宙线（CR）对总压强和软X射线辐射有显著贡献，这可以解释Virgo作为X射线弱冷核（CC）团的多项观测矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Virgo是最近的冷核星系团，也是典型的X射线弱冷核团。其X射线连续谱暗示存在约≳10 M⊙/yr的强冷却率，但对更冷气体相、尘埃和恒星形成的观测显示，除M87核以外几乎没有冷却发生。同时M87*是强宇宙线轻子源，且射电晕呈低频、谱指数快速陡化的特征，暗示存在老化的低能CR种群。文章旨在解释这一明显的冷却率矛盾。

Method: 将X射线与射电观测同更新的Faraday旋转磁场限制相结合，评估CR在CC半径（≲40 kpc）内对总压强的贡献，并分析CR通过库仑加热气体和逆康普顿（IC）散射微波背景光子至约keV能段产生软X射线发射的机制。

Result: CR在冷核半径内必须显著贡献总压强和软X射线发射。该机制可解释：射电/喷流功率与X射线冷却光度之间的表观对应关系；X射线推断的冷却率远高于其他波段观测冷却率的现象；以及≲10 kpc处X射线推断金属丰度相对UV/光学推断受到压制（且呈波长依赖）。与Perseus等更极端的射电+X射线源相比，Virgo需要更软的"逃逸"CR谱（在距M87*约≳1 kpc外逃逸或加速）。

Conclusion: Virgo核心区的宇宙线压强和IC散射软X射线发射能够统一解释其X射线弱冷却、喷流-冷却光度对应关系及金属丰度波长依赖等问题，与更极端射电源相比需要更软的逃逸CR谱。

Abstract: Virgo is the nearest cool core (CC) galaxy cluster, and is archetypal of the class of X-ray weak CCs. Despite the X-ray continuum implying a large cooling rate ($\gtrsim 10\,{\rm M_{\odot}\,yr^{-1}}$), measurements of cooler gas phases, dust, and star formation show almost no cooling outside the M87 nucleus. It is also known that M87$^{\ast}$ is a prodigious source of cosmic ray (CR) leptons observed in radio and $γ$-rays, and recent radio observations have shown that the M87 radio halo is low-frequency and its spectral index steepens rapidly away from the injection region+jets, consistent with an aging, low-energy CR population. We demonstrate that coupling X-ray and radio to newer Faraday rotation limits on the magnetic field, this implies the CRs within the CC radius ($\lesssim 40$kpc) must contribute significantly to the total pressure and soft X-ray emission, through a combination of Coulomb heating the gas and inverse-Compton (IC) scattering microwave background photons to $\sim$keV. We show this can explain the apparent correspondence between jet/radio power and X-ray cooling luminosity, and why the X-ray inferred cooling rate appears much larger than otherwise observed. This would also explain why the central X-ray inferred metallicity at $\lesssim 10\,$kpc appears to be suppressed relative to the UV/optical inferred metallicity (in a wavelength-dependent manner). Compared to more extreme radio+X-ray sources like Perseus, we find the observations require a softer 'escaping' CR spectrum (escaping or accelerated outside $\gtrsim 1\,$kpc from M87$^{\ast}$).

</details>


### [342] [Blasts from the Past: UHECR Excesses from a Population of GRB 21009A-like Transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03886)
*Nestor Mirabal*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出，超高能宇宙线天图中半人马座A（Cen A）附近的显著事件过剩，可能并非源自Cen A本身，而是由类似GRB 221009A的过往伽马射线暴事件造成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高能宇宙线（UHECR）天图中最突出的特征是Cen A附近的事件显著过剩，通常被归因于Cen A本身。作者希望检验是否存在替代解释，即该过剩是否可能由当地一类极亮、窄喷流的暂现源（类似GRB 221009A）的历史事件所产生。

Method: 作者假设一次非凡的伽马射线暴发生在Cen A/M83星系群内的某个矮星系中，星系际磁偏转会自然展宽并延迟其宇宙线信号，使其可见约2×10^5年。基于此，将模型推广至GZK视界内的当地该类极亮窄喷流暂现源群体，采用当地体率约4×10^-8 Mpc^-3 yr^-1、喷流半张角0.8°进行估算，并改变喷流张角、GZK体积和可见时间以考察更广的过剩取值范围。

Result: 在给定参数下，估计任意时刻全天约有3–7个UHECR过剩。改变喷流张角、GZK体积和可见时间会得到更广泛的过剩数目范围。

Conclusion: 该假设可通过将群体预测与Pierre Auger天文台和Telescope Array已获得及即将获得的UHECR各向异性数据进行对比来检验。

Abstract: The most prominent feature in the ultra-high-energy cosmic ray (UHECR) sky is a significant excess of events from the vicinity of Centaurus A (Cen A). In this paper we investigate whether the excess may be caused by a past GRB 221009A-like event rather than Cen A itself. If an extraordinary gamma-ray burst (GRB) went off in one of the dwarf galaxies within the Cen A/M83 group, intergalactic magnetic deflections would naturally spread out and delay its cosmic ray signal, making it visible for $\sim 2\times 10^{5}$ years. Scaling this up, we model a local population of these exceptionally bright, narrow-jet transients within the \text{Greisen--Zatsepin--Kuzmin} (GZK) horizon. For a local volumetric rate of $\dot n_0\simeq4\times10^{-8}\ {\rm Mpc^{-3}\,yr^{-1}}$ and a jet half-opening angle of $0.8^\circ$, we estimate $\simeq3-7$ UHECR excesses across the sky at any given time. Varying the jet opening angle, GZK volume, and visibility time results in a broader range of excess values. It is possible to test this hypothesis by comparing the population predictions against existing and upcoming UHECR anisotropy data obtained by the Pierre Auger Observatory and the Telescope Array.

</details>


### [343] [Polarimetry of Black Hole Spin and Axisymmetric Emission Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03943)
*Vadim Bernshteyn,Daniel C. M. Palumbo,Dominic Chang,Tintin Nguyen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文用半解析锥形辐射模型研究未来空间BHE对黑洞吸积盘强透镜n=1像的β₂极化测量能对时空与等离子体几何施加何种限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHT已测得黑洞吸积盘的平均极化螺旋形态，但该观测由弱透镜的直接像(n=0)主导；未来空间Black Hole Explorer可测得强透镜的n=1像的β₂，从而提供新的约束，但β₂对时空与等离子体几何的约束能力尚不清楚。

Method: 采用半解析的锥形辐射(conical emission)模型，计算锥内磁场取向χ等参数对β₂模式(尤其n=0与n=1)的影响，分析其相位关系与自旋依赖。

Result: 锥内磁场取向定性改变β₂模式对模型参数的依赖。对锥对齐磁场，即使偏离赤道也满足β₂,n=0≈β₂,n=1*，且arg(β₂,n=0 β₂,n=1*)≈4χ。arg(β₂,n=0 β₂,n=1)可最小化磁场几何效应并保留自旋依赖，可用于测自旋方向。光子环β₂观测与直接像偏振互补，即使外部旋转测度未知也能强约束磁场结构；但仅靠β₂难以紧约束自旋与辐射锥张角，除非对磁场几何施加强先验。

Conclusion: 光子环β₂测量可作为直接像偏振的互补手段，强力约束磁场结构，并对自旋方向敏感；但单独使用β₂无法紧约束黑洞自旋和辐射锥张角。

Abstract: The Event Horizon Telescope array has measured the average polarimetric spiral morphology of black hole accretion disks using the so-called ``$β_2$'' coefficient. This measurement is dominated by the weakly lensed, direct ($n=0$, counting photon half-orbits) image of the disk, providing insight into magnetic field structure around the M87* black hole. The future space-based Black Hole Explorer mission is expected to extend these capabilities, allowing for $β_2$ measurement of the strongly lensed $n=1$ image. In this paper, we explore the constraints $β_2$ measurements would impose on spacetime and plasma geometries using a semi-analytic conical emission model. We show that magnetic field orientation with respect to the emission cone qualitatively changes the dependence of $β_2$ modes on model parameters. For cone-aligned magnetic fields, we find that the leading-order theoretical prediction from equatorial emitters of $β_{2,\, n=0} \approxβ_{2,\, n=1}^*$ is the dominant trend even in off-equatorial models. Specifically, we show $\arg(β_{2,\, n=0}\,β_{2,\, n=1}^*) \approx 4χ$, where $χ$ is the angle determining in-cone magnetic field orientation. We also find that $\arg(β_{2,\, n=0}β_{2,\, n=1})$ minimizes the effect of magnetic field geometry while preserving a dependence on spin that may be used to measure spin direction. We find that observations of the photon ring's $β_2$ complement the constraint from direct image polarization, and can greatly constrain magnetic field structure even with an unknown external rotation measure. We also find that black hole spin and emission cone opening angle would not be tightly constrained by $β_2$ alone, unless strong priors are placed on the magnetic field geometry.

</details>


### [344] [A Unified Multi-Band Secular Framework for the Hierarchical Triple PSR J0435+3233 System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03996)
*Achamveedu Gopakumar,Vidit Singh,Abhimanyu Susobhanan,Mauri J. Valtonen,Sayantan Bhattacharya,Di Li,Hong-Liang Yan,Jianping Yuan,Na Wang,Pei Wang,Qingdong Wu,De Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文针对FAST发现的毫秒脉冲星PSR J0435+3233，建立单平均（SA）三体模型以区分其两种可能的几何构型，并预测未来观测判别时间及多波段联合约束方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSR J0435+3233具有异常大的视自转减慢率，可能源自远距离伴星的视线加速度。其层级三体结构（内层脉冲星-氦白矮星双星+外层G型亚巨星）目前存在两种几何解，亟需物理模型加以区分并给出可观测判别方案。

Method: 发展单平均（SA）框架：对内层轨道做平均，同时保留偏心外层轨道的瞬时相位，从而刻画第三体引起的时间依赖四极扰动；将三体物理描述嵌入脉冲星计时分析，并前向演化两种可行构型Tv1与Tv2，预测其计时信号的分离时机；进一步扩展以预测第三体的光谱视向速度与天体测量演化。

Result: SA演化预测两种构型将在未来数年内变得可区分，其中有效投影半长轴x_{1,eff}是最早的判别观测量；同时给出第三体的光谱径向速度与天体测量预测，可连接FAST计时、LAMOST时域光谱与Gaia天体测量。

Conclusion: 该多波段框架有望将PSR J0435+3233确立为三体动力学、致密双星与恒星演化及引力物理的极佳约束实验室。

Abstract: The millisecond pulsar PSR J0435+3233, discovered by FAST, exhibits an unusually large apparent spin-down rate, potentially reflecting line-of-sight acceleration from a distant companion. Recent investigations reveal a hierarchical triple consisting of an inner pulsar-helium white dwarf binary with an orbital period of ~8 days, orbited by a G-type subgiant tertiary on an eccentric ~74 yr orbit. The current timing description admits two distinct geometrical solutions. We develop a singly averaged (SA) formulation that averages over the inner orbit while retaining the instantaneous phase of the eccentric outer orbit, thereby capturing the time-dependent quadrupolar perturbations induced by the tertiary. We show how this physical three-body description can be incorporated naturally into pulsar-timing analyses and use it to forward-propagate the two currently viable configurations, Tv1 and Tv2, to forecast when their timing signatures become distinguishable with FAST. The SA evolution predicts that the two configurations will become distinguishable in the coming years, with the effective projected semi-major axis, $x_{1,\mathrm{eff}}$, providing the earliest discriminating observable. We further extend the framework to predict the tertiary's spectroscopic radial-velocity and astrometric evolution, linking FAST timing with LAMOST time-domain spectroscopy and Gaia astrometry. This multi-band framework has the potential to establish PSR J0435+3233 as an exceptionally well-constrained laboratory for three-body dynamics, compact-binary and stellar evolution, and gravitational physics.

</details>


### [345] [A concordance model of neutron star kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04005)
*Cheyanne Shariat,Kareem El-Badry,Smadar Naoz*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究结合Gaia NS与Be+NS双星的轨道性质、诞生率以及年轻孤立脉冲星的速度约束，构建了统一的NS诞生踢速模型，发现双峰踢速分布可同时描述三类群体：低速分量占约10%但主导幸存双星，高速分量峰约200 km/s。该模型还约束了Gaia NS的祖系为含中等质量剥离星、约1 M☉伴星、au级轨道的超新星前双星，并给出了银河系、LMC、SMC中的预测数量及三体系统中的存活比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星在超新星爆发时获得的诞生踢速会影响其在双星中的存活及在银河系中的运动，但NS群体整体的踢速分布仍不确定。作者希望用Gaia NS、Be+NS双星的轨道与诞生率以及年轻孤立脉冲星的速度约束，建立一个能同时解释孤立脉冲星与幸存NS双星的统一诞生踢速模型。

Method: 将Gaia NS和Be+NS双星的轨道性质与诞生率，与年轻孤立脉冲星的速度约束结合起来，拟合一个共享的双峰踢速分布；再用该分布约束Gaia NS的祖系，并评估其在层级三体中的留存情况。

Result: 三类群体可由共享双峰踢速分布共同描述：低速分量为对数正态分布，μ_ln v=1.87(+0.65/-0.53)，σ_ln v=0.55(+0.85/-0.44)，中位速度6.5(+5.9/-2.7) km/s；高速分量与年轻脉冲星推断一致，峰值约200 km/s。低速分量仅占全部NS诞生约10%，却产生大多数Gaia NS双星和约一半Be+NS双星。模型预测的系统反冲速度与Gaia NS、Be+NS及NS低质量X射线双星的观测运动学一致。Gaia NS祖系倾向为超新星前含中间质量剥离星、伴星约1 M☉、au级轨道的双星，预测银河系约300个、LMC约40个、SMC约30个。层级三体中，2–10%的Gaia NS三体和3–23%的Be+NS三体在超新星后保留第三体。

Conclusion: 一个共享的诞生踢速规律可以同时解释孤立脉冲星与幸存的NS双星：大多数NS获得大踢速，而低踢速在幸存双星中较为常见。

Abstract: Neutron stars (NSs) receive natal kicks during supernovae that affect their survival in binaries and motion through the Galaxy. The global distribution of these kick velocities across the NS population remains uncertain. Here, we combine the orbital properties and birth rates of Gaia NS and Be+NS binaries with velocity constraints from young isolated pulsars to construct a concordance model of NS natal kicks. We find that all three populations can be jointly described by a shared bimodal kick distribution. The low-velocity component follows a lognormal distribution with $μ_{\ln v}=1.87^{+0.65}_{-0.53}$ and $σ_{\ln v}=0.55^{+0.85}_{-0.44}$, corresponding to a median speed of $6.5^{+5.9}_{-2.7}~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$. The high-velocity component is consistent with the distribution inferred from young pulsars, which peaks near $200~{\rm km~s^{-1}}$. The low-velocity component contributes only $\sim10\%$ of all NS births, but produces most Gaia NS binaries and roughly half of Be+NS binaries. The same model predicts systemic recoil velocities consistent with the observed kinematics of Gaia NSs, Be+NS binaries, and NS low-mass X-ray binaries. For Gaia NSs, whose formation history remains unknown, we use this kick distribution to constrain their progenitors. We favor pre-supernova binaries containing intermediate-mass stripped stars with $\sim1~{\rm M_\odot}$ companions in au-scale orbits, and predict $\sim300$ such binaries in the Milky Way today, $\sim40$ in the LMC, and $\sim30$ in the SMC. Finally, we consider the implications of these kicks for NSs in hierarchical triples. We find that 2-10$\%$ of Gaia NS triples and 3-23$\%$ of Be+NS triples retain their tertiary after the supernova. Overall, our results suggest that a shared natal kick law can account for isolated pulsars and surviving NS binaries, where most NSs receive large kicks and low kicks are common among surviving binaries.

</details>


### [346] [Observed x-ray spectral indices support magnetic buoyancy sourced coronae in Seyfert active galactic nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04061)
*Ketevan Kotorashvili,Eric G. Blackman*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文基于"仅大尺度磁场能逃逸湍流撕裂并上升供给日冕"的范式，用广义平均场吸积盘理论把盘磁场能量逃逸比例与日冕光度占比联系起来，约束X射线幂律指数，得到的预测光子指数与观测吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准活动星系核（如Seyfert星系）辐射谱建模通常需要手工调节光学厚盘、光学薄日冕、尘埃环与外流各成分的相对贡献以拟合观测，而对这种相对分配背后的物理机制缺乏精确理解。本文旨在为该能量分配提供物理约束。

Method: 采用包含局地径向角动量输运与向日冕角动量输运的广义平均场吸积盘理论；提出只有尺度大到足以在浮力上升时间内避免湍流撕裂的磁场才能从盘下方供给日冕这一概念范式；并借用已有的磁化湍流吸积盘能谱数值模拟，将盘磁能逃逸比例与日冕光度占总光度比例建立对应关系。不显式计算粒子加速与辐射过程，直接假设盘辐射为热谱、日冕辐射为幂律谱。

Result: 上述日冕形成范式将允许的X射线幂律指数限制在一个较窄区间内，且与观测一致。对本文研究的三个活动星系核，预测平均光子指数为 \overlineΓ=1.76，与观测平均值 \overlineΓ_obs=1.73 相符。

Conclusion: 该范式能自洽地给出与观测相符的X射线幂律指数，从而增强了对其的置信度，并为AGN辐射谱成分相对分配提供了物理基础。

Abstract: Standard radiation spectra modeling of active galactic nuclei like Seyfert galaxies typically involves tuning the relative contributions from an optically thick disc, optically thin corona, dust torus, and outflow by hand to match observations. The physics that quantifies this relative partitioning is not precisely understood. Here we make progress toward improving this understanding by testing the implications of a conceptual paradigm that the corona is supplied from below only by magnetic fields whose scales are large enough to escape turbulent shredding on their buoyant rise time. We use a generalized mean field accretion disc theory that includes both local radial angular momentum transport and angular momentum transport into a corona. We then use previous numerical simulations of magnetized turbulent accretion disc energy spectra to connect the fraction of disc magnetic energy that would escape to the fraction of coronal to total luminosity. Without explicitly calculating detailed particle acceleration and radiative processes, we assume that the disc radiation spectrum is thermal and the coronal radiation spectrum is a power law. We then show that the aforementioned paradigm for corona formation constrains the allowed x-ray power law index to a narrow range which agrees with observations. For the three AGNs studied here the predicted mean photon index is $\overlineΓ=1.76$, consistent with the mean observed value of $\overlineΓ_{\rm obs}=1.73$, bolstering confidence in the paradigm.

</details>


### [347] [Dark matter in neutron stars: two-fluid $f$-mode oscillations in full general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04282)
*Prashant Thakur,Ishfaq Ahmad Rather,Y. Lim*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文在完整广义相对论下计算了混合自相互作用玻色暗物质中子星的四极f模频率与引力波衰减时间，跨越21种核状态方程与1%–20%暗物质分数，揭示核物质主导与暗物质主导两支模式的迥异行为，并发现无构型满足可控线性潮汐共振条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质核心与软的核状态方程可产生相似的质量—半径特征，导致仅凭质量—半径观测难以区分二者，因此需要借助f模频谱这一互补探针来区分两种物理情景。

Method: 在完整广义相对论框架下，采用双流体描述处理冷、非自转中子星与自相互作用玻色暗物质的混合体系；核物质位移与暗物质位移通过度规相互耦合，形成核物质主导与暗物质主导两支f模，其辐射阻尼由外行波边界条件确定。研究涵盖21种核状态方程（核子、超子、Δ重子及超子—Δ混合组分），暗物质分数取1%–20%；主序列采用λ=π、玻色子质量500 MeV（核心）与100 MeV（晕）两种情形，并扩展到ℓ=3。

Result: 在1.4倍太阳质量处，核心暗物质主导支的衰减时间随暗物质分数从1%时的约10^3 s降至20%时的约1 s，近似遵循经验性的F_DM^-2标度关系；而核物质主导支的衰减时间保持在0.12–0.33 s。晕状暗物质主导支的衰减时间则随暗分数增加而增大：同一恒星质量、20%暗分数下，暗半径接近88 km，频率降至0.14 kHz，衰减时间约450 s。部分高质量核心序列表现出暗物质主导支衰减时间的强烈上翘，无法由主导质量四极诊断量再现。代表性核心计算显示暗物质主导支对玻色子自耦合更敏感，且在ℓ=3时两支均保留。

Conclusion: f模频谱（尤其是暗物质主导支的频率与衰减时间）可作为区分暗物质核心与软核状态方程的有力互补探针；但在所采用的静态、圆轨道双星近似下，没有任何被评估的构型能同时满足可控线性潮汐共振所需的几何与微扰条件。

Abstract: A dark-matter core and a soft nuclear equation of state can produce similar mass--radius
  signatures, motivating a complementary probe through the $f$-mode spectrum. We compute
  quadrupolar $f$-mode frequencies and gravitational-wave damping times in full general
  relativity for cold, nonrotating neutron stars admixed with self-interacting bosonic
  dark matter (DM). Our two-fluid survey spans 21 nuclear equations of state covering
  nucleonic, hyperonic, $Δ$-admixed, and combined hyperon--$Δ$ compositions at
  $F_{\rm DM}=1$--$20\%$. The main sequences use $λ=π$ and boson masses of $500$
  and $100\,\mathrm{MeV}$ for the core and halo studies, respectively. Independent
  normal-matter (NM) and DM displacements couple through the metric, yielding NM-led and
  DM-led $f$-mode branches whose radiative damping is determined by outgoing-wave boundary
  conditions. At $1.4\,M_\odot$, the core DM-led damping time decreases from order
  $10^3\,\mathrm{s}$ at $F_{\rm DM}=1\%$ to order $1\,\mathrm{s}$ at $20\%$, approximately
  following an empirical $F_{\rm DM}^{-2}$ dependence, while NM-led damping times remain
  within $0.12$--$0.33\,\mathrm{s}$. Halo DM-led damping instead increases with dark
  fraction: at the same stellar mass and $F_{\rm DM}=20\%$, dark radii approach
  $88\,\mathrm{km}$, frequencies fall to $0.14\,\mathrm{kHz}$, and damping times reach
  approximately $450\,\mathrm{s}$. Selected high-mass core sequences exhibit strong DM-led
  damping-time upturns not reproduced by the leading mass-quadrupole diagnostic.
  Representative core calculations show greater DM-led sensitivity to the bosonic
  self-coupling and retain both branches at $\ell=3$. Under the adopted nonrotating,
  circular-binary prescriptions, no evaluated configuration satisfies all geometric and
  perturbative conditions for a controlled linear tidal resonance.

</details>


### [348] [The fading superorbital clock of Hercules X-1 in its 2025--2026 extended low state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04341)
*Zhenxuan Liao,Xinrui Tie,Yufeng Chi,Guanjun Lin*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于Fermi/GBM、Swift/BAT与MAXI/GSC对食双星吸积脉冲星Her X-1长达二十年的X射线监测，聚焦2025年5月开始的扩展低态（ELS）。结果表明：35天超轨道周期在ELS中并非停止而是衰减，其相位在主流衰减至正常水平几个百分点前保持锁定，随后（约2026年1—3月）失去相位相干；自旋导数与主流硬X射线流量正相关（r≈0.7），ELS记录了史上最长的持续自旋减慢；脉冲轮廓形态在不同扭矩状态下无统计差异，18年内无长期漂移。结论是进动翘曲吸积盘保持节奏，无需中子星自由进动即可解释观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Her X-1是研究吸积脉冲星自旋、超轨道周期及其与吸积盘进动关系的经典目标。2025年5月开始的扩展低态（ELS）是22年来首次此类事件，提供了一个罕见窗口，用以检验超轨道时钟是否停止、自旋扭矩是否跟随吸积流量，以及脉冲束形状是否随状态变化，从而判断是否需要引入中子星自由进动等额外机制。

Method: 利用Fermi/GBM、Swift/BAT和MAXI/GSC对Her X-1进行约20年的X射线监测。分析35天超轨道周期的相位演化与相干性，将ELS前脉冲频率历史划分为自旋加快、自旋减慢与平坦三种扭矩状态，计算自旋导数与主流硬X射线流量的相关性，并比较8—50 keV脉冲轮廓形态及形状参数在不同扭矩状态和长期时间尺度上的差异。

Result: 35天超轨道周期在ELS中逐渐衰减而非突然停止，剩余调制在主流流量降至正常值几个百分点前仍与ELS前的时钟相位锁定，约2026年1—3月失去相位相干。自旋导数与主流硬X射线流量正相关（Pearson r≈0.7）。ELS包含记录中最长的持续自旋减慢，Fermi/GBM覆盖的≳380天内ṙν≈−4.7×10⁻¹³ Hz s⁻¹。8—50 keV脉冲轮廓形态在固定超轨道相位下于不同扭矩状态间统计上不可区分，形状参数在18年记录中无长期漂移。

Conclusion: 进动翘曲吸积盘在脉冲星淡出直接视野时仍保持其节奏：时钟保持相位，扭矩持续跟随吸积流量，波束保持形状。当前监测数据不需要中子星自由进动来解释观测结果，支持超轨道周期源于吸积盘进动的图像。

Abstract: We present two decades of X-ray monitoring of the eclipsing accreting pulsar Hercules X-1 with \textit{Fermi}/GBM, \textit{Swift}/BAT and \textit{MAXI}/GSC, centred on the extended low state (ELS) that began in 2025 May (the first such event in 22 years) and is still ongoing at the time of writing, $\sim$480~d after its onset. The 35-d superorbital clock does not simply stop in the ELS; it fades. The surviving modulation stays phase-locked to the pre-ELS clock until the flux of the main high state (the bright phase of the 35-d cycle) has decayed to a few per cent of its normal level. The in-phase coherence is then lost, around 2026 January--March. We classify the pre-ELS pulse-frequency history into three torque states: spin-up, spin-down and flat. The spin derivative correlates positively with the main-high hard X-ray flux (Pearson $r\simeq0.7$), and the ELS hosts the longest sustained spin-down of the record, $\dotν\approx-4.7\times10^{-13}$~Hz~s$^{-1}$ over the $\gtrsim$380~d of \fer/GBM coverage. The 8--50 keV pulse-profile morphology is statistically indistinguishable across the torque states at fixed superorbital phase, and the shape parameters show no secular drift over the 18-yr record. These results show that the precessing warped accretion disc keeps its rhythm while the pulsar fades from direct view: the clock keeps its phase, the torque keeps tracking the accretion flux, and the beam keeps its shape. No free precession of the neutron star is required by the current monitoring data.

</details>


### [349] [Fast X-ray Transient EP260321a: Shock Breakout through an Extended Circumstellar Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04367)
*Maokai Hu,Yi Yang,Lifan Wang,Xiaofeng Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 针对EP260321a约690秒的快速上升，提出其源于延展星周介质（CSM）中的激波突破，光子先在CSM中囚禁后随喷出物膨胀、不透明度下降而扩散出来，并拟合出致密CSM参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Einstein Probe发现的快速X射线暂现源EP260321a，其光学对应体被证认为宽线Ic型超新星；但其约690秒的上升时标与Wolf-Rayet星激波突破（SBO）的理论预期不符，需给出新的解释机制。

Method: 提出SBO发生在延展星周介质（CSM）中的情景：突破光子先被囚禁，随喷出物膨胀、不透明度降低而自未激波化的CSM中扩散。采用Monte Carlo模拟与解析方法描述光子扩散及温度演化，并用MCMC采样拟合CSM参数。

Result: 最佳拟合CSM参数为：总CSM质量log10(Mcsm/Msun) = -3.3，内半径Rin = 160 Rsun，外半径Rout = 612 Rsun。如此致密的CSM表明前身星在爆炸前数天经历了间歇性质量损失。

Conclusion: 延展CSM中的激波突破可解释EP260321a的快速上升，其致密CSM为爆炸前数天的间歇性质量损失提供了观测证据，对理解大质量恒星晚期演化给出新的观测约束。

Abstract: The Einstein Probe (EP) has discovered a fast X-ray transient, EP260321a, whose optical counterpart was subsequently classified as a broad-lined Type Ic supernova. Its observed rise time of about 690 seconds is inconsistent with theoretical predictions for shock breakout (SBO) from a Wolf-Rayet star, which is typically regarded as the progenitor of envelope-stripped supernovae. In this paper, we propose that EP260321a arose from a SBO within extended circumstellar matter (CSM), where breakout photons were initially trapped, then diffused out of the unshocked CSM as the ejecta expanded and the opacity decreased. We modeled this photon diffusion process using both Monte Carlo simulations and an analytical approach to describe the temperature evolution. Through Markov Chain Monte Carlo sampling, we obtain the best-fit CSM parameters of log10(Mcsm/Msun) = -3.3 for the total CSM mass, Rin = 160 Rsun for the inner CSM radius, and Rout = 612 Rsun for the outer CSM radius. The presence of such a dense CSM indicates that the progenitor of EP260321a underwent episodic mass loss just days before the explosion, providing new observational constraints on the late-stage evolution of massive stars.

</details>


### [350] [A Vortex Avalanche Model for Pulsar Glitches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04481)
*M. Ali Alpar,Erbil Gügercinoğlu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文提出一个二维中子星壳层超流模型，将涡线钉扎环境划分为"陷阱"与周围无涡区，无涡区相互连通形成快速涡线运动的方位角通道，从而解释脉冲星glitch的参数、演化及glitch幅度分布的双峰性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星glitch被认为涉及大量被钉扎涡线的突然脱钉与快速输运，可能由中子星壳层固体的破裂触发。这种涡线雪崩只有在存在快速涡线运动通道时才能在短时间内发生，因此需要理解钉扎环境中快速通道如何形成。

Method: 构建二维中子星壳层超流模型：将涡线钉扎环境铺砌为"陷阱"（钉扎涡线密度高于平均的区域）与其周围的无涡区，无涡区可连通成方位角方向的快速涡线运动通道。陷阱尺寸与铺砌尺寸由壳层固体物理与涡线钉扎物理估算，即壳层固体的临界应变角和涡线脱钉的临界速度。脱钉涡线随超流沿方位角方向运动，同时因互摩擦作用以一个小角度向外径向散射。

Result: 该模型定性解释了年轻脉冲星中glitch参数及其演化，并为glitch幅度分布中的双峰性提供了解释。

Conclusion: 具有空间非均匀钉扎环境的涡线系统的实验室实验与数值模拟，很可能更真实地反映脉冲星glitch；打破空间尺度不变性并监测其结果，也有助于更普遍地理解钉扎涡线系统中的集体行为。

Abstract: Pulsar glitches are thought to involve sudden unpinning and fast transport of large numbers of pinned vortices, possibly triggered by breaking of the neutron star crust solid. Such a vortex avalanche can take place on a fast timescale only if there are pathways for fast vortex motion. Formation of local vortex ``traps'' (regions containing larger than average density of pinned vortices) will provide vortex-free regions surrounding the traps, which can connect to form pathways for fast vortex motion. We propose a simple two-dimensional model for the neutron star crust superfluid, with the vortex pinning landscape tiled into traps and surrounding vortex-free regions which can connect to azimuthal pathways for fast vortex motion. The trap and tile sizes are estimated from the physics of the crust solid and vortex pinning, namely the critical strain angle of the crust solid and the critical velocity for vortex unpinning. Unpinned vortices move with the superfluid flow in the azimuthal direction while also scattering radially outward, by a small angle, due to mutual friction. The model qualitatively explains the glitch parameters and their evolution in young pulsars and provides an explanation for the bi-modality in the distribution of glitch magnitudes. Laboratory experiments and simulations on vortex systems with spatially inhomogeneous vortex pinning landscapes are likely to be more realistic models for pulsar glitches. Breaking the spatial scale-invariance and monitoring the results may also lead to better understanding of collective behaviour in pinned vortex systems in general.

</details>


### [351] [Revisiting damping of Alfvén waves in partially ionized plasmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04586)
*Yutaka Ohira*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过双流体十矩方程的线性分析，研究Alfvén波的阻尼率，重点考察黏性与摩擦阻尼在不同频率、电离度及β条件下的相对重要性，发现高β等离子体中黏性阻尼主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在宽频率范围和宽电离度范围内正确处理黏性效应，需考虑压力张量无迹部分的时间演化，并纳入氢原子之间以及质子与氢原子之间的相互作用。

Method: 采用双流体十矩（ten-moments）系统进行线性分析；在压力张量无迹部分的方程中引入氢—氢及质子—氢碰撞相互作用，并在若干极限下求色散关系的近似解析解。

Result: 在低频极限下，高β等离子体中黏性阻尼强于摩擦阻尼，且该结论与电离度无关。

Conclusion: 氢气黏性会显著影响部分电离高β等离子体中宇宙线的传播。

Abstract: We investigate the damping rate of Alfvén waves by means of the linear analysis of the two-fluid ten-moments system. To treat effects of viscosity in a wide frequency range, we take account of the time evolution of the traceless part of the pressure tensor. In addition, to treat effects of viscosity in a wide range of the ionization degree, we consider interactions between hydrogen atoms and between protons and hydrogen atoms in the equation of the traceless part of the pressure tensor. We find approximate analytical solutions to the dispersion relation for some limits. Our analysis in the low-frequency limit shows that the viscous damping dominates over the frictional damping in the high-beta plasma, which is independent of the ionization degree. Therefore, the viscosity of hydrogen gas significantly affects cosmic-ray propagation in a partially ionized high-beta plasma.

</details>


### [352] [Jets with Streaks: the Global Dynamics and Structure of Striped Poynting flux dominated Jets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04796)
*Ho-Sang Chan,Anna Chashkina,Omer Bromberg,Gregory R. Werner,Hayk Hakobyan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 首次开展全局轴对称GRMHD模拟，追踪条纹喷流从黑洞吸积磁环路诞生到大尺度耗散的完整演化，验证其可作为耀变体与伽马暴高能辐射源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条纹喷流中交替极性的磁主导区可在全局电流片处快速耗散磁能，被认为是耀变体和伽马暴瞬发辐射高能辐射的可行来源，但缺乏从喷流launching到大尺度耗散的全程自洽模拟。

Method: 进行首个全局轴对称GRMHD模拟：通过交替极性极向磁环路吸积到旋转黑洞来驱动条纹喷流，追踪mini-jet的launching、电磁加速、核心-鞘层结构形成，以及尾随mini-jet相遇形成电流片与耗散的完整过程。

Result: 每个环路吸积launching一个Poynting通量主导的mini-jet，携带继承环路极性的螺旋磁场，集合形成喷流核心，外围为非相对论鞘层；有效launching要求吸积盘极向环路形成于r ≲ 50 r_g内，否则受湍流破坏相干性；mini-jet电磁加速，洛伦兹因子随截面半径线性增长（存在横向因果接触时）；电流片于相反磁场mini-jet接触时形成，早期由内部压强驱动的纵向膨胀促进；在10^4 r_g模拟盒内测得耗散率约为喷流Poynting功率的数十百分比（观测者系）。

Conclusion: 条纹喷流可高效耗散磁能，结果可解释BL Lac型耀变体中明亮的超光速结构，支持其作为高能辐射来源的图景。

Abstract: Striped jets, characterized by magnetically dominated regions with alternating polarity, can enable fast magnetic energy dissipation in global current sheets formed in between the stripes, and are thus considered as a viable source for the high-energy emission observed in blazars and gamma-ray burst prompt emission. We perform the first global axisymmetric GRMHD simulations that follow the evolution of striped jets from their launching, by accretion of poloidal magnetic loops with alternating polarity onto a spinning black-hole, to their energy dissipation at large distances. The accretion of each loop launches a Poynting-flux-dominated mini-jet carrying a helical magnetic field whose polarity is inherited from the loop, and the collection of which forms the jet core. The relativistic core is surrounded by a non-relativistic sheath composed of returning fieldlines from previously launched mini-jets in a nested configuration. Efficient mini-jet launching requires poloidal loops in the accretion disk to form close to the black hole ($r \lesssim 50\,r_g$); otherwise, they lose coherency due to turbulence. Once launched, the mini-jets accelerate electromagnetically, similar to steady-state jets, with the Lorentz factor increasing linearly with the jet cross-sectional radius as long as lateral causal contact with the jet axis is maintained. Current sheets form when two trailing mini-jets carrying oppositely directed magnetic fields come into contact. Early sheet formation is facilitated by the longitudinal expansion of the mini-jets, driven by internal pressure before the flow becomes super-fast-magnetosonic. We measure an energy dissipation rate of a few tens of percent of the jet Poynting power in the observer frame, within our simulation box of $10^4\,r_g$. Our results can account for the bright superluminal features observed in BL Lac type blazars.

</details>


### [353] [Fast Bayesian Updating of the Neutron-Star Equation of State with Neural Posterior and Evidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05121)
*Prashant Thakur*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补的神经网络方法来推断中子星物态方程（EOS）：TSNPE+MIS 针对单一观测，A-NET+IS 可跨核输入与 NICER 源复用，另提出基于回归的 Green-function Evidence Network。两类方法在参数后验及质量-半径、潮汐形变带与 UltraNest 一致，误差很小，并显著加速计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中子星 EOS 推断依赖采样方法（如 UltraNest），在多次观测、多种核输入和多个源的分析中计算成本高、难以复用。作者希望发展神经网络推断路线，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算时间，并首次将 Evidence Network 应用于中子星 EOS 推断以快速获得绝对证据。

Method: 提出两种互补路线：TSNPE+MIS（截断序列神经后验估计+混合重要性采样，面向单一观测）和 A-NET+IS（摊销神经后验估计+重要性采样，可跨核输入与 NICER 源复用）。两者均针对原始先验与全似然进行校正，并用有效样本量作诊断。测试对象为核子与超子相对论平均场模型，结合核数据、NICER、GW170817 和 pQCD。此外提出 Green-function Evidence Network（EN），是证据网络的回归变体。

Result: 基准分析中，两种方法在参数后验及 90% 质量-半径、潮汐形变带上与 UltraNest 一致：TSNPE+MIS 带边差异仅 0.024-0.033 km 和 1.02-1.03%，A-NET+IS 为 0.019-0.050 km 和 0.86-1.13%。TSNPE+MIS 计算时间减少 1.6-2.3 倍；A-NET+IS 训练后每个构型快 15-240 倍。冻结的 A-NET 还能分析三个未参与训练的 NICER 源，包括新报告的高质量致密 PSR J1614-2230，其 R1.4 与 UltraNest 差异在核子物质下 0.010 km、超子物质下 0.009 km 内。EN 一次性训练后，新核数据查询约 0.03 s、新 NICER 源约 3.5 s 返回绝对证据；与 UltraNest 的 16 个差异均低于 1σ，独立重要性采样证据与 EN 不确定度一致。

Conclusion: 在所采用似然下，两种证据计算均以约 22:1-27:1 的贝叶斯因子支持核子物质。研究表明神经网络后验与证据推断可准确、可复现地复现传统采样结果，并带来显著计算加速和跨配置复用能力。

Abstract: We present two complementary neural routes for microscopic neutron-star EOS inference: truncated sequential neural posterior estimation with mixture importance sampling (TSNPE+MIS), which targets one observation, and amortized neural posterior estimation with importance sampling (A-NET+IS), which is reused across nuclear inputs and NICER sources. Both are corrected against the original prior and full likelihood, with the effective sample size as a diagnostic. We test nucleonic and hyperonic relativistic mean-field models with nuclear data, NICER, GW170817, and pQCD.
