For the fiducial analyses, both agree with UltraNest in the parameter posteriors and 90% mass-radius and tidal-deformability bands. TSNPE+MIS band-edge differences are only 0.024-0.033 km and 1.02-1.03%, while A-NET+IS differences are 0.019-0.050 km and 0.86-1.13%. TSNPE+MIS reduces the computing time by factors of 1.6-2.3; after training, A-NET+IS is 15-240 times faster per configuration. The frozen A-NET also analyzes three NICER sources absent from training, including the newly reported high-mass, compact PSR J1614-2230 posterior. For this source, the inferred $R_{1.4}$ agrees with UltraNest within 0.010 km for nucleonic matter and 0.009 km for hyperonic matter. We also introduce a Green-function Evidence Network (EN), a regression-based variant of Evidence Networks and, to our knowledge, the first Evidence Network applied to neutron-star EOS inference. After a one-time cost, its network queries return absolute evidences in about 0.03 s for new nuclear data and 3.5 s for a new NICER source; all 16 differences from UltraNest are below $1σ$, and independent importance-sampling evidences agree within the quoted EN uncertainties. Under the adopted likelihood, both evidence calculations favor nucleonic matter by Bayes factors of about 22:1-27:1.

</details>


### [354] [Large offsets of double-neutron-star mergers: degeneracies and implications for host associations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05226)
*Xiao-Tian Xu,Bin-Bin Zhang,Wenjie Wu,Suoning Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟（GRB-offset工具）重新建模双中子星并合相对于宿主星系的偏移，发现偏移分布敏感依赖于超新星物理、星系势、诞生位置、恒星形成历史和红移等物理参数，且存在严重简并，因此单一观测偏移量难以约束前身星与宿主星系性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马射线暴（GRB）相对宿主星系的投影偏移是其物理起源的重要诊断，而宿主关联通常通过偶然重合概率评估，但观测表明该方法在某些情况下存在歧义。因此需要更细致地理解双中子星（DNS）并合的偏移建模。

Method: 采用受控数值实验与蒙特卡洛模拟。为将第二次超新星（SN）的动力学效应与上游双星演化不确定性分离，对DNS的直接前身星进行参数化，并利用galpy将超新星后轨迹积分于星系势中。开发轻量级蒙特卡洛工具GRB-offset，考察超新星物理（抛射质量与踢速度）、星系势（势形状、星系维里质量）、DNS诞生位置、恒星形成历史和红移等因素。

Result: 参考模拟给出的偏移分布在束缚于宿主星系的DNS约30 kpc处、非束缚的约1000 kpc处出现峰值，且敏感依赖于所考虑的物理成分。同一偏移可由这些成分的不同组合产生，导致解释观测偏移时出现显著简并。

Conclusion: 仅凭单一观测到的偏移量难以约束DNS前身星及宿主星系的属性，偏移建模中存在本质性简并，需结合多参数信息才能有效诊断。

Abstract: The projected offset of a Gamma-Ray Burst (GRB) from its host galaxy is an important diagnostic of its physical origin, where the host associations are usually evaluated through chance-coincidence probabilities. However, as shown by observations, the host associations given by this approach may be ambiguous in some cases. In this work, we revisit the modelling of the offsets of double-neutron-star (DNS) mergers with controlled numerical experiments using Monte Carlo simulations. To isolate the dynamical effects of the second supernova (SN) from uncertainties in the upstream binary evolution, we parametrise the direct progenitor of DNSs and integrate the post-SN trajectories in galactic potentials using \texttt{galpy}. Complementary to previous population synthesis calculations, our Monte-Carlo tool, \texttt{GRB-offset}, provides a lightweight framework to identify sensitivities and expose degeneracies in offset modelling. With \texttt{GRB-offset}, we have investigated the following physical ingredients, supernova physics (ejected mass and kick velocity), galactic potential (potential shape, galaxy virial mass), DNS birth location, star formation history, and redshift. The offset distributions given by our reference Monte-Carlo simulation peak at around 30 kpc and 1000 kpc for the DNSs bound to and unbound from the host galaxy, which sensitively depends on the considered physical ingredients. A given offset can arise from various combinations of these ingredients, introducing substantial degeneracies when interpreting observed offsets. Therefore, an observed offset alone can hardly constrain the properties of the DNS progenitor and of the host galaxies.

</details>


### [355] [Pulsations and constraints on time lags in two distinct ULXs: NGC 5204 X-1 and NGC 55 ULX-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05236)
*Sathyaprakash,Rajath,Middleton,M. J.,Done,C*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过时序研究揭示两个经典超亮X射线源（NGC 5204 X-1与NGC 55 ULX-1）的吸积盘尺度与结构，并以>3σ显著性发现两者存在自旋周期信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超亮X射线源（ULX）本质尚不明确：其光度超过10倍太阳质量黑洞的经典爱丁顿极限，但通常被归入超爱丁顿X射线双星分类。回响时延与延迟-能量谱可揭示光学厚超爱丁顿盘中的粘性湍流性质，但需结合多波段谱与协变谱建模；同时对两个经典上性质不同的ULX（均具相对论性风、但观测倾角略有差异）缺乏系统的时序约束。

Method: 对NGC 5204 X-1和NGC 55 ULX-1开展广泛的时序研究；利用此前通过SPEX等光致电离模型拟合发射线谱所确认的相对论性风信息，并结合两者X射线连续谱硬度比差异所指示的观测倾角差异；同时进行加速的脉冲搜索（包含对信号轨道调制的改正）。

Result: 获得了对吸积盘尺度与结构的新约束；在NGC 5204 X-1和NGC 55 ULX-1中均发现了显著的自旋周期性（显著性>3σ）。

Conclusion: 时序分析（含脉冲搜索）可为ULX的吸积盘尺度、结构及自旋周期提供有效诊断，两个经典上不同的ULX均表现出自旋周期信号。

Abstract: The nature of ultra-luminous X-ray sources (ULXs) is shrouded in mystery as they're believed to be point sources with luminosities above the classical Eddington limit for a 10$M_{\odot}$ black hole (BH), but generally fit into the classification tree of super-Eddington X-ray binaries. Studies of reverberation lags and lag-energy spectra provide some insight into the nature of viscous turbulence in optically thick, super-Eddington discs, and are useful in conjunction with modelling multi-wavelength spectra and covariance spectra. In this study, we focus on the timing properties of two classically distinct ULXs: NGC 5204 X-1 and NGC 55 ULX-1, both of which are known to emit relativistic winds, as observed by modelling emission line spectra with photo-ionisation models such as SPEX, but are thought to be viewed at slightly different inclinations due to differences in the hardness-ratio of their X-ray continuum. Here, we perform an extensive timing study of the two ULXs to reveal new constraints on the accretion disc size scale and structure. We also perform an accelerated search for pulsations (including the correction for an orbital modulation of the signal) to discover significant spin-periodicities in both NGC 5204 X-1 and NGC 55 ULX-1 (at $> 3σ$ significance).

</details>


### [356] [A Brush with Turbulence: Probing the ICM Dynamics in the Merging Toothbrush Cluster using XRISM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05351)
*Anjali Madangarli,Aurora Simionescu,Huub Röttgering,Anwesh Majumder,Soeda Haruse,Kazuhiro Nakazawa,Yuusuke Uchida,Daisuke Ito,Gianfranco Brunetti,Reinout van Weeren,Congyao Zhang,Kamlesh Rajpurohit,Priyanka Chakraborty,Hiroki Akamatsu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用167 ks XRISM/Resolve观测对Toothbrush星系团南部X射线最亮区开展ICM动力学分析，发现至少三个子星系团存在跨声速/超声速视向整体速度差异（|Δv|达~1635–2520 km/s），而速度弥散仅~215–310 km/s，对应湍流压强支撑仅3–6%，表明合并几何具有显著视向分量且系统较为年轻、湍流级联尚未充分建立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Toothbrush星系团具有射电遗迹、射电晕及高度扰动的X射线形态，兼具激波驱动与湍流再加速特征，但合并动能如何在不同空间尺度上分配到气体运动仍不清楚，因此需要探测其内禀介质（ICM）的动力学结构。

Method: 使用167 ks XRISM/Resolve观测，聚焦星系团南部X射线最亮区域；在3–8 keV能段以单组分和多组分热模型拟合光谱，开展全阵列与四象限子阵列分析，提取视向整体速度差与一维速度弥散，并约束湍流压强支撑及气体温度分布。

Result: 全阵列谱偏好双ICM组分模型，视向整体速度差Δv_bulk=1635(+165/-110) km/s，速度弥散较低（σ_v,1D~245–310 km/s）；子阵列分析在至少三个象限探测到多组分，视向整体速度从-2520(+295/-230)到+930(+70/-110) km/s，速度弥散仍维持在~215 km/s，对应观测线宽主导尺度上的湍流压强支撑约3–6%；NE与SW探测到较冷气体（kT_NE=3.13(+0.33/-0.30) keV，kT_SW=2.32(+0.43/-0.37) keV），可能与弱透镜和光学研究识别的第三个北侧子星系团相关。

Conclusion: ICM运动学结构复杂，至少存在三个子星系团，呈超声速整体速度偏移而速度弥散很小；这表明合并几何具有显著视向分量，且星系团碰撞可能相对年轻，湍流级联尚未完全建立。

Abstract: The Toothbrush Cluster (1RXS J0603.3+4214) is known for its northern radio relic, radio halo, and highly disturbed X-ray morphology. It shows features of both shock-driven and turbulent reacceleration, the latter of which is invoked to explain its radio halo. However, how kinetic energy from the merger is distributed into gas motions across spatial scales remains unclear. We probe the intracluster medium (ICM) dynamics in the southern, X-ray-brightest region of the cluster using a 167 ks XRISM/Resolve observation. We conduct both full-array and quadrant sub-array analyses by fitting the spectra with single and multi-component thermal models in the 3-8 keV band. The full-array spectrum favours two ICM components with a transonic line-of-sight (LOS) velocity difference of $Δv_{\mathrm{bulk}}=1635^{+165}_{-110}$ km s$^{-1}$, while the velocity dispersions are much lower, at $σ_{v,1\mathrm{D}}\sim$245-310 km s$^{-1}$. The sub-array analysis likewise reveals multiple components in at least three quadrants, with LOS bulk velocities ranging from $-2520^{+295}_{-230}$ to $+930^{+70}_{-110}$ km s$^{-1}$. The velocity dispersions remain modest at $\sim$215 km s$^{-1}$, corresponding to a turbulent pressure support of about 3-6% on scales dominating the observed line broadening. Cooler gas is detected in the NE and SW, with $kT_{\mathrm{NE}}=3.13^{+0.33}_{-0.30}$ keV and $kT_{\mathrm{SW}}=2.32^{+0.43}_{-0.37}$ keV, likely associated with a third, northern subcluster identified in weak-lensing and optical studies. Our results indicate a complex ICM kinematic structure, with evidence for at least three subclusters exhibiting supersonic bulk velocity offsets and much smaller velocity dispersions. This shows that the merger geometry has a significant line-of-sight component and may indicate that the cluster collision is relatively young, with the turbulent cascade not yet fully established.

</details>


### [357] [Radiation Reaction in Relativistic Magnetized Shocks of Neutron Star Magnetospheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05386)
*Arno Vanthieghem,Amir Levinson*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过稳态多流体模型与PIC模拟研究强冷却相对论磁化激波，发现激波转变仅由阿尔芬马赫数和冷却参数τ决定，辐射反作用不会淬灭前驱辐射，而是将谱峰移向更高频（∝τ^{2/7}）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁星内磁层中快磁声波陡化可产生巨型激波，其前驱辐射可能解释快速射电暴（FRB）。对于足够亮的暴，穿越激波的粒子在一个回旋周期内辐射大部分能量，因此需要厘清辐射反作用如何影响激波结构及其相干辐射。

Method: 采用包含Landau-Lifshitz辐射反作用力的稳态多流体模型，并结合巨型激波动力学的粒子模拟（PIC）。

Result: 激波动理学尺度转变仅由两个无量纲参数控制：阿尔芬马赫数与冷却参数τ（衡量每回旋周期的辐射损失）。相干前驱辐射在临界马赫数以上被触发，该阈值随τ增大，此时束流动力学变为混沌。超过阈值后，辐射冷却使前驱谱峰向高频移动，标度为τ^{2/7}。即使在强辐射冷却下，巨型激波仍远高于发射阈值。前驱波光度仅由驱动kHz波的光度与转换效率ε_X∼10^{-3}–10^{-2}决定。

Conclusion: 辐射反作用不会淬灭前驱辐射；相反，它主要将谱峰移入动力学尺度区间。巨型激波在强冷却下依然远高于发射阈值。

Abstract: The steepening of fast magnetosonic waves in the inner magnetospheres of magnetars can produce monster shocks, whose precursor emission may account for fast radio bursts (FRBs). For sufficiently bright bursts, particles crossing the shock radiate their energy within a single gyration period, raising the question of how radiation reaction affects the shock structure and its coherent emission. We investigate strongly cooled, relativistically magnetized shocks using a stationary multifluid model generalized to include the Landau-Lifshitz radiation-reaction force, together with particle-in-cell simulations of monster-shock dynamics. We show that the kinetic-scale shock transition is governed by only two dimensionless parameters: the Alfvénic Mach number and a cooling parameter $τ$ that measures radiative losses over a gyration period. Coherent precursor emission is triggered above a critical Mach number that increases with $τ$, at which the beam dynamics becomes chaotic. Above this threshold, radiative cooling shifts the peak frequency of the precursor to higher values, scaling as $τ^{2/7}$. Remarkably, monster shocks remain far above the emission threshold even under strong radiative cooling. Radiation reaction therefore does not quench the precursor emission; instead, it primarily shifts the spectral peak into the kinetic regime. The luminosity of the precursor wave is determined solely by the luminosity of the driving kHz wave and the conversion efficiency, $ε_X\sim 10^{-3} - 10^{-2}$.

</details>


### [358] [A Physically Motivated Compact Parameterization of Neutron Star Atmosphere Emission for Thermal X-ray Pulse-Profile Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05720)
*Tong Zhao,Renxin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 提出紧致经验参数化描述中子星大气比强度，以8个角度系数+5个谱核系数拟合数值大气表，脉冲轮廓精度约4%-5%，并指出强度角依赖不能被简单的线性函数刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热X射线脉冲轮廓建模依赖中子星大气发射的角度与谱性质，需要高效且准确的比强度描述以提升解析与数值建模效率，并检验此前基于线性角依赖假设的结论。

Method: 基于辐射转移方程形式解构造角度依赖处方（8个系数），用5个系数构造归一化谱核并保持给定热流；对标数值大气表校准该参数族，重点针对回电流加热与完全电离氢大气；将其嵌入半解析脉冲轮廓模型，并用不同束流处方生成合成eXTP观测进行参数推断比较。

Result: 归一化脉冲轮廓在反向对跖热斑下误差约4%以内，非对跖热斑约5%以内；合成eXTP观测比较显示强度角依赖的具体形状与能量依赖显著影响参数推断，无法用发射角余弦的线性函数简单描述。

Conclusion: 该紧致参数化显著提升解析与数值模型效率，为考察大气假设如何影响脉冲轮廓及推断的星体性质提供统一框架；此前线性角依赖结论需要修正。

Abstract: Thermal X-ray pulse-profile modeling depends on the angular and spectral properties of neutron star atmosphere emission. We construct a compact empirical approximation to the specific intensity using eight coefficients for the angular dependence and five for a normalized spectral kernel. The angular prescription is motivated by the formal solution of the radiative-transfer equation, while the spectral normalization preserves a specified bolometric flux. We calibrate this family to numerical atmosphere tables, focusing on return-current-heated and fully ionized hydrogen atmospheres. When incorporated into a semi-analytic pulse-profile model, the approximation reproduces profiles normalized by their phase-averaged flux to within approximately $4\%$ for antipodal hot spots and $5\%$ for non-antipodal hot spots in the tested heated-atmosphere configurations. We also analyze synthetic eXTP observations using different beaming prescriptions to test the conclusion in our previous work based on a linear description of the angular dependence of the emission. The comparisons suggest that the angular dependence of the intensity cannot be simply characterized by a linear function of the cosine value of the emission angle, because its detailed shape and energy dependence will affect the parameter inference. This parameterization can significantly increase the efficiency for analytic and numerical models, and provide a compact framework for examining how atmosphere assumptions affect neutron star pulse profiles and the inferred stellar properties.

</details>


### [359] [Energy-independent X-Ray Polarization in 4U 1630-47 in the Intermediate State from Spectroscopy and Polarimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06218)
*Elena Seifina,Lev Titarchuk,Paolo Soffitta,Lorenzo Marra,Fiamma Capitanio,Fabio Muleri,Anastasiia Emelianova*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用IXPE和NICER对黑洞X射线双星4U 1630−47的观测，发现中间态下2–8 keV偏振度约6%且几乎不随能量变化，并用平几何康普顿云的康普顿化模型解释了该现象并推导出光学厚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IXPE使X射线双星偏振测量成为可能，偏振度对双星倾角与发射介质光学厚度τ₀的依赖成为X射线天文学的新工具。作者选择银河系中性质尚不明确的黑洞X射线双星4U 1630−47作为研究对象，旨在借助这一新工具约束其辐射几何与物理参数。

Method: 分析IXPE与NICER对4U 1630−47爆发期的能谱与偏振观测，选取源处于单一谱态（中间态）的时间区间。用由康普顿化成分与铁线（高斯）成分构成的宽波段物理模型拟合能谱；并针对平几何康普顿云，用迭代法求解完整辐射转移方程，结合倾角69±1°与(P, cos i, τ₀)关系确定光学厚度。

Result: 在该中间态期间，2–8 keV范围内偏振度未随光子能量显著变化，约为6%；能谱拟合给出光子指数Γ≈2.5、电子温度kT_e≈9 keV；由此得到康普顿云半光学厚度τ₀≈1.8。

Conclusion: P(E)的能量无关性可由平康普顿云中的软光子康普顿化（上散射）自然解释，且该模型能同时再现宽波段能谱；该方法可在观测到的能量无关偏振前提下简便地约束康普顿云光学厚度，至少在4U 1630−47的2–8 keV能段得到验证。

Abstract: The IXPE mission enabled measurements of X-ray polarization in accreting binaries. The dependence of the X-ray polarization degree on the binary inclination and the optical depth of the emitting medium $τ_0$ are the new important tools in X-ray Astronomy. We analyzed the black hole X-ray binary 4U~1630--47 as an example of one of the enigmatic sources in our Galaxy. We consider spectral and polarimetric observations of outburst episodes from 4U~1630--47 observed with the IXPE and the NICER. We carefully investigated a time interval of the IXPE observations during which 4U~1630--47 was in a single spectral state (the intermediate state) and found that the polarization degree did not change significantly during this time interval with the photon energy in the 2--8 keV range ($P\sim$ 6\%). We interpreted this energy independence behavior in $P(E)$ as a result of the Comptonization (up-scattering) of soft photons in the flat Compton cloud. We argued that the X-ray source broadband energy spectra can be reproduced by a physical model composed of the Comptonized component and iron-line ({\it Gaussian}) component. Spectral analysis using this model shows that during the intermediate state the 4U~1630--47 spectrum was characterized by a photon index $Γ\sim 2.5$ and the electron temperature $kT_e\sim ~9 ~{\rm keV}$. In fact, this approach allows one to easily find the half-optical depth $τ_0\sim 1.8$ of the Compton cloud in 4U~1630--47 applying the inclination $69\pm1^\circ$ and using the relation $(P,\cos(i),τ_0)$ when solving the full radiative transfer equation for a flat Compton cloud by an iterative method. This approach is also accompanied by the fact that the polarization degree is independent of the photon energy, which we demonstrate observationally, at least for 4U~1630--47 in the 2--8 keV range.

</details>


### [360] [Continuous Gravitational Waves from Thermo-Elastic Mountains in Ultraluminous X-ray Pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06299)
*Hong-Bo Li,Ziming Wang,Lijing Shao,Ren-Xin Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究超亮X射线脉冲星（ULXP）吸积壳层中磁致温度不对称产生的热弹性"山"，求解完整各向异性热传导方程，发现弱场近似在约10^13 G处失效，强场下非线性热输运显著改变四极热响应并产生可观的热弹性质量四极矩；自旋演化表明中子星可以约20 ms周期进入超爱丁顿吸积阶段，这类快速旋转的银河系ULXP有望落入下一代探测器（ET、CE）的探测灵敏度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速自转、非轴对称形变的中子星是连续引力波（CGW）探测的理想目标。ULXP具有超爱丁顿吸积和强磁场，可能产生显著非轴对称形变，但以往对磁致热弹性"山"的处理多采用微扰近似，未覆盖ULXP所需的强磁场区域，因此需要将热山物理推广到强磁化非线性格局。

Method: 建立吸积柱模型并与壳层热结构耦合，求解完整的各向异性热传导方程，将先前的微扰处理拓展至非线性、强磁化区域，计算磁致温度不对称及其产生的热弹性质量四极矩。

Result: 弱场近似在参考磁场约10^13 G附近开始失效，超过该值后非线性各向异性热输运显著修正四极热响应；在更高场强下热不对称产生可观的热弹性质量四极矩。现有已知ULXP因自转较慢且多数距离较远而不适合作为CGW目标，但示例性自旋演化显示中子星可以约20 ms的自转周期进入超爱丁顿吸积阶段。

Conclusion: 快速旋转的银河系ULXP可能处于Einstein Telescope和Cosmic Explorer等下一代探测器的投影灵敏度范围内。该工作将热山物理拓展到与ULXP相关的强磁化区域，并把超爱丁顿吸积与潜在的CGW观测联系起来。

Abstract: Fast-spinning, nonaxisymmetrically deformed neutron stars (NSs) are promising targets for continuous gravitational-wave (CGW) searches with current and next-generation ground-based detectors. Ultraluminous X-ray pulsars (ULXPs), characterized by super-Eddington accretion and strong magnetic fields, provide extreme environments in which substantial nonaxisymmetric deformations may develop. In this Letter, we investigate thermo-elastic mountains generated by magnetically induced temperature asymmetries in the accreted crusts of ULXPs. We connect the crustal thermal structure to an accretion-column model and solve the full anisotropic heat-transport equation, extending previous perturbative treatments into the nonlinear, strongly magnetized regime. We find that the weak-field approximation begins to break down near $B_{\rm ref}\sim10^{13}\,{\rm G}$, beyond which nonlinear anisotropic heat transport significantly modifies the quadrupolar thermal response. The resulting thermal asymmetries generate substantial thermo-elastic mass quadrupoles at higher field strengths. Although the currently known ULXPs are unfavorable CGW targets because of their relatively slow spins and, in most cases, large distances, our illustrative spin-evolution calculations show that a NS can enter the super-Eddington accretor phase with a spin period of order $20\,{\rm ms}$. Such rapidly rotating Galactic ULXPs could lie within the projected sensitivities of next-generation detectors such as the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer. These results extend thermal-mountain physics into the strongly magnetized regime relevant to ULXPs and connect super-Eddington accretion with prospective CGW observations.

</details>


### [361] [Centaurus A: Opportunities to unveil particle acceleration with the CTAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06550)
*Cainã de Oliveira,Edivaldo Moura Santos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用CTAO模拟观测，评估其对半人马座A伽马射线起源（pc级喷流与南巨瓣中的粒子加速机制）的区分能力，涉及轻子与强子模型、点源与扩展源形态的判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半人马座A作为最近的射电星系，是多波段重要观测对象且为已知伽马射线源，但其高能伽马射线辐射的物理机制（粒子加速与辐射过程）仍不明确，需要更高灵敏度和角分辨能力的下一代观测设施加以约束。

Method: 基于CTAO Alpha配置的仪器响应函数（IRF）开展模拟观测，分别针对喷流的轻子与强子起源、不同曝光时间（t=50 h与200 h）以及巨瓣（S1区）的弥散与纤维状发射模板进行谱与形态分析；假设已知背景模型与乐观天顶角θ=20°。

Result: t=200 h长曝光可探测延伸至数十TeV的硬谱成分；形态模拟表明在t=50 h、θ=20°下CTAO能分辨沿喷流的扩展伽马辐射，区分点源与高斯空间模型；对S1区巨瓣，CTAO-South对质子能量超过10 TeV的强子情景具有灵敏度。

Conclusion: CTAO对半人马座A的观测有望在揭示射电星系高能辐射与粒子加速机制方面取得决定性进展。

Abstract: As the nearest radio galaxy, Centaurus A (Cen A) has been observed across multiple wavelengths and over a broad range of angular scales, providing valuable insights into the functioning of radio galaxies. However, despite being a known source of gamma rays, the physical mechanisms responsible for these high energy emissions remain unclear. In this study, we explore the capabilities of the Cherenkov Telescope Array Observatory (CTAO) to provide insights into the origin of the gamma-ray emission from Cen A. Using simulated observations based on the CTAO Instrument Response Functions (IRF) for the Alpha Configuration of telescopes, we investigate both the spectral and morphological domains to assess the observatory's potential to constrain competing models of particle acceleration in the kiloparsec-scale jet and the Southern Giant Lobe. For the jet, we explore CTAO observations with t=50 and 200h for different leptonic and hadronic origin scenarios for the very-high-energy gamma-ray emission. We show that longer observation campaigns (t=200h) would probe the presence of hard spectral components extending to tens of TeV. Morphological simulations demonstrate that CTAO can resolve the extended gamma-ray emission along the jet, discriminating between point-like and Gaussian spatial models within t=50h, assuming a known background model and an optimistic zenith angle $\vartheta=$20°. Under similar hypotheses, we studied the Giant Lobes evaluating both diffuse and filamentary emission templates for the S1 region detected by Fermi-LAT. We found that CTAO-South can be sensitive to hadronic scenarios with proton energies extending beyond 10 TeV, for the Alpha Configuration and $\vartheta=$20°. Overall, our results indicate that CTAO observations of Cen A can provide a decisive step toward unveiling the mechanisms driving the high-energy emission and particle acceleration in radio galaxies

</details>


### [362] [Can Historic Measurements cast light on the Spectral Differences found between the Pierre Auger Observatory and the Telescope Array?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06592)
*Quentin Luce,Alan A. Watson*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过调整踝区以下能量标度，历史宇宙线数据与Auger谱在30 EeV以上吻合，但TA与Auger的谱差异仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pierre Auger Observatory与Telescope Array Collaboration报告的宇宙线能谱存在众所周知的差异，作者希望理解这些差异的来源。

Method: 以Auger Collaboration报告的能谱为参照，研究AGASA、Volcano Ranch和Haverah Park早期测量通量的残差；在踝区之前（预期两半球通量相同的区域）对能量进行经验性调整。

Result: 经能量经验调整后，历史数据与Pierre Auger Observatory在30 EeV以上的测量结果吻合良好。

Conclusion: Telescope Array与Auger Observatory所测能谱之间的张力依然存在。

Abstract: The differences between the energy spectra reported by the Pierre Auger Observatory and the Telescope Array Collaboration are well-known. In an effort to understand these differences, we have studied the residuals of the fluxes measured in early work at AGASA, Volcano Ranch and Haverah Park with reference to the spectrum reported by the Auger Collaboration. After an empirical adjustment to the energies in the region before the ankle, where fluxes from the two hemispheres are expected to be the same, it is found that historic data are in good agreement with measurements made at the Pierre Auger Observatory above 30 EeV. Thus, the tensions between the spectra measured by the Telescope Array and the Auger Observatory remain.

</details>


### [363] [Pair echo model of CTAO/LST-1 signal from GRB 221009A](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06633)
*Andrii Neronov,Ievgen Vovk,Teneman Keita,Davide Miceli*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 结合LHAASO与CTAO/LST-1对GRB 221009A的观测数据，可用"对回波"（pair echo）模型解释延迟信号，并据此估算星系际磁场（IGMF）强度：当相关长度>20 kpc时，log(B/G)≈-16.2±0.7；短相关长度情形下强度约在1e-14–1e-12 G。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用甚高能伽马射线在星系际介质传播中产生的延迟次级"对回波"信号，探测宇宙大尺度结构空洞中的星系际磁场（IGMF）——目前该磁场缺乏直接测量手段。

Method: 将LHAASO与CTAO/LST-1对伽马射线暴GRB 221009A的联合观测数据与对回波模型进行比对；以LST-1在爆发后1.33天测得的源信号作为候选对回波，反演IGMF的场强B与相关长度λ_B。

Result: 数据与对回波模型相符。若相关长度λ_B>20 kpc，LST-1延迟信号对应log(B/G)=-16.2±0.7；若λ_B<20 kpc，场强估计值需按√(20 kpc/λ_B)向上修正。对于源自宇宙学相变的短相关长度遗迹磁场，该模型给出场强约1e-14–1e-12 G。

Conclusion: 所得IGMF参数与现有全部已知约束（包括活动星系核伽马射线观测给出的下限）一致，表明对回波方法可用于探测空洞中的星系际磁场。

Abstract: Interactions of very-high energy gamma-rays during their propagation through the intergalactic medium result in a delayed secondary ``pair echo'' signal. Detection of such pair echo can be used for measurements of intergalactic magnetic fields (IGMF) in the voids of the Large Scale Structure of the Universe. We notice that a combination of data of LHAASO and CTAO/LST-1 telescopes on the gamma-ray burst GRB 221009A is consistent with the pair echo model. The LST-1 measurement of the source signal 1.33 days after the burst can be interpreted as the pair echo produced by very-high-energy gamma-rays propagating through intergalactic medium with magnetic field of the strength log(B/G)=-16.2+/- 0.7, if its correlation length lambda_B is larger than 20 kpc. If the field correlation length is shorter than 20 kpc, the field strength estimate is modified upwards by a square root of (20 kpc / lambda_B). In the specific case of short correlation length IGMF that is relic from cosmological phase transitions, the pair echo model of the LST-1 signal implies the field strength in the 1e-14-1e-12 G range. Such magnetic field parameters are consistent with all known constraints on IGMF, including the lower bounds from gamma-ray observations of active galactic nuclei.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [364] [Noise Emissions from Hyperscale AI Data Centers: A Poorly Characterized Exposure at the Doorstep of Community Health Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03975)
*Davide Bray,Francesca Dominici*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 针对AI数据中心噪声的社区关切，本文通过叙事性综述整合测量、监管、法律与工程证据，指出现有声学数据异质、不可比，无法支撑健康推断，并提出基于标准的现场测量与建模方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模AI算力设施快速扩张，使数据中心噪声成为美国社区反复出现的关切，但缺乏经过校准、符合标准的声学数据，难以进行可信的健康影响推断。

Method: 叙事性综述，综合直接测量数据、监管与法律记录、调查性新闻报道及相关工程文献，梳理已知证据并识别所需测量指标。

Result: 已有数据在住宅、地界和近源位置的声学值差异极大，个体读数从39到超过70 dB(A)；因位置、指标、时长、仪器与来源不同，这些值不可直接比较，也无法构成合并分布或与夜间限值做总体比较。具有频谱细节的案例中，除一例外均报告与投诉相关的连续音调性噪声，多为70–140 Hz低频成分，A计权指标可能低估。工程证据提示冷却架构可能是设施声发射的主要决定因素，但所引15–30 dB区间属设备级声功率估计，并非实测的设施或住宅受点差值，仍属待验证假设。

Conclusion: 作者提出一套基于标准的现场测量与建模协议，在具名设施处获取频率分辨的暴露数据；此类暴露表征是可信评估社区健康效应与环境正义影响的基础。

Abstract: The rapid build-out of hyperscale computing for artificial-intelligence workloads has made data-center noise a recurring source of community concern in the United States, yet calibrated, standards-grade acoustic data remain scarce. This narrative review synthesizes direct measurements, regulatory and legal records, investigative journalism, and relevant engineering literature to assess what is currently known and identify the measurements needed for credible health inference. Available sources report heterogeneous acoustic values at residences, property lines, and near-source positions, with individual readings from 39 to above 70 dB(A). Because location, metric, duration, instrumentation, and provenance differ, these values are not directly comparable community exposures, do not define a pooled distribution, and do not support an aggregate comparison with nighttime limits. Among cases with spectral detail, all but one reported continuous tonal noise associated with complaints, most often low-frequency content near 70-140 Hz, that A-weighted metrics alone may under-represent. Engineering evidence further suggests that cooling architecture may be a major determinant of facility sound emission; the cited 15-30 dB span is an equipment-level sound-power estimate, not a measured facility- or residential-receptor difference, and remains a hypothesis pending comparative field measurements. We propose a standards-based field-measurement and modeling protocol designed to generate frequency-resolved exposure data at named facilities. Such exposure characterization is a necessary foundation for credible assessment of community health effects and environmental justice implications.

</details>


### [365] [Multi-Target Tracking of Cooperating Swarms by an Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04127)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出一种新粒子滤波器，能表示目标间协同集群运动，并证明其收敛至真实贝叶斯后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逐目标标注随机有限集滤波器（如δ-广义标注多伯努利滤波器）无法表示目标间协同的、集群式运动。针对此局限，作者希望构建可建模目标间耦合运动的滤波器。

Method: 将联合粒子滤波器与集合高斯混合滤波器相耦合：测量更新作用于每个粒子处的密度，而非仅对粒子权重进行重加权。

Result: 在粒子数趋于无穷的极限下，证明新粒子滤波器收敛于联合粒子滤波器，从而在特定假设下收敛至真实贝叶斯后验；在Vicsek模型与耦合布朗运动两个耦合系统上的实验验证了滤波器有效性。

Conclusion: 该滤波器突破了逐目标标注随机有限集滤波器对协同集群运动的表示限制，具备理论收敛保证，并在耦合系统中表现有效。

Abstract: Per-target labeled random-finite-set filters, such as the state of the art $δ$-generalized labeled multi-Bernoulli filter, cannot represent cooperating, swarm-like, motion between targets. In this work, we present a new filter that can. The new particle filter couples the joint particle filter with ensemble Gaussian mixture filter such that a measurement updates the density at every particle instead of merely reweighting. We prove that the new particle filter converges to the joint particle filter in the limit of particle number and thus to the true Bayesian posterior, under certain assumptions. Experimental results on two coupled systems, the Vicsek model and coupled Brownian motion provide evidence on the efficacy of the filter.

</details>


### [366] [A Markov Chain Approach to Modeling Rugby Union](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04813)
*Kenny Watts,Jonathan Pipping-Gamón*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个马尔可夫链框架，将橄榄球联盟比赛建模为基于分差、剩余时间和场地位置的有限状态空间，用历史比赛数据估计转移概率，从而支持比赛轨迹模拟与胜负概率、期望得分分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 橄榄球联盟具有连续比赛、控球时长可变、多种得分方式等特点，使标准建模方法难以适用；同时现有模型多只预测最终结果，缺乏对比赛状态演化的刻画。

Method: 构建有限状态空间的马尔可夫链模型，状态由比分差、剩余时间和场地位置组成；利用历史比赛数据估计状态间转移概率，并据此进行比赛模拟，计算任意状态下的获胜概率和期望得分。

Result: 通过校准分析和与仅预测最终结果的模型对比进行验证；该状态空间方法产生了校准良好且具竞争力的估计，并能进一步分析比赛在状态间如何演化。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫链状态空间建模可有效刻画橄榄球联盟比赛动态，在保持预测性能的同时提供对比赛过程中状态转移的可解释分析。

Abstract: Rugby Union's continuous play, variable-length possessions, and multiple scoring outcomes make it difficult to model with standard approaches. We present a Markov Chain framework that represents Rugby Union as a finite state space over score differential, remaining time, and pitch location, with transition probabilities estimated from historical match data. The resulting model supports simulation and analysis of in-game trajectories, including win probability and expected point estimates from any given state. We validate the model through calibration analysis and comparison to models that predict only final outcomes. Our state-space approach produces well-calibrated, competitive estimates while additionally enabling analysis of how games evolve between states.

</details>


### [367] [Reproducibility and Leakage-Controlled Evaluation of Machine-Learning Models for Chronic Kidney Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04926)
*Lening Yang,Yichuan Zhao*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 在严格防数据泄漏的实验流程下复现UCI慢性肾病分类研究，内部性能仍接近完美（交叉验证准确率0.9869，留出集0.9833），但性能主要由临床邻近的实验室/尿检变量与肾病相关缺失模式驱动，不构成临床验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本临床数据集上近乎完美的分类结果往往依赖预处理、数据泄漏、缺失值处理和评估样本的差异。作者希望通过对既有慢性肾病（CKD）分类研究的复现，在明确的泄漏控制条件下检验其高精度是否真实可靠、是否依赖特定流程设计。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400条记录、24个预测变量）上固定分层70:30的开发集—留出集划分（划分先于任何可学习预处理）；插补、编码、缩放、监督式特征选择与PCA仅在训练折内拟合。比较9种分类器、3种特征表示、3种缺失值策略，采用5×3重复分层交叉验证，仅依据训练交叉验证排名（并列如实报告）；冻结一组最终模型在留出集上单次评估（判别、校准、Brier分数），视作锁定的历史内部样本。另做消融、分组SHAP值、三个额外随机种子及事后敏感性分析考察稳健性。

Result: 排名最高的"选定特征"流程（KNN插补+Extra Trees）重复交叉验证准确率0.9869，留出集准确率0.9833（精确95%区间0.9411–0.9980）。PCA（27个主成分，解释95.96%方差）相对可解释的原始特征表示仅有微小且依赖随机种子的优势，多个流程在统计上难以区分。对选择过程做嵌套重采样得到的"外层"准确率为0.9845，比同折上排名最高的流程低0.0095；仅用缺失指示变量即可得到0.85–0.88的ROC面积；移除肾脏、尿液和血液学字段后准确率降至0.89。

Conclusion: 在泄漏控制后高内部性能依然存在，但主要由临床邻近的实验室与尿检变量以及CKD相关的缺失模式驱动；该结果并非临床验证，仍需外部与前瞻性评估。

Abstract: Near-perfect classification of small clinical datasets can hinge on preprocessing, data leakage, missing-data handling, and the evaluation sample. We reproduced a published chronic kidney disease (CKD) classification study on the UCI CKD dataset (400 records, 24 predictors) under explicit leakage controls. A stratified 70:30 development-holdout partition was fixed before any learned preprocessing; imputation, encoding, scaling, supervised feature selection, and PCA were fitted only within training folds. Nine classifiers, three feature representations, and three missing-data strategies were compared by 5x3 repeated stratified cross-validation and ranked on training cross-validation only (ties reported); a frozen set of finalists was evaluated once on the holdout (discrimination, calibration, Brier score), treated as a locked historical internal sample. Ablation, grouped SHAP values, three further seeds, and post hoc sensitivity analyses examined robustness. The top-ranked selected-feature pipeline (KNN imputation, Extra Trees) reached a repeated cross-validation accuracy of 0.9869 and a holdout accuracy of 0.9833 (exact 95% interval 0.9411-0.9980). PCA (27 components, 95.96% of variance) gave only a marginal, seed-dependent advantage over interpretable original-feature representations, and several pipelines were statistically indistinguishable. Nested resampling of the selection procedure gave an outer accuracy of 0.9845, 0.0095 below the top-ranked pipeline on the same folds; missingness indicators alone yielded a ROC area of 0.85-0.88, and removing renal, urinary, and hematologic fields cut accuracy to 0.89. High internal performance persisted after leakage controls but appears driven largely by clinically proximal laboratory and urinalysis variables and CKD-associated missingness; it is not clinical validation, and external, prospective evaluation remains necessary.

</details>


### [368] [Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo-Based Simultaneous Speed Prediction Model for Heterogeneous Traffic on Urban Primary Roads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05027)
*S. M Towhidul Alam,Md. Muhtashim Shahrier,Nazmul Haque,Md Asif Raihan,Md. Hadiuzzaman*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究基于贝叶斯MCMC方法，为达卡城市主干道异质、非车道化交通构建了六类车辆速度的联立预测模型，并验证了其高精度与可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质、非车道化交通环境下，不同车辆类型的运行速度受交通密度影响复杂，缺乏能同时刻画六类车辆速度的预测模型，故需建立联立方程模型以明确各车型专属密度对速度的影响。

Method: 利用无人机视频采集达卡五条城市主干道交通数据，经深度学习方法提取车辆轨迹、交通量、密度与空间平均速度；采用贝叶斯MCMC框架建立覆盖标准小汽车、多用途车、重型车、三轮车、两轮车及非机动车的六个速度预测联立方程，以拟合优度指标评估模型性能。

Result: 六类车辆模型的决定系数为0.922～0.956，均方根误差为0.50～1.56 km/h；运行速度随交通量增加而持续下降，交通组成在中等或高需求时对速度影响显著；剔除非机动车后运行速度提高且拥堵延迟；可迁移性研究表明模型在独立路段上具有可靠的预测能力。

Conclusion: 基于贝叶斯MCMC的联立速度预测模型能高精度刻画异质非车道化交通中各车型运行速度，且具备跨路段迁移预测的可靠性，可为该类交通的运行管理与设计提供依据。

Abstract: Objectives: The study designs a simultaneous speed prediction model based on Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods for heterogeneous, non-lane-based traffic on urban primary roads, with the aim of determining the effects of vehicle-specific traffic densities on the operating speeds of the six vehicle types.
  Methods: The traffic data were obtained from videos recorded by unmanned aerial vehicles on five main urban roads in Dhaka, Bangladesh, and were then processed by a deep learning approach in order to derive information on vehicle trajectories, traffic volumes, densities, and space-mean speeds. Six simultaneous equations for speed prediction were developed using a Bayesian MCMC framework, covering standard cars, utility vehicles, heavy vehicles, three-wheelers, two-wheelers, and non-motorized vehicles. The performance of the models was assessed by means of goodness-of-fit measures.
  Findings: The coefficients of determination for the models varied from 0.922 to 0.956 and the root mean square errors were between 0.50 and 1.56 km/h for the six types of vehicle. Operating speeds decreased steadily as traffic volume increased, although traffic composition had a significant effect on speed when the demand was moderate or high. Operating speeds increased and congestion was delayed when non-motorized vehicles were excluded. The transferability study showed that the models had a reliable ability to make predictions on separate road sections.

</details>


### [369] [How Long Is the Journey to Work? One-Way Commuting Time in the United States from American Community Survey Data, 2006-2024, with Travel-Diary, European and English Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05218)
*Marcus Oyelaran,Daniel R. Whitcombe*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 基于ACS等公开数据，系统并列报告美国及国际通勤时间的分布、方式差异与口径对比：2024年美国16岁以上非居家工作者单程平均27.2分钟，公共交通49.5分钟，长途铁路/轮渡71.0分钟；同时给出长通勤比例、出发时段、州与都市区差异及居家办公比例变化，并强调三种测量口径仅并列不合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均通勤时间是ACS被引用最多的统计量之一，但其分布形态、对交通方式与出发时间的依赖，以及与其他测量口径的关系很少被一并呈现，容易导致跨口径误用与误读。作者旨在把多种公开口径并排展示，明确各自的定义边界。

Method: 使用ACS一年期表格，将公布的总分钟数除以工作者人数得到各交通方式的平均单程通勤时间；统计60分钟以上、90分钟以上通勤者比例及按出发时段、州、大都市区的均值；并引入2022年NHTS、2019年欧盟劳动力调查模块及英格兰出行日记作为对照口径，三套口径仅并列报告、不做合并。

Result: 2024年美国16岁以上非居家工作者单程平均27.2分钟，2006年为25.0分钟，2019年峰值27.6分钟，2021年25.6分钟。通勤铁路、长途火车与轮渡平均71.0分钟，为独自驾车26.5分钟的2.68倍；全部公共交通49.5分钟。2024年9.3%（约1330万人）单程≥60分钟，3.0%≥90分钟；5:00—5:29出发者平均35.1分钟。州均值从纽约州33.2分钟到北达科他州17.5分钟；大都市区从纽约36.5分钟到布法罗21.5分钟。居家办公比例由2019年5.7%升至2021年17.9%，2024年为13.3%。

Conclusion: 美国通勤时间自2006年以来总体小幅上升、疫情期波动明显，且高度依赖交通方式与出发时间，长通勤人群规模可观。国际化对照显示不同调查口径下通勤时长差异显著，三种口径应并列解读而不宜合并，所有输入数据均为公开来源。

Abstract: The mean travel time to work is one of the most cited statistics of the American Community Survey (ACS), yet its distribution, its dependence on mode and departure time and its relation to other measurement bases are rarely set out together. Using ACS 1-year tables, we report that US workers aged 16 and over who did not work from home averaged 27.2 minutes one way in 2024, against 25.0 minutes in 2006, a peak of 27.6 in 2019 and 25.6 in 2021. Dividing published aggregate minutes by workers, commuter rail, long-distance train and ferry riders averaged 71.0 minutes, 2.68 times the 26.5 minutes of solo drivers, and all public transportation 49.5 minutes. In 2024, 9.3% of commuters, about 13.3 million people, travelled 60 minutes or more each way and 3.0% 90 minutes or more; commuters leaving between 5:00 and 5:29 a.m. averaged 35.1 minutes. State means ranged from 33.2 minutes in New York to 17.5 in North Dakota, and big-metro means from 36.5 minutes in New York to 21.5 in Buffalo. Working from home rose from 5.7% of workers in 2019 to 17.9% in 2021 and stood at 13.3% in 2024. The 2022 National Household Travel Survey recorded an average commute trip of 13.43 miles at 25.51 mph. On different definitions, the 2019 EU labour force survey module gave an EU average of 25 minutes and 30 minutes for the United Kingdom, and England's travel diary a commuting trip of 31.5 minutes in 2025 against 26.5 in 2002. The three bases are reported side by side and never pooled. All inputs are public.

</details>


### [370] [From Counting to Continuous Natural Exponential Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05478)
*Shaul K. Bar-Lev*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明：当方差函数 V(m)=m²G(m)（G 为非零非负系数幂级数）时，对应的自然指数族关于勒贝格测度绝对连续；特别地，多项式方差函数中 a₁=0 的情形给出定义在正半轴上的绝对连续 NEF，与 a₁>0 时的计数型 NEF 互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然指数族由其方差函数 (V,M) 刻画，这自然引出反问题：给定函数 V，何时它是某个 NEF 的方差函数，以及能否直接从 V 读出该族的结构性质。已有工作 Bar-Lev (1987) 证明所有非零非负系数多项式都是无穷可分 NEF 的方差函数，且该类按 a₁>0 与 a₁=0 穷尽划分；Bar-Lev, Letac 和 Ridder (2024) 证明 a₁>0（归一化 a₁=1 后）对应支撑在 ℕ₀ 上的计数型 NEF。本文旨在补全 a₁=0 这一互补情形的结构刻画。

Method: 作者直接分析方差函数 V(m)=∑ⱼ aⱼmʲ（aⱼ≥0）的结构。对于 a₁=0 的情形以及更一般的 V(m)=m²G(m)（G 为非零非负系数幂级数），通过方差函数的解析性质论证对应 NEF 的支撑测度关于勒贝格测度绝对连续，并进一步分析当 G 为多项式时均值域 M 的取值。同时考察该结构对累积量结构与统计推断的影响。

Result: 证明 a₁=0 时对应 NEF 关于正半轴上的勒贝格测度绝对连续；更一般地，凡 V(m)=m²G(m)（G 为非零非负系数幂级数）均给出绝对连续 NEF；当 G 为多项式时均值域 M 为整个正半轴 (0,∞)。并给出关于累积量结构与统计推断的若干推论。

Conclusion: 本文与 Bar-Lev, Letac 和 Ridder (2024) 的结果互补，共同给出了非负系数多项式方差函数类的完整结构二分：a₁>0 对应 ℕ₀ 上的计数型 NEF，a₁=0 对应正半轴上的绝对连续 NEF；更一般地，V(m)=m²G(m) 刻画了一类绝对连续 NEF，其均值域在 G 为多项式时为整个正半轴。

Abstract: Natural exponential families (NEFs) are characterized by their variance functions (VFs), namely by the pair \((V,M)\), where \(V\) expresses the variance as a function of the mean and \(M\) is the mean domain. This characterization naturally raises the inverse question: when is a given function \(V\) the VF of an NEF, and what structural properties of the family can be read directly from \(V\)?
  Bar-Lev (1987), as part of a more general result for absolutely monotone functions, showed that every nonzero polynomial \[ V(m)=\sum_{j=1}^{r} a_j m^j,\qquad a_j\ge 0, \] is the VF of an infinitely divisible NEF on a positive mean domain. This polynomial class splits exhaustively into the cases \(a_1>0\) and \(a_1=0\). Bar-Lev, Letac and Ridder (2024) proved that, after scaling so that \(a_1=1\), the first case yields a counting NEF supported on \(\mathbb N_0\).
  We establish the complementary result: when \(a_1=0\), the corresponding NEF is absolutely continuous with respect to Lebesgue measure on the positive half-line. More generally, we prove absolute continuity whenever \[ V(m)=m^2G(m), \] where \(G\) is a nonzero power series with nonnegative coefficients. When \(G\) is a polynomial, the mean domain is the entire positive half-line. We also record several consequences for the cumulant structure and for statistical inference.

</details>


### [371] [LLM Benchmarking via Representation Multi-task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05613)
*Yuqing Xie,Yuxuan Xu,Yang Feng,Yunxiao Chen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个基于表征多任务学习与项目反应理论的统计框架，用于跨多个基准领域更准确地估计LLM的领域得分与总体得分，并证明估计量的极小极大最优性，在MMLU大规模数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在排行榜框架下评估大语言模型能力是现代数据科学与AI的基础难题。现有做法难以解决两个核心问题：(1) 如何跨领域借用信息以获得更准确的领域特定得分；(2) 如何定义并估计跨多领域聚合的总体得分。

Method: 提出结合表征多任务学习（MTL）与项目反应理论（IRT）的统计框架：通过IRT模型定义总体与领域特定的LLM特质，采用MTL方法从项目级响应数据中估计这些特质，并开发计算高效的估计量，在特定渐近条件下证明其极小极大最优性。

Result: 大量模拟实验显示所提方法优于竞争方法；将其应用于Hugging Face Open LLM Leaderboard的MMLU响应数据，覆盖4,272个LLM、56个学科的13,232个项目，实证分析揭示领域规模与难度存在显著异质性，且跨领域依赖呈强正相关。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统化LLM评估提供了严谨的测量基础，兼具理论保证（极小极大最优性）与实证价值，能够生成关于模型能力结构的有意义洞见。

Abstract: Quantifying and evaluating the capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains a fundamental challenge in modern data science and artificial intelligence. In this paper, we consider LLM evaluation based on their performance across items in multiple benchmark domains (e.g., mathematical reasoning and coding) within a leaderboard framework. Our goal is to address two core questions: (1) How do we derive more accurate domain-specific scores by borrowing information across domains? and (2) How do we define and estimate an overall score that aggregates performance across multiple domains? To solve these problems, we propose a novel statistical framework based on representation Multi-task Learning (MTL) and an item response theory model. Specifically, we define overall and domain-specific LLM traits through an Item Response Theory (IRT) model, and propose an MTL approach to estimate these traits from item-level response data. We develop a computationally efficient estimator and establish its minimax optimality under certain asymptotic regimes. This framework provides a rigorous measurement foundation for systematic LLM evaluation. We conduct extensive simulations, demonstrating the superior performance of the proposed method over competing methods. Crucially for the Applications and Case Studies section, we apply the proposed framework to MMLU response data from the Hugging Face Open LLM Leaderboard, covering 4,272 LLMs and 13,232 items across 56 subjects. The empirical analysis reveals substantial heterogeneity in domain size and difficulty, together with strong positive cross-domain dependence, highlighting the practical value and substantive insights generated by our approach.

</details>


### [372] [Within-fire estimates of biomass loss across national forests of the US West Coast: inventory-informed inference with uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05614)
*Elliot S. Shannon,Andrew O. Finley,Paul B. May,Hans-Erik Andersen,Harold Zald,Grant M. Domke,George C. Gaines*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究结合美国森林清查与分析样地数据与30米年度树冠覆盖数据，在贝叶斯时空建模框架下估计2000—2022年美国西部国家森林系统内野火导致的活体地上生物量损失与森林死亡面积，估算结果为9530万吨地上生物量损失和239万公顷森林死亡面积，并揭示火场内部显著的空间异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火正在重塑美国西部森林，但现有野火影响评估多依赖过火面积统计或卫星反演的严重度指标，无法直接量化森林生物量变化及其不确定性。

Method: 将美国森林清查与分析（FIA）样地实测数据与30米分辨率年度树冠覆盖数据耦合，构建贝叶斯时空模型；模型同时刻画活体森林的存在/缺失以及活体森林条件下的地上生物量（AGB），从而在像元尺度上实现火烧前后状态的后验预测估计。

Result: 应用于2000—2022年共6239场火灾，估计野火相关活体地上生物量损失为9530万Mg，森林死亡面积为239万公顷，其中2020年累计影响最大；像元尺度估计显示火场内部存在显著空间异质性，过火面积、森林死亡面积与相应生物量损失之间存在较大差异。

Conclusion: 该基于清查数据、显式考虑不确定性的方法为在广域尺度上量化干扰的森林碳影响提供了可扩展框架，同时保留了火场内部的空间细节。

Abstract: Wildfire is reshaping forests in the western United States (US), yet estimates of wildfire impacts often rely on burned-area summaries or satellite-derived severity metrics that do not directly quantify changes in forest biomass or their uncertainty. We combine US Forest Inventory and Analysis plot measurements with annual 30 m tree canopy cover data in a Bayesian spatio-temporal modeling framework to estimate within-fire changes in live aboveground biomass (AGB) and forest mortality area across National Forest System lands in Washington, Oregon, and California. The model represents both live forest presence or absence and AGB conditional on live forest, allowing posterior predictive estimates of pre- and post-fire conditions at the pixel scale. Applied to 6,239 fires from 2000 to 2022, the analysis estimates 95.3 million Mg of wildfire-associated AGB loss and 2.39 million ha of forest mortality area, with the largest aggregate impacts occurring in 2020. Pixel-level estimates reveal substantial spatial heterogeneity within fire perimeters, including large differences between total burned area, forest mortality area, and associated biomass loss. This inventory-informed, uncertainty-aware approach provides a scalable framework for quantifying forest carbon impacts of disturbance across broad regions while retaining within-fire spatial detail.

</details>


### [373] [Does the AI Productivity Dividend Diminish? Does the AI Productivity Dividend Diminish? A Validated Framework for Identifying the Shape of Firm-Level Returns to Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05629)
*Louis Agyekum*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一套基于设计的框架，用关联的企业—工人数据测量AI对生产率回报的“形状”（线性、递减还是被竞争消解），并以ChatGPT发布为断点进行连续处理双重差分，在多组件上完成校准合成面板验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI生产率红利的预测相差一个数量级，部分原因在于它们外推的是早期高暴露采用者的平均收益。这些收益究竟线性增长、趋于平缓还是被竞争消解，是关于AI回报“形状”而非平均水平的经验问题，但现有研究缺乏可信的测量框架。

Method: 构建基于设计的测量框架，整合：预先确定的、基于职业的企业生成式AI暴露度指标；以2022年11月ChatGPT发布为节点的连续处理双重差分；三项预先设定的凹性检验；将暴露效应分解为采用边际与每采用者回报；使用强度分析的双稳健估计；早期AI采用者的交错采用分析；以及将AI置于生产率运动方程中的生产函数估计。所有组件在按加拿大统计局关联企业微观数据结构校准、并嵌入已知凹效应的2万家企业合成面板上验证。

Result: 估计量能恢复真实剂量—反应曲线并检测其凹性（斜率差-0.010，标准误0.003，单侧p=0.002）；主置信区间的蒙特卡洛覆盖率为0.97，凹性检验功效为0.93；同时暴露出一个实践陷阱：在约40个行业层面聚类的预趋势检验会过度拒绝真实原假设。

Conclusion: 该框架以开源代码形式发布，已可用于加拿大企业微观数据，将有助于估计潜在产出以及加拿大生产率差距的持续性。

Abstract: Forecasts of the productivity dividend from artificial intelligence (AI) differ by an order of magnitude, partly because they extrapolate average gains observed among early, highly exposed adopters. Whether those gains scale linearly, flatten, or are competed away is an empirical question about the shape of the return to AI, not its average. This paper develops and validates a design-based framework for measuring that shape with linked firm-worker data. The framework combines a pre-determined, occupation-based measure of firm exposure to generative AI with a continuous-treatment difference-in-differences design around the release of ChatGPT in November 2022, three pre-specified tests of concavity, a decomposition of exposure effects into an adoption margin and a per-adopter return, a doubly robust analysis of use intensity, a staggered-adoption analysis of earlier AI adopters, and a production-function estimator that places AI in the law of motion of productivity. We validate every component on a calibrated synthetic panel of 20,000 enterprises that reproduces the structure of Statistics Canada's linked business microdata and embeds a known concave effect. The estimators recover the true dose-response curve and detect its concavity (slope difference -0.010, standard error 0.003, one-sided p = 0.002); Monte Carlo coverage of the main confidence interval is 0.97, and the concavity test has power of 0.93. The validation also exposes a practical pitfall: pre-trend tests clustered at roughly 40 industries over-reject a true null. The framework, released as open code, is ready for application to Canadian enterprise microdata, where it will inform estimates of potential output and the persistence of Canada's productivity gap.

</details>


### [374] [Spatio-Temporal Stochastic Interventions for Causal Inference in Climate Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05827)
*Samuel Baugh*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出时空随机干预框架，用正则化估计量处理气候因果推断中的时空依赖与处理重叠不足问题，并应用于历史变暖对蒸汽压亏缺的因果效应估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候科学中估计因果效应（如人为变暖对作物损失的影响）存在困难，原因在于复杂的时空依赖性以及处理变量的高维性；这些因素导致观测情景与反事实情景之间重叠（overlap）很差。

Method: 提出用于从气候观测中估计因果效应的时空随机干预（spatio-temporal stochastic-intervention）框架；引入随机干预处理效应的正则化估计量，以可控偏差换取由重叠不足引起的权重方差降低。

Result: 模拟研究显示，该估计量相比其他加权估计量具有更低的均方误差，并消除了未调整估计量的混杂偏差。应用于估计历史变暖对蒸汽压亏缺（作物胁迫驱动因子）的效应，并对降水进行调整。在GISS-E2-1-G气候模型模拟中，全球效应自1995年起每年均可与零区分；省略降水调整会使全球估计膨胀47%；调整使8%的全球农田（1.25亿公顷）上的估计符号反转。

Conclusion: 调整与否会改变估计符号，未调整分析可能在以热量为主和以水分为主的适应措施之间产生误导；该框架为气候观测的因果推断提供了更稳健的工具。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects in climate science, such as the effect of anthropogenic warming on crop loss, is challenging because of complex spatio-temporal dependence and the high-dimensional nature of the treatment. To address this dependence and the resulting poor overlap between observed and counterfactual scenarios, we develop a spatio-temporal stochastic-intervention framework for estimating causal effects from climate observations. We introduce a regularized estimator of the stochastic-intervention treatment effect that trades a controlled bias for a reduction in the weight variance caused by poor overlap. Simulation studies show that this estimator attains lower mean squared error than alternative weighting estimators and removes the confounding bias of an unadjusted estimator. We apply the framework to estimate the effect of historical warming on vapor-pressure deficit, a driver of crop stress, adjusting for precipitation, which confounds the effect by affecting both temperature and humidity. In GISS-E2-1-G climate-model simulations, the global effect is distinguishable from zero in every year from 1995 onward, and omitting the precipitation adjustment inflates the global estimate by 47%. Adjustment reverses the sign of the estimate over 8% of global cropland (125 million hectares), where an unadjusted analysis could misdirect adaptation between heat-focused and moisture-focused measures.

</details>


### [375] [Evaluating LiDAR Data Sources, Predictor Resolution, and Spatial Random Effects in Bayesian Change-of-Support Models for Forest Inventory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06476)
*Arthur Spitta,Andrew O. Finley,Christoph Gollob,Ralf Kraßnitzer,Tim Ritter,Andreas Tockner,Arne Nothdurft*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该研究利用贝叶斯变化支持模型对比公开ALS与新采集ULS数据在混交林林分蓄积量估计中的表现，发现ULS模型精度更高、不确定性更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林经营需要及时的林分尺度信息。模型化估计将稀疏样地数据与遥感辅助数据结合以估测小面积单元的蓄积量，在混交、结构异质林中尤为重要。无人机激光雷达（ULS）可灵活获取高分辨率LiDAR数据，但其相对于传统机载激光雷达（ALS）在模型化推断中的优势尚不明确。

Method: 在德国东北部一片混交林，采用贝叶斯变化支持模型，对比公开ALS数据与新采集ULS数据用于GSV估计，并评估分布型LiDAR指标与空间随机效应的作用。

Result: ULS模型表现持续优于ALS模型，交叉验证RMSPE分别为68.5 m³/ha与79.5 m³/ha，预测误差降低13.8%。分布型指标对ULS模型的增益更大（RMSPE最多降低10.1%），而空间效应改善有限且计算成本更高。ULS模型对潜变量林分平均GSV的林分尺度预测不确定性更低。

Conclusion: 及时且信息丰富的LiDAR数据对林分尺度GSV估计可能比复杂的空间模型结构更有价值。ULS在可实现及时采集与高分辨率冠层刻画的场景下具有前景。仍需在时间匹配的ALS与ULS数据间开展受控对比，以区分平台效应与时间失配的影响。

Abstract: Forest managers require timely stand-level information for operational planning. Model-based estimation combines sparse field samples with remotely sensed auxiliary data to estimate growing stock volume (GSV) for small-area units. This is particularly relevant in mixed-species and structurally heterogeneous forests, where timely structural information can support management decisions under climate change and associated disturbance pressures. Uncrewed aerial vehicle laser scanning (ULS) provides flexible access to high-resolution LiDAR data, but its benefits over conventional airborne laser scanning (ALS) for model-based inference remain insufficiently understood. We compared public ALS and new ULS data using Bayesian change-of-support models to estimate GSV in a mixed-species forest in north-eastern Germany. We assessed the effects of distributional LiDAR metrics and spatial random effects. ULS models consistently outperformed ALS models, with cross-validated root mean squared prediction errors (RMSPEs) of 68.5 m^3/ha and 79.5 m^3/ha , respectively, a 13.8 % reduction in prediction error. Distributional metrics benefited ULS models more than ALS models, reducing RMSPE by up to 10.1 %, whereas spatial effects yielded minor improvements at greater computational cost. ULS models also exhibited lower stand-level predictive uncertainty for latent stand-mean GSV. This advantage may partly reflect closer temporal alignment with field data and finer-scale predictor information. These findings suggest that timely, information-rich LiDAR data may be more beneficial for stand-level GSV estimation than increasingly complex spatial model structures. ULS is promising where timely acquisition and high-resolution canopy characterization are operationally feasible. Controlled comparisons with temporally matched ALS and ULS data are needed to distinguish platform effects from temporal mismatch.

</details>


### [376] [Scale or speed? When do path signatures improve volatility-regime forecasts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06690)
*Yang Lyu,Kiseop Lee*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 路径签名对波动率状态的样本外预测有所帮助，但其增益高度依赖于状态差异的具体形式，且难以转化为经济收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径签名日益被用作收益率路径的模型无关（model-free）特征，但尚不清楚它能否真正改进波动率状态的样本外预测，以及这种改进的条件与经济价值。

Method: 使用2005—2025年美国及发达市场股票日度收益率，比较机器学习分类器与异质自回归（HAR）已实现波动率回归；将原始签名加入HAR回归；并通过受控的Heston模型实验解释实证模式。

Result: 分类器未能超越HAR，但签名提升了美国分类器的准确率与概率预测；将原始签名加入HAR后，美国下月波动率三分位预测的平衡准确率提高2.7个百分点，排序概率得分下降，且通过多重检验校正，而传统波动率特征无此效果；美国以外无增益。Heston实验表明，签名在状态仅方差水平不同时增益有限（已实现波动率已捕捉该信息），在状态共享同一平稳方差律但均值回复速度不同时增益更大。更好的状态识别并未带来更早的状态转换信号，准确率增益也未转化为波动率管理组合中显著的Sharpe比率提升。

Conclusion: 路径签名提供了已实现波动率预测因子所遗漏的关于波动率如何演化的信息，但其增益依赖于状态差异的具体机制，且未必能转化为经济收益。

Abstract: We study when path signatures, increasingly used as model-free features of return paths, improve out-of-sample forecasts of volatility regimes. In daily United States and developed-market equity returns from 2005 to 2025, machine-learning classifiers do not beat a heterogeneous autoregressive (HAR) regression of realized volatility, although signatures improve the classifiers' accuracy and probability forecasts in the United States. Adding raw signatures to the HAR regression itself improves United States forecasts of next-month volatility terciles, raising balanced accuracy by 2.7 percentage points and lowering the ranked probability score after multiple-testing correction, whereas adding conventional volatility features does not; outside the United States no gain survives correction. Controlled Heston experiments explain the pattern: signatures add modestly when regimes differ in variance level, which realized volatility already captures, and more when regimes share one stationary variance law but differ in mean-reversion speed. Better regime identification does not, however, deliver earlier regime-change signals, and no accuracy gain translates into a resolved Sharpe-ratio gain in a volatility-managed portfolio. Signatures thus add information about how volatility evolves, which realized-volatility predictors miss.

</details>


### [377] [How Low Can You Go? Validating Administrative Union Records for Substate Estimates of US Union Membership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06774)
*John S. Ahlquist,Eric Thai*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文检验了用联邦"LM"申报数据聚合估算美国县级、通勤区、国会选区工会密度的做法是否可靠，发现其与基于CPS的多层回归后分层（MrP）基准在次州级地理单元上相关性大幅下降，结论是LM聚合不宜用于精细地理尺度的工会密度测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工会是地方层面的重要经济与政治行为者，但美国工会会员数据的主要来源CPS在州以下层面不可靠。研究者因此转向工会向联邦"LM"申报的会员数据，通过地理定位并聚合来估算县、通勤区和国会选区的工会密度。作者要追问这一做法是否站得住脚。

Method: 作者构建并论证以CPS为基础的多层回归后分层（MrP）估计作为基准，然后将基于LM的估计从州级向下逐级与该基准进行验证比较，并考察公共部门工会会员缺失等潜在解释因素，最后在通勤区"中国冲击"回归中检验不同密度度量对结果的影响。

Result: 在州级，LM、CPS与MrP高度相关，但在国会选区、通勤区和县级，相关性大约减半。这一失效部分源于申报的"块状性"（lumpiness），产生诸如工会密度超过100%等不合理数值。公共部门工会会员缺失只能解释这种分歧的一小部分。在标准的通勤区"中国冲击"回归中，替换密度度量会改变进口暴露系数的大小、显著性与符号。

Conclusion: LM记录仍是研究工会作为组织的丰富资源，但聚合LM申报的会员数据并非在精细地理分辨率下测量工会密度的可靠方法。

Abstract: Unions are important economic and political actors at the local level, but the primary source on US union membership, the Current Population Survey (CPS), is unreliable below the state level. Researchers have therefore turned to union-reported membership in federal "LM" filings, geolocating unions and aggregating their reported membership to estimate union density for counties, commuting zones, and congressional districts. We ask whether this practice is sound. We develop and defend multilevel regression with poststratification (MrP) on the CPS as a benchmark, then validate LM-based estimates against it from the state level down. LM, CPS, and MrP are highly correlated at the state level, but this correlation is roughly halved at the congressional-district, commuting-zone, and county levels. The breakdown is driven in part by reporting "lumpiness," which produces improbable values, including union densities exceeding 100%. Missing public sector union members explains only a small part of this divergence. These issues are substantively consequential: in a standard commuting-zone "China shock" regression, swapping density measures changes the import-exposure coefficient enough to alter its magnitude, significance, and sign. We conclude that LM records remain a rich resource for studying unions as organizations, but aggregating LM-reported membership is not a reliable way to measure union density at fine geographic resolution.

</details>
